# 151: Chapter 152: Let’s go

"Roar~!"

The roar of Black Kyurem was much deeper and wilder compared to its White Kyurem form.

Along with the roar, Black Kyurem's entire body surged forward, enveloped in bright blue lightning. The arcs of electricity formed a halo, wrapping around Black Kyurem, showcasing its power and brilliance.

This was Zekrom's Fusion Bolt, but Black Kyurem could also use it.

"Necrozma, Photon Geyser," Dylan calmly commanded Dawn Wings Necrozma.

Dawn Wings Necrozma kept retreating, golden light flickering at the tips of its wings.

A Psychic-type Photon Geyser!

The golden beam shot forth, but Black Kyurem charged straight ahead without dodging.

Boom!

The Photon Geyser exploded directly on Black Kyurem's body, but the next moment, the terrifying energy swirling around was pierced by Black Kyurem, now covered in blue arcs of electricity, charging forward. The sight was horrifying.

"So violent," Dylan raised an eyebrow.

The power of Black Kyurem was indeed overwhelming, and as a top-tier Dragon-type legendary, its physical strength was exceptional.

Dawn Wings Necrozma continued to retreat, trying to keep its distance from Black Kyurem, but the gap between them was closing.

In terms of speed, Black Kyurem was significantly faster than Dawn Wings Necrozma.

"Moongeist Beam."

But that was fine—let's go head-to-head then!

Dawn Wings Necrozma spread its wings wide, dark energy swirling around it like a shadowy full moon.

Ghost-type Moongeist Beam!

This was Lunala's signature move, but when Necrozma fused with Lunala, Dawn Wings Necrozma could also use Moongeist Beam!

A brilliant dark beam shot out, hitting Black Kyurem right as it approached.

Boom!!!

The Moongeist Beam and Fusion Bolt collided fiercely, the resulting shockwaves shaking the air as turbulent winds howled like a hurricane.

With a thunderous boom, the Moongeist Beam exploded, and the violent energy knocked both Dawn Wings Necrozma and Black Kyurem backward.

"Roar!!"

But the next moment, Black Kyurem let out an enraged roar, stabilizing its body and once again charging toward Dawn Wings Necrozma.

At the same time, pale blue crystalline energy began to glow on Black Kyurem's body, flickering with arcs of electricity.

This was Black Kyurem's strongest signature move, Freeze Shock!

What a crazy guy—does he never stop attacking? Dylan couldn't help but feel a little helpless seeing this.

"Necrozma, wait for it to come closer."

At Black Kyurem's charging speed, Dawn Wings Necrozma couldn't dodge, but Kyurem was indeed clever.

It seemed to have guessed that Dawn Wings Necrozma was better suited for long-range combat, so it switched to its Black form for close combat.

The colorful parts of Dawn Wings Necrozma's head glowed faintly.

As Black Kyurem rapidly approached with Freeze Shock, the intense cold caused even Dawn Wings Necrozma to feel some discomfort.

"Protect!"

A green barrier of Protect unfolded in front of Dawn Wings Necrozma.

Boom!

Freeze Shock slammed into the Protect barrier with a violent crash. The surface of the barrier trembled, but it barely managed to withstand the impact of Freeze Shock.

The blue-white crystalline energy, mixed with electricity, continued to assault the barrier, and soon, a thick layer of ice began to form on its surface.

Roar~!

With Black Kyurem's fierce roar, cracks started to appear on the Protect barrier.

Crack~!

Freeze Shock exhausted its power, and the Protect barrier shattered into fragments and dissipated.

"Glad it held up," Dylan's eyes flickered slightly. Although Dawn Wings Necrozma's level wasn't as high as Black Kyurem's, the gap shouldn't have been so great that Protect couldn't hold.

But seeing the barrier nearly shatter made Dylan a little worried.

Although the Protect had broken, the distance between Black Kyurem and Dawn Wings Necrozma was now very close.

Black Kyurem immediately raised its powerful arm, slashing the air with Shadow Claw toward Dawn Wings Necrozma.

A Ghost-type Shadow Claw!

"Necrozma, Dragon Pulse!"

Dawn Wings Necrozma retreated sideways as a Dragon Pulse formed in front of it, but Black Kyurem's movements were even faster.

The deep, dark Shadow Claw struck Dawn Wings Necrozma directly, sending it flying back with a painful grunt.

It was four times weak to Ghost-type attacks, and even with the support of Prism Armor, Dawn Wings Necrozma was still vulnerable.

However, as it was knocked back, Dawn Wings Necrozma's Dragon Pulse was already fired, hitting Black Kyurem as it attempted to pursue.

The dragon-shaped pulse exploded on Black Kyurem's body, and it let out a painful roar—dragon versus dragon!

Though Black Kyurem was an Ice-type, Ice-type had no resistance to Dragon-type moves, so Dragon Pulse was still effective against Black Kyurem.

"Roar~!"

But the pain seemed to only fuel Black Kyurem's ferocity as it roared and charged once again toward Dawn Wings Necrozma.

The shadowy claws ripped through the air, slashing toward Dawn Wings Necrozma once more—a Ghost-type Shadow Claw again.

Why does this guy love Shadow Claw so much?

Dylan frowned slightly, recalling that Kyurem in the Sacred Sword movie also had a particular fondness for using Shadow Claw in close combat.

It was strange, but Shadow Claw wasn't good news! Dawn Wings Necrozma was extremely weak to Ghost-types, and being hit by consecutive Shadow Claws was taking a heavy toll on it.

At this rate, it couldn't last. Even if they kept exchanging blows, the first to fall would undoubtedly be Dawn Wings Necrozma. Not only did it lack the level advantage, but it also had no type advantage.

If it were Dusk  Mane Necrozma in its Solgaleo form, there might have been a chance to turn the tide, perhaps even defeat this Kyurem.

Time to retreat, and come back later to take down this Ice Dragon.

"Necrozma, use Prismatic Laser to force it back!"

Dylan made up his mind and telepathically commanded Dawn Wings Necrozma.

"Necrozma~!"

Dawn Wings Necrozma let out a low growl, raising its massive claws as rainbow-colored psychic energy gathered.

The Psychic-type Prismatic Laser!

This was Necrozma's most powerful signature move, with a base power of 160! Normally, it was the strongest Psychic-type move!

Black Kyurem had already closed in, its dark Shadow Claw slashing down, hitting Dawn Wings Necrozma directly.

"Necrozma~!"

Dawn Wings Necrozma cried out in pain, but it raised its claws high as a massive amount of energy began to gather overhead.

Boom! Boom! Boom!

Like a downpour, countless Prismatic Laser beams rained down, hitting Black Kyurem directly.

The barrage of Prismatic Laser beams exploded on Black Kyurem's body, forcing it to the ground as it roared angrily, unable to lift its head.

"Reshiram, let's go!"

Seeing that Black Kyurem was being suppressed by the Prismatic Laser, Dylan didn't hesitate at all. He and Lillie quickly switched to riding Reshiram, preparing to make their escape.

"Good!" Reshiram immediately turned, flapping its wings as it sped away, while Dylan took out the Ultra Ball and recalled Dawn Wings Necrozma.

Looking back at Black Kyurem, which was still being pinned down by the Prismatic Laser, Dylan sighed. It seemed that time was still too short; they hadn't been able to take down this beast yet.

But that's alright—once Necrozma absorbs enough light and transforms into Ultra Necrozma, defeating Kyurem won't be an issue.

That Ice Dragon will be mine sooner or later!

After Reshiram carried Dylan and Lillie far away, the Prismatic Laser finally ended. Black Kyurem looked around, but the surroundings were now empty.

Especially its Giant Chasm, which had been devastated by the aftershocks of the battle and the bombardment from the Prismatic Laser. It was now a ruined mess.

Black Kyurem panted heavily, not from injury, but from sheer, overwhelming anger.

They ran! They actually ran!

"ROAR~!!"

The enraged roar shook the entire Giant Chasm, causing ice to shoot out wildly. In an instant, the entire chasm was covered in a fresh layer of ice crystals, once again looking majestic and beautiful.

But that did nothing to quell Black Kyurem's fury.

Black Kyurem shot up into the sky, sensing the direction of their escape. Its dragon eyes gleamed with a cold light—it wasn't going to let them get away!

Meanwhile, Reshiram, having flown quite a distance, slowed down slightly, finally able to relax a little. It seemed they were safe for now.

"Reshiram, can you sense where Zekrom is?"

"I can, Dylan. Are we going to fight the Black Dragon?"

Reshiram immediately perked up at the mention of Zekrom—it was always eager when it came to fighting Zekrom.

"Weren't you just injured by Kyurem?" Dylan laughed in exasperation.

"This little injury is nothing! Dylan, trust me, I will bring you victory and glory!" Reshiram lifted its head high, dismissing Zekrom as insignificant.

"As long as it's not Kyurem, I, Reshiram, fear no opponent!"

"Really?" Dylan smirked.

"Of course it's true, Dylan! You can't blame me for what happened earlier—Kyurem is too strong, even hurting it would have been difficult."

Reshiram, knowing that its earlier performance in the Giant Chasm had been somewhat embarrassing, tried to salvage its dignity.

Besides, it could have lasted longer, but actually injuring Kyurem would have required a trade of blows. However, it wasn't clear how much damage it could have dealt to Kyurem in return.

Lillie covered her mouth, trying to stifle her laughter.

"Alright then, I'll be watching your performance the next time we face a different opponent," Dylan said as he stroked Reshiram's fur.

"For now, contact Zekrom and arrange a battle here."

"Got it!"

Reshiram immediately sent out its psychic signal. Its connection with Zekrom was special—no matter how far apart they were, they could always locate each other and communicate telepathically. After all, they were twins.

Dylan glanced back. Given Kyurem's temper, it definitely wouldn't let them go easily. There was a 90% chance it would pursue them.

Dylan didn't want to be constantly hounded by Kyurem, so finding someone to take the heat was essential.

Hmm, N and Zekrom would be the perfect candidates for that.

Meanwhile, on the other side...

N, along with Colress, had just hijacked a police car and was now looking worriedly at the disheveled Ghetsis before him.

"Father, are you alright?" For once, N's usually emotionless eyes showed genuine concern.

"I'm fine, N. It's all thanks to you this time," Ghetsis said with a dark expression, forcing a smile to thank N.

Colress, standing nearby, smirked subtly—his tone couldn't have been more fake.

But N, who trusted Ghetsis deeply, didn't notice this at all.

"Sorry, Father. I only received the news today, and you suffered because of it."

"But how did you..."

N was still puzzled, not understanding how Ghetsis had been caught by Officer Jenny in Icirrus City.

"It was a boy. That despicable brat deliberately mocked our ideals, enraging me. I fell into his trap!"

Ghetsis' face darkened further. He, the leader of Team Plasma, had spent the night in Icirrus City's jail!

What a disgrace! An utter humiliation!!

N furrowed his brows deeply, but at that moment...

"N."

"What's the matter, Zekrom?" N responded quietly to Zekrom's telepathic message.

"Reshiram has sent a battle challenge. Should we go?"

Zekrom's deep, calm voice echoed in N's mind. As opposing dragons, it wasn't surprising that Reshiram, now awake and with a new hero, had issued a challenge.

"The true hero, huh?"

N thought for a moment before quickly making a decision. Since the other side wanted a fight, there was no reason to refuse.

Ideals will never lose to truth! Never!

"Let's go. I want to meet this true hero myself."

(End of Chapter)

Happy New Year (2025)

---

---

# 152: Chapter 153: Alright, let’s begin

"The message has already been sent, Dylan. The Black Dragon and its hero should be arriving soon. We..."

Before Reshiram could finish speaking through telepathy, snowflakes had already started to fall.

Reshiram paused, turning to see a grey-black Ice Dragon rapidly approaching from the distance.

"Big brother! The Ice Dragon is chasing us!" Lillie exclaimed in shock, quickly turning to Dylan.

"Don't panic—it's faster than I expected."

Dylan was somewhat surprised. He had expected that Kyurem wouldn't let them off easily, but he hadn't thought it would pursue them so quickly.

Still, it wasn't a big problem. As long as they had Necrozma, Kyurem wouldn't be able to hurt them.

Moreover, Dylan always had an ace up his sleeve, though using that method would cause a massive impact.

"Fly faster, head towards the Black Dragon," Dylan patted Reshiram, signaling it to speed up.

Reshiram didn't respond, but the turbine in its tail began spinning rapidly as flames ignited.

Accelerate!

Reshiram began picking up speed, but Dark Kyurem was faster, and it too was in full pursuit.

The gap between the two dragons shrank rapidly, with snowflakes continuously falling wherever Dark Kyurem passed.

The weather itself was being affected.

Fortunately, thanks to Dylan directing Reshiram away from cities and villages, they had avoided causing an "earthquake" across Unova.

However, the chase between Reshiram and Dark Kyurem didn't go unnoticed by people out in the wild. Though, due to the speed of the two dragons, they couldn't see them clearly.

"Necrozma, use Prismatic Laser."

Dylan opened the Ultra Ball, and Dawn Wings Necrozma appeared. A beam of golden light erupted, shooting towards the rapidly approaching Dark Kyurem.

Dark Kyurem roared in fury, its body crackling with blue and white energy, arcs of electricity flashing wildly.

Freeze Shock!

Boom!

The Prismatic Laser struck Dark Kyurem in its Freeze Shock state, creating a continuous thunderous explosion and shockwaves that rippled outward.

Though Dark Kyurem quickly broke through, Necrozma's attack had indeed slowed it down.

Dylan then recalled Dusk Mane Necrozma, and Reshiram resumed flying at top speed.

About half an hour later, Reshiram stopped above a mountainous wilderness, as a pitch-black dragon floated in front of them.

Riding atop Zekrom was none other than N.

"Hey, long time no see, N," Dylan greeted N warmly with a smile. Not bad—the Black Dragon had arrived faster than he expected.

"So, it's you... Dylan..." N's eyes lit up slightly as he gazed at the pure white Reshiram, then turned to Dylan, a barely noticeable smile tugging at the corners of his lips.

N had always felt something special about Dylan, and now that it was confirmed Dylan was the one who had awakened the True Dragon, N found himself feeling a bit pleased.

Yes, both he and Dylan were special individuals. With their unique traits, didn't that make them perfect rivals?

"In that case, Dylan, let me witness the power of the legendary Reshiram! My Zekrom will never be defeated..." N's voice grew more passionate, already looking forward to this battle of truth versus ideals!

"Wait a second, N. As much as I'd love to have a showdown with you, today's not really the best time. That big guy behind us has been chasing us relentlessly. Could you and the Black Dragon hold it off for a bit?"

Dylan interrupted N, glancing behind him—Dark Kyurem was getting close again.

N: "???"

Zekrom: "..."

"Let's go, Reshiram."

Reshiram let out a melodious dragon cry in response and immediately flapped its wings, flying away from Zekrom's side.

"..."

N frowned as he watched Reshiram and Dylan leave, feeling confused and puzzled—why?

But suddenly, the Black Dragon's expression changed dramatically. It had spotted Dark Kyurem rapidly approaching from the distance.

"N! We need to get out of here, now!"

Zekrom was shocked—was that really Kyurem?! And judging by its aura, it was clearly enraged!

What was Reshiram thinking, provoking Kyurem of all beings?!

Moreover...

This had to be intentional! Reshiram and its hero hadn't summoned them for a battle at all.

They had called them to distract Kyurem and take the heat off themselves!

"Huh?" N blinked in surprise.

But before N could ask any questions, an overwhelming, icy pressure descended. The rampaging form of Dark Kyurem had already entered N's line of sight.

"Black Dragon!!"

Dark Kyurem's enraged roar echoed across the sky.

"First the White Dragon, and now you, Black Dragon! You're both worthless!"

The cruel, ferocious gleam in Dark Kyurem's golden eyes made Zekrom's skin crawl.

What on earth had Reshiram done to Dark Kyurem to make it this angry?!

"I'm going to turn both of you into stone for the next ten thousand years!!"

Dark Kyurem's wrathful roar resounded again as blue-white energy lit up its body, forming an arc-shaped barrier—Freeze Shock!

The overwhelming power surged forward, and N's pupils contracted sharply—what kind of terrifying entity was this?

After being evaded by Reshiram and Dusk Mane Necrozma for half an hour, Dark Kyurem's rage had nearly reached its breaking point.

"Dylan!!" N was both shocked and furious. Now he fully understood—he had been used as a scapegoat!

Dylan had provoked this Ice Dragon, and now he was leaving N to face the consequences!

"Wait! Kyurem! It was the White Dragon that angered you—you should be going after the White Dragon!"

Zekrom's deep, steady voice tried to reason with the furious Ice Dragon. There was no need for this—blame should be placed where it belonged!

Zekrom was so innocent!

"Doesn't matter! Neither of you is getting away!"

Dark Kyurem's sinister and terrifying words rang out as it unleashed Freeze Shock toward Zekrom.

"White Dragon! I curse you!!"

Zekrom cursed Reshiram inwardly but wasted no time—there was no way it was going to fight Kyurem head-on.

Yes, the Black Dragon made a run for it.

Meanwhile...

"Ah~ so refreshing~"

Reshiram landed in a lush green meadow, breathing in the fragrant air free from cold. It felt as though its entire being had been purified.

So satisfying!!

"As expected of you, Dylan. In human terms, this move of 'using another's sword to kill' was brilliant~" Reshiram looked admiringly at its hero. Now, the Black Dragon would either be dead or at least badly injured.

"It feels so real! I love it!"

Lillie: (—)

"Don't get too excited yet. Zekrom won't be able to hold Kyurem off for too long."

Dylan thought to himself. Although they were already far away, if Kyurem's rage wasn't fully vented, it probably wouldn't give up on chasing the white dragon.

"No worries, as long as we run far enough, the ice dragon won't keep pursuing us," Reshiram said confidently.

"Fine, I'll make a call first."

Dylan nodded, knowing that as long as they distanced themselves and Reshiram suppressed its aura, Kyurem wouldn't be able to find them.

However, Dylan had another idea and wanted to take advantage of this opportunity.

"Hello? President Goodshow, good afternoon."

Dylan called President Goodshow, who was far away in Kanto.

"Dylan! How's it going? Did you find Reshiram?" Goodshow asked, laughing.

"Of course! I've already earned Reshiram's recognition, but something unusual happened over here."

"Oh? Hahaha! I knew you'd pull it off, Dylan! I didn't misjudge you!" Goodshow was momentarily surprised but then overjoyed.

The legendary white dragon of the Unova region! Dylan actually managed to awaken and capture it.

What an achievement! And since Dylan was from the Kanto region, this brought great honor to them.

"When are you coming back? Come to the Indigo Plateau, I'll personally prepare a feast for you."

Goodshow was ecstatic. He had always liked Dylan, but now he was even more fond of him. This wasn't just Dylan's victory—it was Kanto's future glory, an even greater one than Lance's!

"President, let me explain first. I've angered the legendary strongest dragon of Unova, Kyurem, and the situation here is a bit tricky."

"Kyurem? Dylan, are you okay?"

"I'm fine for now."

"Good, good. Then hurry back to Kanto. Unova is too dangerous." Goodshow sighed in relief.

"I actually have a way to defeat Kyurem, but it might cause a bit of a stir."

"What kind of stir?" Goodshow was puzzled again.

"Well...the sky might turn dark." Dylan glanced at the clear sky and spoke vaguely.

Lillie was taken aback but quickly understood. Reshiram, on the other hand, tilted its head in confusion—what did he mean by the sky turning dark?

"Turning dark? That's no problem, Dylan. Do what you need to. If anything happens, I've got your back," Goodshow said grandly.

Although he had a nagging feeling that the "dark sky" sounded familiar, he didn't think much of it. It's just a dark sky—what could go wrong? Not a big deal.

"Haha, thanks, President. I felt a bit bad about absorbing all of Unova's light, and it might stay dark for a day or two before it recovers."

A smile spread across Dylan's face.

"No worries, Dylan! I'll always be your strong support…" Goodshow chuckled, but his laughter abruptly stopped as he realized something.

"Wait, Dylan, what did you just say?"

Goodshow wondered if he had misheard. All of Unova's light? A couple of days of darkness? What kind of joke was that?

"Exactly what I just said, but don't worry, President. Other than the darkness, there won't be any other effects. The light will come back in a day or two." Dylan reassured him.

Goodshow was silent. Could he take back his words? It seemed like supporting Dylan was harder than he thought.

"President, you said you'd always have my back…"

"Dylan… this is a bit tricky…" Goodshow said with a bitter smile. This wasn't a small matter—it was an entire region's light. Two days of darkness would cause a significant panic.

"If it's difficult for you, then forget it. I just thought it'd benefit Unova. If I can capture Kyurem…"

"Wait, you're planning to capture Kyurem?"

Goodshow's tone suddenly shifted.

"That's right. As long as I have enough light, I can capture Kyurem. But I understand it might be tough for you to manage." Dylan said with a hint of regret.

"No, no, Dylan! Go for it! As a young man, you should pursue your goals! I do have some influence, after all!" Goodshow said seriously, quickly changing his tune.

The strongest dragon of Unova, Kyurem. As the President of the Pokémon League, Goodshow knew a bit about it.

If anyone else claimed they could capture Kyurem, he wouldn't believe them. But since Dylan had just earned Reshiram's recognition, Goodshow felt Dylan might actually pull it off.

If Dylan could capture Kyurem, wouldn't that make him invincible? And if Dylan was invincible, wouldn't Kanto also become invincible?

Giving up a couple of days of light in Unova in exchange for the legendary ice dragon Kyurem? Goodshow considered it a worthwhile trade.

At worst, they could compensate Unova with a huge sum of money for their lost light—although it would be a staggering amount, it wasn't a big deal. The League wasn't short on funds.

"That's why you're the best, President! I knew you were reliable!" Dylan's grin widened.

He informed Goodshow mainly because the event might be too significant, causing panic in Unova. But with Goodshow backing him, they could issue an announcement to calm the people down, preventing chaos.

After ending the call with Goodshow…

Dylan released Necrozma in its Dawn Wings form.

"Alright, let's begin."

Dylan watched as Necrozma slowly rose into the sky, its body starting to glow. Like a black hole, it began absorbing all the light around it.

Before long, people all over Unova began looking up at the sky.

The sky… had gone dark!

(End of Chapter)

Happy New Year (2025)

# 153: NOT A CHAPTER

Hello everyone,

I wanted to let you know about a recent issue on Webnovel. They have been deleting a lot of translated fan fiction, which has been worrying for many of us. Although my translation hasn't been affected yet, I want to make sure you're all aware of what might happen.

To keep my work safe and ensure you can continue reading without any disruptions, I'm planning to move my translated fan fiction to Scribble Hub or Royal Road. I value your support and want to make sure you can keep enjoying the story. I'll keep you updated on any changes.

Thank you for understanding and being such wonderful readers.

# 154: Chapter 154: Welcome aboard

Everyone in the Unova region was stunned, as the sky turned black all of a sudden. It only lasted for a few seconds—was it even three seconds? And it wasn't even four in the afternoon yet. The sky simply went dark without warning.

What… what is happening?

Some people had their Pokémon use Sunny Day, but the sunlight it created immediately vanished as soon as it appeared.

This sudden change threw everyone into a panic, and the whole Unova region descended into chaos.

Police departments in various towns and cities were inundated with calls. However, the Unova League quickly issued a special statement, claiming it was just a natural phenomenon and there was no need to worry.

The statement urged people to stay indoors for the next few days and wait for the darkness to pass.

Many were still skeptical, but since the Unova League had spoken, they temporarily put their fears to rest.

Meanwhile, on the other side...

The sky and earth were illuminated by a grayish-blue glow, as a massive dragon of light stood between the heavens and the earth.

Shiny Ultra Necrozma!!

Reshiram was stunned. As a fellow Dragon-type Pokémon, it could clearly sense the dragon aura emanating from Ultra Necrozma.

Hmm, its status among Dragon-types dropped by 1 point.

"What is this…?"

Lillie gazed in awe at the dragon of light. It was simply too divine!

This was completely different from the Dawn Wings Necrozma they had encountered before. Under the grayish-blue glow, parts of its body were connected by beams of light, with two pairs of wings hovering at its sides and huge feet planted firmly on the ground.

Not only had its appearance changed, but this new Necrozma gave Lillie a completely different feeling as well.

The sensation was unlike anything she had felt when facing the ferocious Ice Dragon. She had sensed fear from Black Kyurem because of its power and the overwhelming pressure it exuded.

But standing before this Ultra Necrozma felt entirely different.

It was a sensation she couldn't put into words, as if a lower being was naturally aware of its insignificance in the presence of a higher being.

If she had to describe Ultra Necrozma in one word...

It would be "God"!

Ding~ Ultra Necrozma, Psychic/Dragon-type, Prism Pokémon

Ability: Neuroforce

Signature Move: …

Description: The light emitted from its body affects living beings and nature, causing various effects.

Its moves hadn't changed, but clearly, Necrozma had become something completely different.

This was the transformation brought about by absorbing all the light in the Unova region!

"How are you feeling, Necrozma?" Dylan admired the power of this Ultra Necrozma. It was perfect!

"I feel great—better than ever~" Ultra Necrozma's voice, once low and deep, now had a much gentler telepathic tone.

"Good to hear. Now, let's go find Kyurem," Dylan said, a smile tugging at the corners of his mouth.

Kyurem, your time has come!

"Roar!!"

Ultra Necrozma raised its head and let out a dragon roar, using its psychic powers to lift Dylan, Lillie, and Reshiram into the air. The group soared high into the sky, flying at incredible speed toward a specific direction.

Having achieved its ultimate form, Ultra Necrozma's psychic power had been significantly enhanced, allowing it to sense Kyurem's location.

At that very moment, on a distant coastline...

Boom!

Under the darkened sky, Zekrom collapsed to the ground, its black, stone-like body battered and covered with frost. It was severely weakened.

It was clear that Zekrom had been mercilessly beaten.

Inwardly, Zekrom cursed a certain pure-white dragon.

White Dragon! I can't believe you set me up like this!!

"Black Dragon, is this all you've got?"

Under the gloomy sky, Black Kyurem looked down at Zekrom, its body emitting a faint black-and-blue glow, exuding a deep and terrifying aura.

"From today onward, you'll become an eternal stone!"

The turbine at the base of Black Kyurem's tail began to glow with icy blue energy.

It had given up on devouring Zekrom and Reshiram. Instead, it planned to turn them into eternal stones!

When Zekrom and Reshiram's physical bodies were destroyed, they wouldn't die. Instead, they would revert into Dragon Stones, falling into a deep sleep until they were one day awakened again.

The length of this "sleep" depended on the extent of their injuries, and clearly, Black Kyurem was planning to inflict a heavy one on Zekrom.

"Kyurem, don't you get it? The White Dragon lured me here on purpose! Just to distract you!" Zekrom's deep voice sounded again, attempting to negotiate with Kyurem.

"It doesn't matter. After I deal with you, I'll go find the White Dragon."

Black Kyurem's golden eyes flashed with murderous intent, and its aura grew even more oppressive.

But at that moment, a golden beam of light shot across the sky, aimed directly at Black Kyurem.

Sensing the danger, Black Kyurem quickly turned around, ready to retaliate, but the beam of light was even faster than expected.

Photon Geyser!

The attack struck Black Kyurem head-on, sending a wave of intense pain coursing through its body and drawing an enraged roar from it.

The next moment, Black Kyurem was hurled onto a small mountain by the Photon Geyser.

Boom!

The loud explosion shook the heavens, and the golden glow illuminated half the sky.

What… just happened? Zekrom was stunned.

But in the next instant, Zekrom snapped out of it. Now was not the time to think about that. Whoever attacked Black Kyurem was their savior! Thank goodness!

Grateful to its unseen rescuer, Zekrom fought through the pain and cold, struggling to take flight and flee.

In its current state, it was too dangerous to stay here. It needed to retreat and recover. Earlier, during its pursuit by Black Kyurem, it had dropped N off, not wanting him to get caught up in the battle. Now, Zekrom had to go find him.

"Roar!!"

No sooner had Zekrom fled than Black Kyurem's furious roar echoed across the landscape.

Crack, crack!

A wave of freezing cold rapidly spread, instantly turning the entire area into an ice field.

Black Kyurem shot into the sky, its golden eyes glaring at the distant horizon.

A massive grayish-blue dragon was speeding toward it, with the White Dragon—Reshiram—along with Dylan and Lillie on its back, also entering Black Kyurem's sight.

It was them again—Reshiram and these two humans!

Black Kyurem's eyes gleamed with ferocity, but its focus ultimately shifted to the enormous grayish-blue dragon.

Though the pressure it now felt was entirely different, this aura was still familiar.

It was the same pink-and-black bat Pokémon from before, but how had it changed so drastically? And grown so much stronger?

"Kyurem, we meet again," Dylan said with a smile, greeting Black Kyurem.

"Well, well, humans and the White Dragon. You dare return!" Black Kyurem's raspy telepathic voice was full of malice.

"Why wouldn't we? After all, I still want to capture you, Kyurem," Dylan said, his gaze fixed on Unova's strongest dragon.

"Still the same old talk, Kyurem. Are you really content hiding away, despite your immense power? The whole world should know the name Kyurem," Dylan spoke with sincerity.

"What a joke! Do you think I'm the White Dragon? I've never needed recognition from weak humans to validate my power."

Black Kyurem sneered in disdain. To it, humans were frail creatures, unworthy of acknowledging its strength or name. It was pointless.

"I see. I guess I'll have to defeat you to capture you," Dylan sighed, already expecting this outcome.

"Hahaha! Greedy fool! The White Dragon may be weak, but after acquiring it, you still have the nerve to covet me?" Black Kyurem laughed mockingly. It had barely managed to escape earlier, and now Dylan's greed had driven him to return.

Kyurem's opinion of humans remained unchanged.

"Do you think you can beat me with that unfamiliar dragon? You're too arrogant, human!!" Black Kyurem's eyes gleamed with menace.

"Then how about this: if I defeat you, will you follow me, Kyurem?" Dylan proposed.

"Hmph~ There's no chance! Today, you'll all become eternal ice sculptures!"

Dark Kyurem was incredibly arrogant, as it should be, for it was Kyurem—the strongest dragon on earth!

Merely a defeated foe, it thought. Did it think changing its appearance would enable it to defeat Kyurem? Kyurem found it amusing.

"Roar~!"

Blue and white ice energy flowed around Kyurem's body, forming a barrier as electricity crackled. This was Freeze Shock!

A thunderous dragon's roar echoed as Dark Kyurem charged at Ultra Necrozma with wild, overwhelming power.

Feel the might of the strongest dragon! Dark Kyurem could sense that this opponent had also acquired dragon-like energy after transforming, which meant it too was a dragon.

Perfect! As an ice dragon, my favorite opponent to fight is other dragons!

"Such a domineering dragon, Necrozma," Dylan raised his eyebrow at the sight.

"Photon Geyser!"

"Boom~!"

Ultra Necrozma stretched its body as black light swirled before gradually turning golden.

Photon Geyser!

A golden energy sphere shot out, crashing into the charging Dark Kyurem.

Boom!

The Photon Geyser collided with Freeze Shock, golden and blue-white light filling the sky, painting the dark heavens with two colors.

Dark Kyurem's face twisted in fury, desperately forcing out more power, but it could feel Freeze Shock slowly being overwhelmed by Photon Geyser.

How is this possible??!! How could it be this strong?!

Power, come forth!!

Dark Kyurem roared in rage as the Freeze Shock energy around its body swelled.

With a deafening explosion, the Photon Geyser erupted, sending a tangible golden shockwave outward.

The surrounding area was engulfed in a gale as Ultra Necrozma flapped its wings. Psychic energy flooded the scene, instantly calming the raging winds.

Dark Kyurem, blasted back by the explosion, crashed heavily into the ground, causing a minor earthquake.

"That was insane!!" Lillie exclaimed, eyes wide as she saw Ultra Necrozma bring down the mighty and arrogant Kyurem with one move.

Reshiram's dragon eyes widened in disbelief too.

They had expected Ultra Necrozma to be strong, but not this powerful. In a direct clash, it had actually defeated Dark Kyurem.

"So strong~!" Dylan couldn't help but be impressed. Despite Ultra Necrozma's transformation, its actual level hadn't increased.

In other words, in terms of sheer level, Ultra Necrozma was still below Dark Kyurem, but its power had made up for that difference.

Ultra Necrozma's power was truly immense—worthy of being labeled as the Pokémon that surpassed all past legends.

"Roar!!"

But just then, Dark Kyurem's roar echoed once again.

The gray-black dragon rose into the sky, panting heavily, its golden eyes locked onto Ultra Necrozma.

This is impossible! Kyurem couldn't accept it!!

In an instant, flames surged over Dark Kyurem's body, and in the blink of an eye, it transformed into White Kyurem. If close combat wasn't working, it would resort to long-range attacks!

The turbine at White Kyurem's tail lit up red, as spirals of purplish-red flames swirled around its body—scorching and icy cold at the same time.

White Kyurem's signature move: Ice Burn!

With a roar, White Kyurem unleashed a purplish-red flame beam toward Ultra Necrozma.

"Photon Geyser!"

The black hole-like energy condensed once more in front of Ultra Necrozma, turning golden before firing.

Ice Burn and Photon Geyser collided fiercely, and the two terrifying energies clashed violently. White Kyurem's face contorted in extreme effort as it pushed Ice Burn to its limit.

But soon, Photon Geyser began to overpower Ice Burn.

With an incredulous look on White Kyurem's face, Photon Geyser continued to advance until it struck White Kyurem head-on.

Boom!

The explosion echoed painfully through the air, golden light illuminating the sky. White Kyurem's face twisted in agony as it plummeted to the ground like a broken kite, crashing for the third time.

But soon after, White Kyurem, gasping for breath, rose into the sky again, shifting back to Dark Kyurem and launching another Freeze Shock at Ultra Necrozma.

And then...

It was struck down again by Photon Geyser.

"This Kyurem is so stubborn…" Dylan's expression was strange. Did Kyurem really need to keep exchanging blows with Ultra Necrozma to settle this? Or was it simply unable to accept that it was inferior to Ultra Necrozma in pure power?

Perhaps it was that. This had once been an opponent Kyurem had bested, and now, just hours later, with a new look, it had surpassed Kyurem. For a proud and arrogant Kyurem, this was indeed hard to accept.

Dylan remained silent, but maybe this wasn't so bad after all.

After being struck down by Ultra Necrozma for the fifth time, Kyurem could no longer rise, lying on the ground, its chest heaving.

It had no strength left to stand.

"Well, Kyurem? You've lost."

Reshiram landed as Dylan stepped off and walked toward Kyurem, speaking calmly.

Kyurem turned its head with difficulty, its golden eyes glancing at Dylan before remaining silent.

"A proud dragon like yourself wouldn't be a sore loser, would you?"

Dylan chuckled softly. He wasn't worried that Kyurem wouldn't admit defeat; though Kyurem hadn't explicitly stated that it would become his Pokémon upon losing, it was implicitly understood. Kyurem was too proud to deny it.

Kyurem remained silent.

"Well then, welcome aboard, Kyurem~"

Dylan took out a Luxury Ball and tossed it at Kyurem.

Lillie, standing behind Dylan, held her breath, clasping her hands tightly together.

The moment the ball touched Kyurem, it opened, absorbing the massive dragon into a beam of red light. The ball fell to the ground.

Beep~ Beep~ Beep~ Ding!

The ball shook three times before making a crisp sound and stopping.

Lillie's eyes widened as the ball stilled. That meant… her brother had captured Kyurem!

Oh my gosh!!

The atmosphere grew still for a moment, under the darkened sky, as the grayish-blue light from Ultra Necrozma illuminated the area.

Reshiram looked at the deep red Luxury Ball with complicated emotions. Who could have imagined that one day it would be working with the ice dragon, Kyurem…

Would Kyurem bully it every day from now on?

But looking at it from a different perspective, with Kyurem as its second brother and Ultra Necrozma as the eldest, how would the black dragon even dare to fight it now?

This was a sure win! The black dragon was doomed!!

Reality had truly surpassed ideals—this chosen hero was too fierce!!

Dylan slowly walked up to the Luxury Ball, bending down to pick it up. He wiped the dust off the ball, feeling its texture and weight, his grin growing wider.

The legendary ice dragon, Kyurem—captured!!

---

# 155: Chapter 155: Really?

"Come out, Kyurem."

Dylan didn't hurry to leave; instead, he released the Ice Dragon, Kyurem.

Kyurem appeared in its standard form, its golden dragon eyes fixed intently on Ultra Necrozma, as if lost in thought.

"Not bad, my Kyurem is still as imposing as ever," Dylan remarked, growing increasingly fond of his Kyurem as he sprayed it with a Hyper Potion.

Though its appearance wasn't exactly beautiful, Kyurem's fierce look was certainly distinctive.

Upon hearing this, Kyurem lifted its head slightly—'I'm the most imposing one!'

Lillie blinked her big eyes, feeling a little eager to approach Kyurem, but memories of its previous ferocity made her hesitate.

"How are you? Are your injuries still severe?"

After spraying a bottle of healing medicine on Kyurem, Dylan asked it.

"I've recovered a lot," Kyurem responded, moving its body and speaking in a deep, raspy voice through telepathy.

It could clearly feel a significant improvement in its condition; human medicines were quite effective.

However, more than its physical state, it was still fixated on Ultra Necrozma. How could that being be so powerful?!

After staring at Ultra Necrozma for a while, Kyurem shifted its gaze to Reshiram, the White Dragon, with an unrestrained intensity that made Reshiram's feathers stand on end.

Creepy!

"Dylan, let me 'consume' the White Dragon."

After some thought, Kyurem spoke to Dylan.

"???" Reshiram almost plastered a giant question mark onto Kyurem's face.

Dylan chuckled, glancing at Reshiram.

"No, Kyurem, we are all allies," Dylan shook his head at Kyurem.

"If I devour the White Dragon and then merge with the Black Dragon, I could return to my original form!"

If Kyurem were in human form, its brows would certainly be furrowed at this moment.

"Compared to that useless White Dragon, the Dragon God form would naturally be more beneficial to you, and you wouldn't have to worry about me turning against you."

"Since I've decided to follow you, I won't betray you."

Kyurem spoke in a low tone. It was a proud Ice Dragon, and though it had a violent nature, it never went back on its word—that was its pride.

Besides, a human's lifespan was a mere century; for a being like Kyurem, favored by the world, a hundred years was hardly more than a nap.

A century was nothing to Kyurem, so there was no need to break its promise.

"…"

Reshiram stared at Kyurem with its bright blue eyes. If it weren't for knowing it couldn't win, it would have lunged at the Ice Dragon to bite it.

Was it even speaking a language?!

"Kyurem, you're too extreme. Interactions between lives shouldn't be judged solely by strength," Dylan sighed, patting Kyurem as he spoke.

"Reshiram is my companion. Its strength may seem insignificant to you, but that doesn't matter to me."

Dylan~!! What a great friend!

A warm current flowed through Reshiram's heart, making the entire dragon feel cozy. As expected from the chosen hero—so touching~

But…

"Even if it gets scared and doesn't dare to fight, even if it only yells about beating Zekrom, even if its holy appearance hides a Sea King's nature, it's still my White Dragon."

Reshiram's expression turned blank. It no longer felt touched at all.

"Allies should not harm each other, Kyurem. You may not understand this since you've never had much interaction with others, but you need to remember this," Dylan said with a smile, seemingly oblivious to the resentful gaze from behind.

Kyurem looked puzzled upon hearing this. Allies? It never had allies; at best, the White Dragon and Black Dragon were its kin.

Yet, Kyurem hardly acknowledged that.

"...Boring."

But soon, it snorted disdainfully. The strong should never lower themselves to interact with the weak—that was its definition of being a strong one.

Even if it became Dylan's partner, it wouldn't abandon its pride.

"No worries, take your time."

Dylan shrugged. He didn't expect Kyurem's mindset to change quickly after just being captured; it wasn't surprising for a being like Kyurem to have its own will.

"Let's head to a nearby city for some food."

Dylan glanced at his phone's map. This area was a bit remote, but it wasn't a big deal.

"Kyurem, I'll need you to give us a ride."

With his destination set, Dylan recalled Ultra Necrozma and the White Dragon, who was casting a gloomy glance.

Dylan, you big traitor!! Now that you have an Ice Dragon, you're not riding me anymore?! This Ice Dragon is all cold and stiff; it's nowhere near as soft and warm as my feathers!!

However, Dylan indeed wanted to try riding an Ice Dragon for a change.

"Roar~!"

Kyurem did not refuse, raising its head and roaring as flames burst over its body, transforming into its White Kyurem form.

Since it had already decided to follow Dylan for a century, Kyurem naturally accepted him.

"Lillie, come on."

Dylan sat atop White Kyurem, extending his hand to Lillie as the dragon's golden eyes squinted slightly.

Lillie's heart pounded as White Kyurem's golden gaze bore down on her, making her feel goosebumps.

Yet, seeing her brother on the dragon's back, she took his hand and climbed up.

She was on!

From riding the White Dragon to now riding the Ice Dragon, Mother, your daughter is truly accomplished!!

Lillie was filled with excitement. She was probably the second human in the world to ride Kyurem!

It was just a little uncomfortable. Lillie shifted her position—its icy touch was even more chilling than Articuno.

Hmm, personal review: riding Kyurem wasn't as comfortable as Reshiram, but Lillie wouldn't dare say that out loud…

White Kyurem snorted uneasily. Having humans on its back felt strange.

"Let's go, Kyurem."

"Roar~!"

With a thunderous roar, White Kyurem, surrounded by red and blue light, soared into the sky and flew toward a specific direction.

Meanwhile, on the other side…

"Zekrom, are you alright?"

N stood in a forest, startled to see Zekrom wobbling through the sky.

Boom~!

Zekrom landed heavily, but almost stumbled as it did, alarming N.

"Zekrom!"

"I'm okay, N. Some unknown guy saved me."

Zekrom's weak and tired telepathic voice reached N, causing him to breathe a sigh of relief.

N quickly took out a Hyper Potion and an Ice Heal to spray on Zekrom.

"That Dylan guy!!"

Seeing the injuries and frostbite on Zekrom's body, N felt a deep pain, thinking about Dylan. Even someone as calm as N couldn't help but grit his teeth.

Dylan! You're really unforgivable! How could you do this?!

Tricking us into taking the blame for your mess. You should be responsible for your own mistakes—that's a basic principle even children understand!

"White Dragon!!"

Zekrom was also grinding its teeth at the moment. It knew it—Reshiram might appear pure and sacred on the surface, but it was definitely dark on the inside.

This beating today was totally undeserved. The Ice Dragon had hit hard without holding back; it hurt like crazy!

"N, once I recover, let's go find the White Dragon and its hero!"

"Alright!"

Zekrom just couldn't swallow this grievance; it was completely innocent!

N immediately agreed. Dylan had truly gone too far this time, and N wanted to vent his anger too.

But just then, N's phone rang.

"N, we have a lead on the person who framed Father," Ghetsis's voice was as cold as ice.

"Who is it?" N frowned. He couldn't forgive someone who harmed his father and was determined to seek justice.

"The Unova League released the news. I'll send you the link. N, this person is definitely a major enemy on our path to ideals! You have to defeat him!" Ghetsis said sternly.

"I understand, Father." N nodded and then opened the news interview video sent by Ghetsis.

Seeing the handsome, black-haired youth in the video, N's previously dull eyes began to flash—it's you again...

Dylan!!

"Father, he's the one chosen by the Dragon of Truth."

"?" Ghetsis was momentarily stunned, then gritted his teeth.

How could such a vile person gain Reshiram's recognition? Disgusting! Does Reshiram not care about good and evil? Bah!

"Very well! That's perfect! N, get ready. Once we confirm this guy's location, you're going to defeat him!"

Ghetsis spoke sternly. The one chosen by the Dragon of Truth was indeed formidable, which explained why Ghetsis couldn't defeat him.

But it didn't matter!

My adopted son is invincible! A mere White Dragon and its hero—N will surely win!!

Besides, Colress had revived Genesect; they could ambush Reshiram directly. How could they lose?

After N defeated this scoundrel, how should he humiliate him?

A cold, sinister smile crept onto Ghetsis's face. This guy was courting death by crossing him!

At Driftveil City, a port city in Unova, the name Driftveil means "sail and whirlpool."

Driftveil was known as the gateway to the Unova region, a hub for many shipping routes. Although Unova was shrouded in darkness, the port was still bustling with lights.

Yes, Ultra Necrozma was no longer draining the light, so normal lighting was functional.

Hence, Driftveil City appeared just like any other night.

But in reality, it was still different—it wasn't even five o'clock in the afternoon!

Dylan and Lillie had arrived at Driftveil City, the nearest city to their location.

On their way, the Unova League had also contacted him, and his reward had already been arranged for him here in Driftveil.

In front of the Driftveil Gym, Dylan and Lillie found three people waiting for them.

One of them was a burly, rough-looking man wearing a black bowler hat, dressed like a nouveau riche—the Gym Leader, Clay.

Beside Clay stood a sweet, innocent-looking girl with short pink hair, wearing a long pink coat over a white top with a red tie, and blue pants.

Dylan raised an eyebrow; she was Clay's daughter, Lacey, ranked third among the Elite Four of the Blueberry Academy.

The last person was a middle-aged man with long orange hair. His distinctive outfit and the Poké Ball hanging from his neck identified him as Alder, the current Champion of Unova.

"Hahaha~ It seems our guests have arrived. Dylan, the Gym Leader, nice to meet you~"

Noticing Dylan and Lillie approaching, Alder's eyes lit up, and he laughed warmly.

Clay and Lacey looked over with curiosity.

"Champion Alder, you're too kind."

Dylan approached the three and spoke with polite humility.

"This isn't just politeness. Ghetsis is ambitious, cunning, and quite capable. Capturing him is no easy task."

Alder clapped Dylan on the shoulder, his demeanor enthusiastic.

"Dylan, it's impressive you caught him! I heard you're a Gym Leader from Kanto. Ever thought about coming to Unova?"

"You're still young, right? Let me tell you, if your skills are good enough, I'll give you my position as Champion! Plus, Unova has plenty of beautiful women!"

"I've traveled all over Unova these past few years, so I know what I'm talking about—like Tyrantrum and little Rosa, for example. What do you think, Dylan? If you come to Unova, I'll personally introduce you to a girlfriend!"

Alder's arm was around Dylan's shoulder as he spoke with boisterous enthusiasm, as if ready to recruit him on the spot.

Meanwhile, Clay, Lacey, and Lillie were all sporting looks of shock.

Unova's Champion? Really?

# 156: Chapter 156: What does 'Golden' mean

Lacey covered her mouth with her small hands, staring in shock at the champion Alder, who was speaking confidently. At this moment, her idealized image of a champion cracked.

"Cough cough cough..."

Clay kept coughing, trying to interrupt Alder. He understood Alder, but this was still in front of the younger generation! Come on, Alder, pay attention to your image!

"Champion Alder, isn't that a bit inappropriate?" Dylan said with a laugh, looking at Alder, who was acting as if they were best buddies.

Though Alder seemed somewhat unprofessional, in reality, he was an extremely responsible and reliable senior.

The reason he had become this carefree was mainly due to what happened years ago—while protecting the Unova League, Alder's Pokémon partner overexerted itself and became critically weak, eventually passing away. This had been a huge blow to Alder.

Alder had since lost interest in battles. To fill the emptiness in his heart after losing his partner, he wandered across Unova, focusing on the bond between Trainers and Pokémon.

But when the Unova League faced a real crisis, Alder would undoubtedly step up to protect everyone.

In the original story, when N brought Zekrom to pursue his ideals, it was Alder who stood in his way, facing the legendary dragon of ideals and continually trying to persuade N to turn back.

But unfortunately, the outcome was tragic. In a battle where he staked his champion's will, the battle-rusty Alder was defeated by Zekrom, and his already weakened fighting spirit took another severe blow.

It wasn't until Black and White 2 that Iris ascended as the Unova Champion, giving Alder peace of mind to leave the Unova League.

Though Alder now seemed like a wanderer, Dylan still respected him. For a powerful Trainer with a sense of responsibility and a kind heart, Dylan always showed respect.

"Ah~ it's no big deal. I just handle the introductions. Whether it works out is up to you guys, right?"

Alder laughed heartily, unbothered. He felt that he was old now. If there were someone in Unova to take his place, he would have resigned long ago.

Now, traveling around Unova, he was also looking for a suitable successor.

He had already spotted some promising juniors—Lacey, Clay's daughter, had great talent and strength. But whether she could reach champion level in the future depended on her own luck.

But Dylan was different. Alder had specially investigated him. According to some rumors, Dylan had even defeated Lance! Though he wasn't sure if the rumor was true, it was confirmed that Dylan had beaten Ghetsis's Hydreigon so badly that it didn't dare resist.

Dylan was definitely talented!

Stare~!

Lillie widened her eyes, glaring at this Unova champion. This guy wasn't a good person! He was eyeing her brother!

"Thank you for your kindness, senior, but I currently have no plans to switch regions," Dylan said with a warm smile, politely declining. He liked Kanto; as the headquarters of the Pokémon League, it was more interesting than other regions.

"Hahaha~ that's fine. But no plans now doesn't mean never. Dylan, feel free to visit Unova more often. You're always welcome."

Alder wasn't forceful, knowing that this kind of thing ultimately depended on the person's will. However, Alder clearly hadn't given up on recruiting Dylan.

"Champion Alder, it's getting late. Why don't you invite Dylan and this young lady for a meal?" Clay couldn't hold back anymore. You're our Unova champion, for goodness' sake!

"Oh right, right, Dylan, and this lovely young lady, let's go eat," Alder said, patting his forehead and inviting Dylan and Lillie.

The five of them then headed to a famous restaurant in Opelucid City, where they booked a private room.

"Oh, by the way, Dylan, let me introduce you. This is Clay, Gym Leader of Opelucid City, and this is his daughter Lacey. Lacey is currently a second-year student at Blueberry Academy and is already one of the academy's Elite Four members."

Alder smiled as he introduced the two.

"Nice to meet you. I've heard a lot about you. I'm Dylan, Gym Leader of Rota City in Kanto, and this is my sister, Lillie."

"Hello~" Lillie nodded and smiled politely, curiously sizing up the robust Clay and the cute, pretty Lacey.

These two... they didn't exactly look alike. It wasn't that they didn't resemble each other at all, but judging from appearances alone, they hardly seemed like father and daughter.

"Hahaha~ Do you think Clay and Lacey look nothing alike? I think so too! But it's fine; Lacey took after her mother entirely. If she looked like Clay, now that would be a disaster!" Alder laughed loudly.

Clay twitched at that comment but let it go. He's the champion; I won't argue with him.

"Champion Alder, I think that's not very nice of you!" Lacey said with a serious face, crossing her arms to make an "X."

"My dad has worked hard to grow like this! Champion Alder, you shouldn't say such things about him!"

Hmm, a daughter who defends her dad... but...

Clay lit a cigarette with a troubled look, not sure whether to feel touched or worried.

Dylan glanced at Clay and almost laughed. Then, with great interest, he looked at Lacey. So, this is the 'I think that's not very nice' type of person. Senior Lacey is just adorable.

"Ah~ it was just a joke, haha!" Alder scratched his head.

"Oh, Dylan, here's a gift from the Unova League."

Suddenly, Alder seemed to remember something and handed Dylan a shrunken Ultra Ball.

"We asked the Kanto League about it, and President Goodshow mentioned that you've always wanted a Deino. Haha~ it's quite a coincidence."

Alder chuckled. After all, one of the most famous Pokémon in Unova was Hydreigon.

As the pseudo-legendary of Unova, Hydreigon was very strong, one of the few pseudo-legendaries known for its special attack prowess.

"Thank you, Champion Alder." Dylan didn't hesitate, accepting the Ultra Ball.

Exchanging Ghetsis for a Deino was certainly worth it for Dylan—waste not, want not.

"But this afternoon, while Ghetsis was being transferred to Opelucid City's prison, he was rescued by a Dragon Trainer with a Black Dragon."

As Alder said this, his brow furrowed. He couldn't understand why Zekrom would follow the leader of an evil organization.

"Dylan, be careful. Ghetsis is an ambitious man who holds grudges. Since you captured him and sent him to jail, he won't let you off easily."

Alder warned Dylan, concerned that Team Plasma would retaliate. Ghetsis was N's adoptive father and held a high position in Team Plasma.

Lillie tilted her head, her expression strange. Ghetsis wants to take revenge on her brother?

Hmm... Lillie was already looking forward to the moment Ghetsis came knocking.

"Don't worry, Champion Alder. Team Plasma can't harm me," Dylan replied with a smile, not taking the news seriously.

Given N's admiration for Ghetsis, it wasn't surprising that he rescued him. As for Team Plasma seeking revenge...

Dylan could only say, bring it on!

"Hahaha~ The young people these days are really great!" Alder greatly admired Dylan's calm and composed words—this confidence was exactly what he appreciated!

The meal was already served, and as the five of them ate, they chatted about the cultural differences between Unova and Kanto.

Alder showed a lot of interest in Kanto. According to him, he had visited Kanto when he was younger, but it was just a brief trip before he returned to Unova to assume the role of Champion.

Once he found a suitable successor, Alder planned to step down as Champion of Unova and embark on a world tour to relax.

"I've heard, Dylan, that you are a multi-type Trainer and have already assembled five different teams for challengers?" Clay asked with curiosity. As fellow Gym Leaders, Clay and Dylan found they had plenty of topics in common.

"That's right. Currently, I offer five different types for challengers to face," Dylan nodded. Indeed, there were five types, including the Dragon team and the Golden Section.

Speaking of which, his Golden Section team was complete now, though he wondered if any challengers could defeat the first three Pokémon and push him to use Reshiram and the other two legendary Pokémon.

"Does each team correspond to a single type, or do you select a team randomly for each challenger?" Lacey asked curiously.

"It's not random. My brother's gym has a special spinning device. The challenger presses a button, and whichever type the spinner lands on, that's the team he use to battle them," Lillie answered in a soft and refined voice.

"Wow~ I think that setup is super fun!" Lacey's eyes lit up. That way, every time someone challenged the Rota Gym, there could be a different surprise!

"If you find it interesting, why don't you give it a try yourself, Lacey?" Alder stroked his chin and encouraged her.

Lacey was quite tempted. Although Blueberry Academy had a ranking system that fostered competition among students, challenging a gym still held great appeal to her.

Especially a gym in the Kanto region.

"Huh? But isn't Gym Leader Dylan on vacation right now?" Lacey was intrigued but also hesitant, not wanting to inconvenience anyone.

"It's no trouble at all. If you're interested, Miss Lacey, consider it a bit of post-dinner exercise," Dylan said with a smile.

"Then, please guide me, Gym Leader Dylan!" Lacey stood up, smiling brightly. She was thrilled at the opportunity to battle such a renowned Gym Leader from Kanto, believing it would be a valuable learning experience.

"Hahaha~ I didn't expect to witness a battle between Dylan and Lacey today. What a treat!" Alder laughed heartily, enjoying the idea of watching the younger generation engage in a passionate Pokémon battle.

Though in truth, it had been a long, long time since Alder himself had battled.

"Dylan, you better be careful. Lacey is one of the Four Elites of Blueberry Academy now, and her strength has even surpassed Clay's," Alder added with a chuckle.

"Understood, I'll take this battle with Miss Lacey seriously," Dylan responded with a light laugh and a nod.

Blueberry Academy was a sister school to Orange Academy, but unlike the long-established Orange Academy, Blueberry Academy was a more recent institution, focused on Pokémon battle education.

Blueberry Academy even had its own internal Elite Four and Champion system. Though these titles were only recognized within the academy, standing out among so many students to become one of the Elite Four was indeed impressive, and Lacey's skills were certainly formidable.

"But there's no spinner here. Gym Leader Dylan, how will I randomly select the type I'll battle against?" Lacey asked, tapping her chin. She was eager to experience the thrill of random selection.

"It's simple."

Lillie tore a piece of paper into four parts, wrote down the types, and crumpled them into balls, shaking them in her hand.

"Please choose one, Miss Lacey," Dylan gestured toward the paper balls.

"Gym Leader Dylan, isn't something off here?" Lacey looked at the four paper balls. "Didn't you say there were five teams?"

Lacey tilted her head, puzzled. Was he intentionally leaving out a team? There was no need for that—she didn't mind at all.

"Emmm..." Lillie's face turned slightly red as she hastily wrote "Golden" on a fifth slip and mixed the papers again before laying them on the table.

Clay and Alder, intrigued, leaned in to watch.

Lacey stared at the five paper balls, deep in thought, unsure which one to pick.

"Lacey, trust me! Pick this one in the middle! It's definitely the easiest team to battle!" Alder, acting like an experienced elder, immediately persuaded Lacey to choose the middle paper ball.

"Alright, I'll trust you, Champion Alder." Lacey sighed helplessly but gave in. After all, a Champion's advice had to count for something.

She picked up the paper ball, opened it, and froze.

"Well? What type is it?" Alder asked eagerly.

"Gym Leader Dylan, I'm a bit confused. What does 'Golden' mean?" Lacey blinked, staring at the slip of paper in her hand.

(End of chapter)

# 157: Chapter 157: Golden

"Dylan, Gym Leader, what is this 'Golden' team?"

Lacey looked puzzled. Is there even a "Golden" type among all the Pokémon types? It didn't seem like it.

Lillie's delicate little face showed a very subtle expression.

In the Rota Gym, the chance of spinning to the golden section on the roulette is about one percent, so much so that even though she had been at the Rota Gym for over a month, Lillie had never seen a challenger land on it.

But there were only five paper balls just now, so technically the chance of drawing 'Golden' became one in five, which was actually unfair.

That's why Dylan had only let Rosa choose from four teams before, and Lillie had specifically excluded the 'Golden Section' just now.

However, Lacey brought up the existence of the fifth challenge team, so Lillie had no choice but to include it, otherwise, would she have to say, "If you draw the golden section, you just win a prize?"

"Miss Lacey, your luck is truly amazing~" Dylan couldn't help but marvel.

Lillie: (—)

"Huh? Really?" Lacey tilted her head in confusion, but why did Lillie's expression look so strange? Was this really what 'luck' meant?

"Hahaha~ I knew it, Lacey, the team I picked for you can't be that bad, right? I'm the Unova Champion after all~!"

Alder laughed heartily, hands on his hips, looking very proud.

"Thank you very much, Champion Alder."

Lacey still felt something was off but politely thanked Alder nonetheless.

"Since the team is decided, let's head to my gym for the battle. The field outside isn't bright enough."

Clay suggested. His Driftveil Gym was conveniently nearby.

"Let's go, I can't wait any longer." Alder seemed quite eager, as he was very curious to see how strong Dylan was in person.

The five of them finished paying and left the restaurant, and the sky outside remained pitch black.

"It's truly dark now. How odd, how did it suddenly become like this?"

Alder stared at the night sky, bewildered. The abrupt transition from day to night was inexplicable, without any warning. The Unova League was still investigating the cause, but they hadn't figured it out yet.

Moreover, this darkness seemed to only cover the Unova region; other regions didn't experience this strange phenomenon.

Could it be that Unova is haunted?

"Who knows? Maybe it's just some special natural occurrence. It couldn't be caused by a Pokémon, right? As long as it's not dangerous, it'll probably go back to normal by tomorrow."

Dylan mused before replying, while Lillie kept her head down, too nervous to speak or look up.

"True, at least nothing unusual has happened so far." Alder agreed after thinking it over. There wasn't much else they could do but wait and see what tomorrow brought.

"Huh? Lillie, what's wrong? It's dangerous to walk without looking where you're going."

Alder kindly reminded Lillie, noticing her strange demeanor out of the corner of his eye.

"Yes, thank you for the reminder, Champion Alder."

Lillie quickly looked up and sincerely thanked Alder.

"Strange..."

Alder was a bit taken aback by her exaggerated response, but then he recalled that Lillie was indeed very polite, so he didn't think too much of it.

Ugh... I'm still too inexperienced. I need to continue learning from my brother.

Realizing that Alder didn't seem to have connected the dots, Lillie silently lamented to herself. When would she ever develop the same unshakable composure as her brother, who remained calm even in the face of a crumbling mountain?

Driftveil Gym.

This was Clay's gym. Clay was originally from Driftveil City, but Lacey had grown up in Nimbasa City, likely to give her a more beautiful childhood.

In addition to being the Gym Leader of Driftveil, Clay was also the owner of a mining company, making him quite the tycoon. His nickname, after all, was "The Underground Boss."

Yes, he had a strong mob boss vibe, which might explain why he didn't want his daughter, Lacey, to be influenced by that part of his life. That's why he let her grow up in Nimbasa City and sent her to Blueberry Academy to become an elite.

And Lacey certainly lived up to Clay's expectations. By her second year, she had already become one of the Elite Four at Blueberry Academy, and now her strength had even surpassed her father's. Her future at the very least would see her rise to the rank of an Elite Trainer.

The group entered Driftveil Gym, and the bright lights inside the gym were switched on, illuminating the entire space as if it were daylight.

"I'm a Ground-type Trainer, so my battlefield leans toward Ground-type advantages. If you're not used to it, I can change it to a regular battlefield," Clay said to Dylan.

"It's fine, I'm comfortable with any battlefield," Dylan replied, shaking his head.

A Ground-type field wasn't too different from a standard one, except the ground was less level.

"Miss Lacey, shall we begin?"

"Yes!"

Dylan and Lacey took their positions on opposite sides of the battlefield, and the scoreboard activated.

"Let me be the referee!"

Alder enthusiastically pushed Clay aside, stepping onto the battlefield.

"The Rota Gym Badge challenge is now beginning! The challenger is Lacey."

"The battle format is 4-on-4. If all four of Lacey's Pokémon lose the ability to battle, the challenge will be considered a failure."

"The Gym Leader Dylan will send out his Pokémon first and may not switch Pokémon mid-battle."

"Let the battle begin! Lacey, good luck~ You're using the lucky team I picked for you!"

Alder professionally announced the battle rules and then stepped off the battlefield, cheering for the cute Lacey.

Lacey discreetly rolled her eyes. She wasn't sure how lucky she actually was.

"Before the battle, Miss Lacey, allow me to introduce myself."

Dylan undid the top button of his black shirt and casually let it hang open, his demeanor shifting noticeably to a more relaxed and confident one.

"My name is Dylan, the Gym Leader of Rota. I specialize in multi-type battles, and I choose my team based on the fate that guides the challenger."

His clear, carefree voice with a faint smile echoed through the gym.

"Wow, Dylan really knows how to show off, doesn't he?"

Alder crossed his arms, resting his chin on his hand, nodding with approval.

"..." Clay's mouth twitched, and he glanced at the elegant, black-clad youth, falling into thought.

The difference in introductions was stark—Dylan as a Gym Leader seemed to be on a completely different level than me!

Hmm, I need to think of some deep and impressive lines to say to challengers too.

"Miss Lacey, you are incredibly fortunate. Since I became the Gym Leader of Rota, you're only the second person to draw the golden section."

Dylan took out a crimson-colored luxury ball and gently rotated it.

"Only two?" Lacey was taken aback. Something didn't seem quite right.

"That's correct, Miss Lacey. Have you heard the stories of legends?"

Dylan tilted his head slightly, his eyes reflecting the young girl's image. Without waiting for her response, he spoke again.

"The stories that people pass down through generations, the legends hidden in ancient ruins, the myths preserved in historic temples—are you familiar with any of these?"

Hearing this, Lacey paused for a moment. As a top student of Blueberry Academy, she had indeed heard some stories about Unova's legends, but...

What does that have to do with this battle?

"Let me rephrase the question, Miss Lacey. Have you ever seen a legendary Pokémon?"

"No..."

Lacey suddenly felt a strong sense of unease.

"Well then, congratulations, Miss Lacey. From today onward, you can claim that you have," Dylan said teasingly.

"You are about to battle a legendary Pokémon."

Boom!

Lacey's mind went blank. What? What does that mean?

Even Alder and Clay in the audience were stunned.

"Gym Leader Dylan, you don't mean..." Lacey's voice was dry.

"The golden section represents the 'Team of Golden Legends,' Miss Lacey. You will be facing me at my strongest!"

"I will battle you with a team of legendary Pokémon, and if you manage to defeat me, you will earn the most precious Rota Badge of legend!"

"Even if you don't defeat my legendary team, if you earn my approval, you will still receive a random-type Rota Badge."

Hearing this, Lacey was utterly shocked!

"So then, Miss Lacey, let's officially begin this Gym Battle."

Dylan smiled at Lacey and tossed the luxury ball in his hand.

"Regirock, prepare for battle!"

"Regiii!"

A low, mechanical-like roar echoed as the Giant Pokémon landed heavily in front of Dylan. Seven glowing dots on its head slowly lit up.

One of the legendary giants—Regirock!

Lacey stared blankly at the incredibly imposing Regirock. A legendary... Regirock?

"Isn't this a bit too much?"

Clay felt bad for his daughter. I mean, really? A Regirock? This was just a Gym challenge!

Dylan actually used a legendary Pokémon in the battle? Wait a minute!

Clay shot a glare that could kill at the Unova Champion beside him. Right, if this guy hadn't encouraged his precious daughter to pick the middle slip of paper, how could Lacey have drawn the ridiculous "Golden Legend" section???

"Amazing, Dylan! Do you really have a whole team of legendary Pokémon?" Alder's eyes gleamed with wonder.

"It makes me want to try battling Dylan's team myself—legendary Pokémon, wow! Clay, why are you glaring at me like that?"

Alder suddenly noticed the strange look and turned to Clay in confusion.

"What do you think?"

"Uh... hahahaha~ If you think about it differently, this really is quite lucky," Alder awkwardly turned away and muttered.

"Lucky?" Clay twitched again.

"Come on, how many people dream of battling a legendary Pokémon? You can't deny that!"

Alder grew more confident, his voice rising.

That's right—legendary Pokémon are so rare and precious that most people never see one in their entire lives. If Lacey could prove herself, she might see four legendary Pokémon!

Isn't that lucky?!

Hearing this, Clay thought for a moment. Well, it's true, isn't it?

But on the field, poor Miss Lacey was in a state of utter confusion.

"Miss Lacey, it's your turn to send out your Pokémon," Dylan gestured to her.

Lacey took a deep breath, forcing herself to calm down, but seeing the overwhelming presence of Regirock on the other side, her head started spinning.

"Gulp..." She couldn't help but swallow nervously.

Champion Alder, I'm going to thank you for the rest of my life!!!

(End of Chapter)
