# 158: Chapter 158: Send this away!

But no matter how you think about it, I'm just a student!

Lacey pressed her lips tightly together. To suddenly battle a legendary Pokémon...

"Miss Lacey, you can relax a bit, it's just a gym battle," Dylan said soothingly, noticing her barely concealed tension.

"Winning or losing doesn't affect you. What you need to do is put your all into this battle, show me everything you've got and reach your limits."

"As one of the Elite Four from Blueberry Academy, you must have learned a lot."

Dylan's voice was gentle. After all, she was still just a student. There was a big gap between her and mature, strong-willed trainers.

Lacey's strength might surpass Old Agatha, but if Agatha were standing opposite her, she wouldn't be this flustered. Agatha was a seasoned middle-aged woman whose will and temperament were far beyond Lacey's.

But Lacey's potential far exceeded Agatha's; she just needed more growth.

Breathe in~ Deep breath~!

Lacey's chest rose and fell as she tried to steady her emotions. Yes, this is just a gym battle. No matter the outcome, it won't change anything.

At worst, she wouldn't get the gym badge.

"Gym Leader Dylan, I'm going for it!"

Lacey closed her eyes, tightened the pink hairpin in her hair, then, after preparing herself, she took out a Pokéball, aimed it at Dylan, and her clear voice steadied.

The Blueberry Academy's Elite Four member Lacey has issued a challenge!

"Go ahead."

Dylan smiled.

"Primarina!"

Lacey pressed her lips together, her face solemn as she threw her first Pokéball.

With a melodious cry, a Pokémon resembling a mermaid appeared before Lacey.

With sea-green curly hair and sapphire-blue eyes as bright as gems, its body was white with a blue tail.

It was Primarina, the final evolution of the Alola region's starter Pokémon Popplio!

"Primarina, huh~" Lillie sighed, seeing a starter from her own region in another place.

'Beep~ Primarina, Water/Fairy-type, the Soloist Pokémon.

Ability: Liquid Voice

Moves: Hyper Voice, Misty Terrain, Encore, Sparkling Aria, Protect, Hydro Pump, Life Dew (Egg Move)...

Description: Known as the Diva, its singing in the moonlight leading its group is fantastical.'

"Using a Water-type Primarina to counter Regirock? A good choice," Alder nodded, assessing the strategy. Regirock, being a pure Rock-type, was naturally weak to Water-types.

"This Primarina has been raised very well, Miss Lacey. It's quite beautiful."

Dylan complimented, noticing that this Primarina was bigger than the standard size and its hair and skin had a healthy shine, showing its vitality.

"Of course! I like my Pokémon cute, beautiful, and clean, so I give them baths three times a day!"

Lacey said with a sweet smile. As a Fairy-type specialist, she adored cute Pokémon.

"Well then, here I come, Gym Leader Dylan."

Lacey's eyes sharpened slightly. Though the outcome didn't matter, no trainer would step onto the battlefield without aiming for victory.

Besides, this was a gym battle. If she could defeat Gym Leader Dylan, she'd earn his gym badge!

According to him, this was a very rare Rota badge!

"Go ahead," Dylan invited once more.

"Primarina, use Sparkling Aria!" Lacey wasted no time launching her attack.

Primarina sang, its melodious voice filling the air, while water swirled, and orbs of water began forming above it.

Sparkling Aria, a Water-type move, was ready to strike Regirock.

"Earthquake."

Dylan smiled faintly.

Regirock lifted one foot and slammed it down.

Ground-type Earthquake!

Boom!

The battlefield shook violently, sending shockwaves toward Primarina.

It was clear Regirock intended to trade hits, but Earthquake wasn't super effective against Primarina, whereas Sparkling Aria was strong against Regirock.

"Primarina!"

Lacey's mind raced, deciding to play it safe.

Primarina canceled its Sparkling Aria and released a bubble, quickly jumping into it to float above the ground, avoiding the Earthquake's shockwave.

"Regirock, Stone Edge," Dylan commanded, noticing the large floating bubble. Primarina's unique ability to create these soft, sturdy bubbles was impressive.

But it also made for an easy target.

"Regi~!"

Regirock raised its arms, sharp rocks forming in front of it, shooting towards the airborne Primarina.

"Primarina, dodge it!" Lacey quickly called out.

Primarina reacted swiftly, leaping out of the bubble just as the Stone Edge smashed into it.

But as Regirock adjusted its aim, the remaining sharp stones shot towards the descending Primarina.

Lacey's face paled—there was no way to dodge this!

Bang! Bang! Bang!

The Stone Edge struck Primarina directly, and it let out a pained cry as the rocks exploded around it.

When the dust settled, Primarina was left panting heavily, clearly injured.

Lacey exhaled slightly in relief. Thankfully, only a small portion of the stones had hit. Otherwise, she wasn't sure if Primarina would've endured.

Primarina's physical defense was quite weak.

"That Regirock is strong. Stone Edge isn't even super effective against Primarina," Alder commented, rubbing his chin. Even though Primarina had poor defense, only part of the attack had landed.

It was a testament to the power difference—Regirock was legendary, after all.

"Dylan's pretty impressive. But where did he catch that Regirock? If I remember correctly, Hoenn's Brandon already caught one," Alder mused. Regirock was famous, mainly because it had already been caught by Hoenn's Brandon.

Who knew Dylan had found a second one?

"That Regirock was caught by my brother near Rota not too long ago," Lillie responded with a composed smile.

"Oh? Lillie, tell me, does Dylan have more legendary Pokémon?" Alder grinned, clearly intrigued.

It seemed like Dylan had more than just one legendary. After all, this was a 4 vs. 4 battle. Surely Dylan wouldn't have only one legendary, right? But that seemed over the top!

"He does! My brother has gathered a full team of them," Lillie said with a playful wink.

"Hiss~!"

Hearing this, Alder and Leader Clay exchanged glances, both seeing the shock in each other's eyes. What the...?!

"Which specific legends are you talking about?" Alder snapped back to reality, his curiosity overflowing.

"Uh~ I can't tell you that, Champion Alder," Lillie apologized to him.

"...Lillie, it's not right to leave someone hanging like this," Alder said helplessly, his curiosity now skyrocketing.

On the battlefield.

"Going head-to-head isn't wise," Lacey pressed her lips together, constantly thinking of a strategy.

"Primarina, Surf!"

Primarina let out a resonant call, and a deep blue light illuminated the waters.

Crash~ Crash~

The sound of waves echoed as a massive tidal wave surged out of nowhere, carrying Primarina towards Regirock.

A Water-type Surf attack!

"Earthquake," Dylan ordered calmly.

Regirock stomped heavily on the ground, sending a shockwave forward.

The base of the wave was shattered by the tremor, and the giant wave collapsed as Primarina began to fall.

"Primarina, Ice Beam!" Lacey quickly called out.

Primarina's mouth glowed with icy energy, and a twisting, ice-blue beam shot out. The remnants of the collapsed Surf were quickly frozen into crystals, crashing towards Regirock.

"Oh? Not a bad idea," Dylan raised an eyebrow.

Lacey had clearly anticipated that Surf would be interrupted, so the Ice Beam was prepared as a backup.

"But it's not enough."

The strategy was solid, but it was ineffective against Regirock. Those ice crystals wouldn't pose any real threat to its rock-hard body.

The ice fell and shattered upon impact with Regirock's body, as fragile as paper against its incredibly tough physique.

Regirock's physical defense was simply too high.

"Hydro Pump!" Lacey wasn't done yet.

"Stone Edge."

As Primarina landed, a deep blue water glow gathered in its mouth, and a massive water jet shot towards Regirock.

Meanwhile, ice continued pelting Regirock, though it ignored them like mere gusts of wind.

Floating stones formed around Regirock, and with a powerful arm swing, they shot forward.

Boom!

Stone Edge collided with Hydro Pump, the two attacks clashing intensely. But soon enough, the stones overpowered the water and slammed into Primarina, causing a fierce explosion.

"Primarina!"

Strong gusts of wind blew outward, but Lacey shielded her face with her arm, calling out worriedly to her Primarina.

Unfortunately, Primarina was no longer able to respond.

When the dust settled, Primarina was lying on the ground, with fainted eyes.

"Primarina is unable to battle. Regirock wins."

Alder quickly stepped in to announce the result.

On the scoreboard, Primarina's icon dimmed completely.

Lacey pursed her lips, recalled her Primarina, and looked up at Regirock with a frown. Its defense was high, and its attack wasn't low either—she would need a fast Pokémon to counter it.

"Well done, your strategy was sound, just lacking a bit of power," Dylan praised Lacey, giving her credit.

"Miss Lacey, my Regirock's physical defense is much higher than most normal Pokémon."

"Yes, I understand, Leader Dylan."

Lacey took a deep breath. It was indeed very powerful, but that's what made it a worthy challenge!

But how could she deal with Regirock? She thought for a moment. Maybe it was time to switch opponents.

"Granbull! I'm counting on you!"

After some thought, Lacey released her second Pokémon. A light purple, bulldog-like creature appeared in front of her.

Its wrists and neck had black bands, its jaw featured large fangs, and it tilted its head slightly, looking quite fierce.

It was the Fairy-type Granbull!

'Beep~ Granbull, Fairy-type Pokémon.

Ability: Intimidate.

Moves: Play Rough, Roar, Headbutt, Ice Fang, Fire Fang, Thunder Fang, Outrage, Stomp, …

Description: Granbull has a highly developed jaw. Due to the weight of its teeth, its head tilts, and it doesn't bite unless startled.'

Lillie tilted her head slightly. Was this Granbull supposed to be cute? Well, she wasn't a fan of such fierce-looking Pokémon.

On the battlefield, Granbull glared fiercely at Regirock, but Regirock wasn't fazed.

With its Clear Body ability, Regirock's attack wouldn't be lowered.

"Miss Lacey, I just mentioned my Regirock's physical defense is extremely high," Dylan reminded her, as Granbull was purely a physical attacker.

"I know, but I'm not planning to have it fight Regirock."

A sweet smile appeared on Lacey's face.

Dylan was taken aback. Could it be that…?

"Miss Lacey, I advise you…"

"Granbull, Roar!!"

Roar~!

Granbull let out a thunderous roar at Regirock, and a mysterious energy surged forward, sending Regirock back into Dylan's ball in a flash of white light.

At the same time, another Poké Ball on Dylan's belt opened, and a different Pokémon emerged.

"Yes! It worked!" Lacey pumped her small fists excitedly. Roar had successfully forced Regirock out. Its high defense and strong attack were too tough to deal with, so she decided to switch opponents instead.

Even if it was another Legendary Pokémon, it couldn't be harder than Regirock... right?

Lacey's smile froze.

The elegant, pure-white dragon Reshiram looked around in confusion, then glanced back at Dylan. What was going on? Wasn't it just sleeping in its Poké Ball?

It had been dreaming of punching Zekrom and kicking Kyurem, ruling the world!

The whole Driftveil Gym fell silent.

Dylan sighed and rubbed his forehead. He spoke a little too late; he'd wanted to warn Lacey not to use moves like Roar.

"Heh... hehe…"

Lacey looked in horror at the white dragon, which seemed to be a legendary figure in Unova's founding myths. Her lips trembled into a forced laugh, but she could barely manage it.

"Ahhh! Granbull! Quick, use Roar! Send this dragon away!!"

Her panicked scream echoed through the entire Driftveil Gym.

(End of Chapter)

# 159: Chapter 159: Mom, I'm scared

Lacey was almost stunned!

She only thought Regirock was a bit hard to deal with, so she wanted to switch opponents, but what did she just shout out?

What was that??

"This..."

Looking at the elegant, pure white dragon, even Alder and Granbull were both shocked at the moment.

"Is this the white dragon Reshiram from the founding legends? Dylan actually gained Reshiram's allegiance???"

Alder regained his composure, though his voice could not hide his astonishment.

This was definitely the real Dragon God Reshiram. It looked too much like the one from the legends.

"That's right! When my brother came to Unova, he was chosen by Reshiram. We went to Dragonspiral Tower, and my brother successfully awakened Reshiram."

Lillie's delicate, fair face was glowing with a beautiful smile, and her tone was filled with pride.

She had every right to be proud, especially seeing the shock in Alder and Clay's expressions. Lillie felt even more honored.

After all, that was her proud adoptive brother!

"This is unbelievable..." Clay's expression was complex. The dragon god legends of Unova had been passed down for nearly a thousand years.

Most people in Unova only regarded it as a legend, but when the news came from Kanto recently that the Plasma Team's leader, N, had gained the recognition of the black dragon Zekrom, things became real again. N's ideals were also extremely radical.

And now the white dragon had been awakened, but...

Good news: The one who awakened the white dragon is an Alliance trainer.

And not-so-bad 'bad news': He's a Gym Leader from the Kanto region, not the Unova League!

So, this guy came to Unova just to steal their real Dragon God!

Alder realized this as well, feeling quite helpless. The two legendary dragon gods of Unova—one had been taken by the leader of an evil organization, and the other was about to be taken by someone from another region.

This...

This wasn't exactly good news for the Unova League. Even though the Pokémon League stood united, to deal with N and his Zekrom, it would be ideal to have Reshiram on their side.

"We have to convince Dylan to join Unova!"

Alder silently made up his mind. He knew the situation well—he was the only Champion-level power in Unova right now.

Trying to take on Zekrom was almost impossible unless they could set up a trap to lure it in and use advanced technology to interfere with it.

But that was very hard to achieve, nearly impossible.

However, if Dylan were to join Unova, everything would be easily resolved.

Alder was sure of one thing: Dylan was absolutely a Champion-level trainer. And with Dylan on their side, Unova could rise again!

Ever since Alder had withdrawn from the scene, Unova had been on a steady decline, with no one capable of inheriting the title of Champion.

But if Dylan were willing to join Unova and become its Champion, he could certainly restore Unova's glory! He might even surpass Kanto to make Unova the top region.

Do people understand how valuable having Reshiram is?

But the key question now was, what could they offer to entice Dylan to join? A wise bird chooses the best tree to perch on, and Alder wasn't sure if the title of Unova Champion would be appealing enough to Dylan...

Alder pondered this. Should they send Shauntal to try to convince him?

Though that recluse spent her days writing novels, she had a great figure and appearance. Plus, she was one of the Unova Elite Four, holding a high status. Dylan, being of this age, might have certain fantasies about women...right?

Though Alder wasn't sure if she'd agree to it or if Dylan would be into it.

While it felt a bit sneaky, for the sake of Unova, some special tactics were necessary. Besides, if Dylan were to join Unova, all of its resources could be channeled toward him. Everyone would have a bright future.

This was an era of intense competition, and talents like Dylan belonged to the whole Pokémon League! Why shouldn't Unova try to win him over? There was nothing wrong with that!

On the battlefield.

Granbull roared again, unleashing a fierce wind mixed with energy.

Although Reshiram had just been summoned and was still a bit dazed, as a true legendary Pokémon, it instinctively began to resist. With a heavy sweep of its wings and claws, it unleashed a powerful gust of wind, colliding with the incoming wave of energy and preventing itself from being forcibly returned to its Pokéball.

Seeing that Reshiram had not been forced back, Lacey's face turned pale.

"Oooh~!"

Reshiram let out a clear and majestic dragon roar, and the oppressive aura made the very air feel thick.

Though it might seem like just a sidekick when compared to Kyurem and Ultra Necrozma, in front of other Pokémon, it was the legendary true dragon, Reshiram! Do they not understand the majesty of the true dragon?

Lacey could feel the immense pressure, biting her lower lip as cold sweat formed on her forehead. Her heart was racing, thumping faster and faster!

So terrifying, so terrifying, so terrifying!!

Granbull, what kind of monster have you just summoned?!

"Bulu..."

At this point, Granbull's legs began to wobble. It swallowed hard, taking a step back. This was terrifying!

"Don't be afraid... Don't be afraid, Granbull! I believe in you! Don't be scared!"

Though Lacey was under immense pressure, she still gritted her teeth and encouraged Granbull. No matter what, they couldn't give up without a fight.

Lacey couldn't accept such a humiliating defeat, even if they were to lose.

"Bulu..."

Hearing its trainer's words, Granbull finally managed to stop retreating, though it was trembling from head to toe.

It could feel Reshiram's gaze fixed on it, making its skin crawl and sending shivers down its spine.

Please... Please stop looking at me, or I'll start crying! (Granbull crying.jpg)

Indeed, the intimidating presence of a legendary Pokémon like Reshiram was overwhelming for ordinary Pokémon.

Dylan shook his head at the sight. However, this was partly because Lacey and her Pokémon were still quite young—they were, after all, still students at the academy.

But at least Lacey was still holding onto her fighting spirit, even after facing Reshiram, which was commendable.

Her battle skills, tactics, and Pokémon training were all quite good. Dylan thought it might be worth awarding her a badge, but the battle wasn't over yet.

"Miss Lacey, try again."

Dylan finally spoke, smiling warmly at Lacey in encouragement.

Lacey was taken aback.

"Use that move you just attempted."

"I... I understand."

Lacey bit her lip, giving Dylan a grateful look. She knew he was trying to relieve some of her pressure.

Battling Reshiram was far beyond her capabilities.

"Granbull, use Roar!!"

Granbull fought a mental battle with itself. It feared that if it roared, the other side would just roar back even louder...

But remembering how much Lacey cherished it, Granbull gritted its teeth.

The evil hound roared!

Dylan looked at Granbull's "ultra-low-frequency" roar with a strange expression on his face. Well, at least it managed to pull it off.

'Don't resist, Granbull is already scared to the point of collapse.'

Seeing that Reshiram seemed to have the intention of flapping its wings again, Dylan spoke through their telepathic connection.

Reshiram tilted its head, and then, naturally, was sent back into its cherished Pokéball by the Roar.

"Dylan truly is such a kind and gentle young man~"

Witnessing this scene, both Alder and Clay were deeply moved. They could clearly tell that Dylan was being considerate of Lacey's emotions.

So gentle~!

"Ah... thank you so much, Leader Dylan..."

Lacey was overwhelmed with gratitude. Leader Dylan was just too kind, sniffle~

Heaven knows how much psychological pressure she had felt standing on the battlefield against a legendary true dragon! She was barely holding it together!

But before Lacey could finish speaking, a more violent and ferocious presence suddenly descended, even more intense than Reshiram's.

"Roar~!!"

The furious roar of the ice dragon shook the entire Driftveil Gym!!

Alder and Granbull were utterly stunned as dust fell from the ceiling!

What... What kind of roar was that?! Like a small earthquake?!

Lacey's eyes widened in shock, and they started to tear up. Kyurem's aura was simply too fierce, and it was right across from her!

The overwhelming and terrifying pressure felt like a direct assault, freezing her in place, her entire body stiff and unable to move.

Even Granbull, unsure of where it found the strength, dashed behind Lacey, clinging to her leg and trembling, its head buried and refusing to lift up.

("Mom, I'm scared.jpg")

Kyurem's golden dragon eyes were filled with rage as it glared at Lacey and her Granbull. Was this really Dylan's opponent?

Too weak... but, oh well, I'm Dylan's partner now.

"Roar~! (Dylan, I will freeze all your enemies for you!)"

The icy-blue energy around Kyurem began to condense, forming beautiful ice rings that shimmered around its body.

A frozen world! I will turn you into eternal ice sculptures!!

A chilling cold began to spread, and a thick layer of frost formed on the ground around Kyurem's feet.

The temperature inside Driftveil Gym visibly dropped, and frost from the cold air above turned into snowflakes that gently drifted down.

"It's... it's snowing?" Clay dumbly stared at the frost and snow on his cowboy jacket, completely dumbfounded.

"Wait! Kyurem!" Dylan quickly stopped Kyurem.

"?" Kyurem paused, its massive, ferocious head turning toward Dylan in confusion.

'This is a Gym battle, you need to go easy. Don't go all out.'

An easy battle? Kyurem seemed to understand.

"Um... Miss Lacey, how about you try that move again?"

Dylan, wearing an awkward yet polite smile, turned to Lacey, who had tears welling up in her eyes.

The poor girl was on the verge of being scared to tears by Kyurem—no, she was already shedding tiny tears.

But Dylan hadn't done it on purpose! How was he supposed to know? What was this Granbull anyway? It Roared once at Reshiram, and now a second time at Kyurem??

Didn't even glance at Regirock, Articuno, or Darkrai?? What kind of heavenly luck is this?

"Sniff..." Lacey shook her head, her eyes red.

"Trust me! This was really an accident. Have Granbull use Roar one more time, and I promise no more scary Kyurem."

Kyurem: "?"

Dylan quickly stuffed Reshiram's and Ultra Necrozma's Pokéballs into his backpack, ensuring Granbull's Roar wouldn't bring them out.

Dylan truly felt like the most considerate Gym Leader out there. Where else could you find such a kind and gentle leader?

For the challenger's sake, not only was he going easy, he even had to "seal" his Pokémon's Pokéballs!

# 160: Chapter 160: I miss you! Can you come back?

"What kind of dragon is this?"

Alder snapped back to reality, staring at the enormous, wild, gray Tyrannosaurus-like dragon on the field.

This terrifying presence, more fearsome than Reshiram, meant this dragon was definitely another legendary Pokémon.

Alder frowned in deep thought, then suddenly remembered another legend.

"Lillie, what is this Pokémon?"

Unable to confirm for himself, as the Pokédex displayed no data, Alder immediately asked Lillie, who was Dylan's sister and should know about Dylan's Pokémon.

"This is Kyurem, the Ice Dragon Kyurem," Lillie replied with a soft smile.

Since Kyurem had appeared, there was no point in hiding it any longer.

Kyurem! As expected!

Upon hearing this, Alder understood. Kyurem was another one of the legendary Dragon Pokémon of Unova, known in folklore as the man-eating and Pokémon-devouring dragon.

However, Alder was friends with Shaga of Opelucid City, who had once told him the true origin of Kyurem—that it was the empty shell left behind when the original dragon of Unova split into two!

"Dylan..."

Alder gazed at Dylan in the arena, his eyes filled with fiery admiration.

Not only had Dylan captured the Truth Dragon Reshiram, but also Kyurem! Was this guy here to collect all the Dragon Gods of Unova?

But it didn't matter—capturing legendary Pokémon was Dylan's ability, and what Alder needed to focus on now was how to get Dylan to join the Unova League!

On the field.

Buoyed by Dylan's gentle reassurance, Lacey regained her fighting spirit. Although the dragon in front of her still terrified her, she believed she shouldn't give up without a fight.

She had mustered up her courage, but...

"Granbull, show some backbone!"

Lacey looked at Granbull, who was clutching her leg and trembling, and felt a headache coming on—disappointed by her Pokémon's lack of resolve.

She herself wasn't this scared!

"Bulu..." Granbull looked up at Lacey with a pitiful expression, reluctantly letting go of her leg and slowly stepping back into the battlefield.

Technically, Granbull should have been disqualified for running out of the arena just now, as it showed a loss of will to fight, but everyone tacitly agreed not to mention it.

Granbull now stood opposite Kyurem, feeling the overwhelming pressure of the strongest dragon. Its legs began to tremble again, as it realized it was under far more stress than it could handle.

Oh no!

How could a mere Granbull be worthy enough to compete against Reshiram and Kyurem?

Was this fair at all? Not even remotely!

"Granbull, you can do it! Use Roar!"

Lacey clenched her fists, cheering on her Pokémon.

Granbull closed its eyes, trying to block out Kyurem's terrifying face, and let out a roar.

It was a roar that didn't quite sound like a roar, but it unleashed a gust of wind and a mysterious force that struck Kyurem.

At Dylan's signal, Kyurem gave the little dog a bored glance before returning to its Poké Ball as if it were the most natural thing in the world.

Another luxury ball opened, and a dark shadow spread on the ground. From the shadow, the pitch-black nightmare slowly rose.

Darkrai!

Darkrai's calm blue eyes locked onto Granbull, but it was puzzled. Why did this little dog look so relieved, as if a heavy burden had been lifted?

"Phew~ It's the mythical Darkrai..." Lacey sighed with relief, realizing that while Darkrai was certainly formidable, it was far less intimidating than the previous two dragons.

"Darkrai, the mythical Pokémon from Sinnoh... Dylan, you're full of surprises!" Alder marveled. He was eager to know what other legendary Pokémon Dylan possessed.

Boss Clay felt a lot more relaxed as well. He still cared deeply about his precious daughter and didn't want any lasting psychological damage.

"Lacey, don't get too comfortable! My Darkrai is very strong, so don't underestimate it," Dylan gently reminded her with a warm smile.

"Yes! I'll do my best!" Lacey responded seriously.

She knew Dylan had given her an immense advantage, so there was no way she could let him down now.

She had to show her full potential to Dylan!

"Granbull!" Lacey called out to her Pokémon.

Granbull forced itself to focus, glaring at Darkrai with a menacing expression, its Intimidate ability activating.

Darkrai's physical attack was lowered by one stage due to Intimidate.

However, Darkrai narrowed its eyes slightly, surprised that this little dog had the nerve to bare its fangs at it...

"Granbull, use Play Rough!"

Lacey's face was serious. This was the best possible scenario—Darkrai was a Dark-type, which was weak to Fairy-type moves.

She had to put up a good fight! She couldn't disappoint Dylan!

Granbull growled and charged toward Darkrai, but unfortunately, Granbull's fastest running speed was still quite slow.

"Dark Pulse," Dylan said calmly, with a smile.

Darkrai approached Granbull, its black shadow moving like a phantom, dark energy dancing around its claws.

Darkrai quickly reached Granbull, whose teeth and claws were glowing with Fairy-type energy as it lunged at Darkrai.

Play Rough!

However, Darkrai easily dodged the attack with a graceful sidestep, its claws aimed directly at Granbull's side.

"Granbull! Watch out!" Lacey's eyes widened in shock.

Boom!

Dark Pulse exploded upon impact, hitting Granbull squarely. Granbull howled in pain, its body sent flying by the powerful dark energy.

Despite being a Fairy-type, which resisted Dark-type moves, this attack was simply too strong. Luckily, Darkrai had not used its full power.

"Shadow Ball," Dylan commanded.

Darkrai raised its right claw, forming a dark, eerie energy ball.

With a swift motion, Darkrai hurled the Shadow Ball at the fallen Granbull.

Boom!

The Shadow Ball hit its target, erupting in a loud explosion.

The pitch-black nightmare floated back to Dylan, silent and eerie as always.

"Granbull is unable to battle. Darkrai wins," Alder declared.

So strong!

Lacey stared in disbelief at her unconscious Granbull. There was no doubt—this Darkrai was far stronger than the Regirock from the beginning.

Did she really Roar away the weakest one?

Thinking of this, Lacey wanted to bang her head against the wall. How could this happen?!

"In comparison to Regirock, Darkrai's offensive capabilities are definitely much stronger. Miss Lacey, there's a reason I chose Regirock to lead," Dylan said, seemingly guessing Lacey's thoughts.

"Regirock has excellent defense and a decent physical attack, but its speed isn't fast. It's a relatively bulky type. If a suitable battle strategy is found, Regirock isn't particularly hard to handle."

"But Darkrai is different."

Dylan glanced at Lacey. Darkrai was agile and had a fast speed. With its priority move, Dark Void, it would put opponents to sleep upon hitting, and its special attack was exceptionally powerful.

Furthermore, Darkrai was also at a higher level than Regirock, so the difficulty of their battle was clear.

But Dylan was at a loss; he had chosen the weakest and easiest to battle Regirock as the lead, and Lacey had scared it away herself. Now, it was still not returning.

"Wuu…" Lacey covered her face and let out a cute whimper.

Regirock, I miss you! Can you come back?

But the battle had to continue. Lacey quickly adjusted her emotions. After thinking it over, she threw out her third Poké Ball.

"Whimsicott! Please!"

The Poké Ball opened in the air, and a Pokémon with a big fluffy tuft of cotton on its back landed in front of Lacey.

It wasn't very large; its brown body resembled a sheep, with cotton extending from the top of its head and swelling into a large puff.

'Ding~ Whimsicott, Grass, Fairy type, Windveil Pokémon

Ability: Prankster

Moves: Moonblast, Tailwind, Cotton Guard, Light Screen, Cotton Spore, Stun Spore, Leech Seed, Giga Drain…

Description: Like the wind, it can slip through any tiny gap, leaving behind white tufts of fluff.'

It looked quite cute.

Lillie looked at the data of this Whimsicott in the Pokédex.

"Whimsicott, huh…" Dylan raised an eyebrow at the cheerful and cunning-looking Whimsicott.

And if he remembered correctly, Lacey's Whimsicott had the Prankster ability.

"Whimsicott! Use Stun Spore!" Lacey decisively commanded.

Whimsicott grinned, waving its little arms, and a cloud of yellow powder drifted through the air.

Thanks to the Prankster ability, it used Stun Spore at an incredibly fast speed.

"Blizzard, blow it back," Dylan narrowed his eyes.

Darkrai quickly retreated, its claws coming together before violently spreading apart, unleashing a powerful blizzard.

An Ice-type Blizzard!

Under the blizzard, the Stun Spore was directly consumed, and the blizzard continued to surge toward Whimsicott.

"Whimsicott! Protect!" Lacey reacted quickly.

Whimsicott immediately used Protect, and the green barrier expanded instantly under the boost of Prankster.

Dylan shook his head, seeing that Whimsicott couldn't hold it. Lacey's choice was very correct; the area of the blizzard was too large, making it the most rational decision for Whimsicott to use Protect.

But it was futile; his Darkrai was already level seventy-one. The difference in strength was so great that Protect couldn't withstand it, and it would be directly shattered.

The howling blizzard slammed into the Protect barrier, but before Lacey could feel relieved, the barrier began to freeze and then shatter.

Creak~!

Protect was directly broken by the blizzard, which continued to surge toward Whimsicott. Whimsicott cried out in pain, its entire body engulfed by the blizzard.

And since Whimsicott was weak to Ice-type moves, there was no way it could hold on.

"Whimsicott is unable to battle. Darkrai wins," Alder announced the result of the battle.

Lacey bit her lip, looking at her unconscious Whimsicott with a bit of helplessness. It couldn't even protect itself, and this Darkrai really left her feeling powerless.

Hmm, once again, she missed Regirock. How about switching to the Rock Guardian, Dylan?

That gym leader's rule against switching Pokémon could also be overlooked, in her opinion.

"The gap in strength is too big." The observing Clay sighed, filled with paternal worry for his precious daughter.

"I hope Lacey won't be affected by this battle."

The outcome of the battle was already a foregone conclusion. Now Clay was only worried about whether his daughter's mindset would be influenced by this battle.

After all, there were many instances of this; many trainers had fallen into despair after a crushing defeat in a single battle.

"Don't worry. Lacey isn't the type to be downcast just because of one failure. She's not that weak."

Alder was quite confident in Lacey. Even facing Reshiram and Kyurem, she didn't give up on the battle. Alder believed Lacey had a bright future ahead.

"And she's already performed well. Do you think Dylan would give her a badge?"

Alder was quite curious about this. To be honest, he didn't think anyone could defeat Dylan, who used this legendary team.

It was no wonder Dylan said he was at his peak.

Is there really someone in this world who can defeat Reshiram and Kyurem in a legitimate battle? Alder seriously doubted it.

The might of Kyurem made even him feel a shiver down his spine.

So would Dylan really give out that legendary Rota badge? Perhaps the only possibility was to gain the recognition of Dylan, who used a legendary team, and then randomly give a badge.

"If it were me, I would give it." Clay replied naturally.

"I also think he would. To be able to face Kyurem and still have the will to continue battling, just that alone is already no small feat. This girl has already earned a badge."

Alder thought the same; putting aside everything else, Lacey's determination was commendable.

"As far as I know, it seems that the Rota Gym has only given out one badge so far, right, Lillie?"

"Um… if it's the new Rota badge, then yes, only Erika received a ghost-type Rota badge."

Hearing Alder's question, Lillie replied obediently.

"In fact, according to Selene and Asha, Brother has wanted to give out badges several times, but those challengers felt they hadn't defeated Brother, so they didn't accept them."

"After all, many trainers have their pride; it's quite normal."

Clay, as a gym leader, wasn't surprised by this. He had also wanted to give badges to challengers multiple times but was refused.

"This Dylan is really something! Gym badges can be divided by various types. If he encounters a trainer with OCD, hahaha~ How many times would he have to challenge to collect them all?"

Just imagining that scene made Alder laugh out loud.

What a pity that such an extraordinary talent wasn't born in their Unova; what a shame.

"It's making me want to battle Dylan for a Rota badge to collect it."

(End of this chapter)

# 161: Chapter 161: this time was just an accident

Yes, I'll wait for an opportunity to spar with Dylan and collect his Gym Badge as a keepsake.

Alder thought about this, though it seemed a bit odd for a regional champion to challenge a Gym.

But apparently, over in Galar, their Gym Leaders often compete alongside their undefeated champion Leon, so it's not that strange after all.

Meanwhile, back in the battle arena…

Lacey calmed herself and finally sent out her trump card, the last of her four Pokémon.

"Excadrill!"

In a flash of white light, a dark brown Pokémon landed in front of Lacey. It had a robust body, a white face, and its most striking feature—sharp steel blades on its forehead and claws. This was Excadrill.

"Is this one a Fairy-type too?" Lillie asked, puzzled as she observed the fierce-looking Excadrill.

It didn't look like one at all.

'Beep~ Excadrill, Ground/Steel type, Subterranean Pokémon.

Ability: Mold Breaker

Moves: Horn Drill, Drill Run, Swords Dance, Dig, Rock Slide, Slash, Terastallization Burst, Rapid Spin, High Horsepower…

Description: Its drill-like steel body can penetrate iron plates, making it a vital part of tunnel engineering.'

No Fairy-type, huh? Well, that makes sense. Bringing an Excadrill into a Fairy-type team isn't all that unusual.

Lillie blinked and remembered her brother's strategies, and it no longer seemed so strange.

"Gym Leader Dylan, our Blueberry Academy is the sister school to Orange Academy, so our students are also proficient in Terastallization."

Lacey took out a black Tera Orb and addressed Dylan.

"I'm aware."

Dylan smiled and nodded. Terastallization was a power developed by Professor Sada and Professor Turo from the Paldea region. Essentially, it allows a Pokémon's type to change to its Tera Type. Every Pokémon has a specific Tera Type, and Terastallization transforms it into that type.

For example, Lacey's Excadrill was naturally Ground and Steel, but its Tera Type was Fairy. Once Terastallized, it would become a Fairy-type.

After Terastallization, its strengths and weaknesses would change to match the Fairy-type, but it could still get STAB (Same-Type Attack Bonus) for using Ground- and Steel-type moves.

If the Tera Type matches the Pokémon's natural type, the power boost would be even greater. For instance, if this Excadrill Terastallized into its Ground or Steel type, it would gain extra power—effectively boosting its battle strength.

In comparison to Mega Evolution, Z-Moves, and Dynamaxing, Terastallization offered more tactical flexibility, which was one of its major advantages.

While Terastallization doesn't inherently increase a Pokémon's raw strength, it's a powerful tool. Take Gyarados, for example. It's undoubtedly strong among Water-types, but its fatal weakness to Electric moves drags it down significantly.

However, if Gyarados Terastallized into a pure Water-type, it would not only enhance its Water-type moves but also reduce its Electric-type weakness from 4x to 2x, making it much more formidable than one might expect.

That's the advantage of Terastallization.

"You truly are knowledgeable, Gym Leader Dylan," Lacey said as the black Tera Orb in her hand began to glow. She had initially thought she might need to explain Terastallization, unsure if Dylan was familiar with it.

But it turned out there was no need.

"Excadrill!"

Lacey tossed the Tera Orb towards her Excadrill.

The moment the orb touched Excadrill, radiant energy enveloped its entire body, forming crystal-like structures.

"What is that?" Lillie asked, stunned.

"That's Terastallization from Paldea. It changes a Pokémon's type," Alder explained, intrigued as he watched the transformation process.

Alder had some knowledge of it. It was, after all, a way to enhance a Pokémon's abilities, similar to Galar's Dynamaxing or Alola's Z-Moves.

However, unlike Dynamaxing, which could only be used in energy spots in Galar, Terastallization had no such limitations. Alder had even pulled some strings to get a Tera Orb for himself, and he found it quite interesting, especially with the tactical flexibility it offered.

Crrrrack!

The sound of shattering crystals echoed as Terastallized Excadrill emerged.

Its entire body gleamed with a crystalline glow, but the most eye-catching feature was the pink, heart-shaped crown atop its head—a hallmark of the Fairy Tera Type.

"A Fairy Terastallized Excadrill, so it's a pure Fairy-type team?"

After Alder's explanation, Lillie understood, though she found it rather peculiar—but not entirely wrong.

"Excadrill, Dig!"

Lacey quickly commanded her Excadrill after thinking for a moment.

Excadrill dived into the ground with incredible speed, disappearing beneath the battlefield.

"Take to the air," Dylan said with a light chuckle.

Dig allows Ground-type Pokémon to burrow underground, but a well-timed Earthquake would make Excadrill regret that.

Unfortunately, Darkrai doesn't know Earthquake.

Darkrai floated into the air, its blue eyes calmly watching the ground below, waiting for Excadrill to resurface.

"Excadrill, Terastallization Burst!"

Lacey gave another order when she saw Darkrai wasn't giving Excadrill any openings.

The ground beneath Darkrai bulged as Excadrill emerged, its body glowing with pink Fairy-type energy. With a heavy swipe of its claws, a beam of light from Terastallization Burst shot towards Darkrai.

Fairy-type Terastallization Burst!

Terastallization Burst is normally a Normal-type move, but once a Pokémon Terastallizes, the move's type changes to match the Pokémon's Tera Type. It also adapts based on whether the user's Attack or Special Attack is higher.

In Excadrill's case, its Terastallization Burst was, naturally, Attack-based.

"Thunder."

Electric sparks flickered around Darkrai as a massive bolt of lightning descended from the sky.

The Thunder attack collided with the Terastallization Burst, creating a dazzling light display of pink and gold. But soon, Thunder overpowered Terastallization Burst and crashed down.

"Excadrill, dodge!"

Lacey called out urgently to her Pokémon.

Excadrill, now lacking its Ground typing due to Terastallization, was no longer immune to Electric-type moves.

It tried to dive underground again, but Thunder was too fast.

Boom!

The lightning struck Excadrill as it was halfway into the ground, leaving the air sizzling with electricity.

With a loud crash, Excadrill collapsed to the ground, its body smoking and its Terastallization fading—signaling its defeat.

"Excadrill is unable to battle. Darkrai wins. Challenger Lacey's four Pokémon have all been defeated. This challenge ends in failure," Alder announced the final result.

None of the three spectators were surprised by the outcome.

Lacey lowered her head in disappointment. She hadn't managed to take down even one of Dylan's Pokémon.

"You did well, Miss Lacey."

Dylan approached her with his Nightmare Pokémon floating behind him.

"Gym Leader Dylan…"

Lacey raised her head, but then bit her lip and looked down in shame.

"I'm sorry to have let you down. I'm still too weak."

Her voice was soft. She had thought becoming one of the Four Heavenly Kings at the academy meant she was a skilled Trainer.

But after this battle, she felt utterly defeated. Even though Dylan had gone easy on her, she had still failed miserably.

"No, no, Miss Lacey, you're not weak at all," Dylan shook his head.

"Although the final result wasn't great, I noticed quite a few bright spots in you, Miss Lacey, during our battle."

"As for the crushing defeat, I do have some confidence in my own strength. When faced with my team of legendary Pokémon, most people might struggle even more than you did, Miss Lacey."

Dylan didn't think Lacey, who had almost been swept by Darkrai, was weak.

After all, as he said himself, using a team of legendary Pokémon, Dylan could easily claim to be the strongest trainer in the world right now.

Even if Leon himself were here, he'd have to kneel. In an official battle, Kyurem and Ultra Necrozma are no joke.

Even against that old monster, Dylan, with Ultra Necrozma in hand, no longer felt any fear.

"And at the very least, Miss Lacey, you showed me a brilliant and unwavering will." Dylan smiled warmly as he looked at Lacey.

Lacey was stunned and raised her head, staring blankly at the young Gym Leader in front of her.

"So please, Miss Lacey, choose one. I recognize your talent and will as a trainer."

Dylan took out five beautiful, delicate little brocade boxes from his spatial bag and gestured for Lacey to pick one.

These were the five new badges currently available at the Rota Gym: Poison, Dark, Ghost, Dragon, and Legend.

"But I..."

Lacey snapped back to reality, feeling flustered as she looked at the five little brocade boxes in front of her.

She was really embarrassed!

As the daughter of Clay, she knew that a Gym Leader didn't necessarily have to be defeated for a badge to be awarded—if the Gym Leader recognized the challenger, they could still give their badge.

But in this Gym Challenge, she had lost so miserably...

Could someone like her really deserve the Rota Badge? Lacey's face flushed with embarrassment.

"Hahaha~ Lacey, just pick one. You took a beating from legendary Pokémon—getting a badge to make up for it is fair enough."

Alder laughed, teasing her.

"And since Dylan recognizes you, there's no need to doubt yourself. Against that team, even if it were me, the result wouldn't have been much different."

Alder crossed his arms and seriously commented. And he wasn't wrong.

Forget about the absurd dragons, Reshiram and Kyurem—just that Darkrai alone was already Champion-level.

Alder sincerely believed that if he were up against that Darkrai, even his ace Volcarona would struggle in a hard-fought battle, and he wasn't even sure he could win.

"Just accept it, Lacey. After becoming stronger, you can challenge Dylan again to show him your growth—that would be the best way to repay his trust in you."

Clay also encouraged his precious daughter.

"I understand! I will not let down Gym Leader Dylan's trust!"

The Gym Leader's willingness to give his badge to a challenger was a deep form of trust, and for the first time, Lacey felt the joy of being recognized by a powerful trainer.

But it also meant that she had to work even harder, not to let this recognition go to waste, and to make sure others didn't think Dylan had poor judgment!

"I'm looking forward to it. Now, go ahead and pick one—or leave it to chance. One of these boxes contains the most precious Rota Legend Badge."

Dylan nodded in satisfaction and then spoke gently.

There's actually a Legend Rota Badge!!!

Lacey's beautiful eyes immediately lit up, and Alder and Clay were equally astonished.

The Legend Rota Badge was undoubtedly the most special. The other Rota Badges could be obtained through normal challenges, but this Legend Badge… who in the world could defeat Dylan and his team of legendary Pokémon?

The only way was to get the golden Legend team and earn Dylan's recognition, and then to draw it randomly from these Rota badges.

Its rarity was unimaginable.

Lacey's breathing quickened. If she could draw this Legend Badge, she would likely be the only person in the world to have it!

A one-in-five chance! Could she do it?

"Oh, Lacey, trust me, pick this one! It's definitely the right one!"

Alder was excited, pointing to the box in the middle. The thrill of this blind draw was just too much.

"After all, I was the one who picked the golden Legend team for you earlier, wasn't I, Lacey? Believe me!"

Lacey couldn't help but glare at Alder when she heard that. You still have the nerve to mention it! Thank you very much!

But Alder wasn't wrong—he had indeed chosen the golden Legend team for her earlier, so...

Lacey bit her lip, her gloved hand trembling slightly as she reached for the middle box.

Dylan handed her the box and put away the other four.

Lacey took a deep breath, slowly opening it as Alder and Clay curiously leaned in.

The box opened, revealing a beautifully crafted green gemstone badge with a deep purple Poison-type emblem on top.

It was a Poison Rota Badge.

"Huh? It's Poison?" Alder scratched his head.

No way, his instincts were usually spot-on. What a pity.

But suddenly, Alder felt a sharp, blade-like gaze directed at him. Yep, it was from the adorable Lacey.

"Uh... Lacey, this time was just an accident," Alder said awkwardly.

"Champion Alder," Lacey said with a blank expression.

"Thank you so much for your help today!"

Ahhh! I was so close to the Legend Rota Badge!! Ahhhh!!

(End of chapter)

# 162: Chapter 162: Squat down quietly

After the battle, both Dylan and Lillie didn't leave immediately. Instead, they stayed for a while longer at Clay's invitation.

In the guest room:

Lacey was holding the beautiful and delicate Rota Badge, admiring it from every angle with great joy, perfectly embodying what it means to be "blissfully silly."

She couldn't help it—this was a badge she earned from Gym Leader Dylan! It was the result of enduring a 'beating.'

Moreover, this was only the second newly-issued Rota Badge, meaning only two people in the world had one, including her!

Lacey was beyond thrilled! Once she got back, she planned to make it into a bracelet to wear every day.

"Dylan, have you really not considered switching regions?" Alder enthusiastically approached again, sitting down beside Dylan.

He couldn't help it; he was really drawn to Dylan.

"As long as you're willing to come to Unova, I'll talk to the chairman tomorrow and hand over the Champion title to you! You can be Unova's Champion!" Alder offered without hesitation.

"Our Unova isn't inferior to Kanto at all. We have the Elite Four, and the Unova Gym Leaders are top-notch in strength. Not to mention, we have the Blueberry Academy, which nurtures excellent elites."

"Also, I can tell you, this year I've encountered quite a few promising Champion-level talents! Iris from the Village of Dragons has been improving rapidly, and she's likely to reach Champion-level soon."

"I also met two talented rookie girls during my travels, and their potential is exceptional. They're future Champions for sure."

"As long as you come and lead Unova's Trainers, surpassing other regions is absolutely possible!"

Alder spoke with great enthusiasm, and he wasn't exaggerating.

The competitive training at Blueberry Academy was indeed outstanding, with Lacey being a prime example.

"I deeply appreciate your offer, Champion Alder, but I'll need to think it over. For now, I haven't really considered changing regions. But if I ever do, I'll definitely reach out to you," Dylan politely declined.

Lillie covered her mouth, giggling. Look at her brother's charm—Unova's Champion was practically begging to give up his title!

"Ah, alright then. But Dylan, development is development, no matter where you are, right? And Unova is a land of talented people! Plus, you've already captured Reshiram and Kyurem. What does that tell you?"

"It means you're destined to be connected to Unova! It's fate!" Alder said passionately, trying to emphasize Dylan's potential link to Unova.

"Maybe so," Dylan replied tactfully, going along with Alder's sentiment.

After all, Alder meant well—he was only thinking about Unova.

"By the way, Lacey, I heard Blueberry Academy and Orange Academy are planning joint events?" Dylan asked, trying to steer the conversation away from Alder's "sales pitch."

"Emm... yes. Almost every year, Blueberry Academy and Orange Academy co-organize a summer camp in Northfields," Lacey answered after thinking for a moment, knowing this from her time at Blueberry Academy.

"The summer camp should be happening soon. Gym Leader Dylan, are you interested?" Lacey asked curiously.

"To be honest, when I first became interested in becoming a Trainer, I also attended a Pokémon school for a while," Dylan said with a smile.

"But then my dad suddenly decided to take my mom on a honeymoon trip and left the gym to me, even though I had only been in school for a short time."

"It was a bit like being thrown into the deep end, but fortunately, it seems I had some talent as a Trainer," Dylan continued, humbly downplaying his rapid rise.

Alder and Clay couldn't help but feel a bit embarrassed. "Some talent?" Really?

"Gym Leader Dylan is amazing!" Lacey couldn't help but feel genuine admiration. From student to Gym Leader in such a short time, and now Rota Gym was one of the most famous gyms in Kanto—all in less than four months.

"I guess I've just been lucky," Dylan said modestly. Luck, and the help of a system that provided too many advantages.

Without it, even if he could maintain the Rota Gym, he wouldn't have achieved this level of success so quickly. Not to mention, without the system, he wouldn't have been able to rise this fast.

After chatting a bit longer with Alder, Clay, and Lacey, Dylan and Lillie finally left the Driftveil Gym and returned to their hotel to rest.

"Goodnight, brother!"

"Goodnight."

In the hotel suite, after exchanging goodnights with Lillie, Dylan headed to his room, freshened up, and got ready for bed.

"Northfields... I really should prepare for that," Dylan thought. He'd need to make time to visit the place. After all, lonely widows are quite adorable.

And if he could capture Pecharunt, that would be ideal.

As long as Necrozma could suppress Pecharunt, everything would be fine.

As for the Three Treasures, Dylan couldn't help but click his tongue at the thought of them bullying Pecharunt when they first revived in the game.

Honestly, these three are such nasty characters. Dylan figured they were better off staying underground, 'sleeping' for a while longer.

Another night passed in the growth simulation.

The next morning at 8 a.m., Dylan woke up on time. Now that the Alola region was cleared, he had to choose the next region to challenge.

Speaking of the most suitable region, it would have to be Galar from the eighth generation.

Because in the eighth generation, Pecharunt could be captured during the first playthrough. But Dylan was still undecided.

Now that he had Ultra Necrozma and Kyurem, he was already practically invincible. His need for Pecharunt wasn't particularly urgent.

He was more inclined to complete the fourth generation's Sinnoh region, which was undoubtedly the most special among all generations.

Dialga, Palkia, Giratina, and Arceus!

The creator god, Arceus, aside, even the three Sinnoh legends were special.

There are very few Pokémon that are truly referred to as 'gods,' but the fourth generation was different. It was the only one where the official lore explicitly confirmed the existence of true gods!

That is, Arceus and the Sinnoh trio it created.

'Before the universe was born, only it existed, breathing alone.'

'Where the universe began, so too did it.'

'When the universe was born, its shards became the Plates.'

These are the inscriptions on the Stone Plates—the Odd Keystone, Insect Plate, and Earth Plate. But the most striking is the inscription on the Mythic Plate:

'It watches over Pokémon and people in all universes.'

The one true god of the Pokémon world: Arceus!

Dylan wasn't sure if clearing the Sinnoh region would allow him to obtain Arceus, but even if he couldn't, capturing the Sinnoh trio would be just as great.

"In comparison to Pecharunt, the Sinnoh trio definitely feels more appealing," Dylan mused while brushing his teeth.

Pecharunt could still be captured in reality, so he wasn't in a rush. But the Sinnoh trio was different.

In reality, the Sinnoh trio governed time, space, and the Distortion World. Forcibly capturing them could impact the world.

Even if he did capture them, it wouldn't make sense to keep them with him, as they were needed to maintain the balance of time and space. It was much safer and more practical to bring them out from the growth simulation.

"Completing the Sinnoh region might also unlock information about Hisui."

Hisui was the ancient version of the Sinnoh region, and Dylan was certain that Hisui was connected to Sinnoh. Perhaps completing Sinnoh would unlock the Hisui region as well.

Once the Hisui region was unlocked, Dylan would have a chance to fulfill the preconditions for capturing Arceus.

At least, that's how it worked in the game, though Dylan wasn't entirely sure if it would work the same way in the growth simulation. Still, choosing the Sinnoh region felt like the right decision.

Alright, the third region will be Sinnoh! Dylan made up his mind.

Dylan and Lillie, who had also gotten up, finished freshening up and went to have breakfast. Today, they were preparing to return to the Kanto region.

When they stepped outside the hotel, the sky was still dark, but there was a faint glimmer of light.

Outside Driftveil City, Dylan threw out Reshiram's Pokéball.

While Kyurem could also be ridden, Kyurem's body was too rigid, and sitting on it for a long time made your butt hurt. Plus, the cold it emitted wasn't good for one's health after prolonged exposure.

Riding the White Dragon was much more comfortable, and its feathers were soft enough.

Dylan and Lillie mounted the White Dragon and flew towards Mistralton City, where their plane was booked.

"Whew~ The past few days felt like a dream," Lillie said, sitting in front of Dylan, looking down at the faintly illuminated city below. It all seemed a bit surreal to her.

This trip to Unova had been too thrilling for her.

Her brother had captured Reshiram, then challenged Kyurem and was relentlessly pursued by it. Afterward, Necrozma absorbed all the light from the Unova region, transforming into Ultra Necrozma, and finally, her brother captured Kyurem.

Could a young lady from the Aether Foundation like her have ever imagined experiencing such things?

"Then keep this experience deep in your heart," Dylan said with a smile. "Maybe when you look back on it in the future, you'll savor it."

"Mm-hmm~ Big brother, you're amazing~!" Lillie replied, nodding adorably. Her sweet voice was full of admiration.

Yes, right now, Lillie really, really admired her big brother!

"I'll gladly accept Lillie's praise, but for now... we've got guests coming."

Dylan glanced at the dark sky and the gathering storm clouds ahead, a slight smile tugging at his lips.

Lillie blinked in surprise and followed his gaze. The sky was already gray, so it was hard to make out the large dark cloud ahead. However, the flickering blue lightning arcs within the cloud were something she could easily recognize.

That peculiar cloud made Lillie instantly think of Zekrom!

"Dylan, it's the aura of the Black Dragon, just up ahead," Reshiram's ethereal telepathic voice echoed in both Dylan and Lillie's minds, brimming with excitement and anticipation.

"They've come quicker than I expected. It looks like Ghetsis is in a hurry," Dylan scoffed, giving Reshiram a pat.

"Take us down; they're probably waiting for us."

Reshiram understood, quickly descending and landing on the ground.

Dylan and Lillie dismounted from Reshiram and looked ahead.

Ghetsis, dressed in his ornate outfit, stood before them, accompanied by N. Behind them stood Colress, wearing his signature white coat.

At the same time, Plasma Team members began emerging from all directions, surrounding Dylan, Lillie, and Reshiram.

These were Plasma Team's elite forces.

"Tch~ Three generations of Plasma Team leaders. Quite the grand entrance," Dylan muttered in amazement. After all, these three individuals had each taken the role of Plasma Team's leader at different points in both the Black and White timelines.

"Kid, I didn't expect you to be the hero of the Legendary Dragon of Truth. But this works out just fine. Once I take you down, no one will stand in the way of our ideals," Ghetsis growled, his expression dark as he eyed the White Dragon, Reshiram, beside Dylan.

"Hah? It seems like the prison food in Icirrus messed with your head, Ghetsis. I never thought you'd be this foolish," Dylan smirked, his mocking tone causing Ghetsis's expression to darken further.

"If it were just N and his Zekrom, I'm sorry to say, but you guys might want to start preparing yourselves for the fine cuisine at the Unova League prison."

Dylan took out a crimson Premier Ball, toying with it as he spoke.

"You insolent brat! Still so arrogant!" Ghetsis exploded with rage. This guy had been just as cocky last time, and now, even surrounded by Plasma Team, he was still acting this way!

"Arrogant? Ha~" Dylan chuckled as if he'd just heard a great joke.

"Ghetsis, how arrogant must you be to come seeking revenge without even understanding who you're dealing with, rushing here with your men?"

Ghetsis froze for a moment, while N furrowed his brow, remaining silent.

Colress's expression flickered slightly. He glanced at Dylan, then at Ghetsis and N, and discreetly took a few steps away from them.

"What do you mean by that?"

For the first time, Ghetsis felt something was wrong. It had been the same back in Icirrus City. This guy had been equally overconfident back then.

And the result? Ghetsis had been easily defeated by Dylan and handed over to Officer Jenny. But this time, he had N with him!

"Oh, nothing much. I just wanted to show you all something impressive," Dylan grinned, his teeth flashing sinisterly in the dim light. The unsettling atmosphere made even the Plasma Team members feel a bit nervous.

With that, Dylan hurled the Premier Ball high into the sky.

Roar~!

A deafening dragon's roar shattered the silence of the sky, and a massive gray dragon landed heavily behind Dylan, exuding an overwhelming and savage aura.

N's pupils contracted, and the blue lightning in the storm cloud ahead suddenly ceased.

"This... this is..." Ghetsis stammered in disbelief, while Colress's eyes twitched violently, causing him to step even further away from Ghetsis and N.

In the dim light, the black-clad youth stood with a serene smile. The Dragon of Truth stood proudly at his right, while the strongest dragon on land loomed behind him.

What an awe-inspiring sight!

"Well then, I'll ask one more time. Will you kindly drop your Pokéballs, put your hands on your heads, and squat down quietly?" Dylan's mocking tone cut through the tension as he glanced around at the stunned Plasma Team members, finally focusing on Ghetsis's grim face.

"Or... do you intend to resist someone who possesses both Kyurem and Reshiram?"

(End of chapter)

# 163: Chapter 163: You ****

"Are we surrounded by Team Plasma?"

"No, no, no!"

Lillie's eyes sparkled as she looked at the black-clad young man exuding an unparalleled aura. Despite her upbringing as an educated lady, she couldn't find the right words to describe the excitement she felt.

"Brother, you're amazing!"

"Lillie."

"Yes, Brother!"

"Call Champion Alder and ask him to bring officers to take these people away."

Dylan handed his phone to Lillie, keeping his gaze fixed on Ghetsis and N.

"This is just perfect. I was planning to leave Unova soon, and yet here comes such a delightful surprise."

"Got it!"

Lillie felt it was her duty. She grabbed the phone, quickly navigated to Alder's number, and dialed.

"Hello? Champion Alder? This is Lillie. My brother has surrounded Team Plasma single-handedly. Can you send Officer Jenny and her team to handle this?"

"How many? Hmm... I'd say there are about 20 to 30 members here."

Lillie glanced around, roughly estimating the number.

Meanwhile, on Ghetsis's side—

"Father, that's Kyurem!!"

N was stunned. That gray dragon—there was no mistaking it. It was Kyurem! But how? How could Dylan have captured Kyurem? It didn't make sense!

Just yesterday, Kyurem was chasing Dylan and Reshiram. If not for N and Zekrom being lured away, Reshiram might not have survived.

But today, Kyurem was Dylan's Pokémon?

N couldn't comprehend it.

"…"

Ghetsis's face darkened, his fury palpable. Of course, he recognized Kyurem—it was a crucial part of his plan. But that plan wasn't supposed to start yet!

"N, go and defeat Reshiram and Kyurem!"

"??? Father?"

N was genuinely shocked by his adoptive father's command.

What made Ghetsis think he could defeat Kyurem, let alone Reshiram? Zekrom barely escaped with its life yesterday!

"What's wrong? I raised you to fulfill this idealistic path, and now our greatest obstacle stands before us. Are you going to back down, N?"

Ghetsis's frustration was mounting, his eyes boring into N.

Faced with Ghetsis's absurd command, N fell silent.

"N!! Are you going to disappoint me like this?!"

"Father, even Zekrom is no match for Kyurem. We need to retreat." N pressed his lips together, trying to reason with Ghetsis.

"Silence! N, you are the king of Team Plasma! How can you say such things?"

Ghetsis was livid, enraged by N's perceived betrayal.

"…" N's silence deepened.

He couldn't understand how the once-wise Ghetsis had become so irrational.

Colress, observing the scene, stifled a laugh. Ghetsis was losing it, and N wasn't much better, trying to reason with someone whose mind was clearly unhinged.

Should I try to sneak away? Colress pondered, adjusting his glasses. But no—Reshiram and Kyurem were watching them closely. Any attempt to flee would likely provoke an attack.

For now, he decided to wait and see.

"Have you two finished your argument yet?" Dylan's voice interrupted their tense exchange.

"If you want to keep arguing, you can do it in jail. You can even fight each other there."

Dylan's casual tone made Ghetsis's face twitch with fury.

"Champion Alder will be here soon to take over. What a pity—I was planning to wipe out your entire organization someday, but you delivered yourselves right to my doorstep."

"Reshiram."

"Roooar!"

Reshiram's majestic cry echoed as it soared into the sky.

Black Dragon! Fight me to the death! Today, I'll show you what truth is!

Though most couldn't understand Reshiram's roar, Dylan and N heard it loud and clear.

While N looked grim, Dylan found the truth-driven dragon's antics amusing.

Reshiram dove into the storm clouds, searching for Zekrom.

"Kyurem," Dylan called out.

"Roar!"

Kyurem's bellow shook the earth as icy energy radiated from its body, frost spreading rapidly.

Team Plasma members, realizing the danger, either released their Pokémon or cautiously retreated.

"Take it easy. Just incapacitate them," Dylan instructed.

Kyurem unleashed a radiant icy shockwave—Glaciate. The freezing energy spread like a tidal wave, enveloping everything in its path.

Within seconds, the entire area was transformed into a frozen wasteland.

"...So terrifying…"

Lillie clung to Dylan's sleeve, seeking comfort as she stared at the frost-covered landscape.

The once-proud Plasma grunts were now frozen solid, their expressions locked in fear.

Even their Pokémon were encased in ice.

"What a chilling sight." Dylan caught a falling snowflake, his amused gaze fixed on Ghetsis.

"You!!" Ghetsis glared, trembling with rage.

"N! Are you just going to stand there? Use Zekrom to defeat him!"

Ghetsis was desperate, his plan crumbling before his eyes.

"Father, please retreat. I'll hold him off," N tried to persuade him, knowing Zekrom stood no chance.

But Dylan cut in, his tone light yet menacing.

"There's no need to decide. Neither of you is leaving here today."

"There's no need to discuss further. I can responsibly tell you both—you aren't going anywhere."

Dylan clicked his tongue. Coming right to the door and still thinking about leaving? What a joke.

Suddenly, Reshiram's telepathy came through.

"Dylan, I can't find the black dragon..."

"?"

Dylan froze and looked up at the sky. Reshiram emerged from the dark clouds, clearly having searched thoroughly within them but finding no trace of the black dragon.

Seriously? The black dragon ran off this fast, abandoning its own chosen hero?

Dylan's expression turned strange as he glanced behind at Kyurem. There was no need to guess—Zekrom must have bolted so quickly because of Kyurem's presence.

Kyurem: "?"

Kyurem returned the look with a puzzled expression.

"What a shameless black dragon! Ashamed to even share the title of dragon god, it actually abandoned the hero it chose and ran away. How does it deserve to be called a dragon god?"

Reshiram descended from the sky, cursing as it went. It had been planning to give Zekrom a good thrashing today, but Zekrom's utter lack of backbone was beyond belief.

Disgusting! Absolutely disgusting! Even I, the white dragon, have never stooped so low!

"N, where's your Zekrom?"

"..."

N was silent. Evidently, he was well aware of Zekrom's escape.

"As expected of you~"

Seeing this, Dylan raised an eyebrow. Clearly, the moment Kyurem appeared, N had realized things wouldn't end well today and had Zekrom leave in advance.

No wonder the black clouds above had stopped flashing blue lightning for a while—it seemed the black dragon had hidden its presence and flown away long ago, even discarding the clouds it brought.

"Father, you must leave quickly," N urged his adoptive father once again.

"Then I'll leave it to you."

Ghetsis's expression flickered with uncertainty, but he ultimately decided to retreat.

"You aren't going anywhere."

"Dylan! I won't let you harm my father!"

"Oh? But even if I don't act, you still won't be able to leave," Dylan said with a smirk, raising an eyebrow at the stern-faced N.

"?" N froze. What did that mean?

"Take a look behind you," Dylan gestured at something behind N.

When N turned around, he was completely stunned.

Three humanoid, insect-like Pokémon stood in a triangular formation, facing a pale-faced Ghetsis.

These were Genesect, the ultimate ancient hunters revived and modified by Colress.

Ghetsis's face turned ghastly as he glared at Colress, who stood behind the Genesect, wearing a faint smile.

"Colress!!!"

Ghetsis's voice was practically squeezed out of his throat.

"What are you doing!!"

Ghetsis roared. This Colress—using his machines, his laboratory, and the fossils he provided—was now turning the weapons he resurrected against him?!!

What utter betrayal!

"I'm not doing anything, esteemed Sage Ghetsis."

Colress adjusted his glasses, his pristine white coat standing out starkly in the dim environment. His voice was calm but razor-sharp.

"As a man of justice aligned with the Unova League, how could I not apprehend you, the leader of an evil organization, upon encountering you? Do you see any issue with that?"

"Colress!!!"

Ghetsis clutched his chest, his eyes burning red as he glared at Colress in rage.

This guy! He was turning the tables at a time like this!

"Don't worry. I'll be sure to visit you in prison when I have the time, Sage Ghetsis," Colress sighed.

"Colress!! I'll **** you, you ****… blegh~!!"

Ghetsis couldn't hold it back anymore and spat out blood.

"Father!!"

"Oh dear, Sage Ghetsis, you really need to take care of your health. How could you cough up blood just like that?"

Colress spread his hands innocently.

"You all saw it—he did it himself. I didn't even touch him."

(End of chapter)
