# 171: Chapter 171: Prison

Dylan was visiting someone in prison when he heard an unexpected piece of news: Charles Goodshow's grandson's Darkrai had been stolen by Team Rocket. And it happened during the infamous incident aboard the S.S. Anne.

Dylan's expression turned peculiar. Wasn't that Tobias? Tobias is Charles Goodshow's grandson?

"Wait, the president has a grandson?" Dylan asked, his tone filled with surprise. He had never heard of this before.

"Yes," Charles Goodshow admitted. "But as you know, with my position being so prominent, many eyes are constantly on me." He sighed heavily. "Especially those lawless and deranged criminal organizations. They'll stop at nothing."

He continued, looking somewhat ashamed. "So, my grandson was raised in a different region to protect him. I must confess, I've provided him with quite a bit of support over the years, guided by my own selfish desires."

Dylan nodded understandingly. "That's only natural. You're human, and a little favoritism toward family is entirely reasonable." He wasn't surprised. True selflessness is rare, and at least Charles Goodshow was honest about his partiality.

"Unfortunately," Charles Goodshow sighed again, "the boy doesn't have much talent as a Trainer. He even lost his Darkrai to Team Rocket. Such a pity…"

This time, Dylan caught a hint of disappointment, though it was clear Charles Goodshow was more regretful of having helped Tobias acquire Darkrai in the first place.

"Wow, what a coincidence! My brother has a Darkrai too!" Lillie chimed in, her innocent words slipping out before she could think twice.

Charles Goodshow perked up at this revelation. "I've heard about that. Dylan, where did you capture your Darkrai?" he asked, curious. Darkrai, after all, is a mythical Pokémon typically found in the Sinnoh region.

Dylan's expression grew odd. "Not to hide anything from you, President," he began, "but I got my Darkrai… from a Team Rocket base."

Silence.

Charles Goodshow froze, his expression stiffening. Lillie, too, was struck speechless.

It didn't take much thought to connect the dots. The Darkrai stolen from Tobias by Team Rocket—could it possibly be the same one Dylan had now?

Lillie, realizing this, blushed furiously and covered her face in embarrassment.

"So that's how it is," Charles Goodshow eventually said with a wry smile. "It seems this Darkrai has a special connection with you, Dylan."

What else could he say? Ask Dylan to return the Darkrai to Tobias? That was out of the question. Charles Goodshow wasn't shameless enough to make such a demand.

Tobias lost the Darkrai, and that was the reality. It only proved that Tobias's bond with Darkrai wasn't strong enough. Meanwhile, Dylan had reclaimed and bonded with it, making it his own.

"How's the Darkrai doing?" Charles Goodshow asked.

"It's quite talented and incredibly hardworking. It's now one of my main team members," Dylan replied with a smile. Even if Charles Goodshow had asked him to return the Darkrai, Dylan would have refused outright.

Regardless of its past, Darkrai was his Pokémon now. Dylan was confident that even if Tobias were to show up, Darkrai would still choose to stay with him.

"That's good to hear." Charles Goodshow nodded, satisfied. His grandson's lack of aptitude as a Trainer was becoming increasingly clear.

"Anyway, back to the matter at hand," Charles Goodshow said, steering the conversation away. "Dylan, I hope you can speak with Lance. His pent-up negative emotions are growing stronger, and I'm worried they might one day explode."

Dylan nodded thoughtfully. "I think I understand your concern."

The line between good and evil is never simple. When fighting evil, one risks becoming evil themselves. Charles Goodshow was perceptive enough to notice Lance's descent into obsession. But even as the respected president of the Pokémon League, his words often fell on deaf ears.

It wasn't just Lance, though. Lorelei was showing signs of a similar struggle, albeit less severe due to her calm and rational nature.

After some more discussion, Dylan and Lillie prepared to leave but decided to visit the prison where certain high-profile criminals were held.

"Hey there, Ariana! Long time no see. I came to visit you!" Dylan greeted with a smile, leaning against the iron bars.

The red-haired woman inside, who had been reading a book, turned around, her face darkening immediately.

Yes, it was Ariana, one of the four Rocket Executives.

"…Dylan." Ariana's voice was cold.

"How've you been? The League's food seems decent, and your skin looks healthier now. Honestly, you used to look so pale—like a vampire!" Dylan teased.

Snap!

The pencil in Ariana's hand broke cleanly in two.

Lillie: (—)

"Officer Jenny, I refuse to let this man visit me!" Ariana said flatly, placing the broken pencil back on the table.

Officer Jenny looked uncertainly at Dylan.

"Aw, Ariana, don't be like that. Aren't we old friends?" Dylan replied, feigning innocence.

"Old friends?" Ariana laughed dryly. "May I remind you, Dylan, that you're the one who personally threw me into that police car!"

She gritted her teeth, recalling how she had once been a prominent Rocket Executive with dozens of subordinates and the status of an Elite Trainer.

Now? She was stuck eating bland food and forced to read books every day under the guise of "self-reflection and moral rehabilitation."

"Oh, come on. It's for your own good. Being in Team Rocket was far too dangerous. Look at it this way—you've got time to read now!" Dylan said earnestly.

"Don't worry. Soon, I'll bring Archer and Proton here to keep you company. The three of you can even play cards together!" he added, patting her on the shoulder through the bars.

Ariana's dry laughter stopped abruptly as she glared at him, her frustration palpable.

"Hah, anyone can brag. If you have the guts, bring Charr here too," Ariana sneered coldly, trying to provoke him.

"Mr. Charr? It seems, Miss Ariana, that you don't get along well with Mr. Charr," Dylan mused, stroking his chin thoughtfully. Charr, one of Team Rocket's Three Beasts, was infamous for his short temper, cruelty, and obsession with self-destructive tactics—a notorious hot-headed dwarf.

"That idiot? Nobody in Team Rocket likes him. Dylan, if you can bring Charr in and put him in the room next to mine, I can provide you with some critical intel," Ariana stated coldly. Although her single cell was decent, she was utterly bored!

She only had two hours a day to stretch her legs, which was driving her crazy. Reading? Yeah, right. Ariana was not the type to sit and read.

Officer Jenny, who had been silently following, widened her eyes and looked at Dylan hopefully.

"What do you think?" Ariana stared at Dylan.

Ariana had no issue with Proton and Archer being captured—they were coworkers, after all. Chatting with them would pass the time. But that obnoxious little dwarf, Charr? She had endured him for far too long! If he got thrown in here, Ariana could mock him for an entire year. Just imagining his frustrated, angry face lifted her spirits significantly.

"It's not impossible. Rest assured, Miss Ariana, your colleagues will join you soon enough," Dylan replied with a faint smile.

"I'll visit you again, Miss Ariana. I won't disturb your reading anymore. Next time, I'll bring some fruit," Dylan said courteously before bowing slightly and leaving with Lillie.

"Officer Jenny," Ariana called out, grabbing the bars as she watched Dylan's figure disappear into the distance, deep in thought.

"What is it?" Officer Jenny asked, startled.

"Can you prepare some workout equipment for me? Dumbbells, sandbags, things like that."

"???" Officer Jenny looked at Ariana in utter confusion. Why the sudden interest in exercise?

"Please, I insist! I've come to understand the League's teachings deeply. I'm determined to grow stronger, starting with fitness!" Ariana said with utmost seriousness.

No, I feel like that's not what you're thinking, Officer Jenny thought skeptically. Still, providing gym equipment wasn't entirely out of the question. As a criminal at an Elite Four level, Ariana did have some special privileges. Besides, with her Pokémon confiscated and sealed, she was just an ordinary person now. Letting her exercise wouldn't cause any harm.

"Please agree! Officer Jenny, I'm begging you. This is the most earnest request I've ever made in my life!" Ariana pleaded.

"Fine, but you'll need to add an extra hour of reading each day," Officer Jenny countered, her brows furrowed.

Reading? Are you kidding me?! Ariana's face darkened. But…

"Alright! I love reading the most!" she replied through gritted teeth.

"Alright then, I'll prepare it for you," Officer Jenny said with a satisfied smile as she walked off.

Ariana clenched her teeth. What a huge sacrifice! But it didn't matter. She'd use this time to train and, when Charr and Petrel got thrown in here, she'd smack them senseless! Charr, you'll understand soon enough—the advantage of striking first! I'll make you bleed! Dylan, you'd better work hard and bring those fools in quickly so I can use them as my toys!

This was Ariana's inner voice.

Meanwhile, Dylan and Lillie were already riding on Reshiram, heading back to Rota.

"It feels like forever since we've been back," Lillie sighed as the majestic white dragon descended at the entrance to Rota. The duo dismounted gracefully.

"It's been less than a week. Don't make it sound like ages," Dylan chuckled, playfully pinching Lillie's cheek. Still, seeing the familiar bulletin board of Rota brought a sense of nostalgia.

"Dylan?" a surprised young voice called from behind as Dylan and Lillie were about to enter.

"Yo~ Mr. Gary. Long time no see," Dylan said enthusiastically, turning to spot none other than Gary. His eyes lit up as he greeted him warmly.

"..."

Gary, pushing his bike, twitched at the corner of his mouth, especially when his eyes landed on the magnificent, pure white dragon standing next to Dylan.

His gaze sharpened, turning crystal clear.

After more than a month of intense training, Gary had planned to challenge the Rota Gym for the fourth time today.

But...maybe he should come back another day?

(To be continued)

# 172: Chapter 172: Apprentice

"What a coincidence, Mr. Gary. I just got back from a trip and happened to run into you," Dylan remarked, marveling at the serendipity.

Dylan couldn't help but feel there was some extraordinary, unexplainable bond between him and Gary. It was uncanny!

Or perhaps not necessary at all.

Gary's mouth twitched as he stared at Dylan's overwhelming enthusiasm. He wanted to step back but worried it might seem rude.

"I specifically went to Pallet Town to visit Professor Oak recently. I must say, he's such a dedicated and outstanding mentor. No wonder both you and Blue are so exceptional," Dylan said with sincere admiration.

"Speaking of which, Mr. Gary, where's Blue? I haven't seen him for quite some time and miss him greatly," Dylan asked curiously. After all, Blue had mentioned challenging him again, but two months had passed, and there was no sign of him.

"Blue? He's apparently training in Mt. Silver and hasn't come back yet," Gary recalled.

'This Rota Badge? Once I return from Mt. Silver, I will claim it!'

Back when they parted ways, Blue had said this. Gary wondered how his brother's training in Mt. Silver was going.

"Oh, I see. I hope Blue returns safely. I'm really looking forward to our second battle," Dylan said, a bit disappointed. Blue was a strong challenger, and their matches were always exciting.

Mt. Silver, located near the Indigo Plateau, was one of the Kanto region's most famous wilderness areas. It was home to high-level wild Pokémon, a challenge even for seasoned trainers. Deep within the mountain, Tyranitars were said to reside.

Thinking back, Blue, as the Viridian City Gym Leader in the games, had a Tyranitar. Perhaps Blue in this world would obtain one earlier.

"So, are you here today to challenge my gym again, Mr. Gary?" Dylan asked with a warm smile.

"I suppose…" Gary glanced at the pristine white dragon standing nearby. Though unfamiliar with the Pokémon, it was clear this was no ordinary creature.

"You don't need to worry, Mr. Gary. Reshiram is part of the Golden Legends team. As long as you don't switch to the Golden side, it won't be an issue," Dylan explained, noticing Gary's apprehension.

Gary's expression softened. That made sense. He had no plans to switch allegiances—his luck had always been solid.

Still, this legendary Pokémon was truly magnificent, exuding elegance and grandeur. Even Gary's cheerleader girlfriends, who were biking behind him, stared at Reshiram with hearts in their eyes.

"Well then, I—"

Gary was about to declare his intent to challenge the gym when suddenly…

"Gym Leader Dylan~!"

A soft, melodic voice called out. Dylan turned to see a graceful young woman in a kimono, her fair complexion framed by long black hair. Her cherry-colored attire was as elegant as ever—Jeanette, a former challenger.

However, Gary's face darkened upon seeing her.

"Miss Jeanette, you're here at Rota as well," Dylan said, surprised. Today was full of unexpected reunions—Gary and Jeanette were his first two challengers since taking over the gym.

"Yes, I happened to be passing by. I wasn't planning to visit since I heard you weren't in, but I decided to check just in case. What a pleasant surprise!" Jeanette's delight was evident as she spoke in her gentle tone.

"You seem a bit different now, Miss Jeanette—more confident and composed," Dylan said with a smile, complimenting her. Compared to a few months ago, Jeanette's demeanor had matured significantly.

"That's thanks to your guidance, Gym Leader Dylan. I've gained clarity and determination about my path. I'm truly grateful," Jeanette said with a graceful bow, her gratitude sincere.

Gary's eye twitched as he listened. About two weeks ago, he had coincidentally encountered Jeanette in the wild. He recognized her instantly, having once secretly watched her challenge Dylan. Back then, Dylan's apparent favoritism irked him.

Jeanette's distinctive appearance made her unforgettable. When Gary saw her, he decided to provoke her into a battle.

And then…

And then he lost.

This seemingly gentle and elegant woman wielded Poison-type Pokémon with terrifying expertise. Gary's team at the time was ill-equipped to counter Poison-types, and he was utterly frustrated by her tactics.

"Dylan, this is all your fault!" Gary grumbled internally.

Rota Gym

As Dylan and Lillie entered the gym, they were greeted by Asha and Selene holding party poppers.

"Welcome back, Gym Leader Dylan and Lillie!" they cheered.

"Surprise, Gym Leader Dylan! Were you expecting this?" Asha beamed.

Colorful confetti rained down as Dylan chuckled, shaking his head. He hadn't informed the girls of his return—they must've heard from Lillie.

"There are challengers here, so get ready," Dylan said, brushing confetti off himself.

"Got it~!"

Asha and Selene noticed Gary and Jeanette behind Dylan and quickly scampered off.

"Apologies for the commotion," Dylan said.

"No need. The gym's atmosphere feels warm and lively," Jeanette said with a gentle smile. Gary just shrugged.

"Now then, who would like to challenge first?" Dylan asked as he led them to the spin wheel. Both Gary and Jeanette were prepared to challenge the Rota Gym.

"Ladies first," Gary said with a hint of chivalry. In truth, he was curious to see how Jeanette, a Poison-type specialist, would fare against Dylan.

"Randomized attributes?" Jeanette asked, intrigued by the wheel. Although she'd been following updates about the Rota Gym online, it had been a while since she last visited.

"That's correct. However, I have a suggestion—if you're both willing to consider it," Dylan proposed after some thought.

"The Rota Gym allows double battles. You two could team up to challenge me in a doubles match," Dylan said with a smile.

Gary and Jeanette exchanged surprised glances, then began contemplating the idea.

"If you defeat me or perform exceptionally, you'll each earn a Rota Badge," Dylan added.

"You mean the two of us against you?" Gary asked, considering the feasibility. Though Jeanette's Poison-type tactics were annoying, he acknowledged her strength.

Jeanette also weighed the suggestion.

"Normally, yes, but today I'd like my apprentice Selene to partner with me for the battle," Dylan said, gesturing for Selene to join them.

"What's up, Gym Leader Dylan?" Selene asked as she approached.

Selene jogged over, her pure, fair face showing a look of obedience. She appeared to be a harmless, sweet girl.

Jeanette curiously observed Selene, while Gary was trying to recall information about the girl. If he remembered correctly, this girl always a member of the cheering squad for Dylan.

"She's also a Trainer?"

"Selene is currently my apprentice. She received her first Pokémon from me about three months ago and will officially come of age in about two months," Dylan explained with a smile to Jeanette and Gary.

"If the two of you agree, Selene and I will team up to accept your Double Battle challenge."

Selene's clean and bright eyes sparkled instantly. A Double Battle with Gym Leader Bro?

Wow!!!

It was perfect timing. She was getting bored of bullying the local Bug Catcher Boys, Shorts Kids, and Hikers nearby.

"I think that's fine. What about you, Jeanette?" Gary asked nonchalantly, furiously signaling to the hesitant Jeanette with his eyes.

Agree! Just agree already!!

This was clearly Dylan handing them a badge on a silver platter. Selene wasn't even an official Trainer yet. Even if Dylan himself was strong, so what?

Could he possibly handle two opponents at once?

Well, if he used his main team, sure, but there was no way Dylan would do that.

Assuming the Pokémon's levels were similar, Dylan wouldn't stand a chance against both of them, right?

It was obvious that Dylan wanted to give his apprentice some training. This was perfect!

"But isn't this unfair? Selene isn't even an official Trainer..." Jeanette was still hesitant, although she understood Gary's reasoning.

Wasn't this a bit too one-sided?

"Ah? It's fine! I'm actually really strong!"

Selene blinked her big watery eyes and looked at Jeanette pitifully, her gaze practically pleading.

"Don't underestimate Selene. Her talent is extraordinary," Dylan chuckled and shook his head. To avoid them underestimating Selene, he even emphasized her abilities by saying "extraordinary" twice.

Selene's talent as a Trainer was comparable to prodigies like Red and Blue.

"Of course, even though we're novice Trainers, we won't underestimate a newbie," Gary said seriously.

"Then... alright," Jeanette finally agreed after hearing Gary's response.

"Yay!!"

Selene was overjoyed. As the apprentice of the Rota Gym, she was finally getting the chance to face challengers in battle.

And she would be teaming up with her admired Gym Leader Bro in a Double Battle!

Seeing Selene's innocent and cheerful expression, both Gary and Jeanette felt a pang of guilt.

They were definitely going to target this girl's Pokémon as their weak point.

"Now, could one of you spin the roulette to determine the type I'll use?" Dylan asked with a smile.

Naturally, Gary let Jeanette take the spin. As long as it didn't land on Gold, they were fine.

Jeanette curiously pressed the button on the roulette for the first time, and it stopped on the Dark type. This meant Dylan would use a Dark-type team for the Double Battle.

With the type decided, the four of them moved to the battlefield. Dylan and Selene stood on one side, Gary and Jeanette on the other.

"This is a Gym Challenge for the Rota Badge! The battle will be a Double Battle between Gym Leader Dylan and Selene versus Jeanette and Gary!" the referee announced.

"The battle rules are 3 vs. 3. Each participant may use three Pokémon. The Gym Leader's team will send out their Pokémon first and cannot switch during the battle."

"Begin!"

The referee declared the start of the battle.

"A Double Battle with Gym Leader Bro and Selene? I wonder how Selene will perform," Lillie watched from the audience.

Selene seemed a bit nervous—it was her's first official match representing the Rota Gym, after all.

"Selene will be fine! Big Bro has always been very satisfied with her progress," Lillie reassured her.

"I know, but... it should be okay."

Selene pursed her lips, recalling the past few days of wandering around Rota and Mt. Moon with Lillie, challenging local Trainers. Her expression turned odd.

Apparently, Rota and Mt. Moon had begun circulating rumors about a "terrifying black-haired, straw-hat girl."

Although she looked pure and sweet, her methods were said to be ruthless, even reducing children to tears. Not just one—several! Truly terrifying.

On the field.

"Don't hold me back, Jeanette! This is our best chance to win the Rota Badge!" Gary warned solemnly.

"Don't worry, Gary. You know the team I'm using, right? Just don't get in the way," Jeanette replied confidently.

"Of course, I know."

Gary smirked, glancing at the innocent-looking girl in the straw hat. A Gym apprentice? Laughable!

Wasn't this an obvious weak point? If they defeated all of her Pokémon first, they could legally turn this into a 2 vs. 1 battle. How could they possibly lose?

Victory was guaranteed!

(To be continued)

# 173: Chapter 173: You’re Truly Amazing!

"Selene, are you ready?"

"No problem at all, Dylan!"

Dylan asked Selene, who energetically gave an "OK" gesture, her expression excited yet slightly nervous.

Although she had been sparring frequently with trainers in the wild these past few days, and winning easily, this battle was different.

After all, it was a match representing the Rota Gym, making it a bit more significant.

"Well then, Gary and Jeanette, give it your best shot," Dylan said, his gaze turning to Gary and Jeanette on the opposite side as he enlarged a Poké Ball in his hand.

For a battle against Gary and Jeanette, he would have to rely on his backup Pokémon.

"Grimmsnarl! Get ready for battle!"

"Incineroar! Prepare for action!"

Dylan threw his Poké Ball backward, and at nearly the same time, Selene immediately threw her first Poké Ball.

Two beams of white light flashed, and two Pokémon appeared in front of Dylan and Selene.

Gary's face turned pale halfway through, as he saw that the Incineroar had landed in front of Selene, meaning it was her Pokémon.

Wasn't she supposed to have only been a trainer for three months? And she already had an Incineroar?

And the Grimmsnarl in front of Dylan… Gary thought hard for a moment before recalling its typing: Dark and Fairy.

"Ugh… its appearance is way too abstract," someone muttered.

In the audience, Gary's and Jeanette's cheering squads were sitting together—after all, they were allies.

But when they saw the Grimmsnarl, some women in the group let out quiet jeers. Its appearance was a little… startling.

The Grimmsnarl had a humanoid devil-like appearance. Its upper body was red, its lower body green, and its arms were long and thin, reaching down to its feet. The hair on its head extended down to its legs, resembling a tail.

It was the evolved form of Morgrem.

Beep! Grimmsnarl, a Dark and Fairy Pokémon, the Prankster Pokémon.

Ability: Prankster

Moves: False Surrender, Power Whip, Taunt, Spirit Break, Foul Play, Confide, Trick, Sucker Punch, and Parting Shot.

Description: Grimmsnarl prefers sneaky and underhanded tactics because it lacks confidence in its muscle strength.

However, upon seeing Grimmsnarl's move set, the cheerleaders fell silent one by one.

"Dark and Fire-type Incineroar, Dark and Fairy-type Grimmsnarl…" Gary muttered to Jeanette.

Double battles were relatively rare in the Kanto region. Typically, they required strong synergy between the trainers as well as excellent teamwork and adaptability.

Gary wanted to win. He knew Jeanette's skills and capabilities were solid and at least on par with his own.

"I understand," Jeanette replied with a polite nod.

"Go! Nidoqueen!"

"Crobat!"

Jeanette and Gary simultaneously threw their Poké Balls.

A Nidoqueen and a Crobat flapped their wings as they appeared before the two trainers.

Beep! Nidoqueen, a Poison and Ground-type, the Drill Pokémon.

Ability: Poison Point

Moves: Superpower, Earth Power, Crunch, Toxic Spikes, Flamethrower, Iron Tail, Venom Shock…

Description: Its body is covered in tough, spike-like scales, and its spines stand erect when excited.

"So that Nidorina has evolved," Asha murmured as she looked at Nidoqueen's Pokédex entry. She still remembered how Jeanette used a Nidorina to challenge Dylan in the past.

Asha then shifted her gaze to Gary's Crobat. It was clearly his Golbat, which had also evolved.

Beep! Crobat, a Poison and Flying-type, the Bat Pokémon.

Ability: Infiltrator

Moves: Cross Poison, Tailwind, Mean Look, Toxic Fang, Air Slash, Haze, Supersonic, Taunt…

Description: With an additional pair of wings, Crobat can silently glide through the dark. You might not even notice it passing by.

Upon seeing Crobat's move set, Lillie's small face showed a hint of surprise. This was a very targeted approach.

"Oh? Both of them have evolved. You've truly grown a lot. I'm pleased," Dylan said as he looked at the formidable presence of Nidoqueen and Crobat.

It was like a teacher watching the growth of their students with satisfaction.

"Please witness the fruits of my training, Dylan," Jeanette said with a gentle, elegant smile, maintaining her ladylike demeanor.

"That Incineroar will definitely use Fake Out on the first move—it won't deal much damage. Let's focus on the Grimmsnarl," Gary advised Jeanette, his determination evident. He really wanted to win!

"I understand. Although it has been a long time since I last challenged you, I have been paying attention," Jeanette replied with a soft smile.

"Good," Gary said, feeling more at ease.

The battle was about to begin. Incineroar let out a low growl, exuding an intimidating aura.

Intimidate!

Nidoqueen and Crobat's physical attacks dropped by one level.

"Crobat, use Air Slash!"

"Nidoqueen, use Helping Hand!"

Crobat flapped its four wings as a white glow emanated, while Nidoqueen raised its arms to assist.

"Fake Out!"

Selene and Dylan shouted simultaneously.

Gary's eyelid twitched. The Grimmsnarl could also use Fake Out.

Incineroar and Grimmsnarl's eyes flashed black as they darted toward Nidoqueen and Crobat like lightning.

Fake Out!

Smack!

Incineroar and Grimmsnarl's claws struck Nidoqueen and Crobat, causing them to flinch.

That was the effectiveness of Fake Out.

Unlike Dylan's strategy, however, Selene didn't follow up with Parting Shot.

"Incineroar, use Throat Chop!"

Selene raised her arm and shouted at Incineroar, her demeanor completely different from her usual sweet and innocent image. In battle mode, Selene's aura was sharp and commanding.

Incineroar's muscular body spun rapidly, its powerful arms slamming ferociously toward Nidoqueen.

The Dark-type Throat Chop!

Bam!

The move struck Nidoqueen squarely, sending her crashing to the ground with a painful roar.

Seeing Nidoqueen knocked down, Jeanette's eyes narrowed. This attack power... is it really this strong?

"Crobat! Cross Poison!" Gary's expression shifted, and he immediately attempted to support Nidoqueen.

"Swagger," Dylan commanded calmly.

Grimmsnarl crossed its arms over its chest, displaying a smug, mocking grin. It let out a piercing cry, dark energy radiating and enveloping Crobat.

The Dark-type Swagger!

This sharply increased Crobat's physical attack but left it confused.

Crobat's body glowed white as a few symbols of confusion—like "X"s—seemed to hover above its head. It wavered unsteadily, clearly disoriented.

Crobat's physical attack sharply rose, but it became confused!

"Damn it!" Gary clenched his fists.

I even had it learn Taunt specifically for this challenge! But before I could use it, Crobat's already confused...

"Grimmsnarl, Fake Tears," Dylan added with a faint smile.

Grimmsnarl feigned wiping its eyes, its sobs accompanied by waves of dark energy that seeped into Crobat's mind.

The Dark-type Fake Tears!

Crobat's special defense plummeted, the white glow surrounding it dissipating as it continued to reel in confusion.

"Incineroar, Flamethrower!" Selene called without missing a beat.

"Nidoqueen, Superpower!" Jeanette finally issued a counter-command, though everything had happened so quickly that her Nidoqueen had only just managed to get back on her feet.

Incineroar raised its head sharply, unleashing a torrent of searing flames that struck the confused Crobat head-on.

Boom!

The flames exploded, and even in its disoriented state, Crobat screeched in pain. Selene's Incineroar was already powerful, and with Crobat's reduced special defense, the damage was devastating.

The intense flames sent Crobat crashing down like a kite with its string cut.

Meanwhile, Nidoqueen's arm glowed white as it delivered a heavy blow to Incineroar—a Fighting-type Superpower!

The attack knocked Incineroar backward, eliciting a pained growl.

Though Superpower was super effective against Incineroar, the move's side effect caused Nidoqueen's own attack and defense stats to drop.

Gary's expression darkened. The rapid sequence of attacks had left him unable to respond effectively. In the short time it took for Crobat to become confused and get knocked out, the tide had turned drastically.

"Nidoqueen, Toxic Spikes!" Jeanette seized the chance to set up.

"Pursuit!" Dylan shifted his focus back to Crobat.

"Crobat!"

Grimmsnarl's eyes glimmered with dark energy as it lunged toward the fainted Crobat, its claws cloaked in black.

The Dark-type Pursuit!

Pursuit's power doubled since its target had just been attacked.

Slash!

The move struck Crobat, who was still struggling to recover, rendering it completely unable to battle.

Meanwhile, Nidoqueen's Toxic Spikes scattered across the battlefield, releasing streams of unsettling purple liquid into the ground.

A faint purple glow rippled across the arena before fading.

Toxic Spikes are now active!

"Wow, the Gym Leader and Selene knocked out one already!" Asha exclaimed happily.

"But with the Toxic Spikes in play, it's not looking great for them either," Lillie noted, tapping her lips thoughtfully.

"It's fine! The Gym Leader and Selene will figure something out!" Asha said with unwavering confidence in Dylan and Selene.

Back on the field:

"That Incineroar…"

Gary withdrew Crobat with a grim expression, eyeing the standing Incineroar warily.

Something's wrong. This isn't the 'weak point' we thought it would be!

This Incineroar was far stronger than anticipated, clearly the main offensive force rather than Grimmsnarl.

Grimmsnarl seemed more like a support to enhance Incineroar's effectiveness.

The only silver lining was that Jeanette's Toxic Spikes setup was complete. With the battlefield now in their favor, they hoped to regain the advantage.

"Gary, that Incineroar is incredibly strong," Jeanette warned, her gaze fixed on their formidable opponent.

"The power behind that Throat Chop didn't seem like something a Pokémon trained for only three months could achieve. And that Flamethrower? It nearly one-shot Crobat, even with its lowered defenses!"

Compared to Incineroar, Grimmsnarl seemed less threatening. Its disruptive tactics were deadly, but Incineroar was clearly the powerhouse.

"I know... It looks like Dylan wasn't bluffing after all," Gary muttered through gritted teeth.

They had assumed Dylan was cutting them some slack as "old friends," but clearly, this was anything but a freebie.

Using us as a test run for his seemingly innocent apprentice, huh?

How despicable! These trainers are pure evil!

(End of Chapter)

# 174: Chapter 174: How dare you!

"Well done, Selene."

Dylan didn't hold back his praise for Selene; her timing with the Flamethrower just now had been impeccable.

"Hehe~ It's all thanks to you, Gym Leader Dylan!"

Selene's eyes curved into crescents as she beamed.

But compared to the light-hearted atmosphere between Dylan and Selene, things were much more serious on Gary and Jeanette's side.

"Nidoking!"

After a moment's thought, Gary sent out his Nidoking. With Toxic Spikes spread across the field, he couldn't risk using non-Poison types, and his Nidoking had an advantage against Incineroar.

A powerful Nidoking landed heavily in front of Gary.

"A Poison Point Nidoking."

Asha analyzed Gary's Nidoking, noting that Nidoking and Nidoqueen often had the Poison Point ability.

"Selene."

"Leave it to me!" Selene responded energetically.

"Nidoking, Double Kick!"

Gary launched an immediate offensive.

"Toxic Spikes!"

Jeanette ordered Nidoqueen to act as well.

Nidoqueen raised her head, dark purple Poison energy condensing into a sphere. Laying two layers of Toxic Spikes would poison any opposing Pokémon that entered the battlefield!

"Incineroar, Parting Shot!"

Incineroar placed one hand on its hip, pointed at Nidoking with the other, and unleashed a barrage of taunts. Visibly agitated, Nidoking's eyes turned ferocious as its charging speed increased.

The Dark-type Parting Shot!

After delivering its verbal assault, Incineroar transformed into white light and returned to Selene's Poké Ball. Meanwhile, Nidoking's Attack and Special Attack were lowered by one stage.

"Torment," Dylan commanded his Grimmsnarl.

Grimmsnarl let out a sharp, grating screech, black ripples radiating outward.

The Dark-type Torment! Enhanced by its Prankster ability, Grimmsnarl's move quickly took effect, disrupting Nidoqueen's focus. The Poison energy sphere she had been preparing dissipated.

Nidoqueen's Toxic Spikes had failed.

"Nidoking, attack Grimmsnarl!"

With Incineroar gone, Gary immediately targeted Grimmsnarl.

Nidoking charged forward, leaping high and landing two heavy kicks on Grimmsnarl.

The Fighting-type Double Kick!

Grimmsnarl cried out in pain, sent flying by Nidoking's attack. Landing on the poisoned battlefield, its body was immediately engulfed in a sinister purple glow—the effect of Toxic Spikes!

"Pidgeot!"

Selene acted quickly, sending out her second Pokémon. A majestic Pidgeot appeared, spreading its wings and crying out.

The final evolution of Pidgey, Pidgeot!

But upon seeing this Pidgeot, Gary and Jeanette's faces changed, knowing it was capable of a critical move!

"Beep~ Pidgeot: Normal/Flying type, Bird Pokémon.

Ability: Keen Eye

Known Moves: Roost, Hurricane, Quick Attack, Wing Attack, Feather Dance, Defog, Brave Bird (Inherited)...

Description: Pidgeot's powerful wingbeats can bend even large trees."

This was bad.

Gary and Jeanette's cheering squads showed concern as they read Pidgeot's data. As expected, it knew Defog!

In Kanto, plagued by Poison teams, Pokémon capable of learning Defog or Rapid Spin were essential. Otherwise, facing Toxic Spikes was pure agony.

In a way, Dylan had encouraged this trend, making trainers more prepared for Toxic Spikes.

"Pidgeot, Defog!"

As expected, Selene had Pidgeot use Defog right away.

Pidgeot cried out, flapping its wings with immense force. A gale swept across the battlefield, clearing away the deep purple Poison spikes.

"Nidoqueen, Venoshock!"

Jeanette bit her lip. It was hard to stop the move, but at least Pidgeot would pay the price!

Nidoqueen spewed a stream of sticky purple poison toward Pidgeot.

"Nidoking, Double Kick again!"

Gary targeted Grimmsnarl, seeing through its relatively weak defenses.

"Sucker Punch," Dylan said with a calm smile.

Grimmsnarl understood instantly, its eyes flashing with black light as it darted past Nidoking.

Not attacking Nidoking? What's the plan? Gary hesitated.

But the next moment—

Grimmsnarl leaped up, intercepting the Venoshock aimed at Pidgeot.

The deep purple poison seeped into Grimmsnarl, its body glowing ominously as the toxic effects began to take hold. Grimmsnarl winced in pain.

"Gym Leader Dylan!"

Selene's eyes welled up with tears, touched by Dylan's decision to let Grimmsnarl shield Pidgeot from the poison.

"Nidoking!"

Gritting his teeth, Gary grew more frustrated.

Nidoking charged toward Grimmsnarl again, landing another Double Kick!

Grimmsnarl let out a low cry of pain, collapsing and losing its ability to fight.

Dylan had lost his first Pokémon.

"How dare you bully adorable Grimmsnarl!"

Selene's almond-shaped eyes widened as she glared at Nidoking.

"???" Gary was dumbfounded.

Since when is Grimmsnarl adorable?!

"Honchkrow."

Dylan sent out his second Pokémon.

A plump black bird with red feathers under its wings and a white tuft on its chest appeared, resembling a gentleman with a black hat on its head.

It was Honchkrow!

"Beep~ Honchkrow: Dark/Flying type, Big Boss Pokémon.

Ability: Super Luck

Known Moves: Foul Play, Taunt, Swagger, Sucker Punch, Wing Attack, Brave Bird (Inherited)...

Description: Ruthless and unforgiving, Honchkrow is said never to tolerate failure from its Murkrow underlings."

This Honchkrow was one of Dylan's strategic Pokémon, specifically trained to handle mid-level challengers like Gary and Jeanette.

"Two Flying-types, huh?"

Jeanette frowned. This put them at a disadvantage, as both Nidoking and Nidoqueen relied on Ground moves that couldn't touch Flying Pokémon.

"Return, Nidoqueen."

Jeanette recalled her Pokémon, replacing it with her second choice: Glimmora.

A crystalline flower-like Pokémon, Glimmora floated elegantly in front of Jeanette. It was a Rock/Poison type.

"Oh? So you've raised a Glimmora, Jeanette," Dylan remarked, intrigued.

"Yes, thanks to an elder in the village, I obtained a Glimmet and nurtured it into Glimmora."

Jeanette smiled proudly, clearly fond of her Pokémon.

Meanwhile, Gary replaced Nidoking with his third Pokémon: Magneton.

Aiming to counter the Flying-types, Gary had chosen his Electric-type Magneton.

"Glimmora, Ancient Power!"

"Magneton, Thunderbolt!"

Jeanette and Gary issued their commands simultaneously.

In front of Glimmora, chunks of rock materialized—the Rock-type move Ancient Power. Meanwhile, Magneton's body crackled with electricity, unleashing the Electric-type Thunderbolt.

Ancient Power and Thunderbolt struck, both moves being highly effective against Flying-types.

"Use Sucker Punch!"

With a flash of black light in its eyes, Honchkrow darted toward Magneton like a shadow, moving at incredible speed. Enhanced by Sucker Punch's priority boost, Honchkrow closed the gap in the blink of an eye, striking Magneton hard with its wing shrouded in dark energy.

Magneton was knocked back with a heavy thud.

"U-turn!"

At the same time, Pidgeot elegantly dodged Ancient Power and Thunderbolt with its superior flying skills, charging at Magneton.

The Bug-type move U-turn! Not only did it deal damage, but it allowed Pidgeot to return to its Poké Ball and switch out with another Pokémon.

Pidgeot glowed faintly green, ramming into the already stunned Magneton. The next moment, Pidgeot turned into white light, retreating into Selene's Poké Ball.

"???"

Gary's face turned grim as he realized what had just happened. "Why are they targeting me? Do I look like an easy target?"

He glanced at the massive Glimmora nearby. "Why don't you attack that instead? Ignoring it like this is just disrespectful!"

"Glimmora, use Ancient Power again!" Jeanette ordered immediately.

More rocks formed in front of Glimmora.

"Thunderbolt!" Gary gritted his teeth.

Electric currents surged from Magneton's body, golden electricity crackling toward Honchkrow, who had pulled back.

"Sucker Punch!"

Honchkrow, with its incredible speed and priority, easily evaded the incoming Thunderbolt and dodged Glimmora's Ancient Power in one swift motion.

"No good. It's too agile to hit without restricting its movements," Jeanette muttered, her lips pressed tightly together.

With Sucker Punch's priority comparable to Extreme Speed, Honchkrow was simply too overwhelming.

"Incineroar, Fake Out!" Selene quickly sent out Incineroar, her next Pokémon.

The moment Incineroar appeared, its Intimidate ability activated, lowering the physical attack of both Glimmora and Magneton.

Incineroar then charged at Magneton, slapping it sharply across the face with its paw.

Fake Out! Although it didn't deal much damage, it left Magneton flinching.

Meanwhile, Honchkrow swooped in with another Sucker Punch, delivering a solid hit to Magneton, leaving it groaning in pain.

"Magneton!" Gary was alarmed. This was bad!

"Glimmora, support with Ancient Power!" Jeanette's pupils contracted.

Glimmora prepared another Ancient Power, but its target wasn't Honchkrow this time—it was Incineroar.

"Flamethrower!" As expected, Selene immediately issued her command.

"Sharp instincts, but it's no use," Dylan remarked with a light chuckle.

"Honchkrow."

Honchkrow quickly flew above Incineroar, shielding it as Ancient Power slammed into it. Honchkrow let out a sharp cry of pain—it was a Flying-type, and Ancient Power hit hard due to Glimmora's high special attack.

Fortunately, Honchkrow was still at full health and managed to endure the hit.

Incineroar's Flamethrower hit Magneton directly. The intense flames sent Magneton flying backward and crashing to the ground.

When they looked again, Magneton was already down, unable to battle. The two Sucker Punches combined with Flamethrower were too much for it to withstand.

Most of all, Selene's Incineroar's Flamethrower had done massive damage due to its type advantage.

"They're targeting me!!"

Gary clenched his teeth as he stared at the fainted Magneton.

There was no doubt about it—Dylan and Selene were focusing their attacks on him, treating him as the weak link.

"Why? What did I ever do to deserve this? Can't you target her Glimmora instead? What's wrong with you two?!"

"..." Jeanette was at a loss for words.

"Gary, you only have one Pokémon left—Nidoking. If it gets knocked out, this match is over," she subtly reminded him.

She couldn't handle a 1v2 on her own, especially against opponents like Dylan and Selene. Selene's strength was far beyond their expectations.

"But they're blatantly targeting me! Look at Honchkrow and Incineroar—they're completely ignoring your Glimmora!"

Gary's face was a mixture of frustration and despair.

What was with this ridiculous team-up? Why were they so dead set on annoying him?!

Especially that Honchkrow with its Sucker Punch and Selene's Incineroar with Fake Out—it was too much to deal with.

If it weren't for Selene switching to Incineroar, Magneton might have survived a little longer.

This was beyond frustrating—Gary felt utterly defeated.

How could a doubles match feel so one-sided and infuriating?! This blatant targeting was enough to make him sick.

(End of Chapter)

# 175: Chapter 175: Leah

The battle that followed was already without suspense. Gary only had a Nidoking left, while Jeanette still had three Pokémon.

However, it didn't matter.

Under the coordinated efforts of Dylan and Selene, Gary's Nidoking was quickly overwhelmed and lost its fighting ability.

Though Selene's Incineroar also lost its fighting ability, it didn't matter.

Because Jeanette was now going to battle alone. With her double battle partner eliminated, the chances of turning things around were slim.

"I surrender."

After realizing there was no hope, Jeanette helplessly chose to forfeit.

There was no point continuing the battle. Dylan and Selene worked well together, and both had strong Pokémon.

It was just too difficult; she could only say it was too difficult.

Jeanette instinctively glanced at Gary, who nervously turned away, silently avoiding her gaze.

It was awkward, but Gary was more speechless. What could he do?

Sigh~ Jeanette sighed inwardly. She had worked hard to decide to challenge the Rota Gym for the second time.

She should have known better than to do a double battle. Her own challenge might have made more sense, even if it meant commanding two Pokémon alone.

"Dylan Gym Leader, Selene."

When Jeanette regained her composure, Dylan and Selene walked over. She straightened her emotions and spoke up.

Meanwhile, Gary was almost visibly disgusted.

"Unfortunately, you two still haven't defeated us," Dylan said with a smile, meeting their gazes.

"The double battle challenges are more about teamwork and quick reflexes, but unfortunately, you two didn't show that today," he continued, shaking his head. "Though we were targeting Gary for a focused breakthrough, it wasn't without a solution. But neither of you figured out how to defend and counterattack. It's not just about teamwork; it's more than that."

"I feel very ashamed," Jeanette said, a bit helpless. Even though Gary was the first to be overwhelmed, since it was a double battle, Jeanette couldn't do anything about it. She too bore some responsibility.

All in all, both of them didn't perform well in this battle.

"I really don't suit double battles," Gary remarked with a roll of his eyes and a shrug.

"Forget it, Dylan Gym Leader, I'll challenge again next time," he added, waving his hand. There was no point in staying after losing.

"Goodbye."

Dylan smiled and watched Gary and his cheerleaders leave.

"Dylan Gym Leader, is Selene really just an apprentice?" Jeanette asked, curious and amazed. If true, that was incredible.

"Mm-hmm~" Selene blinked her big eyes. She must be impressed by my strength, huh? Haha~

"Yes," Dylan nodded.

"She's so strong~!" Jeanette admired. To reach such a level in just three months—she must be a monster.

"Hehe~ My teacher taught me well," Selene said modestly, though clearly pleased with the praise.

"Selene is very talented. I believe that once she sets off on her journey, she will make even more progress," Dylan said with a smile. Selene was already close to reaching the Elite Four level, truly an amazing growth speed.

Too bad she didn't start her journey at the same time as Red and Blue. Otherwise, Dylan would've loved to see them all competing together.

But then again, if they started at the same time, Selene would likely be on a tour of the Alola region by now.

"Will Selene participate in the Indigo League this year?" Jeanette asked curiously. With only two months left before the Indigo League, it seemed like there wasn't much time, but with Selene's strength, she could probably still get the badges.

"I'm not sure," Selene said, frowning. "I want to be the champion, but I don't think I can beat Champion Lance this year."

"I probably won't challenge the League this year. I'll wait until next year," she added after a moment's thought. "By the time I start my journey, it'll already be the end of the year, and there won't be enough time to travel properly."

"I'll aim for next year instead and work hard. I think I can challenge the regional champion then."

As for which region, Selene hadn't decided yet.

"I see. But honestly, if you weren't participating, it would be much less stressful," Jeanette joked. She understood the vast gap between people.

Selene was clearly one of those gifted monsters, a born trainer. Competing against her would be pointless.

"The difficulty of this year's Indigo League will still be high. Miss Jeanette, just do your best," Dylan reminded Jeanette. Even without Selene, there were still Red and Blue, both incredible trainers.

"Is that so?" Jeanette asked, slightly stunned.

"I understand now. For a newcomer like me, getting into the top four would be a success."

Then, Jeanette smiled gently. She had a calm attitude about it.

"Thank you for your time today, Dylan Gym Leader. I'll take my leave now."

"It was no trouble at all. You're welcome to challenge us again anytime," Dylan replied with a smile.

"I will~ I really want your badge," Jeanette said, smiling sweetly. She then curtsied to both Dylan and Selene before turning and leaving gracefully.

"Jeanette really has the air of a noble lady," Selene said with sparkling eyes, quite envious.

"She's from Crimson City, so it's understandable," Dylan shrugged. The name of Crimson City gave it away.

"Good job today, Selene. Keep it up and aim to take down Champion Lance soon," Dylan said, patting Selene on the shoulder.

"So, Teacher Dylan, please stop setting goals for me!" Selene complained, her eyelids twitching.

"Selene, as an apprentice from Rota Gym, you should aim higher."

"Thank you so much, Teacher Dylan," Selene said sarcastically.

"Hahaha~ Alright, I'm heading to the backyard now," Dylan chuckled, walking toward the back of the gym. He still hadn't released his Pokémon yet.

"Meow~"

As soon as he entered the backyard, a pink light flew toward him.

"Meow~ Meow meow meow~"

The round, chubby Jigglypuff snuggled against Dylan, purring non-stop.

"Alright, alright~ Is the canned food all gone again?" Dylan said, smiling as he held the fat Jigglypuff.

"Meow~!"

"You really need to eat less, little one. It hasn't been that many days, and I feel like you've gained at least five pounds," Dylan laughed, poking Jigglypuff's belly.

"Meow~ I haven't!" Jigglypuff protested, but suddenly sniffed the air and seemed puzzled.

"That's the scent of the big white dragon... and the big ice dragon too!" Jigglypuff's body glowed with a faint pink light as it backed away, looking at Dylan in surprise.

"Their scents are so strong! Did you catch them, Dylan?"

"Yep, surprised? Unexpected?" Dylan grinned, holding Jigglypuff closer and releasing his other Pokémon, including the magical-faced Meow and others. He then sat down on a picnic blanket.

"This trip to Unova was a big success, and I experienced some interesting things."

"Meow~ Dylan, you didn't take me along! I wanted to see the fun too..."

"What are you looking at? All you do is eat and sleep, if I threw you like a weapon you wouldn't even react."

Dylan couldn't help but laugh.

"That was an accident, Meow, and... and it was in Dylan's bag, so I could sleep peacefully, Meow."

Jigglypuff sounded a bit weak, but as she continued speaking, she became more confident.

"Dylan, that's too much, Meow! How could you throw me into the Thunder of the Great Black Dragon?!"

"Yes, yes, I'm sorry," Dylan said, squeezing Jigglypuff's big face.

After playing with Jigglypuff for a while, Dylan lay on the picnic mat, feeling the breeze and thinking about the upcoming schedule.

There hasn't been any news from Zinna in the Hoenn region yet, no need to hurry. The northern journey needs to be expedited, add that to the plan.

Alola could also be considered, with the shiny Necrozma, the Ultra Space can be entered and exited at will.

Each of the Ultra Beasts can be captured a few times, and there's also the sealed Necrozma.

Dylan's eyes flickered slightly, imagining a future where he would have Necrozma: Dawn Wings on his left and Necrozma: Dusk Mane on his right. Both could transform into their Ultra Forms, perfect.

"Now, which team should I form next?"

Dylan thought about his next team. He actually had many Pokémon of different types.

For instance, his partner Pikachu, and partner Eevee, but he didn't have a specific team focused on a particular type.

Soon, Dylan had an idea and quickly sent a text message to his old friend.

Ding Ding~

Suddenly, Dylan's phone rang.

"Hello~"

As soon as the call was answered, a bright and energetic female voice came through.

"Dylan, long time no see, did you miss Leah?"

"Nope."

"Huh? How could that be? Leah is so cute, after losing me, shouldn't you be crying and feeling like the days are dragging on?"

Leah's 'shocked' voice was very loud.

"Just say what you need to say." Dylan lazily responded.

Since Leah called, it was obviously for something important.

"Well... Dylan, I found out that a high-ranking member of Team Rocket is plotting something big."

"What do you mean?"

Dylan raised an eyebrow. Although Leah usually wasn't serious, she was extremely reliable when it came to matters involving Team Rocket.

"Apparently, Team Rocket has started a new plan, and this time, they will have their very mysterious three high-ranking members lead the team to execute it. And Leah here, just happened to learn the target location of one of their plans."

Leah proudly said.

"As expected of you, Leah. When is the operation happening?"

"It should be in a few days. I'll dig around some more in the next few days, and then I'll need you to come over, Dylan."

Leah was very familiar with this process: gather intelligence, and leave the action to the big guy, nice.

"Okay, contact me in advance then."

Dylan nodded.

If Team Rocket's plan required the three high-ranking members to personally act, then it was undoubtedly the three legendary birds project.

Dylan's eyes flickered slightly, wondering which of the three members Leah would have found information about regarding the legendary bird's location.

Well, capturing both the people and the birds would be perfect.

With Dylan's return, the Rota Gym became popular again, even making it onto trending searches along with the news of Lance changing his username to "Doduo."

Unquestionably, the Rota Gym had been bustling recently.

Although there still wasn't any challenger that impressed Dylan, he had Selene handle some of the challenges, claiming it would help her accumulate battle experience.

Then...

'If you can't even beat an apprentice, how do you plan on challenging the gym leader?'

Many challengers left the Rota Gym in tears, after being completely beaten by an apprentice who was still underage, which was a blow to their pride.

By Saturday, Dylan and Lillie flew towards the southeastern part of the Kanto region with Reshiram.

At the Pokémon Center near the Rock Mountain Tunnel, Dylan met Leah again.

"Wow~ Dylan, your Pokémon is so beautiful!"

As Reshiram landed, a disguised Leah quickly approached, her eyes sparkling as she stared at Reshiram.

"It's called Reshiram. I just caught it recently."

"Mm-mm~ It's really too beautiful! Where did you catch it? I want to go too..."

Leah spoke quickly, but before she could finish, she seemed to notice something from the corner of her eye. Her pupils suddenly shrank, and she immediately hid behind Dylan.

"What's wrong?"

Dylan was taken aback and noticed that Leah's grip on his shoulder was tightening, clearly very nervous.

"Look over there," Leah whispered in his ear.

"Mm?"

Dylan followed the direction Leah pointed towards and saw three figures.

One was a tall, muscular man wearing a tank top and baggy pants, with a baseball cap and large sunglasses, obscuring his face.

Next to him was a tall, slender young woman with gray-blue long hair. She wore a tight yellow sexy crop top that revealed her slim waist, white casual three-quarter pants, and yellow high heels.

A lazy, charming aura radiated from her. She was an attractive woman.

Beside this gray-blue-haired woman was a young man with purple hair, wearing a mask and a suit-like outfit.

This was Karen and Will!

The Johto Elite Four from the Gold/Silver era, with Will being the first member, specializing in Psychic-type, and Karen, specializing in Dark-type, had even defeated Bruno, making her the head of the Johto Elite Four.

As for the big guy with the sunglasses, Dylan could tell from his physique that it had to be Lt. Surge!

No wonder Leah had such a big reaction—it turned out she had seen her former colleagues from the organization.

But how did Karen and Will get involved with Lt. Surge? Weren't they active in Johto?

Dylan was a bit puzzled, especially since the side story later showed that the Masked Organization took over the remnants of Team Rocket, but that was after Team Rocket's downfall in Johto.

Yet Team Rocket was still active in Johto, and Karen and Will had come to Kanto and seemed to be quite close to Lt. Surge.

This was getting interesting.

(End of Chapter)

# 176: Chapter 176: Pidgey

"Is there something wrong with those two people?" Dylan asked with a feigned look of confusion, turning to Leah.

"We should keep our distance from them," Leah said.

At this moment, Leah had calmed down a bit, but she still hid behind Dylan, pulling at his clothes as she cautiously moved backwards.

Lillie was equally puzzled, but she still followed the two of them.

Once they reached the riverside, Leah let out a sigh of relief.

"Leah, do you know those two people?" Dylan handed Leah a bottle of water. Leah thanked him, opened it, and took a sip, her gaze drifting blankly over the river.

It seemed like Leah had never looked like this before.

Lillie looked at Leah's melancholic and downcast expression, and a thought suddenly popped into her mind.

It seemed true—Leah always had an energetic and lively personality, exuding a sense of joy and freedom.

"If you trust me, you can tell me about it. Maybe I can help," Dylan said softly, giving her shoulder a comforting pat. His voice was gentle and reassuring.

Leah bit her lip, her soft lips parting as if to speak, but she quickly pressed them together again, as if hesitating.

"If you're worried about some strong enemy, you don't have to be afraid. I'm strong now—very strong."

Dylan spoke again in a soothing tone. He wasn't one to meddle in others' business, but whether it was because of sympathy for Leah's past, his connection to her in reality, or some other reason, he wouldn't refuse to help if Leah asked.

But it had to come from her. She had a lot of mental pressure, and she needed an outlet.

"They... are former colleagues from the same organization," Leah said after a long silence.

Yes, Karen and Will were once her colleagues.

"But I've already left that organization... and I betrayed them."

"So, are they here to capture you, Leah?" Lillie asked, surprised.

"I don't know." Leah shook her head. She truly didn't know, as Karen and Will had always been active around Johto and had rarely been in Kanto.

But now, they had come to Kanto, which confused Leah.

"Do you need me to take care of them for you?" Dylan asked after a moment's thought.

Will was a powerful psychic-type trainer—probably on the level of a king. Karen's strength should be much higher, and she seemed to be of champion-level strength as well. In fact, she had even beaten Bruno in a special episode.

But that didn't matter much; taking down a champion-level dark-type trainer and a psychic-type king wouldn't be too difficult for Dylan.

"No need. We still don't know their purpose."

Leah blinked and looked at Dylan, then suddenly smiled, shaking her head.

She placed a hand on her chest and felt a warm, comforting sensation.

Yes, she realized, this boy really made her feel safe.

"Let's figure out their purpose for coming to Kanto first. If I really need help, I won't hold back from asking you, Dylan."

Leah seemed to regain some of her energy, smiling brightly at Dylan.

After all, Karen and Will were loyal subordinates of the Masked Man. If they caught them now, it could very well lead to trouble with the Masked Man.

Leah wasn't ready for that yet because the Masked Man was far too terrifying.

"Alright," Dylan nodded.

In truth, Leah had hidden a lot, like how she was originally from Pallet Town and had been captured by the Masked Man's controlled Ho-oh before being brought to Johto.

But these were her secrets. Perhaps... she was afraid of getting too involved with the Masked Man's organization, as no one knew better than Leah how terrifying the Masked Man was. The legendary Ho-oh and Lugia of Johto were nothing more than toys to the Masked Man.

"Pryce..."

The Masked Man was none other than the leader of the Kanto Mahogany Gym, Pryce. In his youth, he had been friends with many famous trainers, including Professor Oak and others, but one event completely changed him.

For his own desires, Pryce had sealed his heart, distanced himself from everyone, and focused solely on waiting for the moment in his gym.

It could be said that Pryce embodied the powerful bond between humans and Pokémon. The stronger the belief, the greater the response from the Pokémon.

In the history of Pokémon, very few trainers have defeated Legendary Pokémon. While characters like Red from Pokémon Origins and May from Pokémon Evolutions were among the few to accomplish this, the only other trainer to actually beat a Legendary Pokémon outside of the main characters was Pryce from a special episode.

Though in his youth Pryce might not have been as powerful as others like Professor Oak, after losing his Dragonite, his unwavering belief and willpower resonated with his Pokémon. After years of training, his mastery over Ice-types reached a terrifying level that could be described as "unprecedented"!

This was no exaggeration, as Pryce's achievements were indeed monstrous.

But there was no need to deal with Pryce for now. Besides his own desires, Pryce had no other ambitions, and Leah's emotional struggles would probably have to be resolved by herself.

Abandoned Power Plant

This was an old, abandoned power plant in Kanto, with some machinery still running on autopilot.

"So, their target is here?" Dylan and the others arrived at the abandoned power plant.

"Should be. I've heard rumors—it's part of the 'Divine Bird' plan."

Leah nodded in confirmation, though she hadn't been able to find out their exact objective here.

"Divine Bird plan? Sounds like one of the Legendary Birds. Let's go in and see," Dylan led the way into the plant. Since Team Rocket had begun their plan, it was clear that the bird they were after was definitely inside this plant.

The power plant was large but had been long abandoned. While some equipment was still operational, it had turned into a haven for Electric-type Pokémon.

However, Dylan and the others were not interested in them.

Golden light flashed repeatedly in the depths of the power plant, and it was already visible.

"What is that?" Lillie asked curiously, looking into the distance. Since the view was blocked by a wall, she couldn't see clearly, but the flashing golden light was still noticeable.

"You'll know if you go see it," Dylan said.

Dylan quickened his pace, and the three of them continued deeper into the plant, finally arriving at the very depths. After turning a corner, they saw everything clearly.

A huge generator was still operating, but that wasn't the main focus. The main focus was a golden bird with feather-like spikes, resembling needles, in front of the generator, absorbing the electricity from it.

The bird had an orange, sharp, long beak, with black ring-shaped feathers around its eyes. Its feathers were very sharp, like lightning.

"The legendary Zapdos?" Leah looked at the yellow bird-like Pokémon in surprise, her eyes suddenly widening, and her body began to tremble slightly.

She had a severe fear of birds, and now seeing Zapdos triggered her fear.

"Don't be scared. It's just a Zapdos. The fear in your heart is meant to be overcome. Lillie, help Leah," Dylan gently patted Leah's back to comfort her. He could feel how tense Leah's body was.

But this was something she had to face. Her fear of birds was too severe, and she needed to overcome it.

"Leah, are you okay?" Lillie also noticed Leah's strange behavior and gently comforted her while holding her.

"I'm fine... I'm fine... I..."

Leah gritted her teeth, looking down and refusing to look at Zapdos. She knew she had to overcome her fear of birds, but it was still incredibly hard for her.

"Chirp~!"

At that moment, Zapdos noticed the three humans. Its sharp cry rang out as it flapped its wings and flew up, its piercing gaze looking down on them.

Golden electric sparks flickered all around Zapdos, its presence imposing, a strong pressure filling the air.

"Looking at it this way, it's not so bad," Dylan raised an eyebrow and assessed Zapdos carefully, commenting.

Although Zapdos' feathers were chaotic and sharp, from the front, its lightning-shaped feathers actually looked quite good.

"Ding~ Zapdos, Electric/Flying type, Electric Pokémon

Ability: Pressure

Signature Moves: Thunder, Rain Dance, Discharge, Agility, Peck, Charge, Thunderbolt, Quick Attack, Light Screen, Thunder Wave…

Pokédex: When the sky is dark, and lightning strikes constantly, this legendary Pokémon will appear."

"Chirp~!!"

Zapdos' expression turned increasingly cold with anger. These humans had already been warned, yet they still refused to leave?

What did that mean? A provocation? How dare they provoke me, this bird? They must be tired of living!

The electricity around Zapdos sparked more frequently, its golden electric light almost blinding Dylan's eyes.

"Angry? There's no need for that. I just wanted to ask if you'd like to become my Pokémon. What do you think?" Dylan took out a valuable Pokéball.

"Chirp!!"

In response, Zapdos released a powerful bolt of lightning, an Electric-type Thunder attack!

A beautiful blue figure floated in front of Dylan, firing an Ice Beam in retaliation—it was Articuno.

"??"

Zapdos stared at Articuno in astonishment. As another legendary bird Pokémon, it naturally recognized Articuno, though it didn't know this particular Articuno.

But soon, Zapdos began to laugh. How weak was this Articuno? It had actually been captured by a human? That was laughable.

However...

The Ice Beam collided directly with the Thunder, and after only a brief moment, the Thunder was shattered by the Ice Beam.

In Zapdos' shocked and incredulous shriek, the Ice Beam hit it directly.

Crack! Crack!

Ice crystals rapidly formed, and Zapdos cried out in pain as it fell to the ground, weakened and unable to fly due to the Ice-type's effect on Flying-types.

The cold air quickly invaded its body, causing Zapdos immense suffering.

"Looks like this Zapdos isn't very strong," Dylan shook his head as he observed.

"Yiao~ (Such a weak, scruffy bird~)"

Articuno gently fluttered its magnificent wings, casting a side glance at Zapdos and calling out lazily.

"???"

Zapdos immediately widened its eyes, glaring fiercely at Articuno.

"Chirp Chirp Chirp! (What are you chirping about, you ugly Pidgey-headed fool!)"

"Yiao~ (You're so weak.)"

"(You're a Pidgey-head that's been captured by a human! Who are you to insult me?!)"

"(You scruffy bird! You're weaker than a Pidgey!)"

Zapdos instantly broke down, turning red with anger.

"Brother, what are they saying?" Lillie asked curiously, looking at the two birds, one high and one low, seemingly exchanging words, and turned to Dylan with a puzzled look.

She knew her brother had the power of Viridian and could understand Pokémon speech.

"Emmm..."

Dylan looked somewhat strange and puzzled, then paused for a moment.

"Zapdos is greeting Articuno's mother, and Articuno is greeting Zapdos itself. Something like that?"

(End of chapter)
