# 177: Chapter 177: Pig?

Greeting each other? Lillie tilted her head, a big question mark appearing on her forehead. Is this really just a greeting?

She felt like it wasn't at all like that. The feathers on Zapdos were standing up like a porcupine, and it was chirping non-stop, 'chirp, chirp, chirp'.

It felt... more like it was swearing, right?

Even Leah, who was extremely uncomfortable due to ornithophobia, couldn't help but roll her eyes. She squinted at the two birds—Articuno and Zapdos—yelling at each other. It didn't seem so scary after all.

Even though they were legendary birds, were they really like this?

"Alright, Articuno, stop arguing with it."

Dylan spoke up, interrupting the 'sweet talk' between the two birds, then turned to Zapdos with a gentle smile.

"Zapdos, are you willing to become my Pokémon now?"

"Chirp!"

Zapdos proudly raised its head, showing that it would never bow down to a human, especially not one with a bird like Pidgey.

"Good, very energetic." Dylan nodded in satisfaction.

"Articuno, use Ice Beam and send this distant relative on its way."

Dylan called to Articuno. Since Zapdos wasn't being cooperative, he decided to knock it out first and then capture it.

Articuno let out a cry, and a ball of ice-blue energy formed in front of its beak.

"Chirp? Chirp, chirp, chirp!"

Zapdos was startled and quickly spoke up.

"Aren't you supposed to be unyielding?" Dylan asked with a playful smile.

"Chirp…"

Zapdos hesitated. It didn't want to be captured by a human, but it also didn't want to be knocked out by Articuno.

That would be so embarrassing, especially after just exchanging words with Articuno. No, it couldn't lose face like that!

"Well, let's make a deal. If you become my Pokémon, I can make you as strong as Articuno here."

"Chirp??"

Zapdos's eyes immediately lit up with excitement.

Articuno humanized a roll of its eyes. This foolish bird was so easy to deceive, its brain seemed to have shrunk.

"Of course. When I first captured Articuno, it wasn't as strong as you, but look at it now."

Dylan's smile was warm, and his tone sincere.

"What's the point of absorbing electricity here? You could absorb it for a year, and you still wouldn't surpass my Articuno. Do you understand what I mean?"

"Chirp!!"

Zapdos immediately made its decision. It wanted to become stronger. It wanted to defeat this Pidgey and make itself the boss of birds!

What this human said was true. Although it could absorb the electricity here to recharge, doing so wouldn't help it reach its goal.

And… if it didn't agree, it would still be beaten by this Pidgey. It wasn't worth it.

"Those who know when to yield are wise birds. Zapdos, you have a bright future."

Dylan nodded in satisfaction. This Zapdos was smart and realistic. He liked it.

"Ultra Ball, go!"

Dylan threw the crimson-colored Ultra Ball at Zapdos. Zapdos raised its head arrogantly as if posing.

The Ultra Ball made contact with Zapdos, and it was sucked inside as a red light.

Ding, ding, ding~

The Ultra Ball shook three times before it stopped.

"Zapdos, captured."

Dylan picked up the Ultra Ball with a satisfied expression. It was much easier than he had expected. If Zapdos had resisted, he would have wasted dozens of Great Balls, just like the time with Regirock.

But since Zapdos was willing to be captured, it made things much easier.

"Articuno, good job."

And the main contributor, of course, was Articuno.

"Yiao~" Articuno chirped happily, then looked at Dylan, as if negotiating.

"Alright, you can supervise Zapdos's special training, but don't go too far. We're all teammates now."

Dylan nodded, reminding it. Though he knew Articuno would give Zapdos a hard time, a little competition in the team was fine. It could motivate Zapdos to improve.

"Yiao~" Articuno promised it wouldn't go too far.

Leah: (.)

Lillie: (—)

After watching the entire scene, Leah suddenly lowered her head and thought deeply. It seemed like her ornithophobia wasn't really necessary. Why was she so afraid of birds?

This legendary bird was like this?

"It's really not bad, looks pretty strong."

After recalling Articuno and releasing Zapdos again, Dylan sprayed Zapdos with a high-level healing spray.

Zapdos puffed out its chest, proudly calling out. Of course! Bird Boss is strong!

"By the way, Dylan, didn't your 'Power of Viridian' have healing abilities? Why do you still prefer using healing sprays?"

Leah leaned against Lillie. Though her fear of birds wasn't as strong now, she was still a bit weak and needed to recover.

"The Power of Viridian consumes my mental energy. If I use it too much, I'll get dizzy, while healing sprays only consume useless numbers."

Dylan didn't even turn around. The Power of Viridian was now more of a special ability to communicate and bond with Pokémon.

As for healing abilities… well, he had almost forgotten about that function.

"Really makes sense," Leah muttered, unable to argue. The young master was rich.

"Team Rocket must have come to the power plant to capture Zapdos. We got to it first. What's our next move?"

After a while, Leah was able to stand properly, but she still didn't dare get too close to Zapdos.

"I have a good idea." Dylan's lips curled into a smile as he looked at Zapdos. It looked like they had more fun ahead.

Too bad he didn't capture the Galar Zapdos. Otherwise, it would have been a small shock to those guys from Team Rocket.

"?"

Zapdos tilted its head, looking at its trainer.

...

At the entrance of the abandoned power plant.

At this moment, there was a small elite team of ten members from Team Rocket, and at the front was Lt. Surge, who was slightly disguised.

"This power plant is so run-down. Is Zapdos really here?"

Karen, with her long fingers covering her brows, gazed ahead with a lazy, magnetic voice.

Will silently observed the surroundings, not saying a word.

"According to the intel, it's accurate. We'll see how you all perform once we go inside."

Lt. Surge shrugged, then looked at Karen and Will with interest.

"Since the boss recommended you two, I guess you should be quite capable?"

Lt. Surge wasn't familiar with Karen and Will, two officers transferred from the Celadon City branch, but according to Sakaki, their skills were impressive.

Originally, Zapdos was his task to capture, but Giovanni had sent Karen and Will to assist him, wanting him to evaluate their abilities.

"Ah~ coming to Kanto for the first time, capturing a legendary Pokémon like Zapdos… I wonder if they'll be able to do it."

Karen gently cupped her white cheek with her right hand, a malicious curve on her lips, her voice lazy and casual.

Though she said 'I wonder if they can do it', her demeanor showed no sign of pressure at all.

Lt. Surge glanced at Karen. This woman definitely fell into the "absolutely unpleasant" category, similar to how Proton used to be.

Speaking of Proton...

Lt. Surge's gaze shifted slightly. Proton's personality used to be quite bad, but for some reason, when they met at a base recently, Lt. Surge felt Proton had undergone a subtle change.

Lt. Surge couldn't quite put his finger on it, but Proton seemed much more relaxed about everything. Even his personal hobby of "teasing" subordinates—a term that basically meant "showing off"—appeared to have faded.

And then there was Archer. Archer had also changed subtly. While still reliable and trustworthy in terms of abilities, he seemed to space out often when alone. Nobody knew what he was thinking.

It was truly strange.

"The boss values both of you very much. I heard that in Johto, Charr also thinks highly of you."

Lt. Surge led the group into the abandoned power plant, curiously asking Karen and Will.

"Charr, that short guy... Senior Charr is quite the character," Karen replied with a malicious smile, as if recalling something.

Did you just call him short?

Lt. Surge was slightly surprised and glanced at Karen. Charr, a senior officer among Team Rocket's elite, was not well-liked by many other senior officers.

After all, that short guy was completely unhinged, cruel, and arrogant, always carrying several "bombs" around with him.

Lt. Surge really disliked Charr, but Karen and Will used to work under him.

"Hahaha~ Don't worry. I can't stand that damn shorty Charr either, but you two aren't senior officers yet, so it's best not to offend him openly. That guy holds grudges."

Lt. Surge laughed heartily. He was generally easygoing, and though a senior officer of Team Rocket, he wouldn't have joined if it weren't for Giovanni.

"Ah~ Thank you, senior," Karen said politely with a smile.

The Rocket Team ventured deeper into the power plant, the faint golden light ahead becoming clearer.

"It's just ahead. After all, it's the legendary Zapdos. Be careful. If you need support, let me know," Lt. Surge warned Karen and Will.

Karen raised an eyebrow, her impression of Lt. Surge improving slightly. She hadn't expected him to be so decent.

Will remained silent, continuing to blend into the background.

"Leave it to us, senior. It's just a Zapdos. We can handle it," Karen said with a gentle smile, though her smile always carried an unsettling malice.

A mere wild Zapdos? How hard could it be?

Karen scoffed inwardly. She considered herself to be at champion-level strength, certainly not weaker than other champions.

A mere Zapdos? It probably wouldn't even require her to step in—Will could likely handle it on his own.

As they turned the corner, they entered a spacious generator room, where the source of the golden light finally came into view.

But...

A golden bird stood there, napping. Its golden feathers resembled sharp needles. It definitely looked like Zapdos, but the key issue was...

How could it be so fat?

Its body was round like a ball, especially its belly—a huge, rounded lump. Compared to its chubby, spherical body, its head and neck looked almost miniature.

Was this a bird? If not for the wings, one might have mistaken it for a pig!

Lt. Surge and the others were stunned!

"Is this... Zapdos or a Zapdo pig?"

For a long time, one of the Rocket Team members blankly asked, "Can its wings even make it fly?"

"Meow??"

It seemed like the Zapdos took offense to that comment.

Who's calling me a pig? Who said that??!! Are you showing any manners? I'm a cute and mysterious cat, not a pig!

The atmosphere grew more awkward.

"Meow??"

Is that how Zapdos sounds?

Lt. Surge silently pulled out his Pokédex to scan this "Zapdo pig," suspecting one of his foolish subordinates had made a mistake. Could this really be Zapdos?

He needed to double-check to avoid any errors.

Ding~ Zapdos, Electric/Flying type, Electric Pokémon.

Abilities: Synchronize

Signature Move: ...

Description: "When the sky turns dark..."

Lt. Surge fell silent. This did appear to be Zapdos, and the Pokédex wasn't wrong.

But the image of Zapdos in the Pokédex looked nothing like the creature before him.

"Senior, do you really think capturing this Zapdos will be useful for the organization? Or is it just for research purposes?"

Karen, looking at the plump Zapdos that probably couldn't even walk properly, politely asked Lt. Surge.

"Forget it. It must have been eating too well outside. Anyway, it's the boss's mission, so let's capture it."

Lt. Surge sighed. As an Electric-type specialist, when the "Legendary Birds Project" started, he had volunteered to catch Zapdos.

He had been looking forward to seeing the legendary Zapdos, but after seeing it up close...

Well, it would've been better if he hadn't.

(End of Chapter)

---

"Oh?"

Dylan watched Lt. Surge with great interest.

"Now, I'm rather curious to see the gap between the Rocket Executives."

So there were differences in strength even among Rocket Executives? How amusing.

"Hahaha~ You'll find out soon enough!"

Lt. Surge laughed heartily as he opened a Poké Ball. In a flash of light, a humanoid, yellow-and-black Pokémon appeared before him.

An Electric-type: Electivire!

Alongside it were Will's Jynx and Karen's Umbreon. The three Pokémon stood ready to fight, their piercing gazes fixed.

"An Elite-level Electivire, an Elite-level Jynx, and a Champion-level Umbreon. Interesting."

Dylan's sharp gaze swept over the three Pokémon.

"Hm?" Karen looked surprised. It was true her Umbreon was Champion-level, but just from its aura alone, most wouldn't be able to discern that.

"Dylan, my boy, if you turn a blind eye, we can avoid any unnecessary trouble," Lt. Surge said, raising an eyebrow as he subtly glanced at Karen.

If he could avoid a clash with Dylan, it would be ideal. That 'Zapdos' had to be fake. Though Lt. Surge looked like a musclehead, he wasn't stupid.

There were Pokémon capable of mimicking others, and one that could transform into 'Zapdos' while also using Frenzy Plant? It could only be that mythical Pokémon.

"So, what do you think?"

Though the mission was already a failure, Lt. Surge prioritized reporting to their boss. Despite his itching desire to fight Dylan, he restrained himself.

"You're quite the joker. I'm not interested." Dylan shrugged casually.

Lt. Surge's brow furrowed. If that's the case, there was no choice but to fight. As long as they could take Dylan down quickly, it would be fine. Hopefully, Officer Jenny hadn't been alerted yet. If she showed up, things would get complicated.

"No matter how impressive your lineup looks, before my Kyurem, you're all just ants."

With a smirk, Dylan opened his Luxury Ball.

"ROARRR!!!"

A deafening roar shook the ground as a massive gray dragon landed heavily before Dylan. Its golden, fearsome eyes gazed disdainfully at the humans before it.

The strongest dragon on land—Kyurem!

An overwhelming dragon aura mixed with freezing pressure descended upon the area, sending chills straight to everyone's core. Even breathing became a struggle.

What incredible pressure!

Lt. Surge and Will widened their eyes in shock, staring at the enormous gray dragon.

Even Karen frowned. This… this was over the top!

"Wow! Dylan, your Pokémon looks insanely strong!"

Leah exclaimed, patting Dylan on the shoulder with amazement. The sheer pressure was overwhelming, even from the sidelines.

"Hehe~ This is Kyurem, the legendary Pokémon from Unova. My brother recently captured it there. It's super strong!"

Lillie whispered proudly to Leah, her voice filled with excitement.

"That's amazing~" Leah's wide, sparkling eyes reflected her astonishment. In just over a month, Dylan had grown even stronger. His progress was incredible.

"Don't panic; it could just be a paper tiger," Lt. Surge said, trying to calm Will and Karen.

"Well, let's test that theory."

Will nodded, though he was frowning. The pressure was overwhelming, but they had to confirm its true strength.

"Electivire, Thunder!"

"Jynx, Psychic!"

And then…

"ROARRR!!"

Kyurem's furious roar was accompanied by a flash of icy light.

Before their eyes, Electivire and Jynx were frozen solid, turned into lifelike ice sculptures.

Lt. Surge and Will fell silent, their expressions immediately clearing.

No need to test further. This dragon was indeed a monster.

Dylan's grin widened as he looked at the now-silent Lt. Surge and his group.

"Why so quiet, everyone?"

He walked to Kyurem's side, feigning surprise as he glanced at the trio.

Leah and Lillie couldn't hold back their laughter.

Lt. Surge's face darkened, Will stared at the ground, and Karen's lips twitched slightly.

"Well, no matter how many of you there are, in front of Kyurem, you're just frost on the ground."

Dylan sighed, relishing the moment. Kyurem's overwhelming power truly made him feel unbeatable.

Lt. Surge's expression stiffened further.

"But you should feel honored. This is Kyurem's first appearance in the Kanto region."

"Still not talking? What a letdown. Alright then."

Finding their silence boring, Dylan gestured to Kyurem, signaling it to prepare to attack as he stepped back.

"Don't worry, everyone. Kyurem won't freeze you to death immediately. It's experienced with this sort of thing."

Kyurem's terrifying golden eyes locked onto the trio, and icy-blue energy began to gather around it, forming dazzling, pure rings of ice.

"Wait! Leader Dylan, I think we can talk!"

Lt. Surge's expression changed as he quickly called out.

"Talk? At this point, aren't you joking, Mr. Musclehead?"

Dylan gestured for Kyurem to pause and looked at Lt. Surge with an amused smile.

"This is something both Archer and Proton have said countless times. It doesn't work, you know. Pleading won't help either."

"As a Gym Leader of the League, I won't let you Rocket members off. Otherwise, Lance-senpai would be disappointed."

Though Dylan's voice was gentle, his words were anything but.

Lt. Surge's face darkened further as he frantically sent a string of emergency messages to his boss:

'Boss, save me!'

'Boss, help!!!'

'Boss, come save me ASAP!'

Lt. Surge wasn't taking any chances and resent the messages again.

Lt. Surge wasn't taking any chances and resent the messages again.

"Leader Dylan, I believe there's been a misunderstanding."

Karen, ever the Champion-level trainer, swiftly distanced herself from Lt. Surge and Will.

"Oh?" Dylan looked intrigued.

"I'm not with Team Rocket. You've misunderstood."

Karen brushed her grayish-blue hair aside with a faint smile, effortlessly disassociating herself.

"???"

Lt. Surge and Will were dumbfounded.

Especially Will. 'We're on the same team! How could you do this? If you're cutting ties, take me with you!'

Though Will's expression darkened, he held back as Karen discreetly signaled him.

"What a comedian you are. If you're not with Team Rocket, why are you here with these Rocket thugs?" Dylan spread his hands, unconvinced.

"Bold declarations, silence mid-fight, and now ridiculous excuses."

Leaf's disguised voice chimed in mockingly.

"Dylan, stop listening to her nonsense. Freeze this entire group of Kanto scum!"

Leah eagerly encouraged Dylan, clearly relishing the idea of watching Karen and Will turned into ice sculptures.

"Leader Dylan, you misunderstand. I was just traveling from Johto and happened to be misled by these evil thugs. They told me there was a Zapdos here, so I followed along. Honestly, if it weren't for you, I might've been in real danger."

Karen's tone was sincere, but her story was outright absurd.

Will stood silently, rolling his eyes internally. 'You're a Champion-level trainer, and you expect anyone to believe that?'

Lt. Surge, meanwhile, quietly continued spamming his boss for help.

"Maybe you're telling the truth, but that's too bad. My job is to capture you. You can explain everything to Officer Jenny later."

Dylan waved her off, signaling her to rejoin Lt. Surge and Will.

"For now, please stay put. Kyurem's temper isn't great, and if it sneezes, you might spend the rest of your life as a vegetable."

Dylan spoke gently, though his words sent chills down their spines.

'Is this guy really a Gym Leader? Why does he sound like a villain?'

Karen's eyelid twitched, and she obediently returned to stand beside Lt. Surge and Will.

"This Pokémon is terrifying. Do you have any way to get out of here?" Will whispered to Lt. Surge and Karen.

He suddenly wanted to go back to Johto. He had only just arrived in Kanto today, and while he liked excitement, he wasn't looking to end up in jail!

"What about your Teleport?" Karen asked, her eyebrows knitted tightly in frustration.

"That dragon is staring right at us. How am I supposed to use Teleport? And its oppressive aura is overwhelming—there's no guarantee my Xatu could successfully execute Teleport," Will replied in a low voice.

Teleport could instantly take people away from the area, and as an Elite Psychic Trainer, Will's ace Xatu could easily transport three people under normal circumstances. But was this a normal situation?

With that dragon exuding such a terrifying presence, it was uncertain whether Xatu could even perform Teleport, let alone take multiple people with it.

"I've already called for help from the boss, but I'm not sure how long it will take for them to arrive," Lt. Surge said.

"Karen, are you sure you don't want to give it a try? If your whole team went at it together…"

Will ignored Dylan's comment and addressed Karen directly. After all, she was Champion-level.

"Are you out of your mind? You want me to fight that dragon?" Karen shot Will a cold glance, seriously doubting that he might be planning to run off while she risked her life against Kyurem.

"Then what do we do? Are we really going to jail?" Will said reluctantly.

He had come to Kanto for fun and to complete some tasks, not to end up in a cell.

Karen frowned deeply, while Lt. Surge sighed in despair. He was the one who least wanted to be caught, wasn't he?

As the Gym Leader of Vermilion City, his identity was now at risk of being exposed. What would he do after this? But right now, they had no solutions. Where was the boss?

Time quickly passed, and Officer Jenny from Lavender Town arrived with reinforcements.

"Dylan, you're amazing! You've done it again!" Officer Jenny exclaimed as she excitedly clasped Dylan's hand. He was truly an idol!

Not only had he captured a high-ranking Rocket Executive, but three of them at once!

"This is just what I should do as a Gym Leader," Dylan said modestly.

At his words, Officer Jenny's expression froze slightly, her eyes dimming for a moment. As she was escorting the Rocket members earlier, she had seen Lt. Surge!!

Lt. Surge, the Gym Leader of Vermilion City, was actually a high-ranking Rocket Executive. This revelation… was truly shocking.

"Dylan, you're the real hero of justice! By the way, I'm a fan of yours. Could you give me an autograph?"

This young Officer Jenny's eyes sparkled as she eagerly held out a notebook and pen to Dylan.

"Is that okay? I'm just an ordinary Gym Leader," Dylan said, somewhat surprised.

"No, no, no! You're the 'Rocket Slayer!' My family has so many fans of yours!" Officer Jenny quickly shook her head.

"Please thank your family for their support," Dylan said with a smile as he signed the notebook.

"I will! I still need to oversee the prisoners. Goodbye for now, Dylan," she said before running off to the police car, where the Rockets were being detained.

Inside the police car, Will stared blankly at the ceiling, the silver handcuffs on his wrists glinting.

On his first day in Kanto, he had received an invaluable silver bracelet, but all Will wanted to do was cry.

Karen sighed deeply. What kind of mess was this?

"Feeling better now?"

Dylan smiled as he watched the police cars drive off, then turned to Leah.

"Totally! Thank you so much, Dylan!" Leah exclaimed, immediately leaping at Dylan and wrapping her arms around him in a big bear hug. Her legs clung around his waist, and her big eyes curved into crescent moons as she spoke in a sweet, syrupy tone.

Oh my gosh! Seeing Karen and Will's expressions as they were cuffed had nearly made her laugh her head off!

"Leah, that's not appropriate! You shouldn't act like that!" Lillie exclaimed, her eyes wide as she tried to stop Leah's bold display. How could she do such a thing?!

Was Leah deliberately taking advantage of Dylan?

"Get down already. You're heavy," Dylan said reflexively as he supported the girl's waist and spoke to Leah.

"Huh? Heavy? I just weighed myself this morning, and I'm definitely not heavy! It must be Ditto—it's too heavy," Leah protested incredulously.

Meanwhile, on a distant hilltop.

A middle-aged man in a black suit and gentleman's hat stared deeply at Dylan and the others. Beside him stood a silver Pokémon clad in mechanical armor.

This Pokémon's eyes glowed faintly blue as it stared intently at the "Zapdos."

"Mewtwo, your aura is fluctuating again," the man, Giovanni, said calmly.

"That…"

Mewtwo's deep telepathic voice echoed.

"Yes, that's your original, Mew. But it doesn't matter. You will surpass it, Mewtwo," Giovanni said, his voice magnetic and confident.

"You've only just awakened. This armor will help you fully adapt to your power. You are growing rapidly, Mewtwo. There's no need to rush," Giovanni reassured, smiling.

"…"

Mewtwo remained silent, its glowing blue eyes fixed on the "Zapdos," a mixture of doubt and bewilderment visible.

Was this really its original form? This… "pig"?

Forgive Mewtwo for using the term "pig," but it couldn't find a more fitting description.

And suddenly, Mewtwo began to wonder if there had been some misunderstanding. Perhaps it wasn't actually created from Mew's genetic material at all.

(End of Chapter)

# 178: Chapter 178: It’s Dylan! This is Bad!

"Leave it to you all," Lt. Surge said, his deep frown creasing further. Just looking at this "Zapdo pig" gave him a splitting headache.

You're supposed to be an Electric-type legendary! A legend! How could you be this fat?

Lt. Surge was exhausted and disheartened. Forget it. Out of sight, out of mind.

"Ah~ Since the senior says so, we'll take care of it," Karen replied casually, stretching lazily. Her slender, fair waist was quite eye-catching.

"Will," she called.

Will responded by throwing out a Jynx. Zapdos, being Electric and Flying-type, was weak to Ice-types like Jynx.

"Jynx, Blizzard," Will ordered calmly.

Jynx opened its wide, "charming" lips, and a powerful chill filled the air. A raging blizzard swept toward the "Zapdo pig."

But what happened next was unexpected.

It was as if an invisible barrier appeared in front of Zapdos. The blizzard surged with all its might but couldn't advance an inch further.

This strange scene left Karen, Lt. Surge, and the others dumbfounded.

"Will?" Karen's delicate brows furrowed slightly.

"There's something odd. It's like something's in front of it," Will said, puzzled beneath his mask. Was it something Zapdos had done? But that didn't seem right.

From a glance, nothing seemed to be there. Zapdos hadn't moved at all, yet Jynx's Blizzard couldn't break through.

Meanwhile, the "Zapdo pig" remained perched lazily, looking half-asleep, as if it didn't care about the people before it.

Karen's eyes narrowed.

"I knew there was something off about this 'Zapdos,'" she said with a cold laugh, throwing another Poké Ball. A sleek Umbreon appeared before her.

Shaped like a canine, with long ears and gold ring patterns on its black body, its blood-red eyes gleamed.

"Umbreon, Pursuit!"

Umbreon's eyes flashed with dark light as its black figure darted toward Zapdos.

But as it neared, the same invisible barrier emerged. Radiating dark energy, Umbreon gritted its teeth and pushed forward.

As it did, a faint prismatic glow began to shimmer.

Rip!

The sound of something tearing filled the air as Umbreon shredded the unseen "veil."

"Meow?"

Zapdos opened its groggy eyes and found Umbreon right in front of it.

Zapdos was momentarily stunned. Hadn't it set up a psychic "wall"? Though it hadn't used much psychic power, it still shouldn't have been something a regular Pokémon could breach.

"Pursuit!"

Umbreon lunged at the massive Zapdos.

"Meow!"

Zapdos was displeased. It hadn't even bothered these people, yet they came to trouble it?

A tangible shockwave suddenly rippled out. Umbreon, the first to bear the brunt, let out a grunt as it was knocked back—a pure energy shockwave had sent it flying.

Boom!

The ground quaked violently.

Cracks began to form across the power plant floor, and wild Pokémon like Voltorb, who inhabited the plant, started fleeing in panic.

"What on earth is this?" Lt. Surge was stunned.

Giant plant roots erupted from the cracks, writhing like massive dragons, creating an awe-inspiring sight.

Some of the thicker roots even pierced through the power plant ceiling. A true forest terrain takeover!

"Frenzy Plant? How can a Zapdos even use that? Is this some kind of joke?!"

Lt. Surge was utterly shocked, swearing aloud. Even if Zapdos had unleashed repeated Thunders, he wouldn't have been this dumbfounded.

But a Zapdos using Frenzy Plant? That was utterly nonsensical!

Checking the Pokédex again, he confirmed this Zapdos didn't even have Frenzy Plant in its move pool. How was this possible?

Karen and Will also frowned deeply.

As the roots continued to spread, Lt. Surge and the others realized the increasing danger. The entire power plant was shaking.

"Retreat for now!"

Lt. Surge made the call, leading everyone out of the power plant. The area was too perilous to stay in.

Fortunately, the "Zapdo pig" didn't seem intent on attacking them, allowing them to escape safely.

But the problem remained—they had come to capture Zapdos today!

"This Zapdos is definitely not normal," Karen said, her brows tightly knit. The eerie "air wall" and the absurd Frenzy Plant... it all felt wrong.

"Meow~!"

A melodious cry sounded again as the "Zapdo pig," now glowing faintly with pink light, floated out of the power plant, surprising Lt. Surge and the others.

Wait, it could actually fly?

The Team Rocket members were stunned, but they quickly realized the Zapdos wasn't flapping its wings. Instead, it seemed to be using psychic powers to float.

"Is this really Zapdos?"

Lt. Surge, Karen, and Will couldn't shake the growing doubt in their minds. This was far too strange.

"Hmm? Who upset our little pig-cat?"

A teasing male voice suddenly interrupted.

The group immediately turned to see a man and two women approaching.

"Hmm? It's Dylan? This isn't good."

Lt. Surge pulled his hat down further, sensing trouble. He recognized Dylan.

After all, Dylan's all-black outfit was highly distinctive. Though it was their first meeting, Lt. Surge had seen plenty of photos of him.

"Meow!"

The "Zapdo pig" floated straight to Dylan, clearly intending to dive into his arms affectionately. But Dylan dodged swiftly.

"Not happening," Dylan said.

With Clefairy and Jigglypuff, he could manage. But if this "Zapdo pig" tried, it would definitely flatten him.

"Meow!"

The Zapdos gazed at Dylan mournfully, as if to say: Who do you think I got this ugly for, you heartless jerk?!

"I'll reward you properly later," Dylan said, patting the Zapdos's head. The sharp feathers pricked his hand slightly.

The Zapdos immediately perked up, obediently floating behind Dylan. It hated walking—too tiring.

Lillie, curious, studied the Team Rocket members without fear. She was a girl who'd seen her share of high-stakes situation.

Leah, however, half-hid behind Dylan, worried Karen or Will might recognize her.

"Well, well," Dylan said, smiling warmly. "Didn't expect to run into Team Rocket while out for a casual stroll. And judging by appearances..."

He glanced at Lt. Surge, Karen, and Will.

"The three of you don't seem like ordinary members of Team Rocket."

Lt. Surge: "..."

"Who's this handsome guy? He seems pretty strong," Karen asked Surge enthusiastically. She and Will had just arrived in the Kanto region today and weren't familiar with the area.

"He's the Gym Leader of the Rota Gym. What day is it today?"

"It's Saturday."

"Tch~ Just my luck."

Lt. Surge clicked his tongue in annoyance. How unlucky. The Rota Gym was one of those gyms that closed on weekends, with fixed off-days on Saturdays and Sundays.

Dylan would often leave the gym and wander around. Lt. Surge hadn't expected to bump into him today.

"Senior, is this guy really that strong?"

Karen raised an eyebrow, seeing Lt. Surge's reaction. Could this guy be that formidable? He was just a Gym Leader, after all.

A faintly malicious smile curled at the corners of Karen's lips as her narrowed eyes scanned Dylan and the two girls beside him.

But when her gaze landed on the brunette girl behind Dylan, Karen tilted her head slightly, sensing something vaguely familiar. After thinking for a moment, though, she was certain she'd never seen this round-faced girl before.

"Dylan is one of the most well-known Gym Leaders in the Kanto region right now. Archer and Proton from the organization were both captured by him twice, and Ariana was sent to prison by him as well. He's incredibly strong."

Lt. Surge grinned, suddenly recalling that he had never met Dylan in person. Dylan probably wouldn't recognize him.

As long as his sunglasses and hat stayed on, his identity wouldn't be exposed, and there was no need to worry too much.

"Oh?" Karen looked a bit surprised.

Although she didn't think much of Rocket Admins, they were still Elite Four-level trainers. If this Gym Leader had bested them, he was indeed capable.

But that was about it.

"Are you done talking?" Dylan asked politely.

"If you're finished, would you mind coming with me to the police station?"

Dylan's gentle and refined tone was pleasant to the ears—not forceful but rather like a friendly request.

However, the content of his words was anything but friendly.

"Just last week, I visited Ms. Ariana. She seemed rather lonely, reading by herself in her room."

"If the three of you would be willing to keep her company, I'm sure she'd be delighted," Dylan said with a light sigh.

"???" Lt. Surge and Will both frowned darkly, their faces full of question marks.

"Hahaha~ Kid, you've got a real sense of humor," Lt. Surge said, laughing. Sending them to "accompany" Ariana? What a joke.

"How ridiculous," Will said, his expression darkening slightly.

"Tsk tsk tsk~ What an amusing little pretty boy," Karen said, hands on her curvy hips, clearly intrigued by Dylan.

"It seems the three of you aren't too keen on the idea," Dylan said, looking slightly disappointed.

"Dylan, I know you're strong and capable of defeating Archer and Proton with ease, but get one thing straight," Lt. Surge said with a twisted grin.

"I'm not Archer or Proton! And you're here with two little girls. Are you sure you want to fight us?"

Lt. Surge's arrogance was palpable, and understandably so. As one of Rocket's three top executives, he had every reason to be confident.

Compared to Archer and Proton, Lt. Surge's strength was on an entirely different level.

"Three against one, pretty boy. The odds are in our favor. The situation doesn't look great for you," Karen said, twirling a strand of her bluish-gray hair, her tone both teasing and warning.

Behind her, the cold-eyed Umbreon stared at Dylan and his group with blood-red eyes, its aura icy and intimidating.

"How interesting. It's been a long time since anyone dared to warn me," Dylan said, raising an eyebrow as he enlarged two Poké Balls in his hand.

"As a Gym Leader under the Kanto League, there's no reason for me to let Rocket members off the hook."

"Sending the three of you to keep Ms. Ariana company sounds like a perfect plan. You can play cards together in your spare time—it'd be delightful, don't you think?"

Hearing this, Lt. Surge took out a Poké Ball of his own.

"It seems we won't be reaching an understanding today. Let me show you something, Dylan."

Lt. Surge licked his lips, his excitement growing. He'd been looking forward to fighting Dylan for a long time.

"There's a hierarchy among Rocket's top executives!!"

He was determined to test Dylan's strength and prove his own superiority.

Moreover, he wasn't alone. With Karen, Will, and ten Rocket elites at his side, the odds were clearly in their favor.

A 13-on-1 matchup—was there even a question of who had the advantage?

Today, Lt. Surge was ready to avenge Archer and Proton!

(To be continued...)

# 179: Chapter 180: Confess now

"Mew?"

In front of the abandoned Power Plant, the mythical Pokémon, now disguised as a 'Jigglypuff,' sat comfortably in Dylan's arms. It suddenly turned its head, gazing into the distance as if it had sensed something.

"What's wrong, little one?" Dylan asked, looking down at 'Jigglypuff.'

"It's strange, Mew. Just now, I seemed to feel a very familiar presence," the Pokémon murmured, nibbling on its tiny paws and tilting its head in confusion. It had sensed something strikingly familiar for a fleeting moment but couldn't quite pinpoint what it was.

"Never mind, Mew. Forget it. Dylan, I want twenty boxes of canned food when we get back!"

"What?! How do you dare to demand that?" Dylan exclaimed.

"I transformed into Zapdos for you, Mew!" Jigglypuff puffed up its chest proudly.

"All I asked for was a transformation! Why does that warrant wholesale groceries? Besides, your Zapdos form wasn't even convincing! It didn't even have a neck!" Dylan retorted, recalling the incident. When Jigglypuff had transformed into Zapdos, not only had it looked bizarre, but even Leah, who had a bird phobia, couldn't stop laughing.

The most absurd part was Zapdos itself, whose bug-eyed, horrified expression had perfectly turned it into a living meme.

"I don't care, Mew! I transformed, so you owe me the canned food!"

"Pig-Mew, you really can't keep eating like this..." Dylan sighed.

"Don't call me Pig-Mew! Dylan, you've changed! I even took a hit for you once, Mew!" Jigglypuff pouted dramatically.

"Are you planning to hold that over me forever?" Dylan twitched at the corner of his mouth, helpless against this demanding little Pokémon.

"Fine, fine, I owe you," he relented.

"Yay~ Dylan is the best, Mew~" Jigglypuff beamed, its eyes squinting in delight.

"I'm going to sleep now, Mew. Wake me up when we're home. Goodnight, Dylan~" With that, Jigglypuff's body began to glow faintly as it floated into Dylan's backpack. Before disappearing completely, it politely said, "Goodnight."

Dylan glanced at the bright, sunny sky but decided to let it go.

"Pfft~ Dylan, where did you even find such a perfect Mew?" Leah burst into laughter, holding her stomach. Watching Dylan and Jigglypuff haggling like an old married couple had thoroughly amused her.

Lillie covered her mouth, chuckling softly as well.

"Picked it up in the yard—fate works in mysterious ways," Dylan said helplessly. Despite its quirks—like being gluttonous, lazy, and greedy—Mew had no major flaws. Well, aside from being a bit overweight.

More importantly, when it really mattered, Mew was always reliable and never hesitated to help.

"I'm so envious! It feels like something straight out of a novel. Plus, you're progressing so quickly!" Leah exclaimed as she walked in front of Dylan, hands clasped behind her back. She bent slightly, inspecting him with wide eyes full of admiration.

From the moment she met Dylan on the S.S. Anne, Leah had decided to stick with him and Red. Looking back now, she realized she wasn't just clinging to big opportunities—these were monumental opportunities!

"That's because my brother is amazing~" Lillie chimed in with a proud smile.

"Stop it, stop it. Keep complimenting me, and I'll get cocky," Dylan joked, waving his hand modestly.

"Hmph, I think you deserve to be cocky. You've tamed Unova's Dragon God! Even Lance might not be a match for you now, right?" Leah's bright eyes sparkled as she leaned closer in curiosity.

Dylan rubbed his chin thoughtfully. "Without using legendary Pokémon, I'd say it's hard to tell. I might not be able to beat him."

The last time Dylan sparred with Lance didn't count; Lance hadn't used his full main lineup. Only his last Dragonite could be considered part of his A-team, and even then, it wasn't his ace Dragonite. Despite that, it had easily suppressed Dylan's Dragonite in a direct clash.

Considering that Dylan's Dragon team was his strongest, it spoke volumes about Lance's prowess as a top-tier Champion.

"The Champion of Dragon Masters sure lives up to his title," Leah mused with a tilt of her head.

"But Dylan, does that mean if you used your legendary Pokémon, you could beat Lance?"

"More like, if I used my legendary team, the real question is how Lance could avoid losing too badly," Dylan quipped with a raised eyebrow.

"Wow~ Such confidence!" Leah blinked in surprise. "Are legendary Pokémon really that strong?"

"Who do you think you're underestimating here? I'm the man who has Reshiram, Kyurem, Lunala, Necrozma, and a gluttonous Mew," Dylan said with a shrug.

Leah's face filled with awe. She didn't even need to understand all the names to know they were incredible.

"Then... could you beat Ho-Oh and Lugia?" Leah asked, her voice tinged with anticipation.

"To be modest, they're decent opponents," Dylan replied humbly.

"And if you're not modest?"

"Merely grains of sand." Dylan spread his hands nonchalantly.

Leah's eyes sparkled with admiration. If Dylan could easily handle Ho-Oh and Lugia, did that mean he was on par with the Masked Man?

But then a thought struck her. Dylan's strength was his own achievement; he had no obligation to face the Masked Man for her sake. She bit her lip, hesitant.

"Dylan, does your Gym need more staff?" she asked suddenly.

"What's this? Is the great beauty applying for a job at my Gym?" Dylan chuckled.

Leah met his gaze seriously. "If I ever need help, I'll tell you directly. For now, I just need a place to crash and freeload for a few days. What do you say? Not welcome?"

"Fine, fine. You can take over as the referee for now," Dylan agreed, amused.

"Yay! I'm great at being a referee!" Leah cheered, her bright smile lighting up the atmosphere.

Dylan's phone suddenly rang. A glance revealed that it was President Charles Goodshow calling.

"President," Dylan answered the call.

"Dylan, I've received word. You've captured three Rocket Team executives, haven't you?"

Charles Goodshow's voice carried a somber tone.

"That's correct. I apprehended them near Lavender Town."

"You've done well. Now… one of them is really Lt. Surge?"

"Yes, it's indeed Lt. Surge."

"Phew… good, Dylan. I must trouble you. Could you personally escort Lt. Surge and the others to Indigo Plateau? I can't trust anyone else with this."

Charles took a deep breath before speaking, his voice hoarse. Clearly, this information was distressing for him.

And understandably so—Lt. Surge was the Gym Leader of Vermilion City, Kanto's largest port city. The Vermilion Gym was one of the region's official eight gyms!

For the Gym Leader to be revealed as a Rocket Team infiltrator, it was news that would undoubtedly become a laughingstock if leaked.

"I understand." Dylan raised his brow. It was evident that Charles was concerned about Rocket Team attempting to rescue Lt. Surge and the others. After all, they had seen this happen before with Apollo.

"Thank you, Dylan. I'll contact you later regarding the rewards for this operation."

"No problem, President. Please take care."

Dylan nodded. It seemed the Kanto League was about to take some significant action.

"That was President Charles Goodshow on the phone. We have some work to do," Dylan remarked.

Dylan wasn't overly concerned about whether Lt. Surge and the others would be rescued; after all, they had already been captured. But since Charles personally requested it, Dylan didn't mind lending a hand.

"Reshiram."

Dylan released Reshiram from its Poké Ball. The elegant white dragon carried Dylan and his team as they flew towards Lavender Town.

However, before reaching Lavender Town, Dylan spotted several police cars parked on the roadside.

"What's going on here?"

The white dragon landed, and Dylan saw Officer Jenny and her team treating their injured Arcanine and other Pokémon.

"Gym Leader Dylan, we were just attacked by a Pokémon, and Lt. Surge and the other two were rescued," a young Officer Jenny reported, biting her lip in frustration.

"Sorry, it's our fault for being incompetent."

"As long as no one is hurt. Do you know what kind of Pokémon it was? Which direction did they go?"

Dylan frowned. Rocket Team had acted quickly.

"It was a strange Pokémon with an armor-like device on its body. Its psychic powers were overwhelming and defeated our Pokémon in an instant," Officer Jenny explained rapidly.

"After rescuing Lt. Surge and stealing their Poké Balls, it used teleportation to escape. It's definitely a Psychic-type Pokémon."

"Armored… Armored Mewtwo."

Dylan raised an eyebrow, immediately suspecting Armored Mewtwo. There was no doubt—it must have awakened.

And with Giovanni's charisma and capabilities, persuading Mewtwo to temporarily follow him wasn't surprising.

"Alright, I'll search the surrounding area. You focus on your safety and head back to Lavender Town," Dylan instructed Officer Jenny and her team before taking off again on the white dragon.

"Rocket Team acted faster than expected," Dylan muttered.

"They sure did," Leah agreed, frowning. "I can't believe we couldn't even get Surge and the others locked up properly."

"It seems they secretly called for reinforcements, but the rescuers didn't act immediately, waiting instead to ambush during the police transfer," Dylan analyzed.

"But how are we supposed to track them now?" Lillie asked in confusion. "They used teleportation. We don't even have a direction to search."

"Let's scan the nearby area first," Dylan decided, directing Reshiram to circle around.

Just then, Dylan's phone buzzed with a new message.

Gym Leaders Group Chat

Officer Jenny: @Everyone, URGENT NOTICE! Lt. Surge is a Rocket Team executive! URGENT NOTICE! Lt. Surge is a Rocket Team executive!

Officer Jenny pinged the group three times in quick succession, then tagged Lt. Surge directly—only for him to leave the group immediately after.

The chat exploded.

Cerulean Gym—Daisy: What???

Pewter Gym—Flint: This…

Celadon Gym—Erika: I can't believe it. Lt. Surge… a Rocket Team spy?

Saffron Gym—Sabrina: (shocked.gif)

Fuchsia Gym—Koga: Truly… an appalling revelation. (embarrassed.jpg)

Cinnabar Gym—Blaine: Frightening. Rocket Team's infiltration runs deep.

Officer Jenny: I never thought it could be true. If there are other spies in this group, confess now! (angry.jpg)

Officer Jenny: The League is taking this matter extremely seriously. Everyone will be closely monitored. If misunderstandings arise, please bear with us. The impact of this incident is enormous.

Celadon Gym—Erika: Understood.

Pewter Gym—Flint: Ah… I'm semi-retired and too weak to be of interest, right? Okay, okay, I get it.

Cerulean Gym—Daisy: We sisters are definitely not Rocket Team spies! We only cheer others on. (cute.jpg)

Viridian Gym—Giovanni: I believe everyone here is a staunch supporter of the League's justice. If there are still spies among us, I won't tolerate it and will personally ensure their capture! Evil must not be allowed to flourish!

Viridian Gym—Giovanni: As Gym Leaders of the League, we must always oppose the evil of Rocket Team!

Saffron Gym—Sabrina: (suspicious.gif)

Rota Gym—Dylan: Well said, Giovanni! Bravo! (applause.gif)

(To be continued)

# 180: Chapter 181: Kitakami

Seeing the messages in the group chat, Dylan couldn't help but laugh. As expected of Giovanni, his public demeanor was impeccable.

Still, Dylan took a screenshot of the conversation—these were definitely "words of wisdom" to save for later.

Meanwhile, in a secret Team Rocket base...

"Boss, I sincerely apologize."

Lt. Surge's face was full of shame. Not only had he failed to capture Zapdos, but he also exposed his identity.

If it weren't for the boss personally intervening, he might have ended up in the same boat as Athena.

"It's fine. For now, stay hidden at the base and wait for your next assignment," Giovanni said calmly, shaking his head. Running into Dylan was completely unexpected.

"What Pokémon was Dylan using?" Giovanni asked in a serious tone.

When Surge had called for help, Giovanni had immediately arrived with Armored Mewtwo. However, the aura of Kyurem was overwhelming.

Even Armored Mewtwo felt an unsettling sense of dread, and Giovanni's own instinct, honed by his bond with the power of Viridian, warned him of the danger. Thus, he chose to observe rather than act.

"Kyurem. Dylan called it Kyurem," Surge replied with a frown. The name was unfamiliar to him.

"It must be a legendary Pokémon from another region—and definitely not an ordinary one," Selene interjected.

Giovanni nodded. "I see. I'll look into Kyurem. Knowing our enemy is crucial for future battles. Rest for now. I'll inform you when it's time to act again."

His gaze shifted to Selene and Proton, the two Team Rocket executives brought in from the Johto region. This operation was supposed to assess their capabilities, but encountering Dylan on their first mission in Kanto was simply bad luck.

Giovanni and Armored Mewtwo reappeared in a remote wilderness.

Giovanni clutched his chest and quickly swallowed two pills from a small bottle, his face regaining some color.

"Your condition is worsening," Armored Mewtwo's voice resonated telepathically, its blue eyes glowing faintly.

From the moment it awoke, Mewtwo had observed this human. Although it didn't trust Giovanni, recognizing his cunning and danger, it couldn't deny the man treated it with respect, as an equal.

Mewtwo's feelings toward Giovanni were complicated. It also knew Giovanni's secret—he was suffering from a terminal illness. If left untreated, his life expectancy was no more than a few years.

"I'm not in a rush. I still have time," Giovanni said, his tone magnetic and composed.

"If you abandoned your so-called 'career,' you might have time to find a cure," Mewtwo suggested.

"Life isn't just about survival," Giovanni replied, adjusting the brim of his hat. "Pointless existence isn't my philosophy."

Mewtwo's glowing eyes flickered.

"Team Rocket isn't just an organization—it's a part of my life, cultivated with effort and sacrifice," Giovanni continued. "To abandon it would be to abandon myself."

"Then what has it brought you? You even have to rescue your subordinates yourself," Mewtwo asked, skeptical.

"You'll understand someday," Giovanni said with a faint smile. "When you discover your purpose and value as a being, you will be complete."

Mewtwo fell silent, pondering Giovanni's words.

Back in Vermilion City, the Gym was temporarily closed.

The official statement cited Lt. Surge's health issues as the reason for the closure. This announcement caused a stir, as Lt. Surge was a well-known and popular Gym Leader in Kanto.

Though his tough, military-style battles often left rookie Trainers in tears, his reputation for fair play earned him respect.

Still, Kanto had no shortage of Gyms, so the news quickly faded.

Interestingly, other Gym Leaders seemed subtly affected by the incident. Even typically lax leaders began taking their duties more seriously.

For example, Blaine from Cinnabar Island—known for his Gym's elusive location—suddenly became more active, holding regular challenges.

At the Rota Gym, Dylan was addressing his protégé, Selene.

"Selene, most challengers are kids. You can't go overboard—if someone's using a Metapod, don't send out Incineroar to crush them!" Dylan sighed.

Selene nodded obediently. "Got it, Gym Leader! I'll be more careful!"

"Good. Get back to training," Dylan said, waving her off.

Ilene chuckled. "Selene's growing stronger, but her teams are too advanced for novice challengers."

"She can always use the Pokémon from my reserve," Dylan said. "But she's set on being a Trainer, not a Gym Leader, so I can't blame her."

Selene's growth was astonishing—she was already at the Elite Four level.

Letting her use her main team to face challengers would be overwhelming—the challengers would be completely crushed. So, Dylan suggested to Selene that she could temporarily use his lower-level Pokémon to test challengers instead.

"After all, using something like a Metapod to challenge the gym is indeed problematic. Selene being harsher with them is actually for their own good," Ilene remarked thoughtfully.

How could anyone seriously battle with a Metapod unless it was a specific scenario? Otherwise, what would a Metapod even do in battle? Just harden over and over? Ilene couldn't comprehend the mindset of someone challenging a gym with a Metapod.

"Are you planning to travel again next week?"

"Yeah, I'm planning a trip to Kitakami. I want to catch a Pokémon there," Dylan replied with a smile.

Indeed, by the weekend, he was preparing to head to Kitakami.

"If time permits, I also want to visit Paldea," Dylan added. Kitakami and Paldea weren't exactly close, but compared to the distance between Kanto and Paldea, it was manageable.

"This time, I'm going too!" Ilene's eyes sparkled. Last time, a guest at her castle kept her tied up, so she couldn't accompany Dylan to Unova. It was a regret she'd been brooding over for a while.

"Sure," Dylan agreed with a smile.

A few days later, the Rota Gym closed its doors once again. Dylan and Jenny took a few days off, and Dylan, Ilene, and Lillie boarded a flight to Kitakami.

Kitakami is the setting of the first part of the Pokémon Scarlet/Violet DLC, The Teal Mask.

It isn't a full-fledged region like others but rather a small area.

Dylan had looked up information about Kitakami. Compared to the game version, the real-life Kitakami had a few small villages, though none were larger than Verdant Town.

The trio soon arrived at the Verdant Town airport.

"Ah, the air here is so fresh!"

Ilene, dressed in a flowing yellow dress, stepped out of the airport, took a deep breath, and looked around.

In the distance, towering mountains were visible, and the land around them was a vast expanse of fields, farmland, and orchards.

"Kitakami is famous for its natural scenery. Though it's not very large, it's quite a popular tourist destination," Dylan commented with a smile.

"Let's head into town," he added.

The three made their way to Verdant Town. It wasn't far—after about ten minutes, they reached the town.

They found their pre-booked inn and rested for a bit before setting out to explore.

The real-life Verdant Town was much larger than its game counterpart. However, as they stopped in front of the Momosawa Shop, Dylan's steps faltered when he saw the purplish-red peach-like object on the display shelf.

This wasn't just a simple decoration—it was the mythical Pokémon, Pecharunt!

Hundreds of years ago, it had been gravely injured in a battle with Ogerpon and had no choice but to seal itself within its peach-like shell to recover.

It had been asleep for centuries. By now, its wounds were likely healed, but it still awaited the right moment to awaken.

(To be continued…)

# 181: Chapter 182: Dylan’s Little Admirer

"Ogerpon… let's leave it for now and come back later."

Dylan's eyes flickered slightly as he looked at Pecharunt, then he smiled faintly and walked away. There was no need to rush.

For outsiders, Kitakami held a certain novelty, with customs entirely different from other regions.

"There seem to be a lot of these mask stalls around," Lillie remarked curiously, picking up a yellow mask from a stall displaying various designs.

"That's because Kitakami's oldest tradition is the 'Festival of Masks.' It's a celebration where everyone, adults and children alike, can join in and have a great time," Dylan explained warmly, examining a black mask from the stall.

"You must be visitors, right? You seem to know a lot about our customs," the middle-aged stall owner said, surprised by Dylan's knowledge.

"I've always been interested in the traditions of different regions, so I know a thing or two," Dylan replied with a polite smile.

"Haha! You're absolutely right. The 'Festival of Masks' has been passed down in Kitakami for generations. During the festival, both locals and visitors can wear masks and enjoy themselves," the man explained.

"But there's more to it than that. Masks hold a unique significance for the people of Kitakami. Our region has been renowned for its exquisitely crafted masks for centuries, and their fame even reached overseas long ago."

The stall owner's voice brimmed with pride.

Dylan raised an eyebrow, a faint, sarcastic smile tugging at his lips.

"So, would you like to buy three masks? The Festival of Masks is coming up soon, and you can join in wearing them. My masks are some of the best around here," the man suggested, noticing Ilene and Lillie's interest.

Seeing their enthusiasm, Dylan purchased three masks, and the trio continued their exploration.

"Brother, your expression just now seemed a bit off. Was there something wrong with what that man said?" Lillie asked curiously, tilting her head as the white mask hung loosely on her head.

She had noticed Dylan's subtle reaction when the man claimed Kitakami's masks had been famous overseas for centuries. Having spent so much time together, Lillie's attentiveness allowed her to pick up on these details.

"It wasn't wrong, per se. It just reminded me of a rather amusing story," Dylan chuckled softly.

"Tell us, Dylan!" Ilene urged, intrigued.

"This story is a bit long. Let me think about where to begin," Dylan said, tapping the black mask in his hand as he organized his thoughts.

"Long ago, in a distant village, there lived an elderly couple who had no children. They took in a timid Pokémon, Pecharunt, and loved it as their own child," Dylan began.

Pecharunt?

Ilene and Lillie exchanged puzzled glances. They had never heard of this Pokémon before.

"To gain even more affection, Pecharunt used the poison on its body to create delicious, sticky Chain Mochi, which it offered to the elderly couple. The couple couldn't resist the taste and quickly became enthralled by Pecharunt," Dylan continued.

What?!

Lillie's wide-eyed shock made her expression adorably animated.

"But the Chain Mochi had a side effect—it amplified the desires of those who ate it. The couple became insatiably greedy, demanding more and more from Pecharunt," Dylan explained.

"Pecharunt, eager for their love, complied with their every request. It even commanded the Pokémon Okidogi to fetch luxurious clothes and mythical peaches for the couple. As their desires were fulfilled, their love for Pecharunt only deepened," Dylan recounted slowly.

"This is so twisted! Both Pecharunt and the elderly couple are completely out of line!" Lillie exclaimed.

"Exactly. A relationship built on unchecked desires is doomed to collapse," Dylan agreed.

"One day, the couple heard rumors about exquisitely beautiful masks said to exist in Kitakami. They demanded Pecharunt bring them these masks. Pecharunt, eager to please, set off for Kitakami."

"To travel, it needed companions. Pecharunt brought Okidogi, enchanted by the Chain Mochi, along for the journey. Along the way, it also recruited two other Pokémon: Munkidori, said to foresee glimpses of the future, and Fezandipiti, whose pheromones could entrance others."

Finally, the masks! Ilene and Lillie leaned in, captivated, as the story reached its most intriguing part.

"Meanwhile, before Pecharunt's arrival, a man from a foreign land came to Kitakami with a peculiar Pokémon, Ogerpon. Because their appearances and customs differed greatly, the locals shunned them."

"A kind mask craftsman, moved by their plight, carved four masks for them and decorated them with crystal fragments to hide their faces."

"With the masks, the man and Ogerpon were finally accepted by the villagers. Their beautiful, intricate masks soon became famous, even overseas."

So, those were the masks the elderly couple wanted!

Ilene and Lillie realized what was coming. Pecharunt must have come to steal these masks.

"During the Festival of Masks, Pecharunt spotted the man wearing the Verdant Mask and Ogerpon with the Teal Mask. Confirming the rumors, it began planning to take them."

"On a full moon night, Okidogi and Fezandipiti sneaked into the caves to steal the masks. However, the man returned and confronted them. Okidogi and Fezandipiti had no choice but to fight him, leaving the man injured and sprawled on the ground."

"They fled with three masks, leaving behind the Teal Mask the man had desperately protected."

This is awful!

Lillie bit her lip in frustration. It was heartbreaking to see someone's happiness destroyed by greed.

"The story's climax is yet to come," Dylan smiled faintly.

"Pecharunt was overjoyed with the masks, certain the couple would adore it even more. But an enraged Ogerpon, wearing the Teal Mask, stormed into their midst."

"In its fury, Ogerpon defeated Okidogi, Fezandipiti, and Munkidori one by one. Without their protection, Pecharunt was gravely injured and rolled into obscurity. Grieving, Ogerpon returned to the caves alone."

Hearing this, Lillie let out a relieved sigh. At least Ogerpon and the man hadn't lost everything.

"Ogerpon is incredible!" Lillie exclaimed in admiration.

"But the fight had attracted the attention of many villagers. Unaware of the truth, they only saw Ogerpon's furious, fearsome form and were terrified," Dylan said, shaking his head.

"They mistakenly believed Okidogi, Munkidori, and Fezandipiti had protected them from a demon. Thus, they honored them as heroes and gave them a grand burial."

What?!

Lillie gasped, her expression a mix of shock and indignation.

Upon hearing this, Ilene and Lillie were both stunned.

"They treated the robbers as heroes? And even gave them a grand burial?"

Ilene was shocked. How could there be such ignorant people?! The truly innocent ones were the man and Ogerpon all along.

"There's more," Dylan continued, "Not only did they give them a grand burial, but they even erected statues of them."

Suddenly, a helpless female voice reached the three of them.

Dylan turned around, and three figures came into view, one of whom looked quite familiar.

"Dylan, it really is you!"

A girl in a pink long coat, Lacey, brightened upon seeing him and quickly ran over, full of excitement.

"Miss Lacey, what are you doing here?" Dylan was also surprised to see Lacey.

"Well, Carmine and Kieran were going back home to visit their family, so I came with them to visit Kitakami," Lacey explained.

"Lillie, it's been a while~"

Lacey flashed a sweet smile and waved at Lillie.

"Miss Lacey, it's been so long~"

Though it hadn't been that long—barely a month.

Lillie looked at Lacey curiously, but it felt like a lot of time had passed since their last trip to Unova.

"I heard from my father that before you left Unova, Dylan captured the leader of Team Plasma! Truly, Dylan, you're so amazing!"

Lacey was bubbling with excitement, her big beautiful eyes sparkling with admiration, looking like a total fangirl.

Behind Lacey, her friends Carmine and Kieran exchanged somewhat awkward glances.

In Blueberry Academy, Lacey was usually quite serious, and she was one of the Elite Four of the academy's league. The students all respected her.

But...

Kieran cast a sideways glance at Dylan, his eyes full of curiosity, yet there was a hint of timidity.

"It was just a small favor, and Team Plasma came looking for me themselves," Dylan smiled humbly.

"By the way, are these your juniors?"

Seeing Lacey's excitement about their reunion and forgetting about Carmine and Kieran, Dylan asked her.

"Oh~ Sorry, I completely forgot about Carmine and Kieran!"

Lacey turned back with a surprised expression, apologizing.

"It's alright, Miss Lacey. Who's this?" Carmine asked, looking at Dylan curiously.

She could probably understand Lacey's excitement. Based on the way Lacey was acting, it was clear that this handsome man was likely Lacey's idol.

"This is Mr. Dylan, the Gym Leader of Rota Town in the Kanto Region. He's an extremely~ extremely strong and excellent Gym Leader. He's my idol!"

Lacey grinned brightly, introducing him with a glowing expression, showing no hesitation in admitting her admiration for Dylan.

Since that gym battle, she had become a huge fan of his. She even still wore the Rota Badge on her wrist, caring for it lovingly every day.

"Dylan, these are my fellow students, Carmine and Kieran. They're from Kitakami."

"I came here to visit Kitakami with them."

Lacey introduced Carmine and Kieran to Dylan.

"Hello."

Dylan smiled warmly at the two and gave a special glance to the thin, white-clad young man.

Kieran's face was quite handsome, but his peculiar hairstyle—two black bangs crossing down to his nose—seriously hurt his looks.

Dylan raised an eyebrow at the seemingly meek Kieran. This guy didn't seem as harmless as he appeared.

Compared to Carmine, Kieran's talent as a trainer far exceeded that of his sister. After deciding to get stronger in the original story, Kieran's strength began to soar rapidly.

He even became a champion-level trainer!

And…

This was the same guy who captured the legendary Pokémon Terapagos with a Master Ball in Scarlet/Violet!

Well… though things didn't go so well after that. The Master Ball broke when Terapagos went out of control.

(End of Chapter)

---

# 182: Chapter 183: Bloodmoon

"A Gym Leader from Kanto?"

Carmine found it intriguing. She had heard of the Kanto Region before—it was said to be where the headquarters of the Pokémon League was located.

However, as a student at Blueberry Academy, she hadn't had the chance to challenge any gyms yet.

But since Gym Leader Dylan was someone Lacey admired so much, he must be incredibly powerful.

"Nice to meet you, Gym Leader Dylan," Carmine said gracefully, her demeanor reflecting her mature personality.

"Hello, Gym Leader Dylan," Kieran greeted shyly but politely. Though he was a bit reserved, he was not socially awkward.

"Just now, we overheard you talking about the legend of Ogerpon, didn't we?" Carmine asked curiously. Though she hadn't caught the full story, it was clear that Dylan was knowledgeable about Ogerpon's history.

This intrigued her greatly. Even among the locals of Kitakami, few knew the true story of Ogerpon.

"That's correct. Miss Carmine, are you familiar with it as well?"

"Actually, Kieran and I are descendants of the craftsmen who carved masks for Ogerpon and its former owner. So we know the truth," Carmine explained.

"After the incident with Ogerpon, our ancestor tried to reveal the true story to the villagers. But instead of listening, they labeled him a heretic and persecuted him."

"To protect his descendants, he recorded the truth and passed it down through the family."

Carmine's voice carried a hint of anger as she spoke. When she first learned of this, she had wanted to confront the villagers about it, but her grandparents stopped her.

"That's so cruel…" Lillie murmured, pressing her lips together. It was heartbreaking that the truth was not only ignored but those who shared it were harmed.

"Such is the folly of ignorance," Dylan commented simply.

"People often believe only what they see and form their own conclusions, arrogantly thinking they've grasped the truth without considering the full story. It's a pitiful sort of arrogance," he added calmly, clearly accustomed to such scenarios.

"To what do we owe the pleasure of your visit to Kitakami, Gym Leader Dylan?" Lacey asked, tactfully steering the conversation away from its heavy tone.

"We're here for a bit of relaxation. Also, I have a particular fondness for Ogerpon. If I could capture it, it would be a lucky encounter," Dylan replied.

"Finding Ogerpon is no easy feat, Gym Leader Dylan," Carmine remarked with a raised brow. "Kieran and I have lived here since childhood, but we've never seen it ourselves."

"Besides, Kieran and I are also hoping to capture Ogerpon," she added with a smile.

"Well, it seems we're rivals then, Miss Carmine and Mr. Kieran," Dylan replied good-naturedly. "Catching a Pokémon depends on fate. Let's see who has the stronger bond with Ogerpon."

"Of course. We'll compete fairly," Carmine responded, her eyes sparkling with excitement. Although she wasn't overly attached to the idea of capturing Ogerpon, she knew her brother Kieran was.

"What do you think, Kieran?"

"Yes, I'll definitely earn Ogerpon's recognition!" Kieran declared, his shy demeanor giving way to determination. He had always admired Ogerpon, especially its strength in defeating other Pokémon.

"As a rival, you'll need more confidence, Mr. Kieran. Or is it that you don't believe in yourself?" Dylan teased with a light laugh.

"I… I'll definitely gain Ogerpon's approval!" Kieran shouted, his face turning red under the gaze of Lacey, Lillie, and the others.

"Great spirit. I look forward to seeing your performance," Dylan encouraged with a smile.

"Yes!" Kieran replied eagerly, feeling both encouraged and overwhelmed.

"A man should be confident and stand tall. You're growing up—don't always hide behind your sister," Dylan said, handing Kieran a hair tie.

"Start by tying your hair. From today, begin transforming yourself."

"O-okay," Kieran stammered, surprised but touched. Looking at Dylan's sunny smile, he was momentarily dazed.

Regaining his composure, Kieran tied back his hair, his demeanor immediately changing.

"Hmm, not bad. You actually look quite handsome, Kieran," Carmine said, propping her chin on her hand as she studied him with approval.

"Really?" Kieran touched his face, a little embarrassed.

"A real man stands proud and faces life with confidence," Dylan said, patting Kieran's shoulder.

"Yes, I understand now, Gym Leader Dylan!" Kieran replied gratefully.

"Excellent. Keep up the good work," Dylan encouraged before bidding farewell to Lacey and the others.

Despite Lacey's insistence on treating them to a meal, Dylan politely declined, as he had plans to visit Area Zero in Paldea next.

"Lacey, is Gym Leader Dylan really that strong?" Kieran asked suddenly, now looking more composed with his tied-back hair.

"Very strong," Lacey affirmed. "I'd say even Champion Alder wouldn't stand a chance against him."

Stronger than the Champion of Unova? Carmine and Kieran exchanged astonished glances.

"It seems Mr. Dylan has high expectations for you, Kieran. You have to work hard, okay? If you get stronger, you might even challenge Mr. Dylan someday. Who knows? You might earn his Gym Badge."

Lacey smiled gently.

"Is Gym Leader Dylan's badge hard to obtain?" Carmine asked, sounding surprised.

"It's extremely difficult. The last time I challenged Gym Leader Dylan with all my strength... well, to be honest, I was completely defeated. I couldn't even fight back."

Lacey raised a hand and brushed back her hair, looking a little embarrassed.

"Even you, Senior Lacey…" Kieran was shocked.

In his eyes, Lacey was already an incredibly strong Trainer. But even she was utterly defeated?

"However, due to some special circumstances, Gym Leader Dylan still acknowledged my determination, so he gave me a Rota Badge."

"Look~ Isn't it beautiful?"

Soon, Lacey's face blossomed into a radiant smile as she gracefully raised her wrist to show off the exquisitely designed Poison Rota Badge.

"So pretty~! I want one too!" Carmine's eyes lit up, especially drawn to the purple gemstone embedded in the badge—it was stunning!

Kieran's gaze burned with admiration as he stared at the intricate Rota Badge. This was Gym Leader Dylan's badge!

"Hehe~ Mine is the Poison Rota Badge. My case was a bit special; under normal circumstances, you need Gym Leader Dylan's recognition while using a Poison-type team to earn this badge."

"Let me tell you a secret: so far, only two of these newly designed Rota Badges have been given out! Mine is one of them!"

Lacey proudly tilted her head, her expression adorably smug.

"Impressive~!" Kieran was thrilled.

"I'm going to challenge Gym Leader Dylan! I'm going to earn the Rota Badge too!!"

Carmine and Kieran spoke in unison. As siblings, their shared determination was unmistakable.

"Emmm… well, good luck to both of you! I hope you don't end up pulling the 'Golden Legend' like I did." Lacey, ever the kind senior, encouraged them.

"What's the 'Golden Legend'?" Kieran asked, clearly puzzled.

"How do I put this… It's kind of like…"

Lacey's expression turned quirky as she rested her chin on her hand, pondering for a moment.

"A fluke!"

Meanwhile…

After strolling around for a while longer, Dylan and his group boarded Reshiram, heading to the northeastern part of Kitakami.

The Eternal Forest, one of Kitakami's natural landmarks, was their destination. Dylan had come here specifically to search for a certain Pokémon.

"Finally found it."

After circling the forest from above, Dylan pinpointed the Pokémon's location and signaled Reshiram to descend.

"Roar!!"

A thunderous roar shook the air. A massive, muscular gray-brown bear let out an intimidating growl, exuding an overwhelming aura of savagery.

"What kind of Pokémon is that?"

Ilene stared in astonishment at the fearsome bear, pulling out her Pokédex, which showed no data on it.

The creature resembled an enormous bear. The left side of its head and the upper part of its neck appeared to be covered in dried peat, and it had razor-sharp claws. A blood-red crescent moon pattern adorned its forehead, while its left eye, an eerie gray, gave it a chilling presence.

"Ursaluna—Bloodmoon. That's its name. It's not currently in the Pokédex database. Check the Rotom Dex's description," Dylan said with a grin, his eyes fixed on the ferocious Bloodmoon Ursaluna.

'Beep~ Ursaluna (Bloodmoon Form), Ground/Normal Type, Peat Pokémon

Ability: Keen Eye

Moves: Blood Moon, Hammer Arm, Moonblast, Earth Power, Snore, Rest, Headlong Rush, Slash…

Description: A unique form of Ursaluna that protects its body with iron-hard peat and possesses a left eye capable of piercing through darkness. (Bloodmoon)'

"It looks so strong!"

Lillie gazed in awe at the wild Bloodmoon Ursaluna.

"Ursaluna? I've never heard of it either."

"Ursaluna is the evolution of Ursaring. However, Ursaluna is almost unheard of in modern times because it was a specific evolution of Ursaring in ancient Sinnoh's Hisui region."

Dylan explained further, "This particular Bloodmoon Ursaluna is unique. It crossed the ocean long ago and adapted to the environment here, leading to its current form."

Dylan studied the Bloodmoon Ursaluna with keen interest, clearly satisfied.

This was a one-of-a-kind special Pokémon!

(End of Chapter)

---
