# 183: Chapter 184: A Lonely Widow Afraid of Isolation

"You're strong. How about coming with me to explore the outside world? Staying here would waste your strength," Dylan said as he stepped forward, taking out a Poké Ball and addressing the Bloodmoon Ursaluna.

"Roar!"

The Ursaluna responded with a fierce roar, showcasing its irritable temper.

"It seems I'll have to subdue you first," Dylan remarked, unsurprised, as he flipped the Poké Ball in his hand.

"Meowscarada, prepare for battle."

"Meow~!"

A soft, magnetic cry echoed as Meowscarada rose gracefully. Its gem-like eyes shone with arrogance as it stared at the Bloodmoon Ursaluna.

"Roar~!"

The Ursaluna let out another violent roar, the crimson full-moon pattern on its forehead glowing with an ominous red light.

Blood Moon, a signature move exclusive to the Bloodmoon Ursaluna, unleashed a powerful Normal-type attack. Its drawback, however, was the inability to use it consecutively.

As the Ursaluna roared, a massive red beam erupted towards Meowscarada with overwhelming force.

"Feint Attack."

Dylan smirked slightly.

Meowscarada's eyes gleamed with black light as it vanished from its spot. In an instant, its lithe, green figure appeared behind the Ursaluna, striking its back with sharp black claws.

Feint Attack!

A ripping sound followed. The Ursaluna roared in pain and immediately retaliated, summoning earthen energy around itself.

Earth Power, a Ground-type move!

"Energy Ball."

Meowscarada lightly tapped the ground, retreating nimbly as a green energy sphere formed between its paws.

Boom!

The Earth Power attack erupted but hit only empty air. The Energy Ball flew directly at the enraged Ursaluna, exploding upon impact.

As a Grass-type move, it dealt significant damage to the Ground/Normal-type Ursaluna.

The searing pain sent the Ursaluna into a frenzy, roaring madly as the red energy in its forehead crescent intensified once more.

Another Blood Moon attack was imminent.

"Such a bad temper, but trainable," Dylan muttered thoughtfully. Though the Ursaluna's power was undeniable, its volatile nature posed a challenge. Still, as long as it obeyed commands in battle, its temperament could be addressed over time.

"Meowscarada, dodge and use Frenzy Plant."

"Meow~!"

The Blood Moon beam surged forth once more, but Meowscarada gracefully darted across the ground, avoiding the attack effortlessly.

Boom! Boom! Boom!

The Ursaluna's Blood Moon attack blasted furiously, leaving destruction in its wake. Despite its power, it failed to keep up with Meowscarada's agility, and the move soon exhausted itself.

Meowscarada slammed its paws onto the ground, causing the earth to quake violently. Thick roots burst out from beneath, thrashing wildly in a spectacular display.

Frenzy Plant, a Grass-type ultimate move, unleashed a relentless barrage of strikes upon the Ursaluna, knocking its massive body to the ground.

When the assault ended, the Ursaluna lay unconscious, unable to continue fighting.

This outcome was inevitable—Dylan's Meowscarada had the type advantage, and the overwhelming Frenzy Plant sealed the victory.

"Ultra Ball, go."

Dylan threw an Ultra Ball at the fainted Ursaluna. The ball absorbed it in a flash of red light.

Beep… Beep… Beep… Click!

The Ultra Ball stopped shaking, confirming the successful capture of the Bloodmoon Ursaluna.

"Well done, Meowscarada!"

"Meow~"

Dylan retrieved the ball, showering Meowscarada with praise.

Meowscarada's grin turned mischievous as it crossed its arms, proudly lifting its head in triumph.

"This Ursaluna seems incredibly strong, brother. Its destructive power is astonishing," Lillie remarked, glancing at the devastated battlefield left by the Blood Moon attack.

She knew how powerful her brother's Meowscarada was, yet even it had to rely on Frenzy Plant to secure victory—a testament to the Ursaluna's strength.

"Well, this Ursaluna is practically an 'antique.' Plus, the Ursaluna line is naturally very powerful," Dylan explained with a smile. He added that this Bloodmoon Ursaluna had traveled across oceans from ancient Hisui to Kitakami, where it had adapted and thrived. Its high level was a given.

Although wild Pokémon at Champion-level were rare, they weren't unheard of. This Ursaluna was one such exception.

"Come out, Ursaluna."

Dylan released the Bloodmoon Ursaluna from the Ultra Ball.

The Pokémon appeared before him, its aura weakened from its earlier defeat.

"You're injured, so don't move for now. What do you say? Will you accept me as your Trainer?" Dylan asked gently, a green light emanating from his hand to heal the Ursaluna's wounds while fostering trust.

The Ursaluna's fierce eyes locked onto Dylan. After a moment of hesitation, it let out a low growl, signaling its acceptance.

"Good. Rest in your Poké Ball for now. When we return to town, I'll take you to the Pokémon Center for a full check-up. Living in the wild for so many years, who knows what hidden injuries or ailments you might have?"

Dylan reassured the Ursaluna with a warm tone before recalling it into the ball.

He then turned to Meowscarada and Reshiram, signaling the group to continue their journey. They spent the day exploring Kitakami's famous northern landscapes before returning to Kitakami at dusk.

That evening, Dylan, Ilene, and Lillie enjoyed a leisurely stroll through Kitakami's beautifully decorated streets, basking in the vibrant night atmosphere.

"Brother, look at this kimono! Isn't it beautiful?" Lillie exclaimed, her eyes sparkling as she admired a display in a nearby shop.

"Brother, look at this kimono! Isn't it beautiful?"

Outside a kimono shop, Dylan was pondering how to find the adorable Ogerpon, when Lillie's voice came from behind.

Dylan turned around to see Lillie standing gracefully, her long, golden hair cascading like a waterfall. She was dressed in a pink kimono adorned with cherry blossoms, her slender waist tied with a white sash.

The girl seemed a bit shy, her hands clasped behind her back, her fair face tinged with a faint blush, eyes full of expectation.

"It's beautiful—no surprise, you're my precious little sister."

Dylan smiled warmly, giving a thumbs-up to the girl in her kimono.

"Hehe~ This is my first time trying on a kimono," Lillie said, her radiant smile brightening her expression as she glanced down at the kimono.

In the tropical Alola region, kimono culture didn't exist, so this truly was her first experience wearing one.

In fact, Lillie had been intrigued by the idea since meeting Ilene. Girls always have a penchant for beauty, and Ilene's dignified and elegant appearance inspired Lillie to give it a try.

"It suits you. If you like it, buy it," Dylan said with a smile.

"Though it's not practical for the wild, it's perfect for town or home."

"Alright~!"

"If you find more you like, pick a few. It'll be a feast for my eyes."

"Ugh… Brother, what do you mean by a 'feast for your eyes'?"

Her face turned red again, but with a pleased expression, she continued browsing the other beautiful kimonos.

"Lillie isn't done yet?"

Ilene approached from a distance, carrying a shopping bag in her hand. She had just gone to buy some drinks.

"She seems to really like kimonos. Aren't you going to try one?" Dylan asked with a chuckle.

"I'm not into kimonos. Here, want some fruit tea?" Ilene shook her head. She preferred elegant dresses.

"Sure. Hmm? I'll check over there," Dylan replied, noticing something as his eyes lit up. Taking a cup of fruit tea, he headed toward a tree not far away.

Ilene tilted her head curiously.

"Why are you hiding here all alone?"

Dylan's gentle voice reached the little creature that was curiously observing the crowd from afar.

"Oge?"

The green little Pokémon froze, turning its head to see Dylan smiling warmly at it.

Startled, it quickly hid behind the tree, peeking out cautiously with half its face.

"Am I that scary?"

Amused, Dylan crouched down, observing the timid little creature as his Viridian Force subtly emanated.

The natural, sweet energy calmed Ogerpon's nerves, its curiosity piqued as it tilted its head.

"Oge…"

"Don't worry, I'm not here to harm you. I'm Dylan. What's your name?"

"Oge~Oge!" The little Pokémon chirped more cheerfully, curious as to why this human could understand it.

"Ogerpon? What a cute name. Were you watching them play? Why not join them?"

Dylan glanced at a group of children playing nearby.

"Oge…" Ogerpon's tone turned somber.

"Do they dislike you? I see… How about I play with you instead?"

Dylan's warm and sunny smile left Ogerpon momentarily stunned.

"Oge?"

The little Pokémon hesitated, torn between its longing to approach Dylan and its lingering fear.

It was lonely—desperately yearning for a friend, for someone to play with.

But past experiences had left scars. To the villagers, it was a "terrifying ghost," often driven away in panic whenever it appeared.

This rejection had left a psychological shadow, and whenever it faced the villagers' angry glares, Ogerpon would tremble in fear.

And yet, it never once sought revenge.

Though the villagers were like mere grains of sand before its strength, Ogerpon never wished them harm.

Despite the fear, insults, and even stones hurled at it, it always refrained from retaliation.

Instead, longing for connection, it donned a mask time and again, sneaking into the town's corners to watch the humans, envious of their interactions.

Such was Ogerpon—a lonely, timid little spirit.

"I'm serious. Can I be your friend?"

Recalling the in-game Ogerpon trembling in fear before the villagers, Dylan smiled gently and extended his hand toward it.

"Oge…"

Slowly, Ogerpon inched forward, its cautious movements stirring Dylan's emotions.

Centuries of solitude—how much suffering had this little one endured?

Finally, Ogerpon stood before Dylan.

"From now on, I'll call you Ogy. You're just too cute," Dylan said with a smile.

"Oge?"

(End of Chapter)

# 184: Chapter 185: Who’s to Blame?

"Dylan, who's this little one?"

Ilene's curious voice chimed as she noticed Dylan returning with a Pokémon that resembled a small child.

Spotting Ilene, the timid Ogerpon instinctively hid behind Dylan, peeking out shyly from behind its verdant mask.

"This is Ogerpon. I'm showing it around," Dylan explained with a smile, gently patting Ogerpon's head.

"So, this is Ogerpon?"

Ilene froze for a moment, her gaze softening as she took in the sight of the bashful Pokémon.

Having heard Dylan recount Ogerpon's tale, Ilene already had a strong fondness for it.

"This little one's been through centuries of solitude, so it's a bit afraid of people. But I think it'll get better with time," Dylan said softly.

Ever since its human companion passed away, Ogerpon had been living alone in this place. Tragically, the ignorant villagers, fearful of the "terrifying ghost," had driven it away, leading to its conflicted feelings—longing for human connection yet fearing rejection.

"Hello, Ogerpon. I'm Ilene," Ilene said gently, crouching slightly to greet Ogerpon with the softest tone she could muster.

Ogerpon hesitated, unsure whether to respond.

"No need to be afraid. Ilene likes you very much too," Dylan reassured.

"Oge~"

Ogerpon's large eyes squinted with delight as it let out a cheerful chirp.

Dylan's innate Viridian Force made him naturally approachable to Pokémon, but beyond that, Ogerpon could sense the genuine kindness emanating from him.

"You're so cute!" Ilene exclaimed, charmed by Ogerpon's lively cry. Unfortunately, its verdant mask hid its face from view.

Ogerpon's humanoid form featured two black legs, a green torso, plant-like arms, and a green leaf sprouting from its head. Its face was obscured by the menacing verdant mask adorned with a sparkling crystal, adding an air of mystery.

Before long, Lillie emerged, tucking her newly purchased kimonos into her bag.

"Big brother, Ilene, have you been waiting long? Oh? What Pokémon is this?"

Her eyes lit up with curiosity as she noticed Ogerpon, which was equally intrigued by her.

"Oge~!" Ogerpon chirped, this time not hiding behind Dylan.

"This is Ogerpon. We just met, and we hit it off," Dylan explained with a smile.

"Wow, Ogerpon, huh? Hello, Ogerpon! I'm Lillie. Do you like berries?" Lillie asked eagerly.

"Oge~"

Shyly lifting the edge of its verdant mask, Ogerpon reached out and accepted the berry from Lillie's hand, happily munching on it.

Ilene and Lillie both leaned closer, curious about Ogerpon's face beneath the mask. They caught a glimpse of its orange cheeks, small sharp fangs, and golden eyes sparkling like stars—utterly adorable.

The group wandered the night market, with Ogerpon clinging to Dylan's clothes. Finding it cumbersome, Dylan eventually picked it up.

Ogerpon beamed with joy, gazing excitedly at the bustling crowd. It had been centuries since it experienced such vibrant energy.

However, after half an hour, Dylan set Ogerpon down. Despite its pleading eyes asking for more cuddles, Dylan admitted he was too tired to carry it any longer—Ogerpon was about 1.2 meters tall and weighed nearly 80 pounds!

After two hours exploring the market, the group arrived at a shrine.

"Wait here for a bit," Dylan instructed, leaving Ilene and Lillie with Ogerpon while he entered the shrine.

Releasing Darkrai from its Poké Ball, Dylan whispered a few words to it. Nodding, Darkrai vanished into the shadows.

It wasn't long before Darkrai returned, carrying three masks.

"Oge!!"

Ogerpon's eyes lit up as it stared at the masks in Darkrai's hands.

"Excited?" Dylan teased, holding the masks out toward Ogerpon.

"Oge Oge!!"

Ogerpon hopped around joyfully, circling Dylan to express its delight. These were the masks it had lost centuries ago!

The three masks represented water, fire, and rock types. Ogerpon eagerly tried each one on, its happiness palpable.

When it was time to leave, Ogerpon hesitated, visibly disheartened.

"Oge…"

It expressed its reluctance to part ways.

"How about staying with us at the inn tonight? I'll give you a surprise tomorrow," Dylan offered gently.

"Oge?"

"It's okay. With you around, I'll feel much safer. Right?"

"Oge!!"

Ogerpon nodded firmly.

That night, at the inn, Ogerpon sat on the bed, engrossed in examining its masks. Dylan chuckled at its enthusiasm.

"Time to sleep, Ogy."

"Oge~!"

But Ogerpon, still brimming with excitement, wasn't sleepy.

"How about watching a show? This one's about a hero fighting a three-headed dragon—it's pretty popular," Dylan suggested, handing it a phone.

Curious, Ogerpon quickly became absorbed in the drama, its golden eyes wide with tension as the dragon kidnapped a princess.

By morning, Dylan awoke to find Ogerpon still watching the show, completely captivated.

"Oge~ Oge..."

Ogerpon was seated on a chair, clutching a tissue in one hand and incessantly wiping away its tears, faint sobs escaping from time to time.

Dylan leaned over to check and couldn't help but twitch his lips. The cause of Ogerpon's emotional outburst? The scene on the phone screen depicted the three-headed dragon demon lord incinerating the princess with a single fiery breath, leaving the prince utterly heartbroken.

Hmm, it seemed Ogerpon had a rather strong sense of empathy.

"Ogy, did you watch this all night?"

"Oge? Oge~ Oge Oge~"

Startled, Ogerpon turned around and, upon seeing Dylan, immediately burst into tears and leapt into his arms.

"Alright, alright, it's okay. None of it is real. It's just a TV show. Don't worry, the princess and the prince are just fine," Dylan said soothingly, gently patting the little one's back until it finally calmed down.

But... it seemed this little one had developed a liking for dramas.

Later, as they ate breakfast, Dylan watched Ogerpon still glued to its phone, utterly engrossed in its show, and couldn't help but fall into contemplation.

A foodie Mew and a drama-obsessed Ogerpon—where exactly did things go wrong?

After breakfast, under Ogerpon's plaintive gaze, Dylan dragged it out into the street. Stopping at Momozawa's shop, he spent some money to buy a peach-shaped ornament.

Ogerpon stared at the peach-shaped ornament, deep in thought. It felt an inexplicable sense of familiarity.

Dylan and his group then arrived at Treasure Plaza.

"Necrozma, awaken them," Dylan commanded as he placed the large purple peach ornament on the ground and released Necrozma from its Poké Ball.

In the game, the Loyal Three revived abruptly. As for Pecharunt, players could trigger a side quest after obtaining the item Phantom Peach Fruit.

Dylan didn't have a Phantom Peach Fruit, but that didn't matter—he had other methods.

"Lunaa~"

Necrozma appeared in its normal form before Dylan, its colored parts glowing blue.

A powerful psychic energy enveloped the large peach, stimulating the consciousness within, while the psychic waves radiated across Treasure Plaza.

Before long, the surface of the peach began to glow a deep purple and floated into the air.

"What is that?" Ilene and Lillie exclaimed in surprise.

Ogerpon stared blankly at the floating 'giant peach.'

"Pecha~ruunt!"

A low, raspy voice suddenly echoed as the purple peach split open from the middle, revealing a violet figure within.

The mythical Pokémon Pecharunt had awakened!

Boom!

With a thunderous explosion, the altar at the front of Treasure Plaza shattered, filling the air with dust and debris.

Three distinctly shaped Pokémon emerged slowly from the cloud of dust—Okidogi, Munkidori, and Fezandipiti.

The legendary Loyal Three had been resurrected!

"Oge..."

Ogerpon murmured softly, gazing at the four familiar figures ahead.

"All the past events are etched in your heart, aren't they? I believe you haven't entirely forgiven them. If that's the case, why not go ahead and teach them another lesson?"

Dylan gently rubbed Ogerpon's head, speaking to it in a warm tone.

Ogerpon raised its face to look at Dylan, its eyes shimmering with moving light, and then it nodded firmly.

"Oge!"

In an instant, Ogerpon leapt forward to confront Pecharunt and the three Loyal Three, letting out a fierce cry.

"Pecha~ruunt?"

Pecharunt, still confused after its abrupt revival, saw Ogerpon and was flooded with memories of the past.

The old couple… the mask… the demon…

The old couple… the mask the old couple wanted!

"Pecha~ruunt!!" Pecharunt let out a piercing screech, its white eyes glaring furiously at Ogerpon.

It was this creature! If not for it, Pecharunt would have long since returned the mask to the old couple, fulfilling their wish. They could have loved it more deeply!

Pecharunt's enraged cry was shrill and piercing. Two dark purple chains shot out from within the split peach shell, hurtling toward Ogerpon on the ground.

Pecharunt's signature move—Poison-type Malignant Chain!

"Oge!!"

Ogerpon donned its Verdant Mask, its sharp and intense gaze locking onto the airborne Pecharunt.

It brought the past into its heart. The memories of bygone days would transform into its strength!

A dazzling light suddenly erupted from Ogerpon, enveloping it in crystalline brilliance.

Crackle!

The sound of shattering crystal echoed.

Pecharunt's Malignant Chain struck but were blocked by a massive, radiant mask shimmering with crystalline light.

"Oge~!!"

Now gleaming with brilliance, Ogerpon moved the crystalline Verdant Mask aside and growled deeply at Pecharunt.

The glow of memories reflected!

Important memories would never fade; they would become its strength. This was Ogerpon!

Ogerpon glanced at Pecharunt and then at the three Loyal Three that had rushed over.

It thought: "Perhaps it had gone too easy on them centuries ago. Since they're bouncing around again now, I'll have to hit them harder this time."

(Chapter End)

# 185: Chapter 186: "We Can Be Partners"

"This is…"

Ilene and Lillie were astonished as they gazed at the radiant Ogerpon and the massive crystalline Teal Mask before it.

"What is this?"

"Its memories ignited its fighting spirit, allowing its true strength to emerge," Dylan explained with a smile.

"Ogerpon's mask is unique, adorned with distinctive crystal shards that hold Teracrystal energy. That's why its teracrystallization is so extraordinary."

Unlike typical teracrystallization, Ogerpon's transformation wasn't just about itself; rather, the mask was the main focus of its power.

When Ogerpon teracrystallized, its ability, Embody Aspect, activated, channeling memories into the Teal Mask, making it glow and enhancing its speed. This unique synergy between Ogerpon and its mask defined its teracrystallization.

"Pecha-runttt!!"

The raspy cry of Pecharunt echoed with anger as it glared at the teracrystallized Ogerpon. It needed the mask—only with the mask could it return!

"Pecharunt, why deceive yourself? Don't you see? You've been dormant for centuries. The elderly couple you cherished has long passed away," Dylan said, delivering a harsh truth.

The tragic tale of Pecharunt and the elderly couple was a classic. Out of love, Pecharunt had offered them its poisonous sticky cakes. These treats, however, enhanced the eater's desires and abilities. Whether Pecharunt was aware of this side effect was a matter of debate.

Initially, the couple's care and love for Pecharunt were pure. But the cakes' influence stirred desires—dreams of legendary fruits, fine clothes, and exotic masks. Desperate to fulfill their wishes and earn more affection, Pecharunt's actions led to tragedy for all involved.

Even now, awakened after centuries, its only thought was to reclaim the mask and return to them.

"Pecha?"

Pecharunt hesitated, glancing around in confusion. Time had changed everything. The lush village it remembered had transformed beyond recognition.

Panic and fear overwhelmed Pecharunt. How could this happen? The couple… the mask…!

"Pecha-runttt!!"

Its sharp cry pierced the air as malevolent purple energy surged from its body. It believed that reclaiming the mask would restore everything.

Two massive, poisonous chains shot out from Pecharunt's shell, aiming for Ogerpon.

At the same time, three Pokémon—Pecharunt's loyal companions—charged forward.

"Ogerpon faces its old foes," Lillie remarked while attempting to scan the Okidogi with her Pokédex, only to find no data available.

"Still no data?!" she said, pouting at her brother.

"When the Roto-Dex hits the market, I'll get one for you," Dylan promised, patting her golden hair.

For now, Dylan's customized Rotom Pokédex recorded details about the Pokémon:

Pecharunt

Type: Poison/Ghost

Ability: Puppetmaster

Moves: Toxic Chains, Toxic, Trick, Shadow Ball, Parting Shot, Destiny Bond, Mean Look...

Description: Forces victims to consume toxic cakes that enhance their desires and abilities, then controls them using chains.

Lillie shuddered. "Such a terrifying Pokémon… it can even control humans, right?"

The Pokédex then analyzed the other Pokémon:

Okidogi: Poison/Fighting

Ability: Toxic Chains

Description: Its toxic chain enhances its strength.

Munkidori: Poison/Psychic

Fezandipiti: Poison/Fairy

Despite being surrounded by these legendary Pokémon, Ogerpon stayed calm. Using its Teal Mask to block the toxic chains, it retaliated with its signature Bramble Club, striking with incredible strength.

In no time, the Okidogi were defeated, kneeling in submission.

"How strong!" Lillie's eyes sparkled.

"The gap in power is vast. Ogerpon is incredible," Dylan praised.

Now only Pecharunt remained.

With its bramble club in hand, Ogerpon leapt into the air, heading for the trembling Pecharunt.

"Pecha…"

Memories of that fateful night overwhelmed Pecharunt. The terrifying sight of Ogerpon wielding its bramble club left it frozen in fear.

Bang!

The club struck Pecharunt with immense force, sending it crashing to the ground.

As Pecharunt lay there, its dim, lifeless eyes gazed at the sky. The mask, its companions, and the happiness the couple once gave—it had lost everything.

Tears rolled down Pecharunt's cheeks.

"Pecha… Pecha-runttt…"

Watching its despair, Ogerpon's expression turned complex. Even though it had defeated Pecharunt once more, the past could never be undone.

"How pitiful," Lillie murmured, feeling a pang of sympathy.

"Pitiful people often bring misfortune upon themselves. This is all its own doing," Dylan said, shaking his head.

"But that's all in the past now."

Dylan took steady steps toward Ogerpon.

"Oge…"

Hearing the footsteps, Ogerpon turned its head and saw Dylan walking toward it.

"Well? Do you feel better now?"

"Oge~"

Ogerpon nodded, then shook its head. The light around it dissipated as it de-teracrystallized, tightly embracing Dylan.

Those memories were now part of the past—it was time to embrace a new life.

"Why so sentimental?" Dylan gently picked up Ogerpon, tapping its large, round face.

After all, Ogerpon had just said, "I like Dylan."

"Oge Oge~"

Ogerpon's big eyes squinted into crescents, its happiness radiating as it grinned widely.

"I like you too, Ogerpon. I hope we can stay together from now on."

"Oge yo~!"

Ogerpon chirped happily, overflowing with joy.

"Then come along."

Dylan took out a Luxury Ball.

Ogerpon reached out with its arm, touching the ball willingly. In a flash of red light, it was absorbed into the Luxury Ball, which clicked shut without even a single shake.

"I've caught Ogerpon."

Dylan held the Luxury Ball with a heartfelt smile.

"Oge~"

The ball opened again, and Ogerpon appeared beside Dylan, clinging to his leg and letting out soft, affectionate cries.

"Alright, Ogerpon, let's go check on that one."

Dylan rubbed Ogerpon's head and shifted his gaze to Pecharunt , who was still sobbing on the ground.

"It's a pitiful creature," Dylan said with a sigh. While he still believed Pecharunt 's current state was its own doing, there was a tinge of empathy in his voice.

Tap… tap…

Pecharunt lay flat on the ground, and as the sound of approaching footsteps reached its ears, it turned its head. The sight of Dylan and Ogerpon filled its view.

"Your past… there's nothing left of it now," Dylan said, crouching to meet Pecharunt 's gaze.

"Pecha…"

Tears welled up in Pecharunt 's eyes again. Hearing those words struck a sorrowful chord, making it want to cry even more.

Ogerpon looked at Pecharunt with pity, its emotions deeply conflicted.

"But the present and the future are yours to shape. If you're willing to change, you can come with me."

"Pecha?"

Pecharunt froze, staring blankly at Dylan.

"Yes. We can be your companions and friends, but you must change. You can't let your toxic sticky cakes harm anyone anymore. Can you do that? I'll have Ogerpon keep an eye on you."

"Oge~!"

Dylan spoke firmly, and Ogerpon nodded in agreement.

Pecharunt 's toxic sticky cakes were notorious for enhancing the eater's abilities and desires—even controlling them.

It was an incredibly dangerous ability. Legends said that Pecharunt once used its sticky cakes to control most of the villagers in Kitakami, even manipulating a Champion-level Trainer like Nemona.

The sticky mochi were like a drug, addictive and dangerous to all who consumed them.

"Pecha?"

Pecharunt 's raspy voice carried hope and surprise. Could it really have new companions and happiness?

"Of course, but the choice is yours to make."

"Pecha!"

"Then we'll be in your care from now on, Pecharunt ."

Dylan extended his hand with a smile. He wasn't afraid that Pecharunt might be faking its change of heart—Necrozma was watching carefully from behind.

If Pecharunt harbored any ill intentions, Necrozma could intervene immediately.

Moreover, Dylan, knowing Pecharunt 's story, understood its true nature. In some ways, Pecharunt and Ogerpon were very similar.

Ogerpon yearned for friends and happiness, while Pecharunt longed for care and love.

Fate, however, had intertwined them in tragedy. To fulfill the elderly couple's desires, Pecharunt had destroyed Ogerpon's happiness. Enraged, Ogerpon had, in turn, "taken away" Pecharunt 's "happiness."

But now, that would change.

"Pecha!"

Pecharunt floated up, drawing closer to Dylan, its raspy cries brimming with anticipation.

"From now on, we're all partners."

Green light shimmered from Dylan's hand as he gently patted Pecharunt 's small head, his voice soft and reassuring.

Watching this, Ogerpon tilted its head, its expression a mixture of curiosity and amusement.

"Pecha!" Pecharunt cried out in satisfaction.

"Dylan's captured two more legends~" Ilene said with a warm smile, while Lillie's eyes sparkled with joy for her brother.

Yes, everyone had a bright future ahead—except…

Dylan glanced at the defeated Okidogi, Munkidori, and Fezandipiti lying on the ground.

(End of Chapter)

# 186: Chapter 187: I’ll Challenge You as a Champion!

Dylan was quite conflicted about how to deal with the three troublemakers, Okidogi, Munkidori, and Fezandipiti.

Strictly speaking, these three were merely subordinates of Pecharunt in the Mask Incident centuries ago, playing supporting roles in his crimes.

Their theft of the masks back then was entirely under Pecharunt's orders. Obsessed with his poisonous mochi, they obeyed him blindly.

The mochi amplified their abilities—Okidogi gained strength, Munkidori gained wisdom, and Fezandipiti gained beauty. However, consuming too much of the toxic mochi altered not only their bodies but also corrupted their desires and personalities.

After being resurrected from the game, they even ganged up to bully the maskless Ogerpon, showcasing their lingering toxicity.

Of course, this revenge wasn't entirely surprising since Ogerpon had originally killed them.

"Brother, what's wrong?"

Ilene and Lillie approached.

"I'm thinking about what to do with these three," Dylan mused, pondering whether to seal them here again.

"Booo!"

"Take them away and keep them under supervision?" Dylan heard Ogerpon's suggestion.

"Booo!" Ogerpon nodded. It didn't want anyone else—human or Pokémon—falling victim to Okidogi and its companions in the future.

Ogerpon was determined to keep a close eye on Pecharunt and his cohorts!

"If that's your decision, then so be it," Dylan agreed, reasoning that Ogerpon was more than capable of monitoring them.

Dylan began capturing Okidogi, Munkidori, and Fezandipiti one by one. Though it took some effort, all were successfully caught.

It cost him over 50 Ultra Balls. While they may have seemed weak in front of Ogerpon, they were still legendary Pokémon.

"Perfect finish."

Dylan stored the Ultra Balls containing the three Pokémon and glanced at Ogerpon and Pecharunt with a satisfied smile.

This northern journey had been immensely fruitful, a resounding success.

"Congratulations, Dylan!"

"You're amazing, Brother!" Ilene and Lillie cheered.

"This afternoon, let's head to the Paldea region," Dylan said with a smile, looking at the two girls.

Having explored all six northern sights, there was nothing left to see. Missing the "Masked Festival" was a pity, but not a big deal.

"Alright!"

After lunch, as they prepared to head to the airport, Dylan once again encountered Lacey and her companions.

"Gym Leader Dylan!" Lacey's eyes lit up as she ran over excitedly.

"Miss Lacey, Miss Carmine, and Mr. Kieran."

Dylan greeted them with a smile.

"We must be destined to meet! But are you leaving already?" Kieran asked, visibly surprised.

"Yes, we're about to depart," Dylan said, noticing that Kieran's mental state had significantly improved, with his hair neatly tied back.

"You're not staying to search for Ogerpon?"

Kieran looked startled, recalling Dylan's words from the previous day about competing with him to find it.

"I've already captured it, Mr. Kieran. It's my partner now."

Dylan gestured to the Ogerpon behind him. At the moment, it was engrossed in a drama on its phone, tears streaming down its face.

Ogerpon had a penchant for melodramatic shows. It had binged Hero vs. the Three-Headed Dragon King all night and was now absorbed in The Wandering Life of Princess Venusaur.

"W-What?"

The trio was stunned, especially Kieran, who stared at the tearful, phone-holding Ogerpon in disbelief.

This was the "terrifying ghost" he had always admired?

"Well…" Dylan rubbed his chin and glanced at Ogerpon, who was wiping its tears with tissues. "It does enjoy its dramas, and it's quite emotional, going through tissues fast."

"Truly worthy of you, Gym Leader Dylan!" Lacey exclaimed, unfazed. After all, Dylan was the trainer who had captured both Reshiram and Kyurem.

"That's incredible! Looks like you've lost, Kieran," Carmine remarked.

Kieran bit his lip, lowering his head in disappointment. He had planned to hunt for Ogerpon with enthusiasm, but it had already been caught.

"Don't be discouraged, Mr. Kieran. Even if you lost this time, don't you want to win next time?" Dylan patted the boy's shoulder with an encouraging smile.

"But…" Kieran hesitated, lacking confidence.

"Remember what I said yesterday? Confidence is an essential trait for any strong trainer."

Dylan's words stirred something in Kieran, who looked up at him in a daze.

"I believe in your potential, Mr. Kieran. Aren't you a student at the Blueberry Academy? How about setting a goal for yourself?"

"I have high hopes for you."

Dylan's bright, infectious smile lit a spark in Kieran.

"Climb to the top of the Blueberry Academy by year's end and come to Kanto to challenge my Rota Gym. Show me your progress—what do you say?"

"This won't be an easy task, but I trust you can do it. This is the bond between men. You have the potential to become a top-tier trainer."

Dylan's words carried deep conviction, making Kieran's golden eyes shimmer with emotion.

This unprecedented recognition left Kieran's heart aching with determination.

"When the time comes, I'll battle you with Ogerpon. That's our promise, Mr. Kieran. Can you do it?"

"Yes, Mr. Dylan! I'll make it happen!" Kieran clenched his fists, determination unwavering.

Kieran's steadfast response surprised Carmine, who stared at her usually introverted, scholarly brother.

Lacey's eyes glimmered with curiosity. Could Kieran really possess such potential?

With only a few months left until year-end, was it possible for Kieran to rise to the top of Blueberry Academy?

"You've got great spirit. I'll be waiting for you in Kanto, Mr. Kieran," Dylan said with a smile.

Kieran's talent was undeniable. As a rival in the DLC storyline, he was destined to be a powerful Champion-level trainer in the future.

"Please wait for me! I'll definitely make it!"

Kieran's determined tone resonated.

"Until then, feel free to drop by the Rota Gym for some tea," Dylan said, bidding farewell to Lacey and Carmine before leaving with Ilene, Lillie, and the drama-addicted Ogerpon.

"Kieran, you were too impulsive. What if you can't…"

Watching Dylan's group leave, Carmine began to chastise her brother.

"Reaching the pinnacle of Blueberry Academy before the end of the year? That's impossible!"

"I can do it, Sis."

Kieran calmly interrupted his sister's words.

Carmine was stunned.

"I will do it! I will become the strongest in Blueberry Academy!"

Kieran stared in the direction where Dylan had disappeared, his eyes shining brightly and resolutely. He now had a goal, even if it was one that had been set for him by someone else.

Dylan, the Gym Leader! I will become the champion of Blueberry Academy! Then, I will go to Kanto to challenge you!!

Meanwhile.

"It seems like you hold that boy in high regard, Dylan."

Ilene glanced curiously at Dylan. His attitude toward Kieran was unique, something she could easily discern.

"Is he exceptionally strong?"

"It's not about strength," Dylan chuckled and shook his head.

"Kieran is a genius, but his mindset has limited his growth. I simply gave him a reason to strive forward."

For someone like Kieran, who possesses talent but hasn't fully realized it, a clear goal and purpose can unleash astonishing potential.

"He can become a formidable challenger. And besides…"

Dylan was looking forward to Kieran's next challenge, though that wasn't the most critical factor.

"The Kanto region is preparing to establish Johto as an independent league. This is significant news," Dylan remarked.

"And for Johto to stand as its own region, it will need an Elite Four and a Champion."

"Brother, are you suggesting…?"

Lillie was startled. Was Dylan intending to groom Kieran into an Elite Four member or even the Champion of Johto?

Dylan smiled but said nothing.

In truth, Lillie had guessed wrong. While Dylan did have plans to support Kieran as an Elite Four member, it wasn't for Johto.

Kieran's talent as a trainer was exceptional, second only to those rare protagonists from the games. At the very least, Kieran's potential wouldn't fall short of someone like Diantha, a Champion-level trainer.

As for Lance, his heart harbored growing darkness. Although Vernon wanted Dylan to advise Lance, Dylan doubted that words alone would "wake" him.

It might be better to let Lance's inner "evil" fully erupt, and then deal him a solid defeat to teach him the meaning of reality. Only then would Lance naturally return to normal.

If Lance were to succumb to the darkness, the current Kanto Elite Four would be on the verge of collapse. Lorelei would undoubtedly leave with Lance, while Agatha, with her hidden agenda, might also join them. Bruno, who was dependent on the other three, would have no choice but to follow.

In such a scenario, not only would Johto's Elite Four and Champion positions need to be filled, but Kanto would also be left without an Elite Four, forcing the Kanto League to rebuild it from scratch.

Kieran was Dylan's candidate to serve as one of Kanto's potential "backups."

Dylan had no particular interest in power, as he already possessed strength. However, placing "his people" in strategic positions would undoubtedly be advantageous in the long run, making everything more convenient and providing a sense of personal fulfillment.

As Dylan pondered, he realized he already had a surprising number of current and future Champion-level trainers under his guidance.

Particularly Dylan's apprentice Selene, who was destined to become a top-tier Champion-level trainer.

"Once I return to Kanto, I'll find some time to visit Viridian Forest and bring that child out to serve as a Gym apprentice. Selene won't need much longer before she embarks on her own journey."

Dylan mused. That child, like himself, Lance, and Giovanni, had been blessed by nature and possessed the rare and unique Viridian Force.

That child was also destined to be a top-tier Champion-level trainer in the future.

Perfect.

(End of Chapter)

# 187: Chapter 188: It Seemed a Little Crazy

Medali, Paldea Region.

Located in the western part of the Paldea Region, Medali is a charming settlement where houses are built like a labyrinth of blocks, creating a visually intricate and complex layout.

Despite being a small town, Medali is renowned for its culinary culture. The local eateries are so popular that lines of eager diners resemble those in bustling metropolises.

Dylan and his companions arrived in Medali around 5 p.m. Naturally, their destination was the town's most famous restaurant, Debby Diner.

However, seeing the long queue at the entrance, Dylan found himself in silent contemplation.

And then...

With the power of money paving the way, Dylan and his group secured their place inside the restaurant to enjoy their meal.

"Hmm~ These grilled rice balls smell amazing," Ilene commented after taking a bite.

"Oge~ Oge!"Beside her, the mischievous Ogerpon munched away, its face covered in grains of rice, nodding enthusiastically in agreement.

"Well, it's famous for a reason, but…"Dylan's gaze shifted toward a middle-aged man in a black suit seated at a nearby table, also enjoying a grilled rice ball.

"What's the matter, Brother? Hmm? He looks really tired…"

Lillie noticed Dylan's gaze and followed it, her curiosity piqued by the exhausted appearance of the man. His sallow complexion, heavy dark circles, and lifeless eyes made it seem as though he could fall asleep—or collapse—at any moment.

Has he been pulling all-nighters? He looks like he's on the brink of burnout.

"Brother, do you know him?"

"No, not really."

"That's odd…" Lillie's gaze lingered on the weary man, filled with curiosity.

"It's not that strange, actually. Corporate life does that to people," Dylan shrugged.In truth, he did recognize the man—it was Medali's Gym Leader, Larry.

Larry specialized in Normal-type Pokémon, believing that their ordinary nature reflected the true essence of life.

But Larry had another identity: he was one of the Paldea Elite Four. As an Elite Four member, Larry adopted a Flying-type team when facing challengers, often telling them, "If you have any complaints, take them up with the League Head."

Interestingly, both of these roles—Gym Leader and Elite Four member—were side gigs for Larry. His primary job was as an employee in the Paldea League's Operations Department, where his performance was less than stellar.

In other words, Larry was juggling three jobs at once, which explained his perpetually fatigued demeanor.

"Such dedication…"

Dylan couldn't help but admire this fellow professional from another region. Larry truly lived up to his reputation as the Pokémon world's most famous overworked salaryman.

To what extent was Larry overworked? In the games, when the protagonist challenges the Paldea League, Larry is the third Elite Four member to battle. After giving it his all, he's so exhausted that he lacks the energy to even announce the next opponent.

Moreover, during the climactic showdown between the protagonist and Champion Nemona in Mesagoza, while others gather to watch the duel, Larry simply grabs his briefcase and calmly leaves to continue his work.

Ah, the life of a corporate slave.

"This is why taking vacations is essential," Dylan reaffirmed his stance.Working year-round without breaks? Unthinkable! Absolutely unthinkable!

Encountering Larry at the restaurant was a minor incident. Dylan didn't approach him for a conversation—after all, the man had enough on his plate without additional distractions.

After a night's rest in Medali, Dylan and his companions set off the next day toward the Great Paldean Crater.

"Hmm, this map is so strange!"

Lillie studied Paldea's map with a puzzled expression.

"Why is there a giant basin in the middle?"

She pointed to the central part of the Paldea map. The region was divided into four areas—east, west, south, and north—each with its own distinct climate.

This central peculiarity was one of Paldea's defining features.

"It's called the Great Paldean Crater. It's incredibly mysterious. But I bet our all-knowing Dylan knows all about it, right?" Ilene teased, glancing playfully at Dylan.

"Do you know, Brother?" Lillie asked, her wide eyes filled with curiosity.

"I know a bit," Dylan replied with a soft smile.

Hearing this, both Ilene and Lillie leaned in expectantly, waiting for him to begin.

"The origins of the Great Paldean Crater remain a mystery to this day, but it's believed to have formed over a million years ago.

"About two thousand years ago, the Paldean Empire began ruling the region. Legend has it that the emperor, a tyrannical figure, was obsessed with the myth of treasures hidden within the crater. He sent numerous expeditions into the depths of the crater."

"However, the terrain inside was too complex, and strange rumors deterred exploration. No one reached the bottom—until about two hundred years ago, when an explorer named Heath led the Area Zero Expedition Team to the crater's base. From then on, the area was called Area Zero. Heath documented his experiences in the Scarlet Book and the Violet Book."

"Wow!" Lillie exclaimed in awe.

Even a powerful emperor failed to explore the crater, yet one expedition team managed to succeed!

"Yes, but Heath's writings were considered too fantastical, so most people dismissed them as mere folklore.

"Then, about 140 years ago, Terastallized Pokémon were discovered in Area Zero. While these Pokémon were brought out of the crater, their bodies lost their radiant glow. For a long time, Terastallization remained shrouded in mystery.

"Eighty-seven years ago, observation stations were established in Area Zero, gradually unraveling its secrets. However, access remains restricted to ordinary people.

"Finally, ten years ago, Professors Sada and Turo, two brilliant scientists, uncovered the mechanism of Terastallization and invented the Tera Orb, making the technology accessible to the League and academies."

As Dylan finished his explanation, the group approached the massive basin visible in the distance.

"So, Dylan, do you think Heath's accounts are true?" Ilene asked curiously.

While Dylan shared key information, the true mysteries of the crater remained unsolved. What about the legendary treasure?

"What do you think?" Dylan replied with a teasing smile.

Many of Heath's descriptions—such as the Paradox Pokémon—were indeed real. However, a significant paradox lay within the story itself.

Heath's exploration occurred 200 years ago, yet Paradox Pokémon weren't native to this era. Anyone who played Scarlet/Violet or its DLCs knew the truth: Professors Sada and Turo, inspired by Heath's books, built a time machine that brought ancient and futuristic Pokémon into the present.

And thus, the paradox: the time machine created by the professors was inspired by books detailing creatures that supposedly shouldn't have existed in Heath's time.

So, two hundred years ago, how did Haizhe encounter those Paradox Pokémon and write the Scarlet Book and Violet Book?

Dylan actually had a theory, but now wasn't the time to dwell on it.

"Brother, someone has been attacked by a wild Pokémon," Lillie hurriedly pointed to the distance on the side as she spoke to Dylan.

Dylan looked in the indicated direction, raising an eyebrow, a glimmer of interest flashing in his eyes.

At that moment, up ahead—

"Darn it, how is this Pokémon so strong!"

A young woman named Iono looked at her fainted Luxray, realizing the dire situation she was in, especially with that ferocious Pokémon approaching her step by step.

The savage aura and those wild, untamed eyes sent chills down her spine.

It was a Pokémon with many similar features to Donphan—two long, curved tusks like a mammoth's, feral golden eyes full of malice, and a purple trunk adorned with upright red scales.

Wild! Savage!

It was the Great Tusk, a Paradox Pokémon thought to resemble an ancient form of Donphan!

Seeing the dangerously menacing Great Tusk closing in, Iono's delicate face turned pale. Initially, she had whimsically decided to livestream the Paldea Crater from afar.

But to her misfortune, she had barely arrived before running into this ruthless and unreasonable Great Tusk.

Faced with a wild Pokémon that attacked without provocation, Iono had naturally chosen to fight back. After all, she was a Gym Leader.

However, she hadn't anticipated the sheer strength of this wild Pokémon!

Mismagius, Electrode, Bellibolt… all her main Pokémon had been defeated one after another by this wild beast.

How… how could it be this strong?!

"Run, Iono, run!!"

"What the heck, why is there such a dangerous wild Pokémon in this area?"

"Hang on, Iono! I'm on my way to help you—just hold on!"

"Don't just stand there, Iono!"

"What's wrong with you people? Turning your back and running from a wild Pokémon like this is just asking to be killed!"

Iono's flying livestream equipment was still running, and her livestream chat was flooded with frantic comments.

But Iono had no attention to spare for the chat. Her mind was racing; she had no intention of dying here!

She still had so much life ahead of her!

As for running? How could a human possibly outrun a Pokémon? Iono regretted not bringing her Kilowattrel—it could've at least gotten her out of danger safely.

A low, feral growl interrupted her frantic thoughts. She looked ahead in terror as the Great Tusk began charging toward her. Iono closed her eyes in fear.

Oh no… So this is how it ends for me?

But—

Boom!

A deafening explosion rang out, and a powerful shockwave swept through the area, causing Iono's heavy twin ponytails to flutter. Instinctively, she shielded her face with her hands.

A pained growl echoed, then fell silent.

Iono opened her eyes and looked ahead, only to see the once-ferocious wild Pokémon lying defeated in a crater. Standing at the edge of the crater was a humanoid Pokémon wearing a mask.

"Bo-ne~"

The Ogerpon tilted its head, glanced at Iono, pulled out a smartphone, tapped on it to resume playback, and casually walked toward Dylan's direction.

'The poor Ivysaur princess was once again ambushed by the evil Arbok, plunging her back into crisis…'

Iono stared blankly at Ogerpon's retreating figure. Did she hear it right? Was that narration coming from its phone just now??

This Pokémon, which could easily take down such a ferocious wild Pokémon, was watching a drama on its phone?

Was she out of touch? Had Pokémon become this trendy these days???

A Pokémon that pulls out a phone to watch TV after a battle??!

Iono was sure she hadn't gone mad, but the world… it seemed a little crazy.

(End of chapter)

---

---

# 188: Chapter 189: What a Luck!

"Ultra Ball, go!"

Dylan approached and, after contemplating for a moment, tossed an Ultra Ball at the unconscious Great Tusk.

The instant the Ultra Ball made contact, the Great Tusk was enveloped in a red light and absorbed into the ball.

Beep... Beep... Click!

The Ultra Ball clicked into place, signaling a successful capture.

Captured!

Iono widened his eyes in shock. The Pokémon that nearly sent him to the afterlife had been subdued.

"Is he the trainer of that green Pokémon?"

Iono looked at the boy in black standing nearby. With short, messy black hair and a handsome, gentle face, the boy's expression was calm and friendly. He exuded an air of approachability.

His clean-cut appearance was complemented by a black casual shirt and black jeans, with a well-proportioned physique—a strikingly handsome young man.

"He's amazing!"

A surprised female voice snapped Iono from his thoughts.

Turning around, he saw a girl in an Orange Academy uniform.

"Nemona, what are you doing here?"

"I saw you were in danger and happened to be nearby, so I came to help. But it seems you didn't need me."

Nemona curiously studied Dylan. She had occasionally watched Iono's livestreams but hadn't expected today's broadcast to almost turn into an emergency.

"That was easier than I expected."

Dylan picked up the Ultra Ball and opened it again after some thought. With a flash of white light, Great Tusk reappeared before him.

"Great Tusk. Ground/Fighting Type. Paradox Pokémon.

Ability: Protosynthesis.

Moves: Giga Impact, Earthquake, Knock Off, Stomp, Brick Break, Rapid Spin, Sunny Day, Heavy Slam...

Description: Resembling creatures described in ancient supernatural magazines, this Pokémon is said to have survived since prehistoric times."

The Rotom Dex floated over and diligently introduced the Pokémon.

"Don't be so aggressive. The world is full of wonderful things, delicious food, and fun activities. Learn to enjoy life."

Dylan spoke to Great Tusk with a warm smile.

But Great Tusk's golden eyes remained wild and wary, staring at Dylan. Despite being captured, its primal instincts made it reluctant to accept him.

"It seems you're still hesitant. Very well, Ogy."

His Ogerpon paused its TV binge-watching and morphed its arm into a vine-covered mace, glaring at Great Tusk.

Startled, Great Tusk backed away several steps, its fierce gaze softening.

"See? I don't want to use force. Follow me, and your life will be far better than it is now." Dylan's tone was still gentle.

Great Tusk: "..."

"Looks like you've agreed. Don't worry; soon, you'll be glad you made the right choice."

Dylan pulled out a Max Potion and sprayed it on Great Tusk, calming its agitated spirit.

Paradox Pokémon are notorious for their ferocity, but Dylan believed any Pokémon could be won over with patience.

"You've been watching for a while now. Aren't you going to say anything?"

Dylan turned to Iono and Nemona, smiling.

"Hello, hello! I didn't want to interrupt while you were busy. Thanks so much for saving me!"

Iono's lively tone made it hard to tell she had just faced a life-threatening situation.

"You're really strong! I'm Nemona from Orange Academy," Nemona added, her eyes sparkling with excitement.

"Nice to meet you. I'm Dylan from Kanto's Rota. This is my sister Lillie, and my good friend Ilene."

Dylan introduced himself with elegance and politeness.

"And this is my Pokémon, Ogerpon. It's currently obsessed with watching dramas."

Dylan glanced at Ogerpon, who had already resumed its show, though he hoped the screen time wouldn't affect its eyesight.

"Mr. Dylan, your Ogerpon is amazing! I saw it take down Great Tusk in one hit during the livestream!" Nemona exclaimed, brimming with admiration.

"Thank you for the kind words," Dylan nodded humbly.

"I really can't thank you enough for saving me," Iono chimed in, her gratitude evident.

"No need to thank me. Travelers helping each other is only natural. Besides, as a fellow Gym Leader, it's only right to assist a colleague."

"You're a Gym Leader too?" Nemona's eyes lit up, her enthusiasm growing.

"Yes, he's a very famous Gym Leader from Kanto," Lillie said proudly.

"That's amazing, Dylan! You're so impressive!" Nemona exclaimed.

Seeing the livestream chat teasing her about the difference in their abilities, Iono puffed her cheeks in mock indignation.

"Oh no, I forgot the livestream was still on! I hope you don't mind, Dylan," Iono said apologetically.

"It's fine. I'm not doing anything shady, so there's nothing to hide," Dylan replied with a light laugh.

"That's great! By the way, Dylan, are you here in Paldea for sightseeing?" Iono asked curiously.

"Yes, as you said, being a Gym Leader can get dull, so I like exploring other regions when I have time."

"Sounds wonderful. I haven't been to other regions yet," Iono said wistfully.

Suddenly, Nemona's eyes sparkled with determination.

"Dylan, can I challenge you to a battle?" she asked eagerly.

Dylan raised an eyebrow. Nemona's passion for battles was palpable, and her eager gaze was hard to refuse.

"It's not impossible," Dylan said, smiling at her excitement.

Nemona had already defeated the Paldea Elite Four and Champion before. Despite that, her love for battling only grew stronger, making her one of the fiercest trainers Dylan had ever met.

"Dylan, Nemona is a very strong Trainer. She successfully challenged the Elite Four and the reigning Champion last year, claiming the top spot in Paldea," Iono introduced Nemona to Dylan.

While outsiders might not be aware, as Gym Leaders, they certainly knew this. Nemona had already reached Champion-level last year.

"Champion-level?"

Hearing this, both Ilene and Lillie looked at the clean-auraed Orange Academy student in astonishment.

Are Paldea's students this amazing?!

"Hehe~ It's not really something to boast about. I feel like Champion Geeta wasn't even using her full strength. I don't really like that," Nemona said with a scrunch of her nose.

Although she had become the new Champion, she did not take pride in it.

For her, battles were her passion, her love, and her ideal. She yearned for battles fought with all-out effort.

But when she challenged Champion Geeta last year, she clearly felt Geeta was holding back. And why?

What kind of top Champion uses a Glimmora with Toxic Debris as their trump card?

"That makes sense. Miss Nemona, did you bring your main team with you?" Dylan asked thoughtfully.

"Of course! I always have my Pokémon with me. Are you accepting my challenge, Gym Leader Dylan?" Nemona's eyes sparkled with excitement.

"Of course, but I hope Miss Nemona will give it her all. Otherwise, you won't be earning my badge," Dylan replied with a smile.

"I understand, Gym Leader Dylan. You can count on me!" Nemona's eyes shone brightly, her expression brimming with excitement.

Knowing I'm a Champion-level Trainer and still giving such a reminder... What does that mean?

It means Dylan is definitely a Champion-level Trainer too! And it makes sense, considering the incredible strength of his Pokémon, like that Overqwil.

"Big sister Nemona, you should be cautious. My brother's strength is formidable—it's no weaker than a regional Champion's," Lillie kindly warned Nemona.

She found this straightforward older sister quite likable.

"Hehe~ Thanks, little Lillie. I always give my all in every battle!" Nemona replied with a big grin.

"If we've decided to battle, then let's spin for the type matchup, big sister Nemona."

Lillie took out a small spinner from her spatial bag.

"What's this?" Nemona blinked in surprise.

Iono was curious too. It was just a common spinner, but the sections on it represented different Pokémon types.

"It's a special feature of the Rota Gym! Since my brother is skilled in multiple types, we use this spinner. Whatever type it lands on, my brother will use a team of that type to battle you," Lillie explained.

"Wow~ That's amazing!" Nemona's eyes lit up. Gym Leader Dylan was skilled in so many types?!

"Do I just press this?"

"That's right."

Nemona eagerly pressed the button, and the spinner began to whirl.

Soon, the needle stopped on the deep purple section.

"Poison?"

"Correct, you landed on Poison."

Dylan raised an eyebrow. How fortunate—he had just acquired several Poison-type Pokémon.

"Poison, huh? Not bad!" Nemona blinked with interest.

There was no need to search for a special battleground. This spacious area was perfect for a battle.

"Now, the Rota Gym Challenge begins! The challenger is big sister Nemona, and the chosen type is Poison."

"The battle rules are a 5 vs. 5 match. My brother will send out his Pokémon first and cannot switch. Big sister Nemona wins if she defeats all of my brother's Pokémon."

"Battle, start!"

Lillie raised a small flag and announced the rules.

"Wow~ Lillie, you totally have the vibe of a referee," Iono remarked in surprise.

"I've seen it so many times, I just picked it up naturally," Lillie replied, slightly shy. After all, she watched battles at the Rota Gym every day, so she had memorized the referee's lines.

Plus, being a referee wasn't particularly difficult.

On the battlefield.

"Whew~ I didn't expect to challenge a Gym Leader from another region so soon. What luck!"

Nemona patted her cheeks, her excitement growing as she stretched and warmed up.

"Come at me with everything you've got," Dylan said, his demeanor still calm and refined.

"Alright! Gym Leader Dylan, please guide me!"

Nemona quickly adjusted her mood. She raised her left arm to rest on her right arm, gripping her Poké Ball with her right hand and aiming it at Dylan, her aura sharp and commanding.

"I am Nemona!!"

Champion-level Nemona is challenging you!

(Chapter ends here.)

# 189: Chapter 190: Is It Okay?

"Do you think Nemona will be able to succeed in the challenge?"

Iono pondered aloud. Her live stream was still active, and with the consent of both Dylan and Nemona, she continued broadcasting. Neither of them objected.

"Poison was the first type Dylan mastered, and recently, he's captured a few Poison-type Pokémon," Ilene commented with a graceful and elegant smile.

"But he might not use them, right? Those Pokémon are probably part of his Golden Legend team... or maybe not," Lillie tilted her head but hesitated after glancing at the Pecharunt nearby.

While Pecharunt's performance in defeating Ogerpon and others showcased its strength, it also hinted that Ogerpon and the others weren't particularly strong. Normally, Dylan would categorize Legendary Pokémon under the Golden Legend team, but it wasn't guaranteed that he wouldn't include them in his type-based lineup.

At the battlefield:

Seeing Nemona brimming with energy, Dylan couldn't help but smile slightly.

"I'm quite looking forward to this match with you, Nemona," he said, elegantly adjusting his shirt collar.

"As this is your first challenge at Rota Gym, please allow me to formally introduce myself."

"My name is Dylan, the leader of Rota Gym."

He retrieved a Luxury Ball.

"For this battle, I'll be using a Poison-type team. I wonder if you've faced such Pokémon before?"

"The poison of Poison-types can be quite tricky to handle. Let's see how you deal with them."

Nemona's eyes sparkled with excitement.

"My first Pokémon will be this one."

Dylan stylishly tossed the Luxury Ball behind him.

"Arbok, get ready to battle!"

A flash of white light revealed a large, purple cobra. Its icy gaze fixed on Nemona as its forked tongue flicked rapidly.

"Arbok, huh? Then I'll choose you!"

Nemona considered briefly before confidently throwing her first Poké Ball.

"Armarouge!"

From the ball emerged a humanoid Pokémon clad in striking golden armor, standing tall and regal before Nemona.

It was the evolution of Charcadet, known as Armarouge.

Beep~ Armarouge, Fire and Psychic type, the Fire Warrior Pokémon.

Ability: Weak Armor

Signature moves: Armor Cannon, Expanding Force, Flamethrower, Calm Mind, Smokescreen, Psychic, Destiny Bond (inherited)...

Description: A form that evolved by donning armor of a warrior who accomplished great feats. It's a loyal Pokémon.

"Looks pretty cool," Lillie remarked, reading the Pokédex entry, though she thought her brother's Ceruledge looked even more impressive.

"Choosing a Psychic-type Armarouge against Poison-types is a smart move," Iono commented, blinking her large eyes. Psychic counters Poison, after all.

On the battlefield:

Arbok stared coldly at Armarouge, exuding an invisible aura of intimidation. Its Intimidate ability activated, lowering Armarouge's Attack stat by one stage.

"The challenger moves first," Dylan said graciously, gesturing with a gentlemanly demeanor.

"Then I won't hold back, Leader Dylan!"

Nemona wasted no time in seizing the opportunity.

"Armarouge, Expanding Force!"

As a Champion-level trainer, Nemona understood that battles weren't just about type matchups, but leveraging type advantages was crucial. It was one of the fundamentals of Pokémon battles.

Armarouge extended an arm as multicolored energy coalesced, unleashing a Psychic-type Expanding Force.

The colorful shockwave surged forward with speed. Though it would reach its full power in a Psychic Terrain, even without it, Expanding Force remained a potent move.

"Use Sucker Punch," Dylan commanded.

Arbok's eyes gleamed with a dark light as it slithered at high speed, deftly evading the Expanding Force and closing in on Armarouge.

Its swift Sucker Punch struck before Armarouge could react.

"Too fast!" Nemona frowned as Arbok's speed made countering impossible.

"Armarouge!"

It raised its arm to shield itself, trying to mitigate the damage.

Bang!

Arbok's attack struck with force, pushing Armarouge's arm into its chest. The impact caused it to cry out in pain, but its Weak Armor ability triggered, significantly boosting its speed despite the damage taken.

"And now, Crunch!"

Arbok opened its jaws, revealing dark, gleaming fangs—an Crunch attack!

"Psychic, stop it!" Nemona quickly ordered.

Fighting through the pain, Armarouge released a burst of colorful psychic energy, directly hitting Arbok's midsection and forcing it back.

"Impressive reaction and execution," Dylan observed, raising an eyebrow.

Nemona's Armarouge demonstrated its exceptional training, a testament to her skill as a trainer.

"Don't let up! Armarouge, Expanding Force again!"

Nemona's focused, fiery determination fully emerged as she commanded the next move.

"Dig," Dylan said with a faint smile.

Arbok quickly burrowed into the ground with astonishing speed.

Nemona paused, knowing Armarouge didn't have an effective counter against Dig.

"Use Calm Mind!"

Armarouge closed its eyes, a faint blue glow emanating from it as it raised its Special Attack and Special Defense.

Dylan responded calmly, "Toxic Spikes."

From the hole, Arbok emerged momentarily, releasing a dark purple orb into the air. The orb burst, scattering strange purple fluids onto the ground.

"Toxic Spikes?" Nemona frowned.

"Isn't that a control move?"

"That's rare," Iono remarked curiously.

"Is Toxic Spikes not common in Paldea?" Lillie asked.

"Is it popular in Kanto?" Iono countered.

In her experience as a gym leader, she'd never seen challengers use Toxic Spikes.

"In Kanto, Toxic Spikes is extremely popular," Lillie said with innocent honesty.

The move was controversial but widely used, offering damage, control, and persistence all in one.

"Before Dylan popularized it, Toxic Spikes wasn't well-known," Ilene added, smiling.

On the battlefield:

"Setting up Toxic Spikes instead of stopping Calm Mind... interesting choice," Nemona muttered.

Nemona gazed at the Armarouge, now finished with its meditation. Toxic Spikes—a move rarely seen in Paldea—wasn't commonly used.

But she vaguely remembered its effects: Toxic Spikes would poison opponents upon entry, causing ongoing damage.

Still, it wasn't a big issue. A bit of damage was negligible. After its meditation, her Armarouge's special attack had increased again. All she needed was to take down the opposing Arbok first.

"Armarouge, Psychic!" Nemona initiated her attack.

A brilliant blue glow lit up in Armarouge's eyes as a colorful wave of psychic energy surged toward Arbok.

"Sucker Punch," Dylan responded with a calm smile.

Arbok's eyes flashed with dark energy as it slithered rapidly across the ground toward Armarouge.

"That won't work on me a second time!"

Nemona's confident smile radiated energy.

"Armarouge, Psychic again!"

Armarouge instinctively stepped back to unleash another psychic control move, but that very step triggered a sinister purple glow from the ground. Toxic Spikes' poison!

The unexpected toxin engulfed Armarouge, making it wince and hunch over in pain, its body now shrouded in an eerie purple aura.

"Armarouge!" Nemona's pupils contracted in shock.

Armarouge gritted its teeth, fighting through the pain to stand upright, but Arbok was already upon it.

BAM!

The Sucker Punch struck Armarouge squarely in the chest, sending it flying backward with a pained cry.

This is bad! Nemona's heart sank.

"Armarouge, Protect!"

Nemona's battle instincts were sharp; she anticipated Dylan's follow-up.

"Earthquake," Dylan commanded.

Arbok raised its tail and slammed it to the ground, unleashing a powerful Ground-type Earthquake.

RUMBLE!

The battlefield trembled violently as the shockwave surged forward.

Armarouge, sprawled on the ground, endured its agony to conjure a green protective barrier, blocking the oncoming seismic wave.

"Great read, Nemona," Dylan praised, genuinely impressed. Her battle awareness was exceptional.

"But your Armarouge is already hanging by a thread," Dylan remarked calmly.

"The battle's not over yet, Gym Leader Dylan," Nemona retorted with a broad, excited grin.

This was the kind of opponent she lived for—strong, relentless adversaries!

"Oh? I beg to differ," Dylan replied with a faint smile.

"Arbok, Earthquake."

Arbok coldly locked its gaze on Armarouge before slamming its tail down again, unleashing another Earthquake.

"Armarouge, dodge!" Armarouge leapt into the air immediately.

"Dig," Dylan countered.

Arbok burrowed into the ground just as the Earthquake subsided. Armarouge, unable to fly, began to descend.

The moment it landed, the sinister purple toxin struck again, seeping into Armarouge from the Toxic Spikes. Poison continued to erode its stamina.

This Toxic Spikes strategy was more troublesome than Nemona had expected—it could trigger multiple times!

Just then, the ground beneath Armarouge erupted. The massive, venomous cobra emerged, its body infused with Ground-type energy, crashing into Armarouge.

"Destiny Bond!"

"Hmm?" Dylan raised an eyebrow. So this Armarouge had inherited Destiny Bond.

Armarouge's eyes flashed with ghostly light just as Arbok struck. Armarouge was hurled backward, collapsing unconscious.

At that moment, a dense gray mist emerged from Armarouge's fallen form, swiftly enveloping Arbok.

Destiny Bond—a Ghost-type move!

Arbok's vision darkened as it too slumped to the ground, unable to battle.

"Wow! That was an incredible Destiny Bond from Nemona!" Iono exclaimed, her eyes lighting up. But her attention quickly shifted to Dylan, whose calm expression didn't change. "Huh? Dylan doesn't seem surprised?"

"Of course not," Erin chimed in with a smile. "Dylan's a Ghost-type specialist. He's well-versed in moves like Destiny Bond."

"Arbok and Armarouge are both unable to battle," Lillie declared. As the referee, her judgment was impeccable.

"You're amazing, Gym Leader Dylan!"

Recalling her fainted Armarouge, Nemona's fiery gaze burned brighter. She hadn't felt this overwhelmed in battle for a long time.

"You're strong too, Miss Nemona. Few trainers can take down my main Pokémon," Dylan replied with a smile.

"But the warm-up is over. Now, let's see how you handle this."

Dylan retrieved another Ultra Ball and tossed it.

"Your next opponent is this."

"Okidogi, get ready for battle!"

With a low growl, a strong, black-and-green Pokémon adorned with a purple poison chain around its neck landed before Dylan. It raised an arm in a claw-like pose, flashing a menacing grin at Nemona.

One of the Loyal Three—Okidogi!

Though it was a Legendary Pokémon, the Loyal Three weren't exceptionally powerful. Its level hovered around 70, making it a fitting challenge for Nemona.

"Big brother is using Okidogi and the others after all," Lillie commented, her lovely eyes glinting. Beside her, an intrigued Furret glanced at Okidogi before returning to its drama.

"This Pokémon is called Okidogi? What's special about it?" Iono asked curiously. She didn't recognize the Pokémon.

"Well… Okidogi has a nickname," Lillie explained after a moment's thought. "It's one of the legendary Loyal Three."

"A Legendary Pokémon? Is it even fair for Dylan to use a Legendary against challengers?!" Iono gasped, stunned. A battle-hardened Gym Leader using a Legendary Pokémon—wasn't that overkill?

(To Be Continued)
