# 190: Chapter 191: My First Defeated

"Okidogi?"

Nemona clearly didn't recognize Okidogi but looked it up and down with great curiosity.

"Okidogi is a Poison and Fighting-type Pokémon."

Dylan introduced it gently. He didn't mind revealing this bit of information since it was a gym battle after all.

"Okidogi is far stronger than Arbok, so you should be careful, Miss Nemona."

Dylan reminded her. While Arbok was one of his "veterans," its level wasn't top-tier.

There was no helping it—managing the growth of his team while progressing through various scenarios in the simulation meant it was impossible to train everyone equally.

Even so, Dylan discovered that he had a decent talent for being a trainer. Even without the growth boost from the simulation, his focused training sessions during the day had notably increased his Pokémon's strength.

"Four times weakness to Psychic, huh."

Nemona pondered for a moment. Unfortunately, her Armarouge, which had Psychic moves, had already been defeated. It was her only Psychic-type Pokémon.

Additionally, the Toxic Spikes on the field were a serious problem. They needed to be cleared as soon as possible, or the continuous activation of Toxic Spikes would have a significant impact on the battle.

"Flygon!"

After some thought, Nemona threw out her second Poké Ball.

A flash of white light revealed a green, dragon-like Pokémon hovering in front of Nemona.

It had two antennae on its head, green wings with red edges, and a pair of semi-spherical red structures shielding its eyes.

It was Flygon.

'Beep—Flygon, Ground/Dragon-type, the Mystic Pokémon.

Ability: Levitate.

Moves: Dragon Claw, Boomburst, Dragon Rush, Earthquake, Dragon Dance, Superpower, Defog, Dragon Tail…

Description: Known as the Mystic Pokémon, Flygon kicks up sandstorms when it flaps its wings, always surrounded by swirling sands while in flight.'

This Flygon looked quite elegant.

"As expected, it knows Defog." Iono smiled knowingly as she looked at the Pokédex.

"Toxic Spikes are indeed troublesome. When triggered, the poison damage is devastating," Iono agreed. She now understood why Lillie had asked earlier about dealing with hazards.

Even though Toxic Spikes had only been triggered twice in the previous battle with Armarouge, it had already proven to be a game-changer.

Toxic Spikes were incredibly disruptive during critical moments in battle.

"Flygon, use Defog!" Nemona predictably chose to clear the field first.

"Snarl," Dylan said with a light chuckle.

Flygon flapped its wings furiously, unleashing a strong wind tinged with white light that swept across the battlefield.

Defog!

The purple fluid on the ground was blown away, and the Toxic Spikes were completely cleared.

At the same time, however, Okidogi let out a menacing growl, sending ripples of dark sound waves toward the airborne Flygon.

Snarl – a Dark-type move!

It's worth noting that as Okidogi used Snarl, the deep purple chain around its neck emitted a violet glow, and the sound waves were laced with specks of poisonous light.

The sound waves hit Flygon's body. Flygon winced slightly, closing its eyes in discomfort. Then...

Nothing much happened.

Okidogi's Special Attack stat was low, and Snarl wasn't a STAB (Same-Type Attack Bonus) move. The damage was minimal.

Still, the secondary effect of Snarl successfully activated, and the white aura surrounding Flygon dispersed, lowering its Special Attack.

Typically, Flygon was known for its physical attacks, though it could occasionally be a mixed attacker. Purely Special Attack-focused Flygons were rare.

"That's it?"

Nemona was baffled. The pitiful power of Snarl made her wonder if Dylan was using a newly trained Pokémon to battle her.

Otherwise, this didn't make sense.

"Is this really a Legendary Pokémon?" Iono, watching from the sidelines, was equally stunned.

In her mind, Legendary Pokémon were supposed to be overwhelmingly powerful.

"Okidogi isn't a Special Attack-based Pokémon," Lillie replied with an awkward but polite smile.

On the field—

"Flygon, Dragon Rush!" Nemona hesitated for a moment but decided to test the strength of this Okidogi.

Since Snarl had lowered Flygon's Special Attack, using long-range moves to test the waters wasn't ideal. Besides, her Flygon was trained for close combat.

Roar~!

The ferocious sound of a dragon's cry echoed in her ears. A dark, dragon-shaped aura enveloped Flygon as it dove downward.

Dragon Rush!

Flygon descended in a powerful dive, exuding an overwhelming killing intent as it targeted Okidogi.

"Focus Punch," Dylan said with a calm smile.

Okidogi let out a guttural growl, its right fist glowing white while the purple chain around its neck shimmered with violet light.

Focus Punch – a Fighting-type move!

Faced with the terrifying dragon descending upon it, Okidogi stood its ground and swung its glowing fist with full force.

Boom!

Focus Punch collided with Dragon Rush, sending shockwaves rippling across the battlefield. The ground beneath Okidogi cracked from the impact.

With a roar, Okidogi shattered the dragon-shaped aura of Dragon Rush. Its fist struck Flygon directly.

A heavy thud echoed as Flygon was sent flying backward, crashing hard onto the ground.

"Flygon!" Nemona exclaimed in shock. The sheer power of Okidogi was astonishing!

Flygon struggled to lift itself, but suddenly, a deep purple glow enveloped its body. Horrifying purple blotches appeared on its skin.

Flygon let out a pained cry as the glow continued to flicker—poison coursing through its veins.

"Poison? Toxic?! How is this possible?"

Nemona was stunned. It didn't make sense! Those blotches were clearly from Toxic, but Okidogi hadn't used any poison-type moves.

Focus Punch was a Fighting-type move—how could it inflict Toxic?

"It's entirely possible, Miss Nemona. You don't fully understand Okidogi," Dylan said with a smile, causing Nemona to frown.

"Okidogi's ability is Poison Chain. This ability gives its attacks a chance to inflict Toxic on its opponent."

"Snarl has a high chance of lowering Flygon's Special Attack. Naturally, you would choose to make Flygon engage in close combat—even if only to test things out."

"I originally planned to have Okidogi use Snarl again after this Focus Punch to see if it could inflict Toxic. But as it turns out, that's no longer necessary."

Dylan explained with a chuckle. Poison Chain was an exceptionally powerful ability, far superior to abilities like Poison Point.

Unlike Poison Point, Poison Chain applied to all attacking moves. While Okidogi's Special Attack was low and Snarl wasn't a STAB move, Snarl was easy to hit with and had a high chance of lowering Special Attack.

If lucky, Snarl could even inflict Toxic outright. If not, it still forced opponents into close combat with Okidogi, which was advantageous either way.

"How impressive, Gym Leader Dylan."

Nemona finally understood, but this revelation left her in a difficult position.

Flygon had already suffered a heavy blow from Focus Punch and was now badly poisoned. This situation was incredibly unfavorable.

"This Poison Chain ability is ridiculously overpowered!"

Iono exclaimed in amazement. Any attack having a chance to inflict Toxic made this ability exceptionally strong.

"It really is an impressive ability," Irene agreed. There was no denying the effectiveness of Poison Chain.

"Come back, Flygon."

Nemona decisively recalled Flygon.

"A wise choice."

Dylan nodded in approval.

Flygon could no longer fight effectively. Its close-combat strength was inferior to Okidogi, and the Toxic condition only made matters worse. Continuing to fight would be futile. It was better to withdraw and reevaluate the situation, keeping an eye out for opportunities to counter Okidogi with a better-suited Pokémon.

The ability to assess the situation and adapt was an essential skill for any trainer in competitive battles.

"Revavroom!"

Nemona quickly threw out her third Poké Ball.

A Pokémon resembling a metal vehicle landed heavily in front of her. Its body was a mix of brownish-purple and silver, with a single eye resembling a headlight at the front.

It was Revavroom, a Steel/Poison-type Pokémon.

'Beep—Revavroom, Steel/Poison-type, the Multi-Cylinder Pokémon.

Ability: Filter.

Moves: Gunk Shot, Rapid Spin, Poison Jab, Swagger, Iron Head, High Horsepower, Explosion (Egg Move)…

Description: Its eight cylinders ignite a gas mixture of toxins and rocky particles to generate energy.'

The Pokédex detailed Revavroom's stats and abilities.

"Using a Steel and Poison-type Revavroom to counter Okidogi's Poison Chain—an excellent choice."

Dylan observed the Pokémon. This was a standard Revavroom, not one modified by Team Star.

The Steel/Poison typing was extremely effective, boasting numerous resistances. However, it still had glaring weaknesses—Ground and Fire, especially Ground with a four-times weakness.

However, with the Filter ability, Ground-type damage is reduced to 3x, which is somewhat acceptable.

"Revavroom! Rapid Spin!"

Without hesitation, Nemona gave the command to charge. This Okidogi needed to be dealt with quickly.

"Focus Punch." Dylan chuckled lightly. In terms of direct combat power, Okidogi was incredibly strong as well.

Revavroom's wheels spun at high speed, transforming into a massive silver roller, and it rushed straight at Okidogi.

The Steel-type Spin Out! This was a signature move of the Revavroom line.

Okidogi flashed a savage grin, clenching its right fist, white energy flickering around it. It timed its attack perfectly, swinging a heavy punch toward the incoming Revavroom.

Focus Punch!

"Revavroom!" Nemona shouted sharply.

Revavroom's rolling body suddenly swerved, making an emergency turn and dodging Okidogi's Focus Punch by a hair.

Dylan raised an eyebrow.

In the next moment, Revavroom demonstrated exceptional driving skills, making another sharp turn and charging back toward Okidogi.

Okidogi snarled and turned around, crossing its arms just as the attack was about to land.

Bang!

The Rapid Spin crashed heavily into Okidogi, causing it to growl in pain.

However, Okidogi was still in good condition. While the Rapid Spin was decently powerful, the damage was manageable.

That said, using Rapid Spin caused Revavroom's speed to drop.

"Zen Headbutt!"

Using the recoil, Revavroom leaped backward, gathering multicolored energy around its body before charging at Okidogi once again.

"Okidogi!"

Okidogi immediately stepped back, trying to evade, but it was a moment too late. One of Revavroom's sharp edges scraped against it.

Even though it wasn't a direct hit, the Zen Headbutt still caused significant damage—Okidogi's quadruple weakness to Psychic-type moves was glaring.

The power of this Revavroom was extraordinary, and its level was clearly high.

Seeing this, Dylan was able to confirm it.

"High Horsepower!" Nemona immediately followed up to capitalize on the situation.

Revavroom was now surrounded by earthy energy as it charged toward Okidogi once more.

"You're getting a bit ahead of yourself, Miss Nemona. Okidogi, use Close Combat!"

Okidogi roared as it rushed forward to meet the charging Revavroom, throwing a flurry of punches at it.

The Fighting-type Close Combat—an all-out attack with no defense.

Okidogi's heavy punches rained down on Revavroom, forcibly halting its High Horsepower. With a final powerful blow, Okidogi shattered the energy surrounding Revavroom and sent it flying back, groaning in pain.

Although Revavroom wasn't weak to Fighting-types, the damage from Close Combat was still significant.

However, due to the nature of Close Combat, white light spread across Okidogi's body, reducing both its Defense and Special Defense by one stage.

"This strength…" Nemona frowned immediately upon witnessing the scene.

Okidogi's power was overwhelming. Her Revavroom was also known for its high physical attack, yet it was struggling.

"High Horsepower," Dylan ordered with a faint smile.

Okidogi charged at Revavroom with full momentum, cloaked in Ground-type energy.

"Revavroom, Stomping Tantrum!" Nemona countered quickly.

Revavroom raised its front body and slammed it down forcefully, creating a tangible shockwave that spread across the ground.

The Ground-type Buldldoze! While not as powerful as Earthquake, it was the best option since Revavroom couldn't learn Earthquake.

As Okidogi charged forward, its protective High Horsepower energy reduced the damage from Stomping Tantrum. In turn, Okidogi's own High Horsepower slammed into Revavroom, sending it flying.

Even with its Filter ability reducing the damage, High Horsepower was still a 3x super-effective hit, causing severe damage to Revavroom.

Revavroom was now barely holding on, hanging by a thread.

"One more High Horsepower."

Okidogi began another charge, the distance between it and Revavroom closing rapidly.

"Explosion!"

The moment was right, and Nemona immediately shouted to her weakened Revavroom.

Revavroom's body began to glow brightly, resembling a small sun.

"Protect," Dylan clicked his tongue.

Though he ordered Okidogi to use Protect, Dylan estimated it was unlikely to work—the two Pokémon were already too close.

Okidogi forcibly stopped its charge as a green Protect barrier formed in front of it.

Boom!

A massive explosion rocked the battlefield, unleashing a powerful shockwave that resembled a storm. Nemona had to duck and shield her face from the blast.

The Normal-type Explosion was an incredibly powerful move. True to its name, it caused a self-destructive blast that damaged all nearby Pokémon. Once used, the user would inevitably faint.

As the energy from the Explosion dissipated and the dust settled, the battlefield revealed a massive crater. At the bottom, Revavroom was clearly unconscious. Okidogi, however...

Huff... huff...

Okidogi was gasping heavily, struggling to crawl out of the crater. Its body was battered and bruised, its energy dangerously low.

"It's still standing?!"

Iono was shocked.

Nemona was equally surprised. How was this possible?

In reality, Okidogi had barely survived. Its Protect wasn't fully executed before the Explosion went off, meaning it took almost the full brunt of the blast at close range.

Thud.

Okidogi felt its body grow heavier and heavier. Finally, its vision went black, and it collapsed, fainting with swirls in its eyes.

"Okidogi and Revavroom are both unable to battle."

Lillie declared the result of the match—it was yet another double knockout.

After making the announcement, Lillie glanced at Nemona. This seemingly bright and upright trainer definitely had her own unique style.

She had taken out Dylan's Arbok with Destiny Bond and Okidogi with Explosion. There was definitely something to her approach.

"Well done, Miss Nemona," Dylan praised as he recalled Okidogi.

After all, Okidogi was a Legendary Pokémon with decent combat power. Though it was taken out by Explosion in a double knockout, it had been defeated nonetheless.

This might actually be the first time one of his Legendary Pokémon had been defeated. Huh.

"But it's nothing to be proud of," Nemona shook her head. She still preferred winning fair and square in a head-to-head battle.

"No, you should be proud. Any strategy that falls within the rules is fair game."

Dylan smiled as he spoke. After all, many of his own tactics were often criticized behind his back as "dirty," "despicable," or "shameless." Compared to those, Nemona's Explosion was nothing.

"Well then, Miss Nemona, here comes my third Pokémon. This one is far more challenging than the previous two."

Dylan flipped a luxury ball in his hand and threw it out.

"Pecharunt, prepare for battle!"

(End of Chapter)

# 191: Chapter 192: Steady Approach

"Pecharunt! Prepare for battle!"

Dylan threw out Pecharunt's Luxury Ball, and with a flash of white light, a Pokémon resembling a mochi appeared, floating in front of him.

"Pechapecha!"

A low, raspy cry echoed as the mochi-like shell split open, revealing the Pokémon's true form within.

"...This Pokémon's appearance is definitely a bit... peculiar," Iono remarked, trying to choose her words carefully, as it was someone else's Pokémon.

"While it doesn't align with mainstream aesthetics, Pecharunt is very strong," Lillie said with a blink of her big eyes, defending Dylan's choice.

"But isn't big brother being a bit too harsh? Nemona hasn't even pulled a golden one yet..."

Lillie couldn't help but stick out her tongue adorably. After Okidogi, now it was Pecharunt, making it hard not to wonder if Munkidori and Fezandipiti would follow.

"Actually, it's not too bad. I don't think Okidogi and the others are stronger than his Drapion team, especially since they were only captured yesterday," Ilene shook her head, doubting that Okidogi's group had better combat power than Dylan's well-trained Drapion team.

"Wait, what?!"

Iono froze for a moment after hearing this.

"They were just caught yesterday?"

The Okidogi from earlier was captured yesterday?

Iono was stunned. While Okidogi had ultimately been defeated, its performance had been exceptional!

Not only did it have the Toxic Chain ability, but its sheer strength was impressive—enough to outmatch Nemona's veteran Revavroom in direct combat.

If not for Revavroom's Explosion forcing a double knockout, Okidogi would have been hard to stop.

But to think it was only caught yesterday?

On the field.

"Another Pokémon I've never seen before."

Nemona observed this unfamiliar Pokémon with great interest.

"Pecharunt is a Poison/Ghost-type. You'll need to be careful, Miss Nemona. My Pecharunt is quite strong," Dylan said with a smile.

"Pechapecha"

Pecharunt let out a delighted hum, clearly enjoying the compliment.

"Tauros!"

After some thought, Nemona decided to send out her fourth Pokémon.

With a low roar, a robust black bull landed heavily in front of her.

The Pokémon resembled a bull, with thick black fur around its neck decorated with blue teardrop-shaped markings. It had three black tails and broad horns extending from its head, which had three blue spots on its forehead.

This was the Paldean Tauros (Aqua Breed).

'Beep—Tauros (Paldean Form, Aqua Breed), Fighting/Water-type, the Wild Bull Pokémon.

Ability: Intimidate.

Moves: Close Combat, Wave Crash, Raging Bull, Zen Headbutt, Aqua Jet, Thrash, Earthquake...

Description: This variant of Tauros swims by spraying water from its horns. Its high fat content helps it stay afloat. (Paldean Form, Aqua Breed)'

Another regional form of Tauros.

Ilene watched curiously. This Aqua Breed Tauros was visibly more imposing and intense compared to the Kantonian form.

"An Aqua Breed Tauros, huh," Dylan observed the black, water-type Tauros carefully.

Strictly speaking, Nemona had chosen a Pokémon with a type disadvantage for this battle.

After all, Pecharunt's Ghost typing rendered Fighting moves ineffective.

"Testing the waters, perhaps? Interesting," Dylan smiled faintly.

Flygon had fallen earlier because Nemona wasn't familiar with Okidogi's ability, so it made sense for her to use Aqua Breed Tauros now to probe Pecharunt's abilities.

Although Fighting moves wouldn't work, Tauros's Water typing still gave it options.

Aqua Breed Tauros let out a low growl, its Intimidate ability lowering Pecharunt's Attack by one stage.

"Tauros, Aqua Jet!"

Nemona initiated the attack without hesitation.

"Mean Look," Dylan responded calmly.

Water energy surrounded Tauros as it sprinted toward Pecharunt at high speed.

Aqua Jet – a priority Water-type move!

The speed boost from the move allowed Tauros to close the distance quickly.

Pecharunt's eyes glowed with dark energy as it locked its eerie gaze on the charging Tauros. A layer of black light spread across Tauros's shadow, anchoring it to the field.

With Mean Look, Tauros could no longer be recalled under normal circumstances.

But Aqua Jet had already connected. Tauros leapt into the air and slammed into Pecharunt.

Bang!

Pecharunt grunted as it was knocked back slightly, but that was the extent of the damage.

"High defense," Nemona noted. She realized that Pecharunt was built for endurance. And since it used Mean Look, it seemed Dylan wanted a decisive outcome.

Nemona grinned. In that case—bring it on!

"Raging Bull!" Nemona shouted, full of spirit.

Tauros roared, surrounded by a watery aura. Energy gathered around its horns, forming massive projections.

Raging Bull—a signature move of the Tauros line. The move's typing matched the Tauros's form, meaning this Aqua Breed's Raging Bull was Water-type.

The frenzied bull charged straight at Pecharunt.

"Withdraw."

Dylan's eyes narrowed slightly. Counter it? No need. Pecharunt's strength wasn't in attacking; its greatest asset was its incredible physical defense.

Pecharunt instantly withdrew into its mochi-like shell, its body glowing with a faint blue light—Withdraw, a Water-type move.

Boom!

The raging Tauros crashed into Pecharunt, relentlessly slamming it into the ground.

Crash!

The sheer impact of Aqua Breed Tauros's charge caused the ground to quake, a testament to its raw power.

As the dust settled, Tauros leapt back, trying to maintain its distance.

"Toxic Chain."

Suddenly, two deep purple chains shot out, whipping through the air and coiling tightly around Tauros's neck.

The chains wrapped several times, binding Tauros.

Aqua Breed Tauros roared in frustration, thrashing its neck to break free.

"Pechapecha"

Pecharunt's raspy voice echoed as it floated back up, its mochi shell opening slightly. The toxic chains had emerged from within its shell.

"Tauros, Wave Crash!" Nemona's eyes narrowed. This move was unfamiliar, but it looked ominous.

Water energy surged around Aqua Breed Tauros as waves began to ripple and condense.

Wave Crash – a powerful Water-type move!

"Pecharunt."

Pecharunt's eyes gleamed with purple light as the toxic chains glowed malevolently.

Boom!

The toxic chains suddenly detonated, causing Tauros to cry out in pain. But the explosion didn't interrupt Tauros's Wave Crash.

"It didn't get poisoned? How lucky," Dylan raised an eyebrow. Pecharunt's signature move, Toxic Chain, had a 50% chance of inflicting Toxic on its target—a fairly high probability.

But probability was probability. Sometimes luck just didn't cooperate.

"Let's try again. Toxic Chain."

"Pecha~pecha!"

Pecharunt retreated slightly, purple light emanating from its shell as two thick toxic chains shot out once more.

However, Tauros had already gained momentum. Its Wave Crash tore through the incoming chains, slamming into Pecharunt with a splash of water.

Pecharunt was knocked back again.

However, this was only enough to push Pecharunt back. Its physical defense was extremely high, and Wave Crash wasn't super effective against it. Furthermore, Pecharunt had already bolstered its defenses earlier with Defense Curl.

Although Pecharunt was knocked back, the snake-like Toxic Chains quickly wrapped around Aqua Breed Tauros with alarming speed.

Boom!

The Toxic Chains exploded once more on Tauros's body, causing it to let out a low roar of pain, enduring the sudden impact.

"Earthquake!"

Nemona seized the opportunity and issued a command to Aqua Breed Tauros. Pecharunt had been knocked to the ground by the previous collision.

A Ground-type Earthquake would be super effective against Pecharunt, and as long as it hit, it could deal significant damage.

Tauros began to raise its front legs, preparing to stomp down, but at that moment, a deep purple toxic glow erupted across its body. Dark purple blotches spread across its skin.

The violent and ominous glow of toxicity corroded Aqua Breed Tauros, causing it to whimper in pain. The unexpected agony interrupted its Earthquake motion.

"Poison again?" Nemona was shocked.

"Wait! No, something's wrong."

Nemona quickly noticed more issues. Aqua Breed Tauros's front legs lowered back to the ground, and its body began to sway unsteadily, its steps faltering.

This wasn't just a sign of poisoning—it was confusion. Tauros had not only been badly poisoned but was now confused as well!

"What's going on? Is it because of those Toxic Chains?" Nemona frowned deeply.

Getting poisoned was one thing, as many Poison-type moves had a chance of inflicting the status. But confusion? That was something else entirely.

"It's because of Pecharunt's ability, Toxic Puppeteer."

Noticing Nemona's confusion, Dylan smiled and explained.

There was no reason to hide it. With Nemona's sharp battle instincts, she would have guessed soon enough that it was either due to the Toxic Chains move or Pecharunt's ability.

Pecharunt, surrounded by a faint purple glow, floated back into the air, its eerie gaze fixed on the confused Aqua Breed Tauros.

"Pokémon that are poisoned by Pecharunt's moves also become confused."

Dylan summarized the explanation succinctly. Yes, this was Pecharunt's ability, Toxic Puppeteer. Pokémon poisoned by Pecharunt's moves would also fall into a confused state.

At this moment, Aqua Breed Tauros was completely disoriented, repeatedly smashing its head against the ground, looking like it was in the throes of a seizure.

"This..."

Nemona was stunned. How was she supposed to fight like this?

"This is bad! Tauros has been trapped by Mean Look and can't be recalled," Iono said worriedly, watching from the sidelines.

Confusion could typically be cured simply by recalling a Pokémon to its Poké Ball.

But with Tauros trapped by Mean Look, recall was impossible.

While in a state of confusion, Tauros would also struggle to hear Nemona's commands. This situation was quickly spiraling into a disaster.

On top of that, Tauros wasn't just confused—it was badly poisoned as well!

"There's no way out of this..." Iono clicked her tongue. Having both poison and confusion statuses at the same time—how could Tauros possibly fight like this?

"Nemona can only hope Tauros snaps out of confusion quickly. After all, Pecharunt's health has been chipped away a bit," Lillie said tactfully.

While confusion was an annoying status, its duration was random. Some Pokémon could snap out of it after a few self-inflicted attacks, while others might knock themselves out before recovering. It was entirely unpredictable.

"But the odds of that happening are so low," Iono said, tilting her head. She didn't think confusion was so easy to shake off.

"For now, that's the only hope. Dylan really lives up to his reputation as a master of Poison-types," Iono remarked, her large eyes sparkling. She was secretly rooting for Nemona to hold on somehow—she was cheering for her after all!

"If Tauros can recover quickly and deal more damage to Pecharunt, it might still work out."

Expecting Aqua Breed Tauros to defeat Pecharunt outright was unrealistic, but even dealing more damage could help Nemona prepare for her next Pokémon.

However...

"Pecharunt, Recover."

Dylan showed no intention of attacking.

Pecharunt's body was suddenly enveloped in a gentle white light, healing its injuries rapidly.

The Normal-type move Recover restored a large amount of health.

Nemona: "..."

Ilene: "..."

Lillie: (—)

Iono blinked and glanced at the still-flailing, seizure-like Aqua Breed Tauros. Well, now it couldn't even reduce Pecharunt's health anymore.

This was overkill. The amount of health restored by Recover was probably more than the damage Pecharunt had taken. Truly overkill.

"Mr. Dylan... he really is cautious..."

(End of Chapter)

# 192: Chapter 193: How Much?

"Tauros is unable to battle. Pecharunt wins."

Lillie announced the result of the match.

Indeed, Aqua Breed Tauros had fallen. With severe poisoning and the confusion caused by Toxic Puppeteer, the poor Tauros never stood a chance.

Although Nemona had tried to fight back, her efforts were futile. Under the relentless assault of Pecharunt's Venoshock and the corrosive effects of toxic poison, Aqua Breed Tauros succumbed before it could snap out of its confusion.

"You're so strong, Gym Leader Dylan, just ridiculously strong!"

Recalling her Tauros, Nemona couldn't help but marvel—she had lost count of how many times she had said this already.

The overwhelming pressure Dylan exerted made her heart race, a feeling she had never experienced before—not even when battling her champion, Geeta.

Was this how powerful the gym leaders of Kanto were?

"Miss Nemona, you're an outstanding trainer yourself. It's been a long time since I've needed to use this level of strength to face a challenger."

Dylan didn't hold back on his praise.

"However, we're still in the middle of a battle. Show me how you plan to deal with Pecharunt."

He gestured for Nemona to continue.

"Heh~ Then let me show you my trump card!"

Nemona grinned, her expression sharp and brimming with excitement.

"Dragapult!"

With a flash of white light, a ghostly dragon appeared before Nemona, its icy stare fixed coldly on Pecharunt.

It was Dragapult, the pseudo-legendary Pokémon from the Galar region!

"Dragapult, huh."

Dylan raised an eyebrow. Dragapult's Ghost typing gave it a type advantage over Pecharunt, though the reverse was also true. Clearly, Nemona planned to attack head-on.

This Dragapult's strength and level were likely very high. Nemona's skill as a trainer was as exceptional as always.

After all, she was a prodigy—only the protagonist could truly outshine her in Pokémon Scarlet/Violet.

"A Dragapult with the Clear Body ability… this isn't going to be easy to handle," Lillie remarked as she studied Dragapult's data. She wasn't unfamiliar with Dragapult.

After all, her brother Dylan had a very powerful Dragapult of his own, so she was well aware of how formidable they could be.

"Pecharunt isn't well-suited for direct confrontations. It's no match for Dragapult in a head-to-head fight," Ilene mused aloud.

Dylan had mentioned before that while Pecharunt was a mythical Pokémon, its raw offensive capabilities weren't particularly outstanding.

Ilene trusted Dylan's assessment, especially since the previous battle had already demonstrated this.

Dylan had mainly used defensive and counter-offensive strategies with Pecharunt against Tauros, avoiding direct exchanges of power.

"But if Dragapult gets poisoned, Nemona will be in trouble," Iono chimed in.

On the battlefield.

"Dragapult, use Phantom Force!"

Nemona made the first move.

A black rift opened behind Dragapult, and it slipped inside, vanishing from sight.

The Ghost-type move Phantom Force.

"Withdraw."

Dylan's eyes glimmered faintly as he gave his command.

Pecharunt's mochi-like shell snapped shut instantly, a faint blue energy glowing around it—a Withdraw, bolstering its defenses.

"Dragapult!"

The spatial rift reappeared behind Pecharunt, and Dragapult, cloaked in ghostly light, lunged out and slammed into its target.

Bang!

Pecharunt was knocked backward by the impact. Its mochi shell opened, and two dark purple toxic chains shot out of its core, whipping toward Dragapult.

"I was ready for that!" Nemona grinned.

Dragapult immediately retreated with blinding speed, evading the Toxic Chains with ease.

Dylan wasn't surprised—Dragapult's exceptional speed was its greatest strength.

"Shadow Ball!"

While retreating, Dragapult began charging a Shadow Ball in its mouth.

The Ghost-type projectile sped toward Pecharunt. In response, Pecharunt swung its Toxic Chains to intercept.

Boom!

The Shadow Ball collided with the chains, shattering them, but was neutralized in the process.

"Dragapult, Phantom Force again!"

Nemona decided to test her hypothesis. Pecharunt's defense was simply too high after two uses of Withdraw. Even with super-effective damage, the previous Phantom Force hadn't done much.

With its already impressive natural defenses, Pecharunt was proving to be a tough nut to crack.

Dragapult disappeared into the shadows once more.

"Stay alert," Dylan muttered, his gaze sharpening. He knew Nemona was now fully aware of Pecharunt's capabilities and would be watching out for its Toxic Chains.

Pecharunt carefully scanned its surroundings. Soon, a shadowy distortion appeared behind it, and Dragapult emerged, cloaked in ghostly energy.

"Foul Play!"

Pecharunt turned sharply, its body gleaming with dark energy.

The Dark-type move Foul Play—a technique that uses the opponent's Attack stat to calculate damage.

Bang!

Foul Play collided head-on with Phantom Force, dark energy spilling out from Dragapult and feeding into Pecharunt's attack.

The clash of forces created a shockwave, ultimately throwing both Pokémon backward.

"Toxic Chains!"

"Shadow Ball!"

Two thick, dark purple chains shot out from Pecharunt's mochi shell, aiming for Dragapult. Meanwhile, Dragapult launched a Shadow Ball at the incoming chains.

This time, however, Pecharunt played smarter. The first chain spun rapidly, forming a shield.

Boom!

The Shadow Ball exploded against the shield chain, shattering it, but the second chain shot through the smoke and debris, heading straight for Dragapult.

"Dragapult!" Nemona's expression shifted.

The second chain struck Dragapult directly, detonating in a burst of purple energy.

The toxic energy rippled across Dragapult's body, forcing it to grunt in pain.

The chain's damage wasn't significant—only residual energy had hit Dragapult. But everyone knew that Dylan's goal wasn't to deal damage.

"Will it be poisoned?"

Iono watched Dragapult nervously, her heart in her throat.

Dylan, on the other hand, seemed unconcerned. If it didn't work the first time, he could simply try again. With the high probability of Toxic Chains inflicting severe poison, Dragapult was bound to succumb sooner or later.

As everyone—Nemona, Iono, and the viewers watching the live stream—held their breath, a sinister purple glow began to flicker across Dragapult's sleek, blue-green body. Its expression twisted in pain as dark purple blotches appeared on its skin.

Nemona frowned deeply, and Iono gasped, her mouth agape.

Dragapult had been badly poisoned!

"It seems your luck isn't on your side this time, Miss Nemona," Dylan said with a raised brow.

The toxic corrosion set in quickly, but even worse, a wave of dizziness overwhelmed Dragapult, muddling its mind.

Under the effects of Pecharunt's Toxic Puppeteer ability, Dragapult had now become confused as well.

"Return, Dragapult!"

Nemona's reflexes were lightning-fast. She withdrew Dragapult immediately, leaving Dylan momentarily surprised—he had been about to command Pecharunt to use Mean Look.

Nemona's quick reaction was impressive.

"It seems you're not giving up, Miss Nemona," Dylan said with a small laugh.

"Battling is my favorite thing. No matter the situation, I'll never give up!"

Nemona flashed a bold, fiery smile.

"Even if the odds are against me, as long as I haven't lost, I still have a chance to win!"

Nemona's determination and fighting spirit were unwavering, and even Dylan couldn't help but admire her.

While he believed in analyzing the battlefield and making rational decisions, he also respected her relentless will to fight and her belief in finding a way to turn the tide in even the direst of situations.

"I'll give you my full effort," Dylan said with a smile. It was his way of showing respect.

However, the situation was dire for Nemona. She only had two Pokémon left, both of which were already poisoned.

And her opponent was still Pecharunt, a Pokémon with sky-high defenses and access to Recover. While recalling her Pokémon could reset the poison's effects, it would only delay the inevitable "countdown to defeat."

"Dragapult is unable to battle. Pecharunt wins."

"Miss Nemona has lost all five of her Pokémon, so the winner of this Rota Gym Challenge is my brother."

Lillie announced the final result of the battle.

"Huh? So in the end, I still couldn't defeat Pecharunt," Nemona muttered, patting her head in slight exasperation.

Despite losing, she didn't seem particularly upset.

Nemona loved Pokémon battles, and while she always pursued victory, she wasn't the type to dwell on failure. Whether it was losing to a powerful Gym Leader like Dylan or even to a rookie, Nemona didn't mind—in fact, she often found it thrilling.

What did disappoint her, however, was that even after Dragapult and Flygon had given their all, she still couldn't take down Pecharunt.

"You've already done amazingly, Miss Nemona! After all, you managed to defeat Okidogi, which is a Legendary Pokémon," Lillie said with a soft, polite smile, covering her mouth as she spoke.

"Huh?" Nemona froze for a moment, but then her eyes suddenly lit up.

"Okidogi is a Legendary Pokémon? Gym Leader Dylan, are you actually a trainer who's captured a Legendary Pokémon?!"

Trainers who successfully captured Legendary Pokémon were an incredibly rare "species"!

"Not just Okidogi—Pecharunt is a Mythical Pokémon as well. So, Miss Nemona, you're already incredibly strong," Dylan replied, gently patting Pecharunt's head. The Pokémon squinted its eyes happily, clearly enjoying the praise.

"!!" Nemona was both shocked and delighted.

"I figured as much. As the new Champion-level trainer from the Paldea region, I knew your strength must be remarkable, Miss Nemona. But you've still managed to surprise me."

"This was your first time facing my Poison-type team, and I even used Legendary Pokémon. Miss Nemona, I believe your strength fully qualifies you to receive this badge."

Dylan walked over to Nemona and opened an ornate box containing the Poison Rota Badge.

The exquisite green badge sparkled under the sunlight, its intricate design inlaid with deep purple gemstones shaped like dripping poison. Coming from a prestigious family, Nemona immediately recognized its high value.

Of course, what she loved most about the badge wasn't its price—it was its design!

"But I didn't actually defeat you, Gym Leader Dylan. Isn't it inappropriate for me to accept this badge?" Nemona asked, restraining herself despite her excitement.

"And this badge… Gym Leader Dylan, what kind of family are you from?"

Nemona looked at Dylan with a mix of doubt and curiosity. Based on her rough estimate, the badge's value was incredibly high—not just in terms of its symbolic significance but also in terms of its material cost.

"No, it's entirely appropriate. I fully recognize your skill, Miss Nemona, and this badge is proof of that."

Dylan smiled warmly as he spoke.

"Additionally, this is just my Poison-type badge. If you challenge me again in the future, Poison-types won't be part of my team. Similarly, if you want to earn other Rota badges, their difficulty will progressively increase."

"Wait, Gym Leader Dylan, your badges are divided by type?" Nemona was stunned.

Standing nearby, Iono was completely flabbergasted. Was this really how Gym Leaders worked? Why didn't she know about this?!

Iono fell into a deep contemplation. It seemed like her own position as a Gym Leader was completely different from Dylan's.

"Yep! Nemona, this Poison Rota Badge can only be earned when my brother uses a Poison-type team. If you had drawn a Dark-type challenge earlier, you would've received a Dark Rota Badge instead. There's also another way to get badges, but… well, I wouldn't recommend it."

Lillie giggled adorably.

"This is way too interesting!!" Nemona exclaimed, feeling like she was falling in love with the Rota Gym.

Why didn't Paldea have Gyms like this?!

"Nemona, stop looking at me like that—it's making me nervous. I'm just a streamer!" Iono stammered, feeling a strange pressure as Nemona's gaze landed on her. For the first time in her life, she felt a chill run down her spine.

I mean, who even runs a Gym like Dylan does?

What is he trying to do? Is he trying to make the rest of us regular Gym Leaders look bad?

Luckily for me, I'm just a streamer, and being a Gym Leader isn't all that important anyway.

Thinking this, Iono suddenly felt at ease. No problem here.

"Then I'll gladly accept this badge, Gym Leader Dylan! Heh~ I really love it!"

Nemona didn't hold back anymore. With her cheerful and straightforward personality, she had no issue accepting the badge once Dylan said she deserved it.

Taking the ornate box, she carefully removed the beautiful Poison Rota Badge and admired it. The more she looked at it, the more she loved it.

She loved it more than all the other badges she had ever earned—combined!

"This badge is so gorgeous. The materials must be extraordinary," Iono said as she leaned in to get a closer look, blinking curiously at the Rota Badge.

Gym badges were typically designed by the Gym Leaders themselves, with subsidies available from the Pokémon League for material costs. However, exceeding the budget would mean paying out of pocket.

"It's definitely special. Just the purple gemstone alone must be worth a fortune. Gym Leader Dylan, you still haven't told me about your family," Nemona said, blinking with curiosity. Coming from a wealthy family herself, she could easily tell the badge's value.

"It didn't cost that much," Dylan replied with a casual smile.

How much is "not much"?

Both Iono and Nemona tilted their heads in confusion.

"A newly crafted Rota Badge costs about 10 million Pokédollars, and the special Legendary Badges are around 30 million." Dylan's tone was calm and matter-of-fact.

"??????"

Iono's face was full of question marks, practically wanting to plaster them all over Dylan.

A 10-million-Pokédollar Gym badge?

Seriously, Dylan, are you trying to crush the rest of us regular Gym Leaders?

And you come from a family like this, yet you're a Gym Leader?

(End of Chapter)

# 193: Chapter 194: Area Zero

"This badge... over 10 million Pokédollars???"

Even Nemona, upon hearing Dylan's words, was shocked. She stared at the Poison Rota Badge in her hand.

She had already guessed that the badge wasn't cheap, but she didn't expect it to be worth over 10 million.

"Gym Leader Dylan, how are you even affording this?"

Nemona couldn't help but ask. With badges costing over 10 million each, wouldn't this bankrupt him? And what about those so-called "Legendary Badges"—what could those be worth?

"I have very strict standards for my challengers. Strictly speaking, Miss Nemona, this is only the third newly crafted Rota Badge I've ever officially awarded," Dylan replied with a smile.

He wanted to make the Rota Gym the most prestigious Gym in the world, in every sense of the word.

"Wow~!! So I'm actually really amazing, huh!"

Nemona was thrilled and fell even more in love with the badge.

"Alright then, I'll make it my mission to challenge all of Gym Leader Dylan's types in the future!"

Nemona's eyes burned with determination. One badge wasn't enough for her. With someone as strong as Dylan, she couldn't resist the urge to challenge him again and again.

"Whenever you have the chance, come visit the Rota Gym in Kanto," Dylan said with a warm smile.

"I also enjoy battling strong challengers."

Compared to battling rookies, Dylan much preferred facing experienced and powerful trainers. Those were the battles that truly sparked his fighting spirit.

"Gym Leader Dylan, can I challenge you too?"

Iono was a bit tempted as well. It wasn't because the Rota Badge was worth so much, but because earning it would be proof of her strength.

If she could win the Rota Badge, it would mean Dylan had recognized her as an outstanding trainer, wouldn't it?

Sure, it might seem odd for one Gym Leader to challenge another Gym Leader's badge... it was a bit strange. But so what?

After all, Dylan was so strong—it wouldn't hurt to ask for advice, right?

"Of course, you're always welcome to challenge me," Dylan nodded.

"Actually, the first person to officially receive my newly crafted badge was Gym Leader Erika from Celadon City. She's an excellent Gym Leader," Lillie said with a cheerful smile.

"Wait, Dylan wouldn't use his strongest Pokémon in these challenges, right?" Iono suddenly realized this might be a problem.

If Dylan used his full strength, how could she possibly win? Iono couldn't believe she was actually worrying about this.

"I adjust my team according to the challenger's strength—that's just basic Gym Leader etiquette," Dylan replied calmly.

"Phew~ That's a relief. Alright, I'm going to challenge you next time!"

Iono flashed a big, hopeful grin, her eyes full of envy as she looked at Nemona's Rota Badge. It was so exquisite, so beautiful, and so meaningful. She wanted one too!

"You're more than welcome," Dylan said with a faint smile.

"Well, ladies, the three of us have something to take care of, so we'll take our leave now."

Dylan glanced at the time and politely bid farewell to Nemona and Iono.

"Huh? But I haven't even properly thanked you yet, Dylan!" Iono said, momentarily stunned.

Dylan and his Ogerpon had saved her, after all. The least she could do was treat him to a meal!

"Helping a fellow Gym Leader is only natural. There's no need to be so formal, Miss Iono," Dylan replied gently.

"If you ever visit Kanto, feel free to stop by Rota Street. I'll be sure to extend you the warmest hospitality."

With that, Dylan gave a small wave and said goodbye.

"Goodbye~"

"Alright then... Goodbye, Dylan!"

"Gym Leader Dylan, wait for me! I'll definitely come challenge you again!"

Dylan smiled and nodded. He released Reshiram, and the three of them climbed aboard. Together, they flew toward the Great Crater of Paldea.

Nemona and Iono watched as Reshiram's silhouette disappeared into the distance.

"Another Pokémon I've never seen before! I really want to battle it!"

Nemona was practically bouncing with excitement. The pure white dragon looked incredibly powerful—she couldn't wait for the chance to fight it!

Seeing Nemona's reaction, Iono discreetly rolled her eyes in an adorably exaggerated manner. What a battle maniac.

"Are all Kanto Gym Leaders this... over-the-top?"

Iono rested her delicate chin on her hand, which was hidden beneath her wide sleeves.

"No, no... I think Dylan is just an exception," she concluded quickly.

There was no way such a ridiculously overpowered Gym Leader was the norm in Kanto.

"I need to start training a new powerhouse team right away, then head to Kanto to challenge Gym Leader Dylan!" Nemona said, clenching her fist with fiery determination.

"Seriously, there are so many monsters these days," Iono muttered with a defeated expression.

What was someone like her, with just above-average talent, supposed to do? It was giving her a headache.

Then again, who cares? She was just a livestreamer—she'd let the world do its thing.

Meanwhile...

"Yes, President Goodshow, I'd like to visit Area Zero. Could you help me contact Champion Geeta?"

Seated on Reshiram, Dylan was speaking on the phone with President Goodshow.

He wanted to visit the Zero Lab in Area Zero, where Professors Sada and Turo conducted their research.

"Thanks so much. Don't worry—I won't cause any trouble. Besides, you've got my back, right?" Dylan chuckled as he ended the call.

Not long after, his phone rang again, this time from an unfamiliar number.

"Hello? Is this Gym Leader Dylan?"

A warm, magnetic female voice came through the receiver.

"Yes, this is Dylan. Am I speaking with Champion Geeta?"

"That's correct. President Goodshow just contacted me, saying you'd like to enter Area Zero?" Geeta asked thoughtfully.

"Yes, I'd like to visit Professors Sada and Turo. I was hoping you could assist with the arrangements."

"That won't be a problem, but Gym Leader Dylan, I must warn you—the interior of Area Zero is not very safe right now."

Geeta offered a subtle warning. Since Goodshow had specifically reached out to her, she was willing to give Dylan some leeway.

But as the Champion of the Paldea League and its Top Champion, she knew exactly what dangers lay within Area Zero.

"Please don't worry, Champion Geeta. I'm very strong," Dylan reassured her.

"I see... In that case, I'll contact the professors for you. Please be careful."

Geeta didn't press further. With both Professors Sada and Turo present, she doubted anything overly dangerous would occur.

"Thank you very much."

After finishing his conversation with Geeta, Dylan directed Reshiram to descend into the Great Crater of Paldea.

"I've heard Champion Geeta is also Paldea's Top Champion. That's really impressive," Ilene remarked after Dylan hung up.

Indeed, Geeta was an incredible woman. Not only was she the Champion of the Paldea League, but she also held the title of Top Champion and served as director of Naranja Academy.

Three roles, and none of them were simple.

"Yes, Champion Geeta is indeed very strong," Dylan agreed. Geeta's strength was undeniable.

In the Scarlet/Violet games, it was clear she wasn't going all-out during her first battle with the protagonist.

Nemona had even described her this way:

"Everyone wants to be a Champion, right? Well, this person... she's at the very top of the Champion ranks."

That was Geeta. At the time, Nemona herself had already achieved Champion rank, but her admiration for Geeta remained incredibly high.

It was evident that Geeta wasn't using her full strength during Champion challenges, and Nemona acknowledged this after defeating her again later.

While the protagonist and Nemona may have surpassed Geeta by the endgame, she remained one of the strongest Champions within her rank.

As Reshiram descended, Dylan and his group finally arrived at Area Zero.

Area Zero was divided into the Upper Layer, Middle Layer, Lower Layer, and the Zero Lab at its deepest point. Currently, Dylan and his group were on the Upper Layer.

"Let's move forward," Dylan said as he recalled Reshiram. Together with Ilene, Lillie, and his Ogerpon, they began advancing.

"What's that over there?"

Lillie looked around curiously, taking in the lush green grass and serene environment. Suddenly, she froze, spotting something in the distance.

It was a Pokémon that resembled Volcarona, but its body was covered in white fur. Its limbs were grounded, and three pairs of orange wings adorned its back. Its yellow eyes contained black, cross-shaped pupils.

"Is that... Volcarona? No, it's different."

"Slither Wing," Dylan murmured, a small smirk forming on his lips.

Looks like his luck was good—he had encountered one right after arriving.

"Slither Wing," Dylan murmured, a faint smirk appearing on his lips. It seemed his luck was good—he had encountered one immediately upon arrival.

'Beep~ Slither Wing. Bug/Fighting type. Paradox Pokémon.

Ability: Protosynthesis.

Moves: Bulk Up, Close Combat, Lunge, Morning Sun, Low Kick, Stomp, Sunny Day, Flame Charge...

Description: This Pokémon bears a striking resemblance to what ancient depictions of Volcarona might have looked like, as detailed in a suspicious magazine article.'

The Rotom Dex diligently introduced Slither Wing's data, leaving both Lillie and Ilene stunned.

The words Paradox Pokémon and Protosynthesis immediately reminded them of Roaring Moon.

At this moment, Slither Wing also noticed the three of them. Its wild gaze flashed sharply, and it charged toward Dylan's group with all four limbs propelling it forward.

"Bonk!"

Ogerpon's right arm sprouted a thorny vine club as it leapt into the air, slamming it down on the charging Slither Wing.

Boom!

The vine club struck Slither Wing's head with a loud crash, halting its movements instantly.

Ogerpon leapt back to Dylan's side with ease, calm and composed, as it continued scrolling through its phone. Meanwhile, Slither Wing lay unconscious on the ground.

"Ultra Ball."

Dylan threw an Ultra Ball. After three shakes, it clicked shut, confirming the capture of Slither Wing.

"Not bad at all," Dylan remarked, picking up the Ultra Ball containing Slither Wing. He seemed quite pleased.

"Brother, what kind of Pokémon is this?" Lillie asked curiously.

It looked so much like Volcarona. And there was Roaring Moon as well—so similar to Salamence. Yet both had entirely different classifications and abilities. It was hard not to wonder.

"Dylan, we've come this far. Don't keep us guessing. You must know what's going on, right?" Ilene said with a playful smile.

"I wasn't planning to hide it from you anyway. Let's talk as we move," Dylan replied, putting the Ultra Ball containing Slither Wing into his bag and signaling the two girls to keep going.

"Do you remember the adventurer Heath I mentioned before? Two hundred years ago, he entered the Great Crater of Paldea. He encountered many strange things, which he later chronicled in The Scarlet Book and The Violet Book."

"Over the years, most people dismissed the contents of those books as too far-fetched to be true. They ended up being classified as fantasy or folklore."

Dylan spoke slowly as they walked.

"But they're real, aren't they?" Ilene asked, cutting straight to the point.

"You could say that... or you could say otherwise," Dylan replied with a light chuckle.

"Professors Sada and Turo—absolute geniuses, the kind of geniuses that come around once in a century," Dylan continued. "Their research into the crystals of Area Zero led to the creation of the Tera Orb. But that's not all they invented. Here in Area Zero, they achieved something even more extraordinary, something that defies ordinary imagination—a discovery that could shake the entire world."

Dylan's eyes gleamed with an otherworldly light.

Both Lillie and Ilene paused, their curiosity growing even stronger.

"Professors Sada and Turo were deeply passionate about Pokémon from other timelines. Sada was devoted to ancient Pokémon, while Turo was fascinated by future Pokémon."

"And because they believed in the contents of The Scarlet Book and The Violet Book, they began their research."

"First, using their intelligence and knowledge, they created AI robots modeled after themselves. Then, with the help of these AI counterparts, they developed the devices that brought about the current state of Area Zero!"

Dylan glanced into the distance, where a massive ancient Pokémon that resembled Jigglypuff was prowling with a menacing aura.

"A time machine that could transport Pokémon from ancient times and the far-off future into the present!"

Lillie and Ilene's pupils dilated in shock.

What?!

"That's right. This was the groundbreaking invention created by Professors Sada and Turo, along with their AI counterparts, using the crystals found in Area Zero," Dylan explained.

He pulled out another Ultra Ball and slowly approached the Screaming Tail, which had already noticed them and was now growling aggressively.

"Pokémon from the distant past, primal and instinctual, the very essence of ancient life!"

"Pokémon from the far-off future, refined to the peak of evolution!"

"These are the Paradox Pokémon brought forth by the time machine. They embody the power of both the ancient past and the distant future!"

As Dylan finished speaking, a confident grin spread across his face.

# 194: Chapter 195: Bonk!

A time machine...

Ilene and Lillie were completely dumbfounded. A time machine? Seriously?

With today's level of technology, was it even possible to create something like that? It seemed utterly impossible by all logical standards.

"So, brother, you're saying that both Roaring Moon and the Slither Wing we just encountered are...?" Lillie finally pieced it together.

"That's right. Both of them were brought here through the time machine. They're ancient forms, likely of Salamence and Volcarona," Dylan replied, his gaze fixed on the massive, oddly adorable Scream Tail before him.

"This Scream Tail is the same—an ancient form, likely of Jigglypuff."

Why "likely," though?

Because, according to the storyline, Paradox Pokémon are said to be Pokémon from alternate timelines—modern Pokémon as they might have looked in ancient or future eras.

However, Nintendo had never explicitly confirmed this. Instead, they used vague phrasing like "suspected," "there's a theory," or "it's believed."

And there were contradictions. For instance, there are ancient Paradox forms of Suicune and future forms of Virizion.

The trio of Cobalion, Terrakion, and Virizion are said to have entrusted their future to Keldeo, suggesting they don't have a future themselves.

Meanwhile, Suicune, Entei, and Raikou—the so-called Beasts of Ho-Oh—were Pokémon that, according to lore, died in the fire at the Bell Tower 150 years ago. Ho-Oh resurrected them, giving them new forms and life.

If Suicune and the others were only reborn 150 years ago, how could they have ancient forms?

But this contradiction aligns perfectly with the meaning of "Paradox"—something absurd, illogical, or contradictory.

'Beep~ Scream Tail. Fairy/Psychic type. Paradox Pokémon.

Ability: Protosynthesis.

Moves: Psychic Fangs, Hyper Voice, Play Rough, Rest, Body Slam, Bite, Roar...

Description: A supernatural magazine once described a mysterious Pokémon believed to resemble Jigglypuff from one billion years ago. This Pokémon looks similar to that description.'

The Rotom Dex faithfully presented Scream Tail's data.

"It's so cute... but it's a bit big," Lillie said sincerely.

Lillie found Scream Tail more appealing than Jigglypuff. Its two large yellow eyes, pink, chubby body, tiny fangs, and the tuft of long hair cascading down its back gave it an adorable charm.

But the problem was... it was huge!

Lillie estimated Scream Tail to be at least 1.3 meters tall. Its round body made it look even larger, almost comically so.

"Bonk!"

Boom!

Once again, Ogerpon's thorny vine club slammed down, knocking the massive Scream Tail out cold. Dylan followed up with a precise Ultra Ball throw. One shake, two shakes, three... click.

"It's all ancient forms here. Are the future forms on the other side?"

Dylan picked up Scream Tail's Poké Ball. In the games, Paradox Pokémon appeared based on version exclusivity. But in this world, with both Professor Sada and Professor Turo present, it made sense for both ancient and future forms to coexist.

"Let's keep exploring," Dylan said to Ilene and Lillie with a smile.

Since they were already here, Dylan decided he might as well catch a few of each Paradox Pokémon species. It would save him another trip later.

The group continued through the upper, middle, and lower layers of Area Zero.

Boom!

Ogerpon's vine club struck once again, sending Iron Valiant— a Paradox Pokémon with features of both Gardevoir and Gallade—flying. Dylan followed up with an Ultra Ball that hit its mark.

Ding!

After three shakes, the Ultra Ball clicked shut.

"Perfect, we've caught Iron Valiant too." Dylan picked up the ball, clearly satisfied.

"Brother, you've caught so many Pokémon already!" Lillie exclaimed, amazed.

From the upper layer to the lower layer, Dylan had caught a significant number of Paradox Pokémon.

"Paradox Pokémon are incredibly powerful. If properly trained, even ordinary trainers could use them as ace Pokémon," Dylan said, twirling the Ultra Ball containing Iron Valiant in his hand.

Pokémon like Iron Valiant were almost as strong as pseudo-legendaries, making them excellent choices for competitive trainers.

"We haven't found Walking Wake or the others yet. Maybe Professors Sada and Turo know where they are," Dylan mused, gazing at the Ultra Ball in his hand.

Aside from the "Paradise Guardian Dragons," the most prized Paradox Pokémon included Walking Wake and its counterparts.

However, it wasn't surprising that they hadn't encountered them yet. After all, Area Zero was vast.

"Let's head further down," Dylan said, motioning for Lillie and Ilene to follow.

At the lowest layer of Area Zero, the environment grew dimmer, though the massive, radiant crystals illuminated the area with a surreal glow.

The trio walked toward a cluster of buildings in the distance—the Zero Lab, the research facility of Professors Sada and Turo.

"This is... incredible," Lillie said, unable to hide her amazement as they reached the lab.

The Zero Lab was massive, but much of it was now enveloped in enormous crystalline formations, almost as if it had undergone some kind of Terastallization.

The epic scale of the scene was both breathtaking and overwhelming.

Dududu~

The alarm lights above the lab's main entrance began to flash, and with a loud clunk, the doors slowly opened.

Unlike in the games, where hordes of Paradox Pokémon charged out, the doors opened quietly.

"It seems the professors know we're here. Let's go," Dylan said with a small laugh, stepping into the Zero Lab.

Unlike the grandeur of its exterior, the interior of the lab was sparse and eerily quiet. The lifeless atmosphere was almost unsettling.

"Are you Dylan?"

A calm, emotionless female voice suddenly echoed, startling Lillie.

Looking ahead, two figures had appeared at some point.

So these were Professors Sada and Turo?

Lillie curiously examined the pair. They both looked surprisingly young.

On the left was Professor Sada, wearing a researcher's white coat over an orange tube top and shorts. Her gray hair gave her a wild yet alluring look.

On the right was Professor Turo, also in a lab coat but wearing a sleek, high-tech bodysuit underneath. His beard gave him a mature and composed demeanor.

"Yes, it's an honor to meet you, Professor Sada and Professor Turo," Dylan said, bowing slightly to the two.

Sada and Turo led Dylan, Lillie, and Ilene into the lab's main hall.

"If you've made it to the lab, then you've surely seen what's happening outside. Dylan, what do you think of them?" Turo asked, his voice deep and magnetic, yet eerily devoid of emotion.

"They're powerful... and dangerous," Dylan replied with a nod.

"If they appear in the outside world, the consequences would be unimaginable."

Dylan added this remark, noting that the ancient forms were brutal and savage, while the future forms were cold and emotionless—perfect reflections of what Paradox Pokémon truly represented.

"You're absolutely right," Professor Sada AI agreed. "Whether it's the ferocious and ruthless ancient Pokémon or the icy, unfeeling future Pokémon, they are not suited for the outside world. Their power would destroy the modern ecosystem."

"While we know the dream of the real Professors Sada and Turo was for ancient, future, and modern Pokémon to coexist in harmony, that vision is simply unrealistic," Professor Turo AI added, his tone calm but firm.

"Dream? Wait a second... are you saying—?" Lillie's eyes widened in realization.

"Ah, my apologies. It seems we forgot to mention this. We're not the real Professors Sada and Turo. We are AI robots created by them," Sada AI explained with a slight tilt of her head, then spoke softly as if recalling an old memory.

"!!!"

Lillie and Ilene were stunned. While Dylan had already told them that the professors had created AI, they hadn't expected it to be this advanced.

Aside from the lack of emotion in their voices, how were they any different from real humans?

"There's no need to be surprised," Turo AI said evenly. "With the level of AI technology available in today's world, something like us would be impossible to achieve. However, the crystals embedded within this Zero Lab enabled this cutting-edge development. That's why we cannot leave this facility."

Turo AI's voice remained emotionless as he continued, "Dylan, you are a very strong trainer."

"How would you know?" Dylan raised an eyebrow. Though technically speaking to Sada AI, Dylan still addressed them as "Professors" out of respect—they deserved the title.

"When you entered Area Zero, Koraidon and Miraidon alerted us. They sensed a terrifying and dangerous presence," Sada AI said calmly.

Koraidon and Miraidon—known as the "Paradise Guardian Dragons" and featured on the covers of The Scarlet Book and The Violet Book—were Paradox Pokémon themselves, likely ancient and future forms of Cyclizar.

But unlike other Paradox Pokémon, Koraidon and Miraidon were true legendary Pokémon.

Honestly, why did Cyclizar fare so poorly in comparison? While not weak, it didn't hold a candle to Koraidon or Miraidon in strength or stature.

"I see," Dylan said, understanding. It made sense that Koraidon and Miraidon could sense the presence of the Kyurem on his team.

"That's why we'd like to ask for your help, Dylan," Turo AI continued.

"Please, Professors, go ahead." Dylan already had an inkling of what they were about to say.

"As we mentioned before, the time machine continues to operate, endlessly transporting ancient and future Pokémon into the modern era. If this continues, sooner or later, they will leave Area Zero and spread into the outside world," Sada AI explained.

"Their vitality and power will devastate the modern ecosystem. While the real Professors believed this would simply be a natural progression…" Sada AI trailed off briefly.

"As AI replicas of the Professors, we do not think it is right for such a tragedy to unfold," she continued, her voice still calm yet tinged with subtle melancholy.

"To prevent this, Dylan, we would like you to defeat the strongest AI left behind by the Professors and use your strength to shatter their—our—dreams," Turo AI said solemnly. For a brief moment, his voice seemed to carry a faint, almost imperceptible weight.

"Why is that?" Lillie asked, her mind struggling to process what she was hearing.

"As AI, we are programmed in such a way that we cannot shut down the time machine ourselves. Only an outsider can do it," Sada AI explained.

"And if anyone tries to shut down the machine, the safety protocols in our programming will hijack our consciousness, transforming us into combat robots whose sole purpose is to eliminate any intruders," she added, her tone calm but with a trace of regret.

"…I see."

Hearing this, Lillie understood the reasoning. She pursed her lips, trying her best not to let her sympathy for Sada AI and Turo AI show.

"I understand. I'll do everything I can," Dylan said after a brief pause, his voice resolute.

Both AIs nodded in acknowledgment.

As the most advanced combat robots, these two AI were incredibly powerful. They analyzed and learned from all Champion-level battles worldwide to continuously improve their strategies.

This made them far more dangerous than their in-game counterparts, who only analyzed battles within Paldea.

Even more crucially, Sada AI controlled Koraidon, while Turo AI commanded Miraidon.

Ordinarily, their own wills aligned with the goal of shutting down the time machine, so they wouldn't use Koraidon or Miraidon in battle.

However, the real Professors' obsession with their dream ran deeper than the AIs themselves realized. If Sada AI and Turo AI were defeated, the hidden Paradise Defense Protocol would activate, forcibly taking over their consciousness and unleashing Koraidon and Miraidon against the intruders.

This was why Koraidon and Miraidon were called the "Paradise Guardian Dragons."

The two AIs, equipped with all the battle techniques of the world's Champions, commanding Paradox Pokémon of Champion-tier strength, alongside two legendary Pokémon, Koraidon and Miraidon...

It wouldn't be an exaggeration to call them two of the strongest Champion-level trainers in the world.

Not to mention, to shut down the time machine, one would have to battle both of them simultaneously. That meant facing two legendary Pokémon at the same time—a far greater challenge than anything the games presented.

But...

Dylan remained calm. Two motorcycles? Just a couple of bikes. Once he shut down the time machine, he'd make sure to capture them both.

(End of Chapter)

# 195: Chapter 196: Ancient and Future

Dylan agreed to their request, but to shut down the time machine, he needed a specific item: *The Scarlet Book* and *The Violet Book* containing the IDs of Professors Sada and Turo.

This task required Dylan to retrieve the books personally, as Sada AI and Turo AI could not leave the Zero Lab.

Fortunately, it wasn't a difficult endeavor.

Riding on Reshiram, Dylan and the others quickly flew to Mesagoza and contacted Arven using the phone number provided by Sada AI.

Arven was a young man with ash-brown hair and a stern, serious expression, though beneath it all, he was kindhearted and gentle.

"You're saying you can take me to see my parents?" Arven asked in a low voice as he approached Dylan. Arven was, of course, the son of Professors Sada and Turo.

"My parents are in the…"

"In the Zero Lab, right?" Dylan cut him off gently with a smile. "I know, because I just came from there."

Dylan had already noticed some differences between the reality of this world and the game he remembered.

In the game, depending on the version, the protagonist would encounter one of the Paradise Guardian Dragons—Koraidon or Miraidon—early in their journey.

For example, in *Scarlet*, the protagonist found a severely injured Koraidon (referred to as "Koraidon One"). The reason for its injuries?

The time machine had summoned another, more savage and feral Koraidon (referred to as "Koraidon Two"), and the two Koraidon battled fiercely over territory. Koraidon One lost and was driven out of Area Zero, sustaining critical injuries that forced it to adopt its "Limited Build" form to survive.

The same storyline applied in *Violet*, but with Miraidon in place of Koraidon.

However, things were different in this world. Dylan had asked Sada AI and Turo AI about this, and they confirmed that Koraidon Two and Miraidon Two had indeed been summoned by the time machine.

To prevent them from clashing with Koraidon One and Miraidon One, the first pair had been released from Area Zero by the AIs. Their current whereabouts were unknown.

This left Dylan curious—did Nemona have a "Koraidon/Miraidon One" with her? He made a mental note to ask her about it later.

"You…"

Arven eyed Dylan with suspicion. He had once snuck into Area Zero in search of his parents, only to be attacked by Paradox Pokémon.

In the process, his loyal Mabosstiff had been gravely injured trying to protect him.

"Don't worry, I'm strong. I can protect you in Area Zero," Dylan said, pausing briefly before continuing. "But Arven, the truth may be hard for you to face. If you're determined to go, you'll need to be prepared."

Arven clenched his fists. "I understand. But no matter what, I need to see them. They're…"

Though Dylan's words gave him a bad feeling, Arven gritted his teeth and pushed forward.

Koraidon and Miraidon were among the first Paradox Pokémon brought to the modern era. However, their wild and combative instincts often led them to attack other Pokémon.

To prevent them from being discovered or harming more Pokémon, Professors Sada and Turo brought them back to the Great Crater of Paldea and chose to live in isolation within Area Zero.

As a result, Arven had been "abandoned." He resented Koraidon and Miraidon for "stealing" his parents, and he was angry at his parents for neglecting him in pursuit of their dream.

But as he grew older, Arven realized he hadn't contacted his parents in years.

He hated them, yet he wanted to know why. Why had they so easily and coldly forgotten about him?

"Alright, then. I'll take you to the Zero Lab. I'll need to borrow *The Scarlet Book* and *The Violet Book* when we get there," Dylan said, tossing out Dragonite's Poké Ball.

Arven climbed onto Dragonite's back, and with Reshiram and Dragonite leading the way, the group flew toward Area Zero.

---

At the bottommost layer of Area Zero, after some time navigating through the depths, Dylan and his group returned to the Zero Lab.

"So… they're already dead?"

Arven's face turned pale as he looked at Sada AI and Turo AI, who calmly explained the truth to him.

"I'm sorry we never contacted you," Sada AI said. Her once emotionless voice now carried a faint trace of warmth.

"I inherited all of the real Professor Sada's emotions. I completely understand how she felt," she added softly.

"Arven, your mother truly loved you deeply."

Her words caused Arven's eyes to glisten with tears, though he stubbornly bowed his head to hide his emotions.

Seeing this, Lillie and Ilene both fell silent, their hearts heavy.

Arven had spent so many years searching for his parents, longing to confront them and demand why they had abandoned him.

And now, he had learned the truth—that they were already gone.

"…So you need these two books to shut down the time machine, right?"

After a long silence, Arven retrieved *The Scarlet Book* and *The Violet Book* from his bag and handed them to Dylan.

"Please, you have to destroy that cursed machine!" Arven pleaded earnestly.

Though he understood that the time machine represented his parents' dream, he could not accept it after learning the full story.

"I will," Dylan promised, taking the two books.

"Follow us," Sada AI and Turo AI said, leading the group to an elevator.

The elevator descended further, eventually opening into a dazzling crystalline space.

"Wow!!"

Lillie's eyes widened in amazement as she gazed around. The walls were made entirely of crystal, sparkling and otherworldly.

The ground was black, and at the center of the room stood a small black platform.

"Look closely. This is the time machine, created using the power of Terastal energy," Turo AI said, stepping toward the platform.

"To shut it down, you need the books with the Professors' IDs. They placed the final key in their favorite books—a very fitting choice for them," Sada AI said, brushing her hand gently over the platform. She then turned to Dylan.

"Dylan, when you're ready, place the books on the platform."

"Understood."

Dylan nodded and placed *The Scarlet Book* and *The Violet Book* onto the platform.

'Professors Sada and Turo's IDs confirmed. Connecting…'

A hollow mechanical voice echoed throughout the room—the central voice of the time machine.

Dylan took a few steps back, positioning himself in front of Lillie, Ilene, and Arven, as Sada AI and Turo AI crouched down.

'Preparing to shut down the time machine… preparing… preparing…'

The mechanical voice continued.

'Shutdown interrupted. Time machine rebooting.'

"Please, Dylan," Sada AI's voice called out suddenly.

'Switching Sada AI and Turo AI to sleep mode. Initiating battle protocol.'

"Defeat us, and ensure that our… dream…"

BOOM!

Before Turo AI could finish his sentence, the entire underground space trembled violently. Machines roared to life, and the platforms beneath Sada AI and Turo AI began to rise.

Above them, black mechanical petals unfolded like a blooming flower, revealing the true form of the time machine at its center, glowing with a mysterious light.

"Who dares meddle with our work… LEAVE THIS PLACE!"

Sada AI and Turo AI's voices overlapped as their pupils glowed with an eerie light, their auras radiating terrifying power.

The time machine above shimmered, and two crimson-colored Luxury Balls descended into their hands.

Gripping the balls, the two stepped forward to the edge of the platform, looking down at Dylan and the others.

The Luxury Balls opened, releasing beams of light.

To the left stood Slither Wing, and to the right emerged another Volcarona-like Pokémon with distinct mechanical features—three pairs of orange wings floating ominously behind it.

It was Iron Moth!

'Beep~ Iron Moth. Fire/Poison type. Paradox Pokémon.

Ability: Quark Drive.

Moves: Hurricane, Morning Sun, Fiery Dance, Sludge Wave, Discharge, Screech, Air Slash…

Description: Resembles the UFOs described in supernatural magazines that are said to monitor humanity.'

The Rotom Dex dutifully provided a detailed description of Iron Moth.

"So, it's a double battle, huh?"

Dylan raised an eyebrow. He had already suspected that he'd need to face both Sada AI and Turo AI simultaneously.

"Brother…"

"Leave it to me," Dylan said, stepping forward. With a flick of his wrist, he tossed out two Poké Balls.

"Reshiram, Lunala."

With a flash of white light, Reshiram and Lunala appeared before him.

The legendary dragon of truth from Unova and the beast that calls forth the moonlight from Alola.

"Two versus two. That's fair, don't you think?"

Dylan glanced up at Sada AI and Turo AI standing on the black platform above. A radiant, confident smile spread across his face.

A massive wave of oppressive energy filled the space. Reshiram stood firmly on the ground, while Lunala hovered majestically in the air.

The overwhelming presence caused both Slither Wing and Iron Moth to let out uneasy growls. Despite their violent and cold nature, their biological instincts screamed at them in warning—this was dangerous!

"What are those Pokémon?"

Arven was stunned.

'Beep~ Reshiram…'

'Beep~ Lunala. Psychic/Ghost type. Moone Pokémon.

Ability: Shadow Shield.

Moves: Moongeist Beam, Phantom Force, Psychic, Dark Pulse, Shadow Ball, Hypnosis, Dream Eater…

Description: Known as the Beast that Calls the Moon. It sustains itself by converting all light into energy.'

Amazing!

Arven was utterly shocked. Just hearing their descriptions made these Pokémon sound beyond incredible!

"I don't know who you are, but if you're here to meddle, then I'll drive you out!"

Sada AI roared with commanding energy, slamming her arm downward and shouting at Slither Wing below.

"Slither Wing, First Impression!"

"Iron Moth, Hurricane!"

Turo AI, in contrast, gave his command with the cold precision of a machine.

Slither Wing growled and hesitated briefly, its wild instincts battling against its trainer's orders. But under Sada AI's command, it pushed aside reason and charged forward, its body glowing with pale green energy as it raced toward Lunala at high speed.

Meanwhile, Iron Moth's three mechanical wings began to spin rapidly, unleashing a violent, chaotic gust of wind.

"Moongeist Beam."

"Flamethrower."

Dylan smiled faintly.

Lunala folded its massive wings together, gathering an immense black energy darker than the night.

Reshiram opened its jaws, crimson flames swirling within its mouth.

With an ear-splitting cry, Lunala unleashed its Moongeist Beam—a pitch-black energy wave tearing through the air. At the same time, Reshiram's searing flames roared toward Slither Wing.

BOOM!

Two deafening explosions rocked the battlefield. Both Slither Wing and Iron Moth were flung backward, their bodies hitting the ground with a thud. Neither moved again.

They'd been one-shot!

Sada AI and Turo AI stared at the fallen Paradox Pokémon, seemingly frozen for a second, their heads tilting slightly in confusion.

"?"

It was almost as if a question mark had materialized above their heads.

"Awesome! Lunala is so cool!"

Lillie squealed with excitement as she admired Lunala, one of her favorite Pokémon.

"This is just… unbelievably strong," Arven murmured, completely stunned.

"It's nothing special."

Dylan adjusted the collar of his shirt with a calm elegance.

"Now, come on—show me more of your strength. That so-called power of the ancient past and the distant future…

"Let's see how well you can dance before the legend I create."

(End of Chapter)
