# 196: Chapter 197: The White Dragon and Moon God!

Dylan didn't waste any time. After all, the key challenge in battling Sada AI and Turo AI was ultimately the two Paradise Guardian Dragons.

As for the earlier Pokémon? They were nothing more than appetizers.

"Who is this guy, really?"

Arven stared at Dylan's back, astonished by the sheer strength he had just witnessed.

Having faced Paradox Pokémon himself, Arven knew just how terrifying they were. His loyal Mabosstiff had been gravely injured by one.

Yet, the two Paradox Pokémon they had just encountered—whose presence felt far more threatening than the one that attacked him—had been defeated instantly.

This is beyond ridiculous!

"Scream Tail!"

"Iron Bundle!"

Sada AI and Turo AI quickly sent out their second Pokémon. Scream Tail and Iron Bundle materialized on the battlefield.

But…

"Moongeist Beam."

"Flamethrower."

Dylan remained perfectly composed as he gave his commands.

BOOM!

Another pair of deafening explosions. Turo AI had even ordered Iron Bundle to use Protect, but it made no difference—Moongeist Beam tore through it with ease.

Another one-shot.

It was clear that while the AIs used Champion-level Paradox Pokémon, they were little more than clouds of smoke before Dylan's Reshiram and Lunala.

One after another, the Paradox Pokémon fell.

And as the defeated Pokémon piled up, the energy radiating from Sada AI and Turo AI began to weaken.

"This… this can't be!!"

Finally, with the thunderous defeat of Fluttermane and Iron Valiant, the two AIs seemed to physically slump, as if drained of power.

For a moment, the battlefield fell into silence.

"Is it over?"

"Did we really beat them? So now we can shut down the time machine, right?"

"Probably…?"

Lillie, Ilene, and Arven whispered among themselves, uncertain yet hopeful.

Dylan narrowed his eyes slightly.

"Thank you, Dylan. You've done well. At last, the time mach…"

The black platform descended once more. Sada AI struggled to lift herself, a faint, gratified smile appearing on her face.

Her voice was still halting, occasionally breaking between words as her system faltered, her consciousness incomplete from the strain.

"...has been stopped. You've halted the… Professor's wishes. Truly, thank you."

Sada AI's voice was weak, her body trembling as she spoke. It seemed she was using the last of her strength.

But just as the atmosphere began to calm, the warning lights in the underground space began flashing red.

'Warning: Anomaly detected in the safety system. Anomaly detected in the safety system.'

The mechanical voice of the time machine echoed ominously throughout the space.

'The time machine is in a dangerous state. The time machine is in a dangerous state.'

The underground space darkened, and a strange, foreboding glow enveloped the room.

"What's happening?!"

The sudden change left Arven shocked.

"This… could it be…?!"

Both Sada AI and Turo AI's expressions changed drastically.

'To eliminate the anomaly, activating Paradise Defense Protocol.'

The icy, mechanical voice of the time machine reverberated through the space.

"What…? Did the Professors program this to ensure the time machine couldn't be shut down under any circumstances?"

Turo AI let out a bitter laugh—a strange emotion for an AI to express.

Dylan calmly opened the Luxury Ball in his hand, and Kyurem appeared behind him.

'Sealing all Poké Balls not linked to Sada and Turo's IDs.'

'Initiating program… gathering Terastal energy…'

The time machine's voice continued as both Sada AI and Turo AI's bodies began to undergo partial crystallization.

The AIs attempted to resist, clearly unwilling to fight anymore, but they were powerless. In their final moments of clarity, they turned to Dylan.

"Sorry, Dylan… but we'll need to trouble you again. Defeat us…"

Their expressions turned cold and emotionless once more, their eyes glowing with a sinister light as their consciousnesses were hijacked.

Reaching into their lab coats, the two AIs pulled out crimson Luxury Balls and pointed them at Dylan.

The Paradise Defense Protocol was issuing its challenge!

Two beams of white light burst forth, accompanied by a savage roar and a low, icy dragon's growl. Two massive dragons materialized before Dylan.

"What kind of Pokémon are those?"

Arven froze in place.

To the left stood a bipedal, crimson dragon with a long, thick tail. Its body was adorned with white, spiked fur on its head and tail, and a wheel-like structure rested on its chest. Its aura was feral and terrifying.

'Beep~ Koraidon. Fighting/Dragon type. Paradox Pokémon.

Ability: Orichalcum Pulse.

Moves: Close Combat, Outrage, Counter, Collision Course, Flamethrower, Brick Break, Drain Punch…

Description: This seems to be the Winged King mentioned in ancient explorer's accounts. It is said to have shattered the earth with its fists. (Full Form)'

It was Koraidon.

Dylan's gaze shifted to the Pokémon on the right.

Its sleek, high-tech design gave it an air of regal sophistication. It had a long tail and two claws resting by its sides, with a protruding chest and an aura of cold indifference.

'Beep~ Miraidon. Electric/Dragon type. Paradox Pokémon.

Ability: Hadron Engine.

Moves: Thunder, Wild Charge, Outrage, Dragon Pulse, Parabolic Charge, Discharge, Metal Sound…

Description: This appears to be the Iron Serpent referenced in ancient texts. It is said to have reduced the land to ash with its lightning. (Full Mode)'

These two Pokémon were clearly powerful.

Even Lillie could feel it—this overwhelming, majestic pressure was nearly on par with Reshiram and Lunala. She bit her lip nervously.

"Brother?"

"No problem."

Dylan remained calm. He had tested it earlier: with the Paradise Defense Protocol active, a special electromagnetic wave now enveloped the underground space, sealing all Poké Balls except those tied to the Professors' IDs.

However, Necrozma had already established a psychic link with him and assured him that it could forcibly break out of its Luxury Ball if needed.

For now, Dylan had Kyurem out and ready to fight.

"Raaawr!"

Koraidon roared, its body glowing faintly red as the underground space was suddenly bathed in harsh sunlight.

Under the Orichalcum Pulse ability, the weather shifted to intense sunlight, boosting Koraidon's attack power.

At the same time, Miraidon's body began to emit purple sparks as the ground crackled with faint electricity.

Hadron Engine activated!

The electric terrain spread across the battlefield, enhancing Miraidon's special attack.

With both the weather and terrain in their favor, the two dragons exuded an overwhelming presence.

"Interesting," Dylan muttered with a smirk.

Though Koraidon and Miraidon didn't carry much weight in the game's storyline, their strength as legendary Pokémon was undeniable—especially these "Second Units," who were strong enough to defeat and banish their "First Unit" counterparts in combat.

"Raaaawr!"

Kyurem let out a furious roar, clearly unimpressed by the two dragons before it.

The oppressive aura Kyurem radiated caused Koraidon and Miraidon to momentarily falter, their gazes growing more serious.

Kyurem's presence was nothing short of monstrous.

"What kind of monster is that?"

Arven couldn't stop trembling. Despite his efforts to stay calm, his body instinctively shivered under Kyurem's overwhelming aura.

Kyurem was standing right behind them—it was terrifying!

"Kyurem, you up for this fight?" Dylan asked with a cheerful grin.

"Raaawr!"

Kyurem grunted in approval. It was bored from lack of action lately. Following Dylan meant it couldn't afford to hibernate anymore—who knew how many centuries would pass if it did?

"Then switch to Black Kyurem."

Electric currents crackled across Kyurem's body as it transformed. In a flash, Black Kyurem stood tall and menacing.

Reshiram and Lunala both shifted slightly to the side, making room for their "family's second-born."

"Raaawr!"

Black Kyurem landed heavily in front of Dylan, its fierce golden eyes locking onto Koraidon and Miraidon.

A dragon? Black Kyurem loved fighting dragons.

"Koraidon, Collision Course!"

"Miraidon, Thunder!"

Sada AI and Turo AI gave their commands simultaneously.

Koraidon roared, its body glowing as it charged forward, becoming a high-speed, spinning fiery wheel.

Miraidon gathered a surge of lightning, preparing to strike.

"Kyurem, Freeze Volt."

Dylan remained calm as he issued his order. Then, with a glance at Miraidon, he smirked.

"Reshiram, Lunala—handle Miraidon as you see fit."

He left the task to them, confident that the duo could handle one Miraidon without his input.

Dylan, you heartless man!

Reshiram and Lunala exchanged glances, seeing the same annoyance reflected in each other's eyes.

The moment Black Kyurem showed up, Dylan didn't even bother to give them proper instructions anymore. How could he?!

What was so special about Black Kyurem? Was it prettier than them? Ugh!

Miraidon's lightning struck toward them, but Reshiram responded with a mighty roar, its tail's turbine igniting with flames.

Overdrive Mode activated!

Lunala, meanwhile, spread its wings wide, glowing like a full moon.

Full Moon Mode activated!

Reshiram unleashed Blue Flare, while Lunala fired Moongeist Beam.

The fiery blue and shadowy beams combined to counter Miraidon's Thunder.

Miraidon: "???"

Lillie blinked in confusion, watching the duo enthusiastically chase Miraidon.

"Why do Reshiram and Lunala look so fired up?"

"They probably just want to finish the battle quickly," Ilene said, also puzzled but impressed.

Meanwhile, Black Kyurem faced Koraidon head-on.

Its Freeze Volt collided with Koraidon's Collision Course, sending shockwaves across the battlefield. Slowly but surely, Kyurem's icy power overwhelmed Koraidon's fiery charge.

With one final surge, Freeze Volt shattered Collision Course, slamming into Koraidon and sending it flying into a crystal wall.

"Even with Orichalcum Pulse, your strength is no match for my Kyurem," Dylan remarked with a faint smirk.

"Kyurem, Icicle Spear."

Black Kyurem roared, summoning a barrage of icy spears that rained down on Koraidon, pinning it to the ground as it howled in pain.

Meanwhile, Dylan glanced over at Reshiram and Lunala, who were relentlessly chasing Miraidon. He nodded approvingly.

"My White Dragon and Moon God—they're amazing as always!"

(End of Chapter)

# 197: Chapter 198: Terapagos

"Raaaawr!!"

After enduring the relentless barrage of ice spears, the battered and bruised Koraidon let out a furious roar at Black Kyurem, its primal instincts fully provoked.

"Fusion Bolt."

Dylan raised an eyebrow, clearly unimpressed. It seemed Black Kyurem hadn't gone all out yet.

Black Kyurem's eyes glinted with feral light as radiant blue-white energy gathered around its body, forming a crackling, electric arc.

Fusion Bolt!

With a thunderous roar, Black Kyurem charged at Koraidon, electricity surging wildly around its massive form.

"Collision Course!"

Sada AI immediately gave her command. There was no way to dodge; she had to counterattack head-on.

Koraidon roared, its body glowing once more with orangish-yellow Fighting-type energy. It transformed into a blazing wheel of light and lunged forward to meet Black Kyurem.

BOOM!

The two attacks collided with an earth-shattering impact. The overwhelming energy rippled out in shockwaves, shaking the crystalline cavern.

But just a moment later, Fusion Bolt overwhelmed Collision Course, breaking through and slamming into Koraidon's body for the second time.

Crash!

Koraidon was sent flying, slamming into the crystalline walls with a heavy thud. The searing pain and frost accumulating on its body left it completely immobilized. It fell to the ground, gasping for air, barely able to move.

Two Fusion Bolts, combined with the earlier ice spear barrage, had brought Koraidon to its absolute limit.

"Avalanche."

Dylan smiled faintly. It seemed Koraidon had reached its breaking point.

Black Kyurem roared, summoning massive chunks of ice high above Koraidon.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

The avalanche of ice crashed down on the incapacitated Koraidon, burying it entirely. When the ice settled, Koraidon lay unconscious, unable to continue the fight.

"Wow~!! Kyurem is so strong!"

Ilene's eyes sparkled as she watched the display of power. It was her first time seeing Kyurem in action, and its sheer dominance left her in awe.

That Koraidon was clearly a tremendously powerful legendary Pokémon—its strength was palpable.

But against Black Kyurem, it had been defeated so quickly!

"Kyurem really is incredible," Lillie agreed wholeheartedly.

"Yeah! Reshiram and Lunala haven't even taken down Miraidon yet!" Ilene exclaimed in amazement.

Hearing this, Reshiram and Lunala, both blessed with heightened senses, instantly caught Ilene's comment.

Their expressions changed.

How infuriating!!

How could that second-born finish its battle so quickly?!

"Grrr!!"

"RAAA!!"

Reshiram and Lunala let out determined cries, doubling down on their attacks. Their relentless assault left Miraidon scrambling, unable to find an opening to counter.

Cold and calculating as it was, even Miraidon was now inwardly cursing.

What's wrong with these two?! It felt like they were on steroids, ganging up on it with such ferocity.

Fight me one-on-one if you dare!!

Finally, under the onslaught of Reshiram and Lunala's relentless attacks, Miraidon was forced to collapse in defeat, its body sparking faintly as it fell.

With both Paradise Guardian Dragons defeated, the light in the cavern returned to normal. The strange glow in Sada AI and Turo AI's eyes faded, and their bodies relaxed.

But the forced battle had clearly taken a toll on their systems.

"You… you're truly incredible!"

Turo AI, supporting his now-limp left arm, looked at Dylan with a mix of astonishment and respect.

"You even defeated the Professors' ultimate failsafe. Dylan, your strength is truly unparalleled," Sada AI added, her voice filled with admiration. With the system interference gone, their speech patterns had returned to normal.

"Have they returned to normal? Does this mean the time machine has been shut down?" Lillie asked excitedly.

"No," Dylan shook his head, his gaze fixed on the black, mechanical "flower" atop the platform. Though it had stopped momentarily, it wasn't truly deactivated—only paused because the Paradise Defense Protocol had been broken.

It wouldn't be long before the time machine restarted. The reason why…

"It seems you've realized the truth, Dylan," Turo AI said with a faint, resigned smile. "As long as we exist, the time machine cannot be shut down. It appears we've become part of its self-repair system."

Turo's calm admission stunned everyone.

"What… what does that mean?"

Arven froze in place as the weight of those words hit him, while Ilene and Lillie seemed to grasp the implications as well.

"As long as we, who are part of the time machine, remain here, it will continue to function," Sada AI explained with a faint smile.

"And so, we've decided to use the time machine to travel to the worlds we've always dreamed of—the ancient past and the far-off future."

Sada AI's voice carried a hint of bittersweet emotion.

"It's been wonderful meeting all of you. We rarely had the chance to interact with humans while confined to the research lab. But now… it's time to say goodbye."

Sada AI and Turo AI took a step back, positioning themselves beneath the "flower" of the time machine. Their departure would disable the system's self-repair function, causing it to fully shut down.

Dylan remained silent.

"But… is this really okay? Professor Sada, you've said it yourself—you cannot leave the lab."

Lillie bit her lip, unable to stay silent.

It was true—Sada AI and Turo AI were creations of Area Zero's mysterious Terastal energy. They couldn't survive outside the lab.

Even if they reached the ancient past or the future, how would they "live"? Surely they understood this…

But they were willing to sacrifice themselves to ensure the time machine could never restart.

Arven's eyes reddened as he lowered his head.

"Maybe so," Turo AI said softly, "but this is the best outcome, isn't it?"

"This isn't just about shutting down the time machine. We've always dreamed of seeing the ancient and future worlds with our own eyes."

Lillie bit her lip harder, feeling her heart ache.

"Brother…"

Lillie turned to Dylan, her voice tinged with sadness.

"What if there's another way to seal the time machine?" Dylan said suddenly, drawing everyone's attention.

Sada AI and Turo AI froze.

"Dylan, you know as well as we do that we're part of the time machine. As long as we exist, it can't be destroyed," Turo AI reminded him.

"Yes, but what if we froze the time machine? If Kyurem's absolute freezing ability can seal the transmission pathways, wouldn't that stop Pokémon from being sent here?"

Dylan rubbed his chin thoughtfully as he spoke. Destroying the time machine outright would likely harm the AIs. But freezing its functionality might work.

"Technically… technically, that could work," Sada AI said after running some calculations. "But the freezing power needed would be…"

"Oh? Conveniently, I happen to have the strongest Ice-type Pokémon," Dylan said with a smile.

Aside from Arceus with the Ice Plate, Kyurem was undoubtedly the most powerful Ice-type Pokémon.

"Roar…"

Black Kyurem grunted, about to argue that it was the strongest Dragon-type, before remembering Reshiram and Zekrom. It fell silent.

Fine, it would let that go for now.

"Kyurem, switch to White Kyurem form," Dylan instructed, patting its leg.

With a low growl, Black Kyurem's body ignited with flames, transforming into White Kyurem.

"Professors, please step aside," Dylan said politely to the AIs.

Sada AI and Turo AI exchanged a glance, then silently stepped back. If there was a way to resolve this without their sacrifice, they were willing to try.

"Kyurem, you know what to do. Use your strongest freezing breath and seal that machine," Dylan said. "Make sure it won't start again, not even in ten thousand years."

White Kyurem hovered slightly above the ground, its tail turbine spinning at full power as a bone-chilling frost began to spread.

The temperature in the space plummeted, and snowflakes began to form in the air.

Freezing!

White Kyurem opened its mouth, unleashing an incredibly powerful icy breath.

Crack… crack… crack…

The freezing sound echoed as the core of the time machine was encased in layers of thick, crystalline ice.

Within moments, the glow of the time machine dimmed completely, frozen solid.

Kyurem descended, its body glowing as it reverted to its neutral form.

"Well done, Kyurem. How long will it stay frozen?" Dylan asked, exhaling into his hands to warm them.

"Without external interference, at least a few thousand years," Kyurem replied via telepathy. "The machine's internal components are frozen too—it's completely inoperable."

"Perfect. You've done great, Kyurem."

"Thank you, Dylan," Sada AI said, stepping forward.

"No need to thank me," Dylan replied. "We couldn't let those Paradox Pokémon loose in the outside world, after all."

The group returned to the research lab's main hall, where Sada AI and Turo AI expressed their gratitude once more.

Before they parted, Turo AI handed Dylan two crimson Luxury Balls.

"These are?"

"Koraidon and Miraidon. They don't belong here anymore," Turo AI said. "Their strength is immense, and their temperaments are difficult to handle. But we believe you can guide them."

Dylan hesitated briefly before accepting the balls.

"Well, looks like I've got two more legendary dragons to train. My dragon collection keeps growing."

He smirked.

"By the way, Professors, do you know where Terapagos is?"

"Terapagos? It's at the bottom of Area Zero," Sada AI replied. "You're interested in it?"

"Of course," Dylan said with a grin.

Since he was already here, he wasn't going to leave without claiming that little turtle known as the Treasure of Zero.

(End of Chapter)

# 198: Chapter 199: That Little Turtle Has No Respect

The Great Hole of Zero is an even deeper part of Paldea's Area Zero.

The Terastal energy here is far more intense, causing the rocks and terrain to become crystallized, and even some trees have taken on a crystalline form.

Arven remained in the Zero Lab while Dylan and his group, guided by Sada AI, made their way to the Great Hole of Zero.

"This place… it's incredible!"

Lillie gasped as she observed the surroundings. Everything, from the ground to the trees, gleamed with a crystalline sheen.

"That's because we're close to the source of the Terastal phenomenon," Dylan explained, his gaze glinting with intrigue.

"So, Dylan, is the source of Terastal energy really this Terapagos?" Ilene asked curiously.

"That's right. All of the mysteries surrounding the Great Crater of Paldea ultimately trace back to Terapagos," Dylan said with a nod, as they continued deeper into the Hole, guarded by his Galarian Slowking.

"Terapagos has been alive for over two million years. But during that time, violent geological activity nearly wiped out its species—only one Terapagos survived.

"This lone Terapagos was able to crystallize its internal energy, using it to protect itself while in a dormant state.

"It has many names: 'The Blue Disk,' 'The Treasure of Zero,' and 'The Source of Terastal.' All of the mysterious crystals and Terastal energy found in Area Zero are merely byproducts of Terapagos's slumber."

Dylan's explanation was calm and steady, but the deeper meaning left both Lillie and Ilene in awe.

Even with the conclusion of Scarlet and Violet, Terapagos remained shrouded in mystery.

For example, when players brought Terapagos to the crystal pool in Kitakami after completing the main story, a unique event would occur. Terapagos would display its powers, summoning the past version of Professor Sada—not Sada AI, nor a version from a parallel world, but the real Sada from the past timeline.

This version of Sada, who had not yet built the time machine, would take interest in the protagonist's Koraidon, recognizing it as the "Winged King."

After exchanging her Scarlet Book for the protagonist's Violet Book, this encounter would eventually be recorded in Sada's notes.

This proved that Terapagos had the ability to manipulate time—not through parallel worlds, but through actual time travel or causality manipulation.

In fact, this was why Sada had such faith in the Scarlet Book's accounts and became determined to build the time machine to meet ancient Pokémon.

It also explained how, two centuries earlier, Heath—the explorer who authored the books—encountered Paradox Pokémon in Area Zero despite their absence during his era. Heath likely saw Terapagos in the Great Hole, triggering a time-travel event that brought him forward in time, where he witnessed Paradox Pokémon in Area Zero.

This created a closed time loop: Heath documented Paradox Pokémon in the Scarlet Book, inspiring Sada to build the time machine, which then brought the Paradox Pokémon into the present.

So, what was the cause? What was the effect? The entire narrative was rife with paradoxes and loops.

But to Dylan, none of that mattered. Whatever abilities Terapagos had, he was determined to capture it.

At the deepest point of the Great Hole, Dylan and his group finally arrived. Before them stood a landscape of intersecting crystalline pillars.

"This is beautiful~ but where's Terapagos?"

Lillie scanned the surroundings, her curiosity piqued. Despite the dazzling view, there wasn't a single Pokémon in sight.

"It's right there," Dylan said, his eyes fixed on a pale green stone resting atop a crystalline platform.

Walking up to the stone, Dylan raised his hand, which began to glow faintly green as the energy flowed toward the stone.

"Taa…"

A soft, adorable cry echoed through the Hole as the stone emitted a radiant glow.

Moments later, a small, deep-blue turtle Pokémon emerged, shaking its head and looking up at Dylan with curious eyes.

It was Terapagos.

"Ta-le~!"

Terapagos's large, sparkling eyes blinked as it observed Dylan, its soft chirps brimming with innocence.

"So this is Terapagos?"

Ilene and Lillie approached, their eyes wide with wonder.

This was the so-called "Source of Terastal"? It was far cuter than they had expected!

Terapagos was quite small, only about 0.3 meters tall. Its body was a deep blue, with large, bright blue eyes accented by rose-colored eyeliner. Its shell and parts of its body were made of hexagonal, teal-colored crystals.

It didn't seem to radiate the overwhelming aura one would expect from the "Source of Terastal."

'Beep~ Terapagos. Normal type. Terastal Pokémon.

Ability: Terastal Morph.

Moves: Tera Starburst, Rock Polish, Double-Edge, Heavy Slam, Earth Power, Protect, Tri Attack…

Description: Converts energy into hardened crystals to protect itself. Known as the Source of Terastal. (Base Form)'

The Rotom Dex floated over, providing an introduction.

"This is just its base form? So it must have other forms," Lillie realized. No wonder it looked so small and harmless.

"Little one, you've been sleeping for so long. How about coming out to explore the world?" Dylan asked with a gentle smile.

"Ta-le?" Terapagos tilted its head, confused.

"I can show you many fun things and let you eat delicious food," Dylan said enticingly.

"Ta-le!"

Terapagos's eyes lit up.

Despite having lived for millions of years, its mindset was quite simple—likely due to its long periods of dormancy. It found Dylan's offer quite appealing.

"Then why don't you become my Pokémon? That way, we can be partners," Dylan said, holding out a Luxury Ball and giving it a slight shake.

Terapagos hesitated, its innocent nature causing it to pause.

"Ta-le?"

"You want a battle first? Fine by me."

Dylan smirked, stepping back with his companions as Terapagos leapt down from the crystal platform.

"Taa-goooos!!"

With a cry, Terapagos's body erupted with dazzling light, and a powerful gust of wind swept through the area.

When the light faded, a completely transformed Pokémon stood before them.

Terastral Morph!

In battle, Terapagos's body underwent a dramatic crystallization, taking on a form resembling a large disk. Its shell became adorned with 18 deep blue pentagonal crystals surrounding a central hexagonal core, each crystal bearing the symbol of a different Pokémon type. Its neck and tail sprouted shimmering blue crystal-like manes.

"This is…" Lillie's eyes sparkled. This form of Terapagos was completely different—it now exuded a commanding presence worthy of its title as the "Source of Terastal."

'Beep~ Terapagos (Terastal Form). Normal type. Terastal Pokémon.

Ability: Terastal Shell.

Description: Senses danger and creates a sturdy crystal shell for protection. (Terastal Form)'

"Necrozma, Lunala," Dylan called, tossing out two Luxury Balls.

Necrozma and Lunala appeared in front of Dylan. The latter glanced at Dylan with a slight hint of annoyance.

"It's alright, Lunala. This won't take long," Dylan reassured his giant bat.

Lunala seemed unconvinced, its gaze growing more resentful.

Ever since Necrozma had merged with it, Lunala had been in an awkward position. Though the merger no longer suppressed its consciousness, Lunala couldn't deny its fascination with the immense power it gained in its Dawn Wings form.

It even felt guilty for enjoying the power so much, considering its bond with Solgaleo.

"Necrozma."

At Dylan's command, Necrozma disassembled itself into black crystals, fusing seamlessly with Lunala's body.

With a flash of light, Necrozma: Dawn Wings emerged.

"Necrozma, Ultra Burst."

"Liiiii~!!"

A deep, raspy cry echoed, and a certain bat's excited voice could be faintly heard.

Dylan sighed. Lunala, weren't you the one who said you didn't want to merge?

A surge of gray-blue energy engulfed the fused Pokémon, transforming it into a massive, radiant dragon of light—Ultra Necrozma.

The cavern was bathed in blinding light as the overwhelming pressure of Ultra Necrozma's presence filled the air.

"Taa…"

Terapagos's demeanor changed instantly, sensing the overwhelming threat before it.

"Alright, Terapagos, let's begin!" Dylan said with a smile. "Show me the full extent of your power!"

But…

"Taa-le~"

Terapagos's glow suddenly faded as it reverted to its base form—a small, adorable turtle.

"Ta-le~ Ta-le~!"

It chirped innocently at Dylan, its message clear: Why fight? Fighting is bad. Peace forever!

Lillie: (—)

Ilene: (.)

"I just had Necrozma transform… Terapagos, you can't do this to me…"

Dylan was left speechless as he stared at the playful little turtle. Sure, he didn't expect the battle to be difficult, but he at least wanted some action!

"Ta-le~!"

Terapagos happily floated over, landing on Dylan's shoulder and nuzzling his cheek.

Ultra Necrozma turned its head, its gaze almost mocking.

'Should we blast it just once? That little turtle has no respect,' Lunala's voice suggested in Dylan's mind.

"No need," Dylan said with a sigh, pulling out a Luxury Ball.

Terapagos curiously tapped the ball with its nose and was instantly captured.

Beep… Beep… Click!

The ball locked.

"Terapagos, captured."

Dylan gazed at the Luxury Ball in his hand, his smile deepening.

The legendary "Source of Terastal" was now his.

(End of Chapter)

# 199: Chapter 200: Grass Team

"Alright, goodbye, Professor Sada AI and Professor Turo AI. We'll visit you again when we get the chance."

At the Zero Lab, Dylan and his group were bidding farewell to the AI versions of Professor Sada and Professor Turo.

On Dylan's shoulder, Terapagos perched curiously, its bright eyes scanning the surroundings. Having slept for so long, it now found everything around it novel and fascinating—much like Aloy's companion in Horizon: Forbidden West.

"Alright, come visit us if you have time," said the Sada AI with a gentle smile.

"I... I can visit you again too, right?"

Arven hesitated, reluctant to leave. Though he knew these AIs weren't his real parents, they still shared the same appearance and retained all of Sada and Turo's memories.

"Of course you can, Arven," said the Turo AI softly.

"We've inherited the feelings of Professor Sada and Professor Turo. Believe us, the professors truly loved you."

"We may not understand how to be proper parents, but you can always think of us as your closest family."

"Thank you... thank you both..."

Arven's eyes welled up with tears again. He had spoken at length with the two AI professors while Dylan and the others explored the Great Crater, finally coming to terms with his lingering pain.

"Alright then, time to go. Goodbye, Professors," Dylan said, patting Arven on the shoulder. He then released Reshiram and Dragonite from their Poké Balls.

"Goodbye," the AI professors said with a wave.

"Goodbye, Professors! We'll come back to see you again," said Lillie and Ilene as they waved.

With that, Reshiram and Dragonite took to the skies, carrying the four of them away.

By the time they returned to Mesagoza and Naranja Academy, the sun was setting, casting a warm glow over the campus.

"Thank you, Dylan. Thank you so much."

Standing before the familiar sight of the academy, Arven felt dazed. The experiences in Area Zero had been so surreal.

"If it weren't for you, I might still be in the dark about everything," Arven said, lips trembling with gratitude.

Without Dylan, he might have still harbored resentment toward Sada and Turo. But now…

"You don't need to thank me. You helped us as well," Dylan replied with a smile. Seeing Arven's downcast expression, Dylan thought for a moment.

"Arven, I don't know how to comfort you, but I will say this: grieve for the ones you've lost."

Dylan sighed softly.

"And no matter what happens, even though you've lost your parents, you still have the Sada AI and Turo AI. They care about you deeply. Don't think of them as replacements for your real parents; they're more than that."

"I understand," Arven nodded.

"Then keep moving forward—work hard at what you love. I'm sure the professors would be proud of you in the future."

Dylan smiled encouragingly. Though Arven didn't have exceptional talent as a Pokémon Trainer, he was an incredibly skilled cook.

After all, his role in the protagonist group was essentially that of a chef, someone who excelled at culinary arts.

"I will do my best," Arven said earnestly, nodding with determination.

"Make the most of your talent. I believe in you."

Dylan had already invested in Arven's future. He believed that one day, Arven could become a top-tier chef—a gesture of support and sympathy for the challenges Arven had endured.

"Dylan!"

A familiar, energetic voice suddenly called out behind them.

Dylan turned to see three girls approaching.

Nemona was beaming with excitement, while to her left stood a petite, gentle girl in the Naranja Academy uniform. To her right was another girl in a gray hoodie.

Juliana and Penny!

Of course, this Penny wasn't the Gym Leader from Cerulean City in Kanto. This Penny was the true leader of Team Star, originally from Galar.

"Nemona, what a coincidence," Dylan said with a smile, greeting her.

"Right? What are the odds? Since we've run into each other, Gym Leader Dylan, how about we have another battle? I've figured out how to beat your Pecharunt!"

Nemona jogged over, her excitement palpable.

"Not today. I'm pretty exhausted. Let's save it for next time, or when you visit Kanto," Dylan replied tactfully, gently turning her down. It was getting late, after all.

"What?! Oh, come on..." Nemona pouted, her energy visibly deflating as she gazed at Dylan with mock disappointment.

Both Ilene and Lillie couldn't help but laugh, covering their mouths to stifle their giggles.

"And who might these two be?" Dylan asked, changing the subject as he turned his attention to Juliana and Penny.

Nemona was a battle fanatic, the kind who couldn't go a day without a match. Her reaction was entirely expected.

"Oh! Right, Gym Leader Dylan, this is Juliana, my rival! And this is Penny, Juliana's friend."

"I ran into them on my way back to the academy."

Nemona's energy bounced back immediately as she introduced the pair.

"Hello, Gym Leader Dylan. I'm Juliana," Juliana said politely, her demeanor sweet and respectful.

"I'm Penny," Penny added in a quieter voice, clearly a bit shy and reserved.

"Nice to meet you both. I'm Dylan, from the Kanto region," Dylan replied warmly, greeting them with a friendly smile.

With Juliana, Nemona, and Arven together, the Scarlet/Violet protagonist group was now complete.

Dylan glanced at Arven, who was still standing quietly behind him. This was the full team from the games—though Arven seemed blissfully unaware of it.

"Juliana, Gym Leader Dylan is super strong! He beat me, even when I was going all out."

"Maybe you should challenge him sometime?" Nemona suggested, trying to encourage her rival.

After all, if Juliana was her chosen rival, she had to prove herself by earning Dylan's recognition through a Rotar Gym challenge.

"That doesn't seem right. Gym Leader Dylan said he was already tired," Juliana said, blinking with innocent politeness. She might have been straightforward, but she was always mindful of others' feelings.

"Don't listen to Nemona's nonsense. Even if Dylan agreed to battle, you wouldn't stand a chance," Arven cut in with a glance at Juliana.

Nemona, as usual, was up to her usual antics—trying to push her rival into situations just to "train" her. Arven shook his head in exasperation.

"By the way, Arven, why are you with Gym Leader Dylan?" Nemona asked curiously.

"None of your business," Arven replied curtly before bowing to Dylan and heading back into the academy.

"He's still such a loner," Nemona said, rolling her eyes but letting it slide.

"Arven's actually a really good guy," Juliana chimed in with a tilt of her head.

"Yes, he is," Dylan agreed. Then, turning to Penny, he added, "Penny, your father is Peony, correct?"

"Huh? How do you know that, Gym Leader Dylan?"

Penny looked up in surprise, her curiosity piqued. She had been trying to stay out of the conversation until now.

"Let's just say I have access to certain information. If you get the chance, say hello to Peony and your uncle Chairman Rose for me. I'd like to visit Galar someday," Dylan said with a kind smile.

So Penny was Peony's daughter. Dylan's eyes glimmered briefly.

Peony, a former Champion of Galar and a Steel-type specialist, had resigned from the Elite Four after a falling-out with his brother, Rose, who was now the chairman of the Galar League.

In other words, Penny came from a very influential family.

Later that evening, after finding a hotel in Mesagoza, Dylan received a delivery containing three Poké Balls from his trusted associate.

"These will be great additions to the team," Dylan mused as he examined the Poké Balls.

He returned to his suite and released the Pokémon inside.

With a flash of light, three Pokémon appeared before him.

On the left was a Cottonee, a fluffy, round Pokémon with white cotton-like tufts on its head and underside. Its sides featured curled, leaf-like appendages.

The second was a Ferroseed, a Pokémon resembling a durian, its metallic body covered in protective spikes.

And lastly, a Grookey, a small monkey-like Pokémon with innocent, curious eyes.

These were the newest members of Dylan's Grass-type team—each one filled with potential!

"Hello, little ones. I'm Dylan, your new Trainer," he said warmly. His kind smile and the faint aura of his Viridian Power immediately put the three Pokémon at ease.

These three would surely charm his challengers. After all, green was the color of life—what could be healthier than that?

(End of Chapter)

# 200: Chapter 201:  Go Become the Kanto Champion!

"Ah~ Farewell, Paldea!"

On the plane, Lillie gazed out the window at the shrinking city of Mesagoza, excitement already building for her next visit.

This time, they hadn't had much time to properly explore, but Lillie felt content. She was confident they'd have more opportunities to return in the future.

"I hope Arven brings us some good news soon."

Dylan pondered as he leaned back in his seat. He had tasked Arven with gathering information from Ms. Raifort, the history teacher at Naranja Academy, about the Treasures of Ruin.

Raifort was an expert in historical research. Unlike those obsessed with the present or the future, she had an insatiable curiosity about the legends of the past.

Through her studies, she had gained extensive knowledge about the Treasures of Ruin, ancient Pokémon sealed away in Paldea's history. In the game, she was the one who revealed the location of their shrines to the protagonist.

Dylan believed Arven could manage the task, given that he was already a student at the academy with plenty of free time on his hands.

Rota Street.

By the afternoon, Reshiram descended gracefully in front of Rota Street.

"Ah~ Rota Street, we're back!"

Lillie looked at the familiar scenery with a joyful smile. She felt a deep sense of fulfillment, like she was returning home.

Unknowingly, Rota Street had become a place she considered home, though she reminded herself that she could never forget Alola, her true home.

Although, to be honest, it had been a while since she'd really thought about Alola...

"Let's head back and get some rest. The gym reopens tomorrow," Dylan said, chuckling as he ruffled Lillie's hair before recalling Reshiram.

They had been away for five days, meaning the Rota Gym had been closed for a full week. Dylan sighed inwardly—his freedom as a Gym Leader sometimes felt too flexible.

Officer Jenny had grumbled about this in their group chat multiple times, but with Chairman Charles's approval, there wasn't much she could do about Dylan's laid-back attitude.

"Ugh, nepotism! It's infuriating!"

"Wow! I knew it was you guys!"

A delighted voice called out from behind them.

Dylan turned in surprise to see two familiar young women approaching—Selene and Asha, both members of the Rota Gym.

But they weren't alone. Behind them stood a petite, golden-haired girl with a long ponytail, her expression a mix of nervousness and shyness.

"Selene, Asha, what are you two doing here?"

"We went exploring in Viridian Forest and made a surprising discovery," Asha said with a sweet smile.

"This is our new friend!" she added, gently nudging the golden-haired girl forward.

"Hello, Gym Leader Dylan... I'm Yellow. It's an honor to meet you," the girl said nervously, bowing politely as she introduced herself.

"Hello, I'm Dylan," he replied, raising an eyebrow. So this really is Yellow.

"Selene and Asha didn't cause you any trouble, did they?" Dylan asked, his tone playful.

"No, not at all! Selene and Asha helped me a lot. They even said they could help me find a good job," Yellow replied earnestly, her voice soft and timid.

Dylan turned his amused gaze toward Selene and Asha, who smiled sheepishly. They had clearly tried to coach Yellow before bringing her here, but she was too honest to play along.

"Did you two think my gym was a shelter?" Dylan said, shaking his head in exasperation.

"You want me to take in another child worker?"

Yellow's expression faltered, her face falling into a look of quiet sadness. Her family wasn't well-off, and she had hoped to find work to help ease their burden.

"Hehe~"

But Selene and Asha knew Dylan too well. They could tell from his tone that he wasn't truly angry.

"Gym Leader Dylan, Yellow is really special! She's just like you!" Selene said quickly, trying to convince him.

"Yellow, show Gym Leader Dylan what I mean."

"O-okay..."

Yellow hesitated, then stretched out her hand. A faint green glow flickered to life in her palm.

"Is that... the Viridian Power?"

Both Lillie and Ilene were stunned. As far as they knew, only Dylan and Champion Lance possessed this mysterious gift from Viridian Forest.

And yet, this simple, pure-hearted girl also had it.

"See? She's just like you, Gym Leader Dylan!" Selene exclaimed, slinging her arm around Yellow's shoulders with a cheeky grin.

Dylan had once told them that the Viridian Power was an incredibly rare blessing from nature itself. Anyone chosen by Viridian Forest was bound to be extraordinary.

"Well, this is unexpected," Dylan said, pretending to ponder. He walked over to Yellow, who was standing nervously, and extended his own hand. A faint green glow emanated from his palm.

Yellow's eyes widened in surprise and delight. "You... you have the same power as me!"

"There's no need to be nervous. You're someone blessed by nature too. Can you tell me your full name?" Dylan asked gently, crouching slightly to meet her gaze.

"My name is Yellow de Tokiwa Lily," she replied, her voice soft but sincere.

"Yellow from Viridian Forest, huh? That's a lovely name," Dylan said with a nod.

"Yellow, would you like to join my Rota Gym?"

Yellow looked uncertainly at Selene and Asha, who immediately started nodding furiously, motioning for her to agree.

"Don't mind those two troublemakers," Dylan said with a chuckle, shooting them a glare. They immediately straightened up, trying to look innocent.

"Yellow, even though you're still young, you should learn to make your own decisions and not let others influence you too much," Dylan advised.

Yellow hesitated for a moment before nodding. "Yes, Gym Leader Dylan, I'd like to join the Rota Gym."

"Good. I'll accept you as an employee. But more than that, I'd like you to become my apprentice," Dylan said warmly.

"Eh? Apprentice?"

"Yes. You can think of it as being my student. Selene here is my first apprentice," Dylan explained.

"Selene... you're an apprentice?" Yellow asked in surprise.

"Of course! Don't worry, Yellow. As your senior apprentice, I'll teach you everything!" Selene declared proudly, puffing out her chest.

Dylan couldn't help but laugh. "I'll warn you, though, Selene isn't exactly the best role model."

"I'll train you properly, Yellow. With your potential, you could even surpass Lance someday and become Kanto's Champion!"

Yellow's eyes widened in awe. "Kanto Champion?"

"Yes," Dylan said with a grin, patting her on the shoulder.

"One day, you'll achieve what your senior apprentice wouldn't dare to attempt."

From the sidelines, Selene rolled her eyes playfully. Dylan's really laying it on thick, huh?

Later that evening, Dylan treated everyone to a lavish meal at Rota Street's finest restaurant to celebrate Yellow joining the Rota Gym.

Sipping on a coconut drink, Dylan leaned back with a contented smile.

"Yellow has the same Viridian Power as me. I'm sure her future will be bright," he said to Lillie, who was seated beside him.

"She definitely has the potential to surpass Selene," he added with a chuckle.

Still, as he watched Yellow chatting with Selene and Asha, a faint worry crept into his mind.

Let's hope she doesn't end up as mischievous as Selene. I'll need to keep a close eye on this one.

(End of Chapter)
